### [1] [Learning the Platform, Not the Learner: Sequencing Shortcuts in the Offline Evaluation of Educational Recommender Systems and LLM Rankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33240)
*Youssef Jdidou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文指出教育推荐系统离线评测的"下一活动预测"任务被平台主导的结构规律（总体转移）大量解释：四种仅依赖上一活动的结构性预测器即可达到任何最佳方法准确率的0.81–1.00（中位0.95）。作者据此提出考虑课程结构的评测协议与检查清单，并发布Python包报告推荐器相对结构基线的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 教育推荐器通常以离线预测日志中"下一活动"来比较，但这些日志中的下一活动很大程度由平台（教师布置、导师选题、引擎选问）决定，而非学习者自主选择，导致评测可能高估个性化模型的价值、混淆"模仿平台"与"满足学习者需求"。因此需要检验结构规律能解释多少性能，并重新设计更贴近有用性的评测协议。

Method: 在来自5个平台的8个公开数据集上，比较四类结构性预测器（仅用上一活动信息）、10种个性化推荐器，以及0.5–7B参数的开源权重LLM排序器；采用两种评测协议。进一步构造负样本（随机负样本 vs 符合课程结构的合理负样本）、区分"训练中未见的转移步骤"与"学习者自主选择步骤"，并进行已知学习者需求的仿真实验，提出按结构不可能程度对测试步骤加权，提出syllabus-aware评测检查清单并发布Python包量化相对结构基线的lift。

Result: 结构性预测器达到最佳方法准确率的0.81–1.00（中位0.95），主要依靠总体转移而非课程顺序；最佳个性化推荐器在5个数据集上显著更优，NDCG@10提升0.007–0.102，Eedi上4次训练中3次显著。所有方法在未见转移步骤和学习者自选步骤上精度大幅下降。LLM排序器在随机负样本下看似表现不错，但仅略优于词匹配基线；在结构合理负样本下接近随机或明显落后于转移启发式，且与模型规模无关。仿真显示结构份额随平台决定步骤比例变化，离线准确率将"学习者需求oracle"排在"平台模仿者"之下。按结构不可能程度加权测试步骤可恢复与有用性的联系，并在8个数据集中7个上揭示出显著个性化信号。

Conclusion: 现有离线"下一活动预测"评测主要衡量对平台结构规律的模仿，而非对学习者需求的有用性；应使用syllabus-aware评测与结构加权协议来评估推荐器真实个性化价值，作者提供检查清单与开源Python包以报告相对结构基线的lift。

Abstract: Educational recommenders are usually compared offline, by how well they predict the next activity in logs of tutoring systems, learning applications and MOOCs, where the next activity is largely decided by the platform: teachers assign problem sets, tutors draw problems until mastery, engines select questions. On eight public datasets from five platforms, we compare four structural predictors, which use nothing about the learner beyond the last activity, with ten personalised recommenders and with open-weight LLM rankers from 0.5 to 7 billion parameters, under two protocols. The structural predictors reach between 0.81 and 1.00 of the best accuracy of any method (median 0.95), mostly through population transitions rather than the course order; the best personalised recommender is significantly better on five datasets, by 0.007 to 0.102 NDCG@10, and on Eedi in three of four training runs. Every method is far less accurate on steps along transitions never seen in training and on steps chosen by learners. LLM rankers look competent with random negatives but are never much better than a word-matching baseline, and with negatives that are plausible under the course structure they stay close to random or well behind the transition heuristic, whatever their size. In a simulation with known learner needs, the structural share tracks the share of steps decided by the platform, and offline accuracy ranks an oracle of the learners' needs below imitators of the platform. Weighting test steps by how unlikely the structure makes them restores much of the link with usefulness and, on the real data, reveals a significant personalisation signal on seven of the eight datasets. We propose a syllabus-aware evaluation checklist and release a Python package that reports the lift of any recommender over the structure.

</details>


### [2] [Multi-Modal Causal Disentanglement for Multi-Domain Sequential Audio Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33628)
*Yu Ren,Pengcheng Li,Weijun Xu,Hengzhong Zhou,Shufeng Ye,Qiang Xiao,Dongjing Wang,Chuanjiang Luo,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CAMAR多模态因果解耦框架，将用户表示分解为领域不变与领域特有分量以抑制负迁移，并用残差量化构建语义ID实现跨域语义耦合，在公开与工业数据集及网易云音乐线上A/B实验中均取得稳定增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多域序列推荐（MDSR）在异构音频内容（歌曲、歌单、播客）中面临两大障碍：一是跨异构域联合优化易产生负迁移，高流量域主导共享参数空间并压制弱域；二是传统基于ID的协同信号无法桥接语义相关但行为重叠稀少的域。

Method: 从因果视角将用户行为建模为领域不变偏好与领域特有偏好的联合结果；据此将用户表示因子化为不变分量与特有分量，并施加领域对抗不变性约束与正交约束，把跨域交互限制在不变子空间内以阻断有害迁移路径；同时通过残差量化从预训练的文本、视觉、音频特征中派生可训练的语义ID嵌入，实现面向推荐的端到端多模态适配；再用迁移网络为不活跃域补全用户表示以缓解跨域冷启动。

Result: 在公开数据集与工业数据集上均持续优于强基线；在网易云音乐（国内最大音乐流媒体平台之一）的线上A/B测试中，三个域的主要流量场景均取得一致增益，验证了其大规模多域音频推荐的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过因果解耦抑制负迁移、以多模态语义ID强化跨域语义耦合并辅以迁移网络解决冷启动，CAMAR为多域音频推荐提供了在大规模真实场景中有效的方案。

Abstract: Modern music platforms expose users to heterogeneous audio content domains such as songs, playlists, and podcasts. Multi-domain sequential recommendation (MDSR) seeks to exploit such cross-domain behavior, yet two obstacles persist in practice. First, joint optimization across heterogeneous domains is prone to negative transfer, with high-traffic domains dominating the shared parameter space and suppressing weaker domains. Second, conventional ID-based collaborative signals fail to bridge domains that are semantically related but exhibit little behavioral overlap. This paper presents CAMAR, a multi-modal causal disentanglement framework for multi-domain audio recommendation. From a causal perspective, user behavior is modeled as the joint outcome of a domain-invariant preference and domain-specific preferences. Guided by this view, CAMAR factorizes user representations into domain-invariant and domain-specific components and enforces domain adversarial invariance together with orthogonality, restricting cross-domain interaction to the invariant subspace and suppressing harmful transfer paths. To strengthen cross-domain semantic coupling, CAMAR derives trainable semantic-ID embeddings from pre-trained text, vision, and audio features through residual quantization, yielding an end-to-end multi-modal adaptation tailored to recommendation. A transfer network imputes user representations for inactive domains, mitigating cross-domain cold start. Experiments on both public and industrial datasets demonstrate consistent gains over strong baselines. In online A/B tests on NetEase Cloud Music, one of China's largest music streaming platforms, CAMAR delivers consistent gains across all three domains in their main traffic scenarios, validating its effectiveness in multi-domain audio recommendation at scale.

</details>


### [3] [Structure-Aware Multimodal Retrieval Under Data Scarcity, Privacy Constraints and Out-of-Domain Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33649)
*Argyrios Zafeiriou,Dionysios Koulouris,Ilias Maglogiannis*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种结构感知的图检索流水线：用版面几何与本地开源8B视觉语言模型恢复页面结构，在无标签、无训练对、数据不出本地硬件的条件下实现图谱检索，性能显著优于跨模态编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法医病理、牙科、地质等专家领域的参考图集检索面临三重约束：缺乏标签与训练对；内容常不能离开本地硬件；图像超出通用图文编码器的训练分布。现有跨模态检索方法难以满足。

Method: 引入基于几何的图文配对方法：向模型输入检测框的示意图（不含像素与文字），由其选出每张图片对应的图注；随后在图注层面进行检索。在三个私有语料（29本书、10,421张图、218条查询）上以盲评人工打分评估，对比词汇基线、生成式描述与五种图文编码器。

Result: 几何配对准确率在三领域分别为96%、92%、77%，优于两个文档解析器中较优者的76%、45%、53%；托管大模型使用示意图精度相当，但把坐标当文本输入损失22个点。图注检索nDCG@5为0.85、0.87、0.76，对比最佳跨模态编码器的0.54、0.51、0.27及生成描述的0.41、0.28、0.30；配对错误会传递至检索环节。

Conclusion: 该流水线以小规模本地模型、无需域内训练即实现高精度图检索，显著超越通用与生物医学图文编码器；其仅用几何的配对在五种方法中最准，且不向外部（含托管模型）暴露任何内容；代码与提示词已开源。

Abstract: Purpose: Figure retrieval from reference atlases in expert domains like forensic pathology, dentistry and geology must work without labels or training pairs, on content that often cannot leave local hardware, and on images outside the training data of image-text encoders. This work proposes a structure-aware pipeline that satisfies all three, retrieving figures through the page structure recovered by layout geometry and a local open-weight 8B VLM. Methods: A geometry-based pairing method is introduced, in which the model receives a schematic of the detected boxes and selects each picture's caption without access to pixels or text. Caption retrieval is evaluated on three private corpora (29 books, 10,421 figures, 218 queries) with blind human grading, against a lexical baseline, generated descriptions and five image-text encoders. Results: Geometric pairing was correct for 96%, 92% and 77% of pictures in the three domains, against 76%, 45% and 53% for the better of two document parsers. A hosted model given the schematic was equally accurate, while coordinates as text lost 22 points. Caption retrieval obtained an nDCG@5 of 0.85, 0.87 and 0.76, against 0.54, 0.51 and 0.27 for the best cross-modal encoder and 0.41, 0.28 and 0.30 for generated descriptions. Pairing errors carried over to retrieval. Conclusion: The proposed pipeline achieves accurate figure retrieval with small local models and no in-domain training, where general and biomedical image-text encoders fall far behind. Its geometry-only pairing is the most accurate of five methods and exposes no content, even to a hosted model. Code and prompts are released.

</details>


### [4] [OneQuery: Unified Sequence-to-Structure Generation for Multilingual E-commerce Query Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33653)
*Mingyu Zhang,Jiejie Qin,Jianhui Ji,Rong Xiao,Meishan Zhang,Xiaoyi Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 OneQuery 统一生成式框架，用电商领域大模型把语言识别、拼写纠错、翻译与 Query 打标四个子任务统一为受 schema 约束的 Sequence-to-Structure 自回归生成，并采用三阶段渐进训练与多任务强化学习对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言电商搜索需将跨语言、语言模式多样的用户查询转化为标准化语义表示以支撑检索与排序；传统 Query Understanding 系统存在误差累积、子任务独立优化忽略相互关联、维护成本高等问题。

Method: 基于电商领域专用大语言模型构建统一生成式框架；将语言识别、拼写纠错、翻译、Query 打标四个子任务重构为由预定义 schema 约束的 Sequence-to-Structure 自回归生成过程；设计三阶段渐进式训练流水线，并结合多任务强化学习对齐。

Result: 在所有任务上相较基线取得一致提升，且在不同骨干模型规模下均保持有效性；在 AliExpress 电商搜索场景的线上实验进一步验证了其工业部署价值。

Conclusion: 统一生成式建模可有效缓解误差累积与子任务割裂问题，降低维护成本，并具备多语言电商搜索场景下的实际落地能力。

Abstract: Multilingual e-commerce search requires robust query understanding to convert cross-lingual user queries with varied linguistic patterns into standardized semantic representations that support retrieval and ranking. However, traditional Query Understanding systems suffer from error accumulation, separately optimized subtasks that ignore their mutual correlations, and high maintenance costs. To address these issues, this paper proposes OneQuery, a unified generative framework for multilingual query understanding based on domain-specific large language models in e-commerce. We reformulate four subtasks, namely Language Recognition, Spelling Correction, Translation, and Query Tagging, into a unified Sequenceto-Structure autoregressive generation process constrained by predefined schemas, and design a three-stage progressive training pipeline with multi-task reinforcement learning alignment. OneQuery demonstrates consistent gains over baselines across all tasks and remains effective across different backbone sizes, and online experiments in the AliExpress e-commerce search scenario further confirm its practical value for industrial deployment.

</details>


### [5] [Privacy-Aware Adaptive Client Selection for Federated Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33890)
*Ali Shams Saryazd,Abolfazl Gandomi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 PACS-FedRAG 在线选择器，按扰动语义画像与证据反馈、延迟、载荷成本、探索选择客户端；三选十配置下证据重叠 0.372、答案准确率 0.287，查询接收方减少 70%，但存在重叠上限 0.375、基线不可比与隐私保障不足等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦 RAG 需从分布式语料检索证据，但广播查询到所有客户端会加重检索负担并暴露查询；因此在降低通信与暴露的同时需判断质量是否保持。

Method: PACS-FedRAG：在线客户端选择器，输入扰动后的语义画像，并结合证据反馈、延迟、载荷成本与探索项；十客户端中每轮选 3 个。评估含端到端基准与仅检索评估（复用全广播基线、采用不同融合平局策略）。

Result: 证据重叠 0.372，答案准确率 0.287。不相交客户端语料上的排序融合带来 0.375 重叠上限，削弱其作为相关性度量的解释力；准确率低于固定标签诊断值 0.645；原始生成器输出缺失，无法核验解析器。接收方减少 70%。

Conclusion: 画像扰动不提供查询或段落机密性。需校正基线并引入答案级评估，方能判断选择性检索是否保持质量。

Abstract: Federated retrieval-augmented generation retrieves evidence from distributed corpora. However, broadcasting queries to all clients increases the retrieval burden and query exposure. We present Privacy-Aware Adaptive Client Selection for Federated Retrieval-Augmented Generation (PACS-FedRAG), an online selector that takes into account perturbed semantic profiles along with evidence feedback, latency, payload cost, and exploration. In a ten-client setting where three clients are chosen, the archived end-to-end benchmark reports an evidence overlap of 0.372 and an answer accuracy of 0.287. The retrieval-only evaluation, on the other hand, reuses a full-broadcast baseline with a different fusion tie-breaking policy. Rank fusion across disjoint client corpora imposes an overlap ceiling of 0.375, limiting its interpretation as a relevance measure. Answer accuracy is below the fixed-label diagnostic of 0.645, and raw generator responses are unavailable for parser verification. Corrected baselines and a new answer-level evaluation are needed to assess whether quality is preserved. Selecting three clients reduces the number of query recipients by 70%. Profile perturbation does not provide confidentiality of queries or passages.

</details>


### [6] [MulCLIP: A Multi-level Alignment Framework for Enhancing Fine-grained Long-context CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34083)
*Chau Truong,Hieu Ta Quang,Zhenzhen Liu,Dung D. Le*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MulCLIP：一种无需区域提议的端到端多层级对齐框架，直接利用长文本的天然结构（长描述、短摘要、子描述），通过全局对比、词-块重建、子描述聚合块三种兼容训练信号实现视觉-语言对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等视觉-语言模型因在短标题上训练，难以处理冗长、细致的描述；现有方法借助区域提议将视觉区域与句子对齐，但部署成本高昂。

Method: 提出MulCLIP端到端多层级对齐框架，不使用区域提议，而是对齐不同文本粒度并设计相互兼容的训练信号：(1) 面向长描述与短摘要的全局对比对齐；(2) 在局部校准特征上进行Word-Patch Reconstruction，建模样本内词-块语义；(3) Subcaption-Aggregated Patch对齐，实现富含上下文的子描述视觉接地。

Result: 在涵盖不同描述长度的多个基准上取得一致的性能提升；消融实验证实各目标相互补充，并共同改进整体框架。

Conclusion: 多层级文本粒度的兼容对齐可在无区域提议的前提下有效提升长描述场景的视觉-语言对齐性能，兼顾效果与部署效率。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as CLIP excel at image-text alignment but struggle with long, detailed descriptions due to training on short captions. Recent methods address this limitation using region proposals to align visual regions with sentences, but at substantial deployment cost. We present MulCLIP, an end-to-end multi-level alignment framework that directly exploits natural long-text structure without region proposals. Instead of simply stacking objectives, MulCLIP aligns different textual granularities through compatible training signals: global contrastive alignment for long captions and short summaries, Word-Patch Reconstruction over locally calibrated features for within-sample word-patch semantics, and Subcaption-Aggregated Patch alignment for context-rich subcaption grounding. Experiments on benchmarks spanning varying caption lengths show consistent gains, and ablations confirm that the proposed objectives are complementary and jointly improve the overall framework. Code will be released at https://github.com/truongchau2602/MulCLIP.

</details>


### [7] [RecField: Embedding Recommendations into Spatial Movie Organization as Affordances for Serendipitous Encounters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34330)
*Kenta Oku*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究提出 RecField——一种空间化电影整理界面，将推荐作为环境可供性（而非主任务）嵌入用户活动，并通过组间实验（N=57）在环境层与单条电影层评估其促进偶然性发现（serendipity）的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有偶然性推荐研究多聚焦算法输出本身，而偶然性被视为由算法结果与系统功能共同塑造的用户体验。作者希望把推荐从"用户主动追求的目标"转变为"在其他活动中创造发现机会的环境可供性"，以考察嵌入式推荐如何影响用户自发探索与再评价。

Method: 提出 RecField：用户将电影自由排布为收藏区域（collection zones），系统为每个收藏提供基于属性的推荐列表，并按不同推荐视角（perspective）添加标签。开展组间实验（N=57），前景任务为创建与整理个人电影档案，比较有/无推荐条件下用户在整理过程中产生的发现；在环境层（整体偶然性感知）与单条电影层（唤起观看欲望、拓宽偏好、新颖性、意外性）两级进行评分分析，并区分用户是否直接从推荐列表添加。

Result: 推荐条件对环境层整体偶然性感知无显著影响，但"邂逅相关"维度的评分略高（效应量小）。在单条电影层，推荐条件下的收藏项在"激发观看欲望"和"拓宽偏好"上评分显著更高，而在新颖性与意外性上无显著差异。值得注意：推荐条件下被评价的收藏项中仅14%直接来自推荐列表；即便将分析限制为通过其他途径添加的收藏项，评分差异的效应量仍相近。

Conclusion: 嵌入式推荐既可通过条目被直接采纳产生作用，也可通过促使用户自主探索与对已选条目的再评价间接促进偶然性体验；应同时在环境层与条目层评估偶然性，且仅依赖直接推荐采纳率会低估推荐的实际价值。

Abstract: Research on serendipity-oriented recommender systems has focused on algorithms that recommend unexpected yet useful items. However, serendipity is increasingly viewed as a user experience shaped by both algorithmic outputs and system features. This study positions recommendations not as the primary goal users pursue but as an environmental affordance that creates opportunities for discovery during other activities. We propose RecField, a spatial movie organization interface where users freely arrange movies into collection zones. For each collection, RecField provides attribute-based recommendation lists labeled according to their recommendation perspectives. A between-subjects experiment ($N$ = 57) examined the effects of recommendations on discoveries made during a foreground task of creating and organizing personal movie profiles. Although recommendations did not significantly affect environment-level perceived serendipity, ratings of encounter-related aspects tended to be higher, with small effect sizes. At the individual-movie level, favorites in the recommendation condition received significantly higher ratings for stimulating the desire to watch and broadening preferences, but not for novelty or unexpectedness. Notably, only 14\% of evaluated favorites in the recommendation condition were added directly from recommendation lists. Differences in ratings showed similar effect sizes even when the analysis was restricted to favorites added through other routes. These findings suggest that embedded recommendations can contribute to serendipitous experiences both directly through item acceptance and indirectly by prompting users' own exploration and re-evaluation. They also highlight the value of evaluating serendipity at both the environment and item levels and the limitations of relying solely on direct recommendation acceptance rates.

</details>


### [8] [ConTra: A Training Framework for Effective Contextual Retrieval via Prefix Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34366)
*Jiajun Hou,Jingbang Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出 ConTra，一个基于强化学习的轻量级前缀生成器训练框架，为每个文本块生成可跨查询复用的上下文前缀，兼顾向量检索与重排序；同时发布 RealContextBench 基准，ConTra-2B 以更小模型、更快索引速度超越 9B 提示式基线与 PPLX-4B 上下文编码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文档需切分为独立索引的块以支持检索，但切块会使文本脱离其标识性上下文。现有做法两难：将上下文仅编码进块向量的方式无法被文本重排序器利用；而提示式前缀虽同时服务于向量检索与重排序，却在索引阶段生成成本高，且效果可能不及上下文编码器。因此需要一种低成本、可复用、且对两类检索环节都有效的上下文注入方式。

Method: 提出 ConTra（Context That Travels）强化学习框架，训练轻量级前缀生成器。奖励由三部分构成：冻结的嵌入模型与重排序模型的反馈、事实性校验、长度控制。索引构建阶段为每个块生成一个前缀，存储增强文本及其向量，从而在多个查询间复用。评测方面构建 RealContextBench，包含五个应用集合与三种受控条件，用于隔离干扰文本的影响，考察跨工作负载与干扰下的上下文检索表现。

Result: 在 ConTEB 与 RealContextBench 上，ConTra-2B 优于使用 Anthropic 方法提示的 9B 基线和 PPLX-4B 上下文编码器，总体最佳分数领先最多 3.15 分；短文档索引速度分别为二者的 7.74 倍和 2.15 倍。仅在 ConTEB 上后训练，经重排序后在 RealContextBench 上仍领先各独立方法，且相比提示式 2B 基线对随机与主题相近干扰的敏感性更低。

Conclusion: 以强化学习训练的轻量前缀生成器可在索引期一次性生成、跨查询复用，在效果与效率上同时优于更大模型规模的提示式方案与专用上下文编码器，并具备较好的泛化性与抗干扰鲁棒性。代码、数据集与模型已开源。

Abstract: Data management systems organize large text collections for tasks such as finding relevant studies and extracting contract clauses. Systems make long documents searchable by splitting them into independently indexed chunks. Chunking can separate text from its identifying details. The indexing challenge is to keep each chunk independently searchable while preserving the context needed to find and rank it. Existing methods either encode that context in chunk vectors or attach it as a text prefix. Context encoded only in vectors is unavailable to text rerankers. Prompted prefixes serve both vector search and reranking, but are costly to generate at indexing time and can underperform contextual encoders. We propose ConTra (Context That Travels), a reinforcement learning framework for training lightweight prefix generators. Its reward combines feedback from frozen embedding and reranking models with factual verification and length control. ConTra generates one prefix per chunk during index construction and stores the augmented text and its embedding for reuse across queries. To evaluate contextual retrieval across workloads and under interference, we introduce RealContextBench, a benchmark with five application collections and three controlled conditions that isolate the effect of distracting text. On ConTEB and RealContextBench, ConTra-2B outperforms the prompted 9B baseline using Anthropic's method and the PPLX-4B contextual encoder, with a margin of up to 3.15 points over their best overall scores, while indexing short documents 7.74 times and 2.15 times as fast as those baselines, respectively. Post-trained only on ConTEB, it leads the standalone methods on RealContextBench after reranking and is less sensitive than the prompted 2B baseline to random and topically similar distractors. Code, datasets, and trained models are available at https://github.com/SStan1/ConTra.

</details>


### [9] [Grounding User Preferences in Shared Multimodal Concepts for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34436)
*Ao Xu,Jinhui Huang,Wenqiang Tang,Tiehua Mei,Hongru Hou,Hengrui Chen,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 MKGE 多模态知识图谱框架，将用户偏好中反复出现的语义因素表示为规范可寻址概念，从而实现跨物品的偏好证据累积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态推荐虽借助视觉与文本提升物品理解，但用户偏好仍以绑定于单个物品的隐式信号编码，反复出现的语义因素缺乏可跨物品共享的持久身份，导致证据无法聚合。

Method: MKGE 从物品图像与标题归纳出域自适应概念词表，将每个用户交互历史锚定到受支持的概念上，并联合建模直接的用户—物品证据与概念中介的偏好信号；通过将反复语义转化为共享坐标，使不同物品的证据围绕同一偏好因素累积。

Result: 在三个公开基准上，MKGE 在全部六项指标上稳定优于协同过滤、知识图谱、多模态及近期 LLM/MLLM 推荐器；消融与受控实验表明规范概念身份与个性化用户—概念对应关系至关重要，其中移除或重连用户图对低活跃用户的性能下降尤为显著。

Conclusion: 规范化的多模态概念可作为有效的偏好建模与迁移单元；代码、构建的知识图谱与提示词已开源。

Abstract: Multimodal recommendation has substantially advanced item understanding by incorporating rich visual and textual content. Yet user preferences are still commonly encoded as implicit signals tied to individual items, leaving recurring semantic factors without persistent identities that can be shared across items. We introduce MKGE, a multimodal knowledge graph framework that represents these factors as canonical, addressable concepts. MKGE induces a domain-adaptive concept vocabulary from item images and titles, grounds each user's interaction history in supported concepts, and jointly models direct user-item evidence and concept-mediated preference signals. By turning recurring semantics into shared coordinates, MKGE enables evidence from different items to accumulate around the same preference factors. Extensive experiments on three public benchmarks demonstrate that MKGE consistently outperforms collaborative, knowledge-graph, multimodal, and recent LLM/MLLM-based recommenders across all six reported metrics. Controlled studies further demonstrate the importance of canonical concept identities and personalized user-concept correspondence, with especially pronounced gains over removing or rewiring the user graph for low-activity users. These results establish canonical multimodal concepts as effective units for preference modeling and transfer. Code, constructed knowledge graphs, and prompts are available at https://github.com/Garden1a-X/MKGE.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

### [10] [Barron Smoothness for Layers in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34805)
*G. Welper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将深度网络的逼近误差界分解为"选取各层最优无穷宽度极限"与"对全部层做最优离散化得到有限网络"两步，并给出期望意义下的刻画与优化景观分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为深度神经网络建立可控制的逼近误差上界，需要同时处理网络宽度趋于无穷时的表达能力与有限规模网络离散化带来的误差，而这两类问题此前往往被混在一起处理。

Method: 把误差界问题拆成两个子问题：先为每一层确定最优的无穷宽度极限，再对所有层进行最优离散化以获得有限规模网络；在期望意义下证明第二步问题等价于Barron范数的逐层推广；同时对损失/误差的优化景观进行分析。

Result: 证明在期望意义下，最优离散化问题可由逐层推广的Barron范数等价刻画；并表明局部极小值满足一种"均匀分布（等分）原则"，与自适应样条及有限元逼近中的类似思想相对应。

Conclusion: 逐层推广的Barron范数可作为控制深度网络离散化误差的自然工具，且该误差目标的局部最优具有等分结构，为深度网络的宽度/离散化设计与误差分析提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In order to bound approximation errors for deep neural networks, we split the problem into two steps: First the selection of optimal infinite width limits for each layer and second an optimal discretization of all layers to obtain finite sized networks. We show that in expectation, the latter problem is equivalently characterized by a layer wise generalization of Barron norms. Moreover, we provide an analysis of the optimization landscape and show that local minima are characterized by an equidistribution principle akin to similar ideas in adaptive spline and finite element approximation.

</details>


### [11] [Attributing Agentic Failure in Reasoning Vision-Language Models with Interactive 2D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33162)
*Pranav Guruprasad,Sean Rivera,Helen Lu,Arushi Jain,Hangliang Ren,Harshvardhan Sikka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出用于归因分析而非排名的交互式迷宫环境 MultiNet v2.0-Gridworld，通过可独立增删的机制模块与 BFS 精确最优步数，将智能体失败拆解为不同类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准仅报告成功率，混淆了多种本质不同的失败原因（误读环境、不理解组件运作方式、操作顺序错误、无法从错误中恢复），而每种失败对应不同改进方向，因此需要一个以"归因"为目标的评测环境。

Method: 构建网格迷宫环境，支持四类机制（钥匙-门对、开关-闸门对、干扰项与诱饵、以及上述机制的有序组合）独立增删，同时保持渲染器、动作空间与评分函数不变。在位置、朝向、库存与机制状态的联合状态空间上做广度优先搜索，得到每个实例的精确最优动作数，用于设定难度并评分未完成回合。评测协议固定为 15 个留出迷宫、540 个回合；另在 50 个迷宫上评测三个视觉语言模型。

Result: 在 50 个迷宫、150 个回合中，三个视觉语言模型仅成功 6 次；50 个迷宫中有 45 个无任何模型解出；所有包含开关-闸门机制的回合均未被解出。

Conclusion: 该环境能够将智能体能力缺口定位到具体机制维度，揭示了现有模型在机制理解与有序组合任务上的系统性缺陷，为可归因的智能体评测提供了可控实验平台。

Abstract: Agentic benchmarks report a success rate that confounds distinct causes of failure: misreading the environment, never working out how components of the environment operates, operating the components in the wrong order, and failing to recover from a mistake, each of which calls for a different fix. We present an early preview of MultiNet v2.0-Gridworld - an interactive maze environment built for attribution rather than ranking. Four different types of mechanisms, namely key-door pairs, switch-gate pairs, distractors and decoys, and ordered combinations of these mechanisms, can be added, modified, or removed independently while the renderer, the action space, and the scoring function stay fixed. A breadth-first search over the joint state of position, facing direction, inventory, and mechanism state gives every instance an exact optimal action count, which sets its difficulty and grades unfinished episodes. We fix the evaluation protocol on 15 held-out mazes over 540 episodes, then evaluate three vision-language models on 50 mazes. They solve 6 of 150 episodes, 45 of the 50 mazes were not solved by any model, and no episode containing a switch-gate mechanism was solved.

</details>


### [12] [Quantum Machine Learning for Signature Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33163)
*Nabila,Md Farhan Shahriyar,Nowshin Tabassum Puspa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出量子机器学习流水线用于离线手写签名伪造检测，混合模型单折准确率92.62%，五折均值91.26%±1.13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线手写签名是法律与金融文件中的主要行为生物特征，但静态栅格图像缺失笔压、时序与笔顺信息，导致熟练伪造检测困难。

Method: 基于Kaggle公开二分类图像语料，将ZZ特征映射量子核与KNN、SVM、随机森林三种经典学习器组合；并进一步提出混合模型：以冻结的EfficientNetB0提取1280维特征，经量子兼容的主成分投影降至量子比特寄存器规模子空间，再用最近邻规则判定类别。保持可迁移至NISQ硬件的量子嵌入步骤，并进行六种量子变体的受控消融、混淆矩阵、t-SNE投影及分类别精确率/召回率/F1分析。

Result: 单折留出测试准确率92.62%；分层五折交叉验证平均准确率91.26%，标准差1.13%。

Conclusion: 量子核与深度特征降维结合的混合流水线在离线签名伪造检测上表现稳定，且量子嵌入部分具备NISQ硬件可移植性。

Abstract: Offline handwritten signatures remain a primary behavioural biometric in legal and financial documents. Detecting skilled forgeries from a static raster image is hard because pen pressure, timing and stroke order are missing. This work studies a family of quantum machine learning pipelines for offline signature forgery detection on a public Kaggle corpus of images split into two classes. We pair a ZZ feature map quantum kernel with three classical learners: K-Nearest Neighbours, Support Vector Machine and Random Forest. We then propose a deeper hybrid that uses EfficientNetB0 as a frozen feature extractor, reduces the resulting 1280 dimensional representation to a qubit register sized subspace through a quantum compatible principal component projection and decides class membership with a nearest neighbour rule. On a single held out test fold the proposed hybrid reaches 92.62 percent accuracy. On stratified five fold cross validation the same pipeline yields a mean accuracy of 91.26 percent with a standard deviation of 1.13 percent. The pipeline keeps a quantum embedding step that is portable to NISQ hardware. We report a controlled ablation across six quantum variants, confusion matrices, a t SNE projection and per class precision, recall and F1 score.

</details>


### [13] [Code availability is not enough: schemas for documenting and extending data analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33167)
*H. Sherry Zhang,Roger D. Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种用自然语言"决策集合"表示的 schema 作为手稿与代码之间的中间表示，LLM 可据此生成并校验分析脚本，并支持扩展为 multiverse 备选分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复现结果不等于理解决策链条：代码把决策与实现细节混杂，手稿只陈述主要决策且散落各处、遗漏次要决策；读者想问"换一种决策结果会怎样"，但实操困难。

Method: 以自然语言决策集合构建 schema，令 LLM 直接生成与验证分析脚本；通过 alternative file 将单一分析扩展为可比较的 multiverse；在两个应用（堆叠吸烟数据的回归分析、many-analysts 足球研究）中演示，后者无 schema 时用作可复现性检查。

Result: 第一个应用中 schema 复现了已发表的估计，审稿人建议的替代方案可由 alternative file 指定并自动转化为代码；第二个应用中工作流充当可复现性检验。

Conclusion: schema 作为手稿与脚本间的中间表示，提供了分析的自足性描述，并从读者视角使其可扩展。

Abstract: A data analysis involves a sequence of decisions, from data cleaning to model specification, each of which can affect the reported result. Reproducing that result is not the same as understanding the sequence of decisions that produced it and the reasons for each: the code mixes those decisions with implementation details, while the manuscript states the major decisions and their rationale but scatters them across its sections and often leaves out the minor ones. A reader may then ask how the result would change under a different decision: the question itself is easy to pose, but implementing it is often non-trivial in practice. In this work, we propose a schema that represents a data analysis as a collection of decisions in natural language, from which large language models (LLMs) can generate and validate analysis scripts directly. This workflow can also expand a single analysis into a full multiverse of alternatives, specified in an alternative file, ready for comparison. We demonstrate the proposed workflow in two applications. In the first, we construct a schema for a published regression analysis of heaped smoking data and show that it reproduces the reported estimates, and that a reviewer-suggested alternative can be specified through the alternative file and turned into code automatically. In the second, where no schema is provided, we show how the workflow serves instead as a reproducibility check, applied to the many-analysts football study. As an intermediate representation between the manuscript and code scripts, our schema gives a self-contained account of an analysis and makes it extensible from a reader's perspective.

</details>


### [14] [Temporal Exposure to Byzantine Vulnerability Under Intermittent Participation in Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33168)
*Sasa Maric Robert Abbas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"动态参与暴露"(DPE)概念,刻画全局拜占庭比例低于聚合规则容忍阈值、但单轮参与客户端集合却违反该假设的现象,将拜占庭鲁棒性由静态群体属性转化为随机、时序属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒聚合分析通常假设拜占庭比例是全局、静态的群体性质,忽视了客户端参与波动。间歇性参与会使某一轮的活跃集合拜占庭占比偏离全局比例,从而在"全局安全"的前提下产生单轮脆弱性。

Method: 形式化定义 DPE;在随机参与模型下推导单轮暴露概率,分析活跃集规模对观测拜占庭比例方差的影响;进一步刻画多轮暴露的累积效应,并考虑仅在有利轮次选择性参与的对手模型。

Result: 间歇参与并不必然导致聚合失败,但会带来非零的单轮暴露概率,且暴露可跨轮次累积;活跃集越小,观测拜占庭比例的波动越大、暴露风险越高;选择性参与对手可显著放大暴露。

Conclusion: 拜占庭鲁棒性是随机且时序的性质而非静态总体性质;给出基于最小活跃集约束、时序监控、参与历史和自适应聚合的设计建议以缓解 DPE 风险。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the effect of participation variability on per-round Byzantine vulnerability. We formalise Dynamic Participation Exposure (DPE), a phenomenon in which the global Byzantine fraction remains below an aggregation rule's tolerance threshold, while a participating client set violates, or approaches violation of, the assumptions required for robust aggregation. Under stochastic participation, we derive the probability of per round exposure and analyse how active-set size affects the variability of the observed Byzantine fraction. We further examine the accumulation of exposure over multiple rounds and consider adversaries that selectively participate during favourable rounds. Our analysis shows that intermittent participation does not automatically imply aggregation failure. Rather, it transforms Byzantine robustness from a static population-level property into a stochastic and temporal property. To mitigate this risk, we outline actionable design recommendations based on minimum active-set constraints, temporal monitoring, participation history, and adaptive aggregation.

</details>


### [15] [Geophysics-informed invertible operator network for Bayesian geophysical inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33178)
*Yangwen Zhang,Xintong Wang,Xiaofei Guan,Peng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出地球物理信息可逆算子网络（GI-ION），在深度算子学习框架中嵌入可逆神经网络（INN）作为branch网络，实现高效贝叶斯地球物理反演与条件采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球物理反问题因观测稀疏和测量噪声而本质病态：MCMC等采样贝叶斯方法需大量正演模拟，计算代价高；数据驱动机器学习方法在训练数据有限时难以给出物理一致的解。

Method: GI-ION将INN嵌入深度算子学习框架，INN作为branch网络，将模型参数变换为表征正演解的branch系数与刻画反问题不确定性的隐变量；通过学习正演算子并强制branch系数与隐变量统计独立，从学得的条件近似中高效采样，仅需稀疏边界观测。

Result: 在三个代表性案例上验证：一维面波频散、二维合成随机场走时层析及地震切片模型；数值实验显示在不同问题设置下性能稳健，条件采样高效。

Conclusion: GI-ION可在稀疏观测下兼顾物理一致性、不确定量化与计算效率，为地球物理贝叶斯反演提供可行方案。

Abstract: Geophysical inverse problems are inherently ill-posed because of sparse observations and measurement noise. Classical sampling-based Bayesian inference methods, such as Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC), are computationally prohibitive because they require many forward simulations. Data-driven machine-learning methods often struggle to produce physically consistent solutions when training data are limited. To address these issues, we propose a geophysics-informed invertible operator network (GI-ION) that integrates an invertible neural network (INN) within a deep operator-learning framework for efficient Bayesian geophysical inversion. The INN serves as the branch network and transforms the model parameters into branch coefficients that represent the forward solution and a latent variable that captures uncertainty in the inverse problem. By learning the forward operator and enforcing statistical independence between the branch coefficients and the latent variable, GI-ION enables efficient sampling from a learned conditional approximation, even with sparse boundary observations. We validated GI-ION on three representative cases: 1D surface-wave dispersion and travel-time tomography for a 2D synthetic random-field model and a seismic-slice model. Numerical experiments demonstrate robust performance across diverse problem settings and efficient conditional sampling.

</details>


### [16] [Deep Learning-Based Mesoscopic Emission Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33179)
*Abdelkader Dib,Milos Balac,Antonio Sciarretta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于GRU的深度学习排放模型DEP，在序列化的路段级输入上直接预测CO₂与NOx，兼顾微观精度与宏观效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通排放模型存在宏观模型（计算高效但依赖平均速度、误差系统性）与微观模型（精度高但大规模仿真计算不可行）之间的固有权衡，缺乏兼顾精度与效率的介观方案。

Method: 以真实GNSS速度曲线与微观排放模型MOBSim耦合生成超百万条合成行程数据训练GRU模型；输入为路段级序列信息（车辆属性、道路属性、环境温度），直接预测CO₂与NOx；集成进MATSim工作流并应用于法兰西岛区域。

Result: 与混合介观-微观参考模型SPG-M相比，CO₂误差0.35%、NOx误差1.87%（含冷/热启动场景），运行时间仅为2.2%；对遥感实测数据验证显示DEP在Euro 2-6柴油车NOx水平上吻合良好，而COPERT、HBEFA系统性低估；并能刻画冷启动排放的空间分布与城市密集区热点。

Conclusion: 基于深度学习的介观框架可在极小计算代价下达到微观级精度，支持不依赖预计算排放因子库的可扩展、高分辨率城市排放建模。

Abstract: Traffic-related emissions are a major contributor to urban air pollution. However, existing emission models face a fundamental trade-off: macroscopic models are computationally efficient but rely on average speeds, leading to systematic errors, while microscopic models achieve high accuracy but are computationally prohibitive for large scale simulations. To bridge this gap, this study introduces Direct Emission Prediction (DEP), a Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU)-based deep learning model. DEP was trained on over one million synthetic trips generated by coupling real-world GNSS speed profiles with the microscopic emission model MOBSim. By processing sequential link-level inputs, including vehicle characteristics, road attributes, and ambient temperature, DEP predicts CO$_2$ and NOx emissions. We integrated DEP into a MATSim-based workflow and applied it to the Ile-de-France region. DEP matches the accuracy of SPG-M, a hybrid mesoscopic-microscopic reference model, within 0.35% for CO$_2$ and 1.87% for NOx across both hot and cold-start scenarios, while reducing computational runtime to just 2.2%. Furthermore, validation against empirical remote sensing data confirms that DEP accurately reproduces real-world NOx emission levels across Euro 2-6 diesel vehicles, whereas traditional macroscopic tools like COPERT and HBEFA systematically underestimate them. Finally, DEP effectively captures the spatial distribution of cold-start emissions, identifying key hotspots in dense urban areas. This work demonstrates that deep learning-based mesoscopic frameworks can achieve microscopic accuracy at a fraction of the computational cost, enabling scalable, high-resolution urban emission modeling without relying on pre-computed emission factor databases.

</details>


### [17] [Neighbor Crowding Predicts Feature Stability in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33182)
*Denis Federico Lim,Naveen Dasari,George Flint,Kevin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明TopK SAE中跨种子特征稳定性可由单次训练得到的六个廉价统计量高度预测，其中"邻域拥挤度"是最强单一预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE用于将语言模型的多义特征分解为稀疏可解释特征，但难以区分哪些学习到的特征是稳定的、哪些只是初始化造成的伪影。常规做法是训练多个随机种子并用余弦相似度比较，但计算代价高昂，因此作者希望找到无需多次训练即可识别稳定特征的方法。

Method: 考察不同训练token规模下的跨种子特征稳定性，并基于单次训练运行计算六个计算代价低廉的统计量，用其预测跨种子稳定性；重点分析字典的几何性质（特征在单位球上的分布密度，即邻域拥挤度）。

Result: 在TopK SAE中，跨种子特征稳定性可由这六个廉价统计量高度预测；邻域拥挤度（neighbor crowding）是最强的单一预测因子，位于单位球稠密区域的特征比孤立特征更可能稳定。

Conclusion: 稳定特征无需通过训练多个种子来识别，单次训练即可通过廉价统计量高效预测特征稳定性，显著降低识别稳定特征的计算成本。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) offer an approach to identify language model representations by breaking apart polysemantic features into more sparse, interpretable ones. Yet, it is challenging to identify which of the learned features are actually stable, rather than artifacts of initialization. One approach is to train multiple SAE seeds, and compare the learned features across seeds by cosine similarity, but this is notably costly. This raises the question of whether stable features can be identified without the computational expense of training multiple seeds. In this work, we examine cross-seed feature stability across differing levels of training tokens, and demonstrate that in TopK SAEs, it is highly predictable using six computationally cheap statistics derived from a single training run. Neighbor crowding, a geometric property of the dictionary, is the strongest single predictor, with features that sit in dense regions of the unit sphere more likely to be stable than isolated ones.

</details>


### [18] [REI: Learning Ideation from Repository Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33183)
*Muyu Zou,Xiang Feng,Yufei Xu,Shanshan Ye,Zulong Chen,Lingyu Mu,Jinxin Hu,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 REI 框架，从代码仓库演化中学习可复用的 ideation 策略；并针对语义多样性坍缩问题提出 SPOD 去偏对齐机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着 AI 系统具备执行与评估实验的能力，ideation（构思/提出假设）成为自动化科研与工程迭代的瓶颈。真实场景中有效想法稀疏且验证成本高，直接在线探索效率低。作者观察到仓库演化（代码变更=技术假设，后续修订/采纳/复用=效用信号）是可扩展的技术经验来源。

Method: 1) REI：Repository Evolution-driven Ideation 框架，从仓库演化中学习可复用的 ideation 策略，将领域特定技术经验注入模型参数，降低对高成本试错的依赖。2) 揭示质量驱动 ideation 优化中的隐性失效模式——语义多样性坍缩（词汇多样但方法族集中）。3) SPOD（Semantic Prior Optimization and Debiasing）：显式化方法身份、校正类别级奖励偏差、抑制同方法族内冗余积累同时保留强代表样本的保多样性对齐机制。

Result: 在公开基准与真实工业场景上，ideation 质量、方法多样性、跨任务泛化与长尾探索均有提升。

Conclusion: 仓库演化可作为可扩展的 ideation 经验来源；质量驱动的优化需显式引入语义层多样性约束与去偏，才能避免方法族收敛并提升泛化与长尾探索能力。

Abstract: As AI systems become increasingly capable of executing and evaluating experiments, ideation is emerging as a bottleneck in automated scientific and engineering iteration. In many real-world domains, useful ideas are sparse and their validation is costly, making direct online exploration inefficient. We observe that repository evolution provides a scalable source of reusable technical experience: code changes record concrete technical hypotheses, while subsequent revisions, adoption, and reuse provide signals about their utility. Based on this observation, we propose REI, a Repository Evolution-driven Ideation framework that learns reusable ideation policies from repository evolution, injecting domain-specific technical experience into model parameters and reducing reliance on costly trial-and-error. We further identify a hidden failure mode in quality-driven ideation optimization: semantic diversity collapse. Although generated ideas can remain lexically diverse, they increasingly concentrate in a small number of dominant method families, limiting cross-task generalization and long-tail exploration. To address this problem, we introduce Semantic Prior Optimization and Debiasing (SPOD), a diversity-preserving alignment mechanism that makes method identity explicit, corrects category-level reward bias, and limits redundant accumulation within the same method family while retaining strong representatives. Experiments on public benchmarks and a real-world industrial setting demonstrate improvements in ideation quality, method diversity, cross-task generalization, and long-tail exploration.

</details>


### [19] [Semantic Density Does Not Predict Agent-Memory Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33184)
*Saksham Gupta,Yash Choudhary,Arpit Upadhyay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究检验记忆库统计量能否在LLM智能体任务精度下降前发出预警，结论是否定的：全局语义密度（如平均成对余弦相似度ρ）无预测力，局部拥挤指标虽有前置信号但无法稳定迁移；真正可预测的失败是整库提示超出模型输入容量，由token增长直接预示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化文本记忆可提升LLM智能体性能，但反复更新会侵蚀任务表现。作者希望验证记忆库的几何、检索与图统计量能否作为"事前预警信号"，在留出集精度下降之前给出提示，从而为记忆管理提供可监测的健康指标。

Method: 预注册平均成对余弦相似度ρ及13个几何/检索/图统计量；在ALFWorld、AppWorld、ScienceWorld、WebShop及两个ARC-AGI设置上，采集13种记忆方法（轨迹、规则、工作流、策略、关联记录；选择性检索或整库提示）共805个快照。以留出集精度下降点（主）与峰值后检查点（次）为标签，评估各统计量的领先量；再在语料上训练分类器，原样迁移至PropWorld最长5000任务的运行。

Result: 无任何统计量能可靠预测精度下降的早期迹象，ρ的中位领先量为0。以峰值后检查点为次级下降点时，高相似对比例与最近邻拥挤度变化更早，但未能稳定优于已存储条目计数。迁移实验中，分类器对规模受限且精度稳定的存储误报56.9%的检查点，对任务成功率降至0后的检查点仅检出18%。长程运行中规模受限存储保持稳定，唯一失败源于无界整库提示超出模型输入容量。

Conclusion: 全局语义密度不是可靠的预警信号，局部拥挤指标不能跨记忆设计一致迁移；记忆库统计量难以作为任务性能下降的通用代理。实践上应优先监测token/上下文长度等容量约束，并采用规模受限的记忆存储以维持长程稳定性。

Abstract: Persistent textual memory can improve LLM agents, but repeated updates can erode task performance. We ask whether memory-store statistics warn before held-out accuracy declines. We registered mean pairwise cosine similarity ($ρ$) and 13 additional geometry, retrieval, and graph statistics on 805 snapshots from 13 memory methods across ALFWorld, AppWorld, ScienceWorld, WebShop, and two ARC-AGI settings. These methods store trajectories, rules, workflows, strategies, or linked records, using selective retrieval or whole-store prompting. No such statistic reliably predicted early signs of task accuracy decline, and $ρ$ had zero median lead. With the checkpoint after peak accuracy as a secondary decline point, measures of highly similar pairs and nearest-neighbor crowding changed earlier but did not consistently outperform stored-item count. We trained classifiers on this corpus and applied them unchanged to PropWorld runs of up to 5,000 crafting tasks. They falsely flagged 56.9% of checkpoints from size-limited stores with stable accuracy and detected only 18% of checkpoints after task success fell to zero. Thus, global semantic density did not provide a reliable warning, while local crowding did not transfer consistently across memory designs. In these long-horizon runs, size-limited stores remained stable, while the sole failure arose when unbounded whole-store prompting exceeded model input capacity, which token growth predicted directly.

</details>


### [20] [KiRA: Kronecker Input-Gated Rank Adaptation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning of Pretrained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33236)
*Akil Ahmad Taki,Shaikh Anowarul Fattah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 KiRA（Kronecker input-gated rank adaptation），用「冻结随机矩阵 A ⊗ 可训练因子 M(x)」的 Kronecker 积更新突破低秩瓶颈，并通过输入门控 g(x) 以步长 p 自适应调节秩，从而在更少可训练参数下取得优于 LoRA 类基线的最佳平均成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全量微调大模型代价高昂，PEFT 成为标准替代方案；但主流 PEFT 采用低秩更新，其秩远低于全量微调且对所有输入固定不变。输入自适应变体虽按样本调节容量，但仍受限于低秩瓶颈，且任务是否需要输入条件化在训练前无法预知。因此需要一种既突破秩上限、又能按输入调节秩、并可事先判断其收益的方法。

Method: KiRA 将更新 ΔW 设为冻结随机 p×p 矩阵 A 与可训练因子 M(x) 的 Kronecker 积：由于秩在 Kronecker 积下相乘，A 将 M(x) 的秩放大 p 倍而不增加可训练参数。进一步将 M(x) 分解为 B1·diag(g(x))·B2，其中输入门 g(x) 由跨所有适配层共享的网络逐样本计算一次，以 p 为步长调节更新秩。在无需输入条件化的场景，部署时可将门折叠进 B1。由于门仅以离散步长改变秩，输入条件化的收益可在训练前用数百个带标签输入进行预测。

Result: 在六个医学影像与语言基准上，KiRA 相对五个已发表基线的平均得分最高，且可训练参数比低秩适配（LoRA）少 8.7–9.7 倍；训练前的预测准则在全部六个任务上均与实测的输入条件化收益一致。

Conclusion: Kronecker 积结构可在不增加参数的前提下突破 PEFT 的秩上限，配合共享的输入门控实现按样本的秩自适应；同时该方法提供了可在训练前预判输入条件化是否有效的实用准则，实验验证了其参数效率与跨模态有效性。

Abstract: Full fine-tuning of large pretrained models is expensive, and parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) is the standard alternative. Most PEFT methods use low-rank updates, whose rank lies far below that of full fine-tuning and is the same for every input. Input-adaptive variants vary the capacity per input but remain within the low-rank bottleneck, and whether a task benefits from input conditioning is not known before training. This paper introduces Kronecker input-gated rank adaptation (KiRA). Instead of the conventional low-rank product, the update $ΔW$ is the Kronecker product of a frozen random $p\times p$ matrix $A$ and a trainable factor $M(x)$, which overcomes the rank ceiling: because rank is multiplicative under this product, $A$ multiplies the rank of $M(x)$ by $p$ without adding trainable parameters. The factor $M(x)$ is further decomposed as $B_1\,\mathrm{diag}(g(x))\,B_2$, where the input gate $g(x)$, computed once per sample by a network shared across all adapted layers, adjusts the rank of the update to each input in steps of $p$. Where input conditioning is unnecessary, the gate is folded into $B_1$ at deployment. Because the gate changes the rank only in discrete steps, the benefit of input conditioning can be predicted before training from a few hundred labeled inputs. Across six medical imaging and language benchmarks, KiRA attains the highest mean score against five published baselines with 8.7--9.7 times fewer trainable parameters than low-rank adaptation, and the pre-training criterion agrees with the measured benefit of input conditioning on all six tasks.

</details>


### [21] [Resource-Efficient Multimodal Fusion for Early Rice Blast Forecasting in Sustainable Agriculture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33237)
*Shubhajyoti Das,Pritam Bikram,Arindam Biswas,Samrat Paul,Parimal Sinha,Bhargab B. Bhattacharya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出轻量多模态融合框架（注意力位置编码Transformer + CNN-GRU），实现稻瘟病症状出现前5–10天的资源高效预警，验证准确率96.93%、F1 95.79%，并显著降低算力、时延与碳排放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可持续农业场景下需在算力与能耗受限条件下实现及时病害预测；稻瘟病早期估计可优化农事管理、减少过量用药，但现有框架计算开销高，且难以建模异构气象与遥感数据中复杂的光谱—时序依赖。

Method: 轻量多模态融合框架：①基于注意力位置编码的Transformer，为序列气象token分配自适应重要性以建模时序特征；②轻量CNN提取判别性光谱特征，GRU对两个时相的遥感影像建模时序动态；③多模态融合层整合异构表征，跨不同农业气候区预测发病风险。

Result: 实验表明优于现有SOTA：验证准确率96.93%，F1-score 95.79%；同时显著降低计算复杂度、推理时延与碳排放。

Conclusion: 该框架为可持续农业中的资源高效人工智能提供了实用基础，兼顾预测性能与能效，适用于大规模病害预测。

Abstract: Resource-efficient artificial intelligence has become increasingly important for sustainable agriculture, where timely disease forecasting must be achieved under computational and energy constraints. Early estimation of rice blast can enhance food security by optimizing crop management practices while reducing excessive pesticide use. However, existing frameworks often suffer from high computational overhead and limited capability to model the complex spectral-temporal dependencies present in heterogeneous meteorological and remote sensing data. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a lightweight multimodal fusion framework comprising an attentive positional encoding-based Transformer and a convolutional neural network (CNN)-embedded gated recurrent unit (GRU) for efficient spectral-temporal representation learning. The attention-enhanced positional encoding assigns adaptive importance to sequential meteorological tokens, enabling effective modelling of temporal characteristics. In parallel, the lightweight CNN extracts discriminative spectral features, while the GRU captures temporal dynamics from remote sensing imagery acquired over two time-instances. A multimodal fusion layer integrates these heterogeneous representations to forecast rice blast risk 5-10 days before symptom manifestation across diverse agro-climatic zones. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving a validation accuracy of 96.93% and an F1-score of 95.79%. In addition to its predictive performance, the lightweight multimodal architecture substantially reduces computational complexity, inference latency, and carbon emissions, making it an energy-efficient solution for large-scale disease forecasting. The proposed framework provides a practical foundation for resource-efficient artificial intelligence in sustainable agriculture.

</details>


### [22] [Sequential Testing on Complex and High Dimensional Data Streams Leveraging Unsupervised Model Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33238)
*George Stamatelis,Iordanis Koutsopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种无分布假设的检验统计量，分别用于二值序贯假设检验(SHT)与序贯变点检测(SCPD)，仅利用在零假设/变点前数据上训练的ML模型的预测误差来监测流式数据，并提供严格非渐近保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式环境（如网络流量元数据）数据结构复杂，传统基于似然比的检验所需的精确概率建模难以实现；同时存在信息不对称问题——仅有零假设/变点前数据，缺乏备择/变点后（如零日攻击、新兴疾病）数据。

Method: 基于仅在零假设/变点前数据上训练的预测或重构ML模型，利用其泛化不全（在备择/变点后数&#x636E;上性能退化）的性质，监测标量误差构造两种通用、无分布的检验统计量；误差仅以标量形式处理，无需在线重训练或核函数计算。

Result: 在模型误差的温和假设下，给出SHT的严格非渐近错误控制，以及SCPD的平均运行长度(ARL)与期望检测延迟(EDD)的显式界；在目标跟踪、混沌动力系统及真实网络安全数据集上，该框架持续满足预设约束，而现有算法出现漏检和/或误报膨胀。

Conclusion: 该框架解决了复杂流式数据下无法精确建模及信息不对称的序贯检测难题，具备理论保证、高可扩展性，且实验上优于现有算法。

Abstract: We propose two general, distribution-free test statistics for binary Sequential Hypothesis Testing (SHT) and Sequential Change Point Detection (SCPD) respectively, in streaming environments where the data (e.g., network traffic metadata) are too complicated for the exact probabilistic modeling required by conventional likelihood ratio-driven tests. Our framework also addresses the challenge of information asymmetry, where data is available only from the null/pre-change regime and not from the alternative/post-change one such as zero-day attacks or emerging disease related regimes. The proposed statistics monitor the errors of a predictive or reconstructive machine learning model trained solely on null/pre-change data, relying on imperfect generalization (the model's performance will degrade when deployed on data from the alternative/post-change class). Under mild assumptions on the model's errors, we establish rigorous analytical guarantees: strict non-asymptotic error control for SHT, and explicit bounds on the Average Run Length (ARL) and the Expected Detection Delay (EDD) for SCPD. Extensive evaluations in target tracking, chaotic dynamical systems, and real-world network security datasets, demonstrate that our framework consistently satisfies prescribed constraints where existing algorithms succumb to inflated missed detections and/or false alarms. Finally, because our methods operate on scalar errors without requiring online retraining or kernel evaluations, they are highly scalable.

</details>


### [23] [Typical Angina, Reversed: A Long-Standing Documentation Error in the UCI Heart Disease Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33248)
*Tejas Tovinkere Pattabhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文揭示UCI心脏病数据集中"胸痛类型"变量的编码与其文档记载相反，并评估该错误对既有文献与模型的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UCI Heart Disease是被广泛使用的机器学习基准数据集，但其胸痛变量的语义若存在编码反转，会使大量基于该数据的特征解释与文献结论出错，需加以核实。

Method: 两项互补验证：(i)复现1986年Cleveland原始研究的胸痛频数表与回归系数，仅在纠正读法下成立；(ii)在Budapest、Switzerland、Long Beach数据中检验编码4患者的典型心绞痛特征占比。同时对Scholar检索到的SHAP相关文献随机抽样150篇（124篇可读）进行审计。

Result: 纠正读法下：编码4实为典型心绞痛，编码1为几乎无典型特征（Cleveland常无胸痛）；三站点93–96%的编码4患者具备全部三项典型心绞痛特征。文献审计中，32篇明确陈述方向者25篇（78%，95%CI 61–89%）与纠正后类别身份不一致，22篇可归因于文档错误，9篇给出临床解释；124篇均未识别该反转。常规预测模型基本不受影响，但基于标签名解释效应及零样本语言模型分类器被误导，三款开放权重模型在给定年龄、性别与胸痛类型时AUC下降0.12–0.33。

Conclusion: UCI心脏病数据的胸痛编码与文档记载相反，导致大量文献解释方向错误，需采用纠正后的映射并在解读与提示设计时修正。

Abstract: The UCI Heart Disease data are a widely used machine-learning benchmark. We show that their chest-pain variable is stored in reverse of its documentation. At all four sites, the code documented as "asymptomatic" (4) marks patients with typical angina, and the code documented as "typical angina" (1) marks patients with almost none of the classical angina features (at Cleveland, no chest pain).
  Two complementary checks establish this: (i) the chest-pain table and regression coefficients of the original 1986 Cleveland study are reproduced only under the corrected reading; (ii) in the Budapest, Switzerland and Long Beach files, 93-96% of patients coded 4 have all three classical angina features. A 1990 word-labeled Cleveland file already uses the documented labels, and Statlog (Heart), a Cleveland subset, stores the same codes. OpenML, PMLB and two highly voted Kaggle copies that name the categories use the same documented labels (about 400,000 downloads combined).
  We audited a seeded random sample of 150 records from a pre-specified Scholar query for SHAP studies of these data mentioning asymptomatic chest pain (124 readable). Of 32 papers that state a direction for a named category, 25 (78%; 95% CI 61-89%) state one inconsistent with the corrected category identities; 22 (69%; 51-82%; post hoc) are attributable to the documentation error, and nine of these explain it clinically. None of the 124 readable papers identifies the reversal.
  Conventional models are essentially unaffected, one reason the error went unnoticed. Interpretations that attach the documented names to model effects are reversed, and zero-shot language-model classifiers that read the labels are misled: with the documented labels, three open-weight models lose 0.12-0.33 in area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) given age, sex and chest-pain type. We give the corrected mapping, evidence and code.

</details>


### [24] [FERRETS: A Scalable Symbolic Transform for Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33249)
*Antoine Guillaume*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FERRETS：基于WEASEL 2的无标签字典变换，用随机长度与膨胀的8位精确词+均值/方差层次词（多变量加跨通道词），在UCR上最快，精度与WEASEL 2无显著差异而快125倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列字典方法构建符号词袋，但精确方法扩展性差，其精度已不足以抵消计算代价，需在保持精度的同时大幅降低开销。

Method: 基于WEASEL 2的无标签字典变换：将随机长度与膨胀的子序列映射为精确八位词；为每个窗口加入均值和离散度构成的“层次词”；多变量数据上额外加入跨通道词；分类器支持流式处理。

Result: 在UCR十线程预算下为11种方法中最快；仅MultiRocket-Hydra显著更准（慢24.7倍）；与WEASEL 2无显著差异（WEASEL 2慢125倍）；显著优于其他字典方法。Multiverse核心上与HIVE-COTE 2、MultiRocket-Hydra精度无显著差异（UCR上落后）；UCR官方划分上显著优于5个冻结基础模型。MONSTER上拟合比MiniRocket快3.7倍且显著更准。

Conclusion: FERRETS以极低计算代价达到字典方法乃至顶级分类器的竞争性精度，兼具速度、精度与流式可扩展性，是可替代传统字典方法与深度/基础模型的高效方案。

Abstract: Dictionary methods for time series build bags of symbolic words from subsequences, but the accurate ones scale poorly. Against other families of methods, their accuracy no longer pays for their cost. We present FERRETS, a label-free dictionary transform built on WEASEL~2. It keys windows of random length and dilation into exact eight-bit words, adds a level word of each window's mean and spread, and on multivariate data adds cross-channel words. We compare it with leading classifiers, deep networks and foundation models included, on the UCR, Multiverse and MONSTER archives. FERRETS is the fastest of the eleven methods timed on UCR under a ten-thread budget. Of the ten others, only MultiRocket-Hydra is significantly more accurate, taking 24.7 times as long. FERRETS is not significantly different from WEASEL~2, the most accurate earlier dictionary method, which takes 125 times as long, and is significantly ahead of the other dictionary methods. On the Multiverse core, it is not significantly different in accuracy from HIVE-COTE~2 and MultiRocket-Hydra, though behind both on UCR. It is also significantly more accurate than five frozen foundation models on UCR's published split. On the MONSTER problems both complete, MiniRocket takes 3.7 times as long to fit as the streaming FERRETS classifier, which is significantly more accurate.

</details>


### [25] [One Token, Many Lessons: Sliding-Window Attention within Gated Delta Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33257)
*Yuchen Song,Yangbin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 从测试时回归视角出发，研究线性注意力中"用于状态更新的键值学习对如何构造"，提出用滑窗注意力替代短卷积做局部聚合的SWGDN，在不改变状态更新方程与分块并行算法的前提下提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力把历史上下文压缩为固定大小的循环状态，其有效性不仅取决于更新规则，也取决于更新所用键值学习对的构造方式，而现有工作多聚焦于状态如何更新与组织，较少研究学习对的构造。

Method: 将状态矩阵视为在线学习的回归模型（键为输入、值为目标、查询用于读取），用滑动窗口注意力替换Delta-Rule线性注意力中的短卷积，对投影后的查询、键、值执行内容相关的局部聚合，得到SWGDN；该局部混合策略可直接嵌入任意Delta-Rule类架构，不改动状态更新方程与chunkwise并行算法，并在GDN2与KDA上验证。

Result: SWGDN使GDN2与KDA中连续更新所用的键值对更相似；同一token信息跨更新被携带，有助于循环状态强化已学到的关联；在多个基准上优于原始Delta-Rule基线。

Conclusion: 状态更新所用学习对的构造与更新规则同等重要，内容相关的滑窗局部聚合是一种通用、即插即用的改进手段，可推广到各类Delta-Rule线性注意力架构。

Abstract: Linear attention compresses historical context into a fixed-size recurrent state, reducing the computational and memory costs of long-sequence modeling. From the perspective of test-time regression (TTR), the state matrix can be viewed as an online-learned regression model, where keys and values serve as inputs and targets respectively, and queries are used to read from the model. From this perspective, the effectiveness of state learning depends not only on the update rule but also on how the key-value learning pairs used for state updates are constructed. While existing work has largely focused on how the state is updated and organized, we investigate how the learning pairs used for state updates are constructed. To this end, we propose Sliding-Window Gated DeltaNet (SWGDN), which replaces short convolutions in Delta-Rule linear attention with sliding-window attention to perform content-dependent local aggregation of projected queries, keys, and values. Our experiments show that SWGDN makes the key-value pairs used in consecutive updates more similar in both GDN2 and KDA. In SWGDN, carrying information from the same token across updates can help the recurrent state reinforce a previously learned association. The local mixing strategy can be incorporated into any Delta-Rule-based linear attention architecture without changing its state update equations or chunkwise parallel algorithms. We apply this strategy to two Delta-Rule variants, GDN2 and KDA, and experimental results demonstrate that SWGDN outperforms the original Delta-Rule baseline across multiple benchmarks.

</details>


### [26] [Fast and Scalable Abstract Interpretation for Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33259)
*Avaljot Singh,Dushyant Bharadwaj,Yasmin Chandini Sarita,Heng Zhong,Charith Mendis,Gagandeep Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Scepter通过"结构语义"将DNN抽象解释器的数值求值延迟并分离，把连续操作的结构计算合并生成可复用的张量程序，在精确算术下与原解释器求得相同边界，并可由张量编译器整体优化；在8种抽象解释器、44个网络上相较6个基线实现最高153倍规模扩展、11.9x–44.4x的GPU加速与2.60x峰值内存降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抽象解释可为DNN提供性质认证，但精确分析代价高昂。通用实现在同一架构、不同输入与性质下重复执行相同工作；且将结构性工作与数值运算交错进行，既降低GPU利用率，又限制了张量编译器可整体处理的粒度。

Method: 提出基于结构语义（structural semantics）的DNN抽象解释器特化框架：语义只决定中间抽象值的结构，而将数值求值推迟，从而把相继操作的结构计算合并执行后再做认证，生成可执行张量程序。解释器与网络架构固定后，该程序可复用于不同权重与输入区域；生成程序显式暴露算子及依赖，便于张量编译与内存规划。在Neuton从ConstraintFlow规格生成的张量程序上实现Scepter。

Result: 在GPU与CPU上以8种抽象解释器、44个DNN对比6个基线系统：可处理网络规模最高达对应基线可完成最大规模的153倍神经元；完成DeepPoly于ResNet50与VGG-16的分析，而所评估的DeepPoly基线均无法完成；在双方均可完成的配置上，GPU几何平均加速11.9x–44.4x，峰值内存几何平均降低2.60x；输入分裂案例中Scepter证明68条性质而基线超时。

Conclusion: 将数值求值与结构计算解耦、生成可复用的特化张量程序，是提升DNN抽象解释可扩展性与硬件效率的有效途径，且不损失分析精确性（精确算术下边界等价）。

Abstract: Abstract interpretation can certify properties of Deep Neural Networks (DNNs), but precise analyses remain expensive. General implementations repeat work when analyzing the same architecture under different inputs and properties. Interleaving this work with numerical operations limits GPU utilization and restricts how much of the analysis a tensor compiler can process together.
  We present Scepter, a framework for specializing DNN abstract interpreters through structural semantics. The semantics determine the structure of intermediate abstract values while deferring their numerical evaluation. Structural computations from successive operations can therefore be performed together before certification, generating an executable tensor program. With the abstract interpreter and DNN architecture fixed, the program can be reused with different weights and input regions. We prove that it computes the same bounds as the general interpreter in exact arithmetic. The generated program also exposes operations and their dependencies for tensor compilation and memory planning. We implement Scepter for the tensor programs that Neuton generates from ConstraintFlow specifications.
  We evaluate Scepter on eight abstract interpreters and 44 DNNs against six baseline systems on GPUs and CPUs. Scepter handles networks with up to 153x as many neurons as the largest completed by the corresponding baselines. It completes DeepPoly on ResNet50 and VGG-16, whereas none of the evaluated DeepPoly baselines completes either network. On configurations completed by both systems, GPU geometric-mean speedups over Neuton range from 11.9x to 44.4x across eight analyses, with a 2.60x geometric-mean reduction in peak memory. In an input-splitting case study, Scepter proves 68 properties for which the baselines time out.
  Code: https://github.com/uiuc-focal-lab/constraintflow.git

</details>


### [27] [Two Timescales of Forgetting in Fine-tuning of Language Models: Fast Behavioral Suppression and Slow Representational Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33302)
*Yilin Luo,Jin Hwa Lee,Andrew M. Saxe,Antonio Sclocchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在随机层次模型(RHM)上预训练再受限微调，发现SFT存在"行为抑制快、表征遗忘慢"的双时间尺度分离现象，并在自然语言实验中复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对"在受限数据分布上微调如何改变预训练所得表征"理解不足，尤其不清楚表层行为的抑制是否伴随支撑该行为的潜在表征被擦除。

Method: 在具有层次潜在结构的受控上下文无关文法（Random Hierarchy Model）上预训练Transformer，再用其生成规则的受限子集进行监督微调（SFT）；追踪被删除规则对应的生成行为与内部隐表征随训练时间的变化，并在自然语言真实场景中做对照实验。

Result: (i) 被删除规则的生成行为在快时间尺度上改变，尺度与token–潜在相关性的强度成反比，而对应表征保持不变；(ii) 被删除规则的隐表征在长得多的时间尺度上才被遗忘，并伴随网络参数的缓慢漂移；自然语言实验中观察到类似结果。

Conclusion: SFT中行为抑制与表征擦除存在显著的时间尺度分离，表明仅凭行为消失不能推断表征已被移除；该现象可能是语言模型微调的普遍特性。

Abstract: Today, a core pipeline of artificial intelligence systems is pretraining on a broad range of text corpora and then fine-tuning on specific tasks or desired behaviors. Yet we still have limited understanding of how fine-tuning on a restricted data distribution modifies representations acquired during pretraining. In particular, it remains unclear whether surface behavioral suppression happens together with the erasure of latent representations that support it. In this work, we study supervised fine-tuning (SFT) by pretraining transformers on a controlled context-free grammar with hierarchical latent structure, the Random Hierarchy Model (RHM), and fine-tuning with a restricted subset of its generative rules. While during pretraining the model's ability to generate with the correct grammar rules coincides with building the internal representations of the corresponding latent, we find that SFT exhibits a very different dynamics: (i) the generation of deleted rules changes on a fast timescale inversely proportional to the magnitude of the token-latent correlation; meanwhile, their corresponding representations stay intact; (ii) the hidden representations of the deleted rules become forgotten on a much longer timescale, associated with a slow drift of the network parameters. Finally, we observe analogous results in a realistic setup with natural language, suggesting that this timescale separation in forgetting is a more general phenomenon of fine-tuning language models.

</details>


### [28] [Change Partners at Every Layer: Balanced Random Regrouping for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33308)
*Kaiyang Li,Shaobo Han,Shihao Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型合并可将多个微调专家整合为单一模型以服务多任务而无需重训，但合并大量专家会显著降低精度。作者提出 Balanced Random Regrouping（BRR）：在每层或每个权重矩阵上，将 N 个专家独立随机划分为 K 个等大小分组并分别合并。实验表明 BRR 优于跨层固定分组和基于相似度的 FlexMerge，并用一阶分析解释了增益来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接合并大量专家会导致精度大幅下降；分组合并是折中方案（每层/每个权重矩阵将 N 个专家分为 K<N 组，组内共享模块）。现有自然选择是跨层保持固定伙伴或按相似性分组，作者希望探究是否存在更优的分组策略。

Method: 提出 BRR：在每一层（BRR-layer）或每个权重矩阵（BRR-matrix）上独立地将专家随机划分为 K 个等大小组，每组使用现有合并方法合并，并采用验证集选择的合并缩放系数。另用一阶分析刻画合并伙伴导致的输出特征偏移，并基于该分析设计分组搜索策略。

Result: 在全部 24 个 CLIP 设置中，两种 BRR 变体均比跨层复用同一分组高出 1.0–6.8 个点；BRR-matrix 在所有设置中也优于基于相似度的 FlexMerge。一阶分析表明：固定伙伴时各层偏移方向相近会累积叠加，而每层更换伙伴使偏移方向独立、平均累加更小；系列实验支持该解释，并据此指导的分组搜索可进一步超越 BRR。

Conclusion: 随机在各层重新分组（BRR）作为一种简单策略，反而优于固定分组与相似度分组，其优势可由合并引起的偏移累积效应解释；该分析还能指导更优的分组搜索。说明分组多样性而非伙伴一致性是降低合并误差的关键因素。

Abstract: Model merging combines fine-tuned experts into one model that serves many tasks without retraining, but merging many experts this way substantially reduces accuracy. Grouping offers a middle ground: at every layer or weight matrix, the $N$ experts are divided into $K<N$ groups, the experts in a group are merged into one shared module, and a task uses the module merged from its expert's group. Natural choices are to keep the same partners across layers or to group similar experts. Surprisingly, we find that randomly changing partners at every layer outperforms both. We call this Balanced Random Regrouping (BRR): it independently partitions the experts into $K$ equal-sized random groups at each layer (BRR-layer) or weight matrix (BRR-matrix) and merges each group with an existing method. With validation-selected merge scales, both BRR variants outperform reusing one grouping across layers in all 24 CLIP settings, by 1.0-6.8 points, and BRR-matrix also outperforms the similarity-based FlexMerge in every setting. A first-order analysis explains this gain. At each layer, merging a task's expert with its partners shifts the task's output features; when the partners are the same at every layer, these shifts tend to point in similar directions and add up, whereas with new partners at every layer they vary independently and add up less on average. A series of experiments supports this analysis, which further guides a grouping search that can outperform BRR.

</details>


### [29] [Sparse Adaptation for In-Context Learning on Data Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33325)
*João Neves,Afonso Lourenço,Lizy K. John,Eric P. Xing,João Gama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAIL：面向表格基础模型（TFM）的稀疏自适应框架，通过复用 KV 缓存并仅在流结构漂移时重建支持集，实现低开销的在线自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT 产生海量数据流，边缘计算与 5G 需要低延迟推理；TFM 依赖上下文示例而非参数更新进行适应，但持续演化的数据流会使旧上下文失效，且全量重算代价高昂。

Method: 1) 复用模型 key–value 缓存，在适应事件间避免重复计算；2) 结合序贯（prequential）误差监控与在线、按类别的微聚类，检测簇质量、类可分性与特征空间覆盖的变化；3) 仅在流结构漂移表明上下文可能失效时重建支持集，而结构表示则由预测性能变化触发更新；4) 重建时按类质量比例分配上下文预算，取最近更新的微簇作为代表性样本进行重拟合。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数据；声明该方法可降低上下文冗余与计算开销，同时保留近期结构信息。

Conclusion: 以结构性漂移驱动、按类质量加权的稀疏自适应，为 TFM 在演化数据流上的高效在线部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The Internet of Things (IoT) generates massive data streams, making edge computing essential for low-latency applications and 5G networks. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) enable adaptation through in-context examples rather than parameter updates, but evolving streams challenge context relevance and computational efficiency. This work introduces SAIL, a framework for sparse adaptation of TFMs that reuses the model's key--value cache between adaptation events. SAIL reconstructs its support set only when drifts in stream structure indicate potential context inadequacy, while the structural representation itself is updated by changes in predictive performance. SAIL combines prequential error monitoring with online, class-specific micro-clustering to detect changes in cluster mass, class separability, and feature-space coverage. Upon adaptation, a context budget is allocated proportionally to class mass, and the most recently updated micro-clusters provide representative observations for refitting. This limits context redundancy and computational cost while preserving recent structural information.

</details>


### [30] [Online Prediction System for Proactive Autoscaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33332)
*Jun Gao,Areg Poghosyan,Pavel Paulau,Sumit Mohanty,Tengfei Mu,Shawn Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出面向大规模分布式系统主动自动扩缩容的在线短时时间序列预测系统，采用"季节性分解+残差校正"两阶段轻量线性模型架构，每个预测周期即时重训，生产环境每小时处理90万+次预测，MAPE降低18.6%，日均节省约11.8万台服务器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统预测方法侧重建模季节性与长期趋势，难以满足互联网级基础设施动态资源管理对"紧邻未来区间"高精度、低延迟预测的需求；同时需要应对需求模式漂移，并避免持久化模型存储与特征漂移监控的运维负担。

Method: 两阶段架构：第一阶段进行季节性分解（STL配置），第二阶段以工程化特征驱动的轻量线性模型对残差进行校正；对每个预测周期在线即时重训模型（retraining on-the-fly），从而持续自适应需求变化，无需持久化模型存储或特征漂移监控。

Result: 系统部署于多种异构服务，每小时处理超过90万次预测；在基于生产流量的离线评估中，基于STL的配置相对既有生产模型将预测误差（MAPE）降低18.6%；生产环境中估计每日节省约11.8万台服务器。

Conclusion: 面向短时预测的"分解+残差校正+即时重训"轻量在线方案，可在保证低延迟的同时显著提升预测精度并产生可观资源节省，适用于互联网规模分布式系统的主动自动扩缩容。

Abstract: We present an online learning system for short-term time series forecasting that enables proactive autoscaling in large-scale distributed systems. Unlike traditional forecasting methods that focus on modeling seasonality and long-term trends, the system targets high-accuracy, low-latency predictions for immediate future intervals for dynamic resource management in internet-scale infrastructures. The system employs a two-stage architecture: a seasonality decomposition stage followed by a residual correction stage using lightweight linear models with engineered features. By retraining models on-the-fly for every prediction cycle, the online prediction system continuously adapts to changing demand patterns without requiring persistent model storage or feature drift monitoring. Deployed across many heterogeneous services, the system processes over 900K predictions per hour; in an offline evaluation on production traffic, its STL-based configuration reduces prediction error (MAPE) by 18.6\% relative to the previous production model. In production, the system has saved an estimated 118K servers daily.

</details>


### [31] [A Hierarchy of Risk Certificates for Representation-Based Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33333)
*Mohit Kumar,Somayeh Kargaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究给定表征下Lipschitz解码器类的最优总体MSE，证明期望分块得分是风险的非降下界并在紧致度量特征空间上收敛；结合验证对偶下界与独立校准数据，给出最优类风险的同时下置信界与拟合预测器风险的上置信界，从而对"是否存在满足MSE阈值的解码器"进行有原则的认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于表征的预测系统中，某个已拟合预测器未达到精度要求，并不能说明其表征-解码器类本身无法达标。因此需要一种针对整个解码器类最优风险的统计评估手段，以区分"配置不佳"与"类本身不可行"，并支撑表征-解码器配置的评估与选择。

Method: 以固定表征上Lipschitz解码器类的总体最优MSE为研究对象。对b≥2个iid观测构成的块，在成对Lipschitz约束下最小化经(b-1)归一化的总平方误差，构造分块得分；证明期望分块得分构成最优类风险的非降下界，并在紧致度量特征空间上收敛到该风险，同时说明不存在统一次优的相对保证。再结合经核验的对偶下界、与模型开发独立且目标与预测有界的iid校准观测，构造最优类风险的同时下置信界与拟合预测器风险的上置信界。

Result: 理论上给出一族可计算且单调紧的下界，并刻画其收敛性与无统一相对保证的局限；统计上得到同时置信界，可用于判定所有可行解码器均超过给定MSE阈值，或某个可行拟合预测器满足该阈值。受控实验展示总体风险认证流程，固定PTB-XL与Bike Sharing实验给出确定性的经验风险结论。

Conclusion: 结果支持对表征-解码器配置进行有原则的评估与选择：既可通过下界说明解码器类整体不可行，也可通过上界确认可行预测器达标。大规模高效认证及向相依观测的推广仍为开放问题。

Abstract: Representation-based prediction underlies many machine-learning systems. Yet a fitted predictor's failure to meet an accuracy requirement does not establish that its representation-decoder class cannot meet it. We study optimal population mean squared error (MSE) over Lipschitz decoders acting on a fixed representation. For blocks of $b\geq2$ independent and identically distributed (iid) observations, we minimize total squared error (normalized by $b-1$) under pairwise Lipschitz constraints. We prove that expected block scores form nondecreasing lower bounds on optimal class risk and converge to that risk on compact metric feature spaces, although no fixed block size provides a uniform relative guarantee. Verified dual lower bounds and iid calibration observations independent of model development, with bounded targets and predictions, yield simultaneous lower confidence bounds on optimal class risk and upper confidence bounds on fitted-predictor risk. These bounds can establish that every admissible decoder exceeds a prescribed MSE threshold or that an admissible fitted predictor meets it. Controlled experiments illustrate population-risk certification; fixed PTB-XL and Bike Sharing experiments provide deterministic empirical-risk conclusions. The results support principled assessment and selection of representation-decoder configurations. Efficient certification at scale and extensions to dependent observations remain open problems.

</details>


### [32] [FlowersBench: A FluidFlower-Inspired Dataset for Benchmarking Carbon Storage Surrogates Across Geological Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33335)
*Farah Rabie,Hannah Menke,Florian Doster,Amy Gough,Ahmed H. Elsheikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FlowersBench：包含5,000个二维储层尺度CO2封存模拟（覆盖244种构造几何）的基准数据集，并训练U-Net代理模型以评估对未见地质构造的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地质碳封存（GCS）代理模型的基准数据集多在固定地质背景下仅变化岩石物理与运行参数，对未见地质构造的泛化性缺乏评估手段。

Method: 基于FluidFlower实验与SPE11B案例启发自研的构造生成器采样244种几何，用OPM Flow运行50年注入+500年注入后监测的5000组模拟；训练几何与测试几何分离；另设两个子集评估结构性偏移（参数范围外推、多平行断层的新拓扑）；以U-Net预测CO2气体饱和度作为基线。

Result: 代表性测试中代理模型能重现主要运移路径（低渗层下横向铺展、沿断层损伤带向上流动），误差集中于羽流边界；两种结构性偏移下误差均高于留出几何，且未见断层拓扑的相对误差增幅更大。

Conclusion: FlowersBench公开可用，为跨地质构造评估GCS代理模型提供统一基准，并揭示构造泛化仍是显著挑战。

Abstract: Machine learning surrogates are emerging as a computationally efficient tool for uncertainty quantification in geological carbon storage (GCS). Geological structure constitutes a major source of uncertainty; however, existing benchmark simulation datasets predominantly vary petrophysical and operational parameters within a fixed geological setting. Generalisation to unseen geological structures therefore remains largely unexplored. We introduce FlowersBench, a benchmark dataset of 5,000 two-dimensional, reservoir-scale CO$_2$ storage simulations across 244 structural geometries. The geometries are sampled from an in-house structural generator inspired by the FluidFlower experiments and Case B of the 11th Society of Petroleum Engineers Comparative Solution Project (SPE11B). Each simulation is run with Open Porous Media (OPM) Flow over a 50-year injection period and a 500-year post-injection monitoring period. Test geometries are withheld from training to assess generalisation to unseen structural geometries. Two additional subsets support evaluation under structural shift. The first extends the parameter ranges of the structural generator beyond those used for training, and the second introduces an unseen topology of multiple parallel faults. A baseline U-Net surrogate is trained to predict CO$_2$ gas saturation. In representative test cases, the surrogate recovers the main migration pathways, including lateral spreading beneath low-permeability layers and upward flow along fault damage zones, with errors concentrated near the plume boundary. Under both structural shifts, prediction error exceeds that on the held-out geometries, and the increase in relative error is greater for the unseen fault topology. FlowersBench is publicly available and provides a common basis for evaluating GCS surrogates across geological structures.

</details>


### [33] [Understanding Failure Patterns in LLM-Based Mathematical Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33351)
*Ryoichi Takase,Xian Yeow Lee,Hikaru Tomonari,Masaya Tsunokake*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将LLM优化建模分解为数学推理、求解器使用和领域知识三部分并构建针对性数据集，分析各组件与错误类型的关联，发现可执行但错误的解是主要失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业优化约束复杂，LLM端到端生成优化模型可靠性不足，常产出不可执行、不可行或可行但错误的解，需厘清失效来源。

Method: 将建模过程解耦为数学推理、求解器使用、领域知识三个组件，为每个方面构建专门数据集，并分析各组件与错误模式的关联。

Result: 三个组件各自关联不同类型的失效，但关联强度与一致性有限；组合组件可提升执行可靠性，然而"可行但错误"的解仍占主导。

Conclusion: 现有数据集设计存在局限，需构建更能刻画约束完备性与模型表述准确性的数据以提升LLM优化建模可靠性。

Abstract: Industrial optimization involves complex constraints, making optimization modeling difficult to automate. Large language models (LLMs) have been explored for generating optimization models from natural language, but their reliability in end-to-end settings remains limited, often producing non-executable, infeasible, or feasible but incorrect solutions. In this work, we study failure modes in LLM-based optimization modeling by decomposing the process into three components: mathematical reasoning, solver usage, and domain knowledge, and constructing datasets that target each aspect. Using these datasets, we analyze how different components relate to errors and find that they are associated with different types of failure, although these relationships are not always strong or consistent. Furthermore, while combining these components improves execution reliability, feasible but incorrect solutions remain the dominant failure mode, suggesting a remaining gap between executability and correctness. These findings highlight limitations of current dataset designs and suggest the importance of data that better capture constraint completeness and formulation accuracy to enhance reliability.

</details>


### [34] [The Marauder's Map: Bézier Manifolds Reveal Hidden Surfaces for Model Merging and Ensembling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33353)
*Abhiram Iyer,Mark T. Harnett,Sarthak Chandra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Nimbus，用低复杂度（仅3个交互阶）高维Bézier流形连接 N 个不同任务的专家模型，实现"灵活退化"的深度学习类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的鲁棒性常被归因于退化性（degeneracy），但生物系统具有更灵活的退化形式：专门回路通过局部改变持续获得新能力而保留旧功能。深度学习中现有工作仅通过 mode connectivity（低维路径/曲面/体积）研究退化性，缺乏对应"灵活退化"的概念，即多个独立初始化、不同任务的模型能否由高维多任务解区域连通。

Method: 定义"灵活退化"的类比：N 个不同任务专家模型须由高维多任务解区域连通。提出 Nimbus，学习连接 N 个专家的高维 Bézier 流形，超越一维路径与二维曲面；朴素参数化随 N 组合爆炸，Nimbus 只需学习 3 个交互阶的修正，表明连接流形复杂度意外低。所得流形构成可导航的"Marauder's Map"。

Result: 在视觉与语言任务上训练，所得流形在功能与局部权重上均平坦：从流形采样的集成在输入扰动和权重扰动下退化更平缓，在 NLL 损失与校准误差上收益最大。

Conclusion: Nimbus 为"灵活退化"提供深度学习解释，将模型连通性由远端解之间的低维路径重构为邻近解构成的高维体积。

Abstract: Neural circuits are famously more robust than deep networks. This is often explained through degeneracy, where structurally distinct configurations support the same function. However, real biological systems exhibit a more flexible form of degeneracy in which specialized circuits continually adapt towards new competencies through localized changes while preserving existing function. Deep learning studies degeneracy through mode connectivity, which connects same-task networks by low-loss curves, sheets, or volumes. However, the field lacks a counterpart to flexible degeneracy, which demands more than ordinary mode connectivity. Defining such an analogue requires asking whether many independently initialized models trained on distinct tasks can be connected by a high-dimensional region of multi-task solutions. We introduce $\textit{Nimbus}$, which learns a high-dimensional Bézier manifold connecting $N$ specialists, going beyond the one-dimensional paths and two-dimensional sheets of mode connectivity work. A naive parameterization would scale combinatorially with $N$, but $\textit{Nimbus}$ requires learning corrections of only 3 interaction orders, showing that the connecting manifold has surprisingly low complexity yet supports multi-task competencies. The resulting manifold forms a navigable "Marauder's Map" that connects specialist models through a shared geometry of competent solutions. Trained across vision and language domains, $\textit{Nimbus}$ produces manifolds that are functionally and locally weight-flat: ensembles sampled from them degrade more gracefully than baselines under input and weight perturbations, with the largest gains in NLL loss and calibration error. $\textit{Nimbus}$ supplies a deep learning interpretation of flexible degeneracy, recasting model connectivity from a low-dimensional path between distant solutions into a high-dimensional volume of nearby ones.

</details>


### [35] [Discovering Explicit Magnetic Core-Loss Equations via Symbolic Sparse Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33364)
*Haoyu Wang,Jialin Zheng,Ziyang Xu,Yihao Wu,Alex Hanson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文提出符号稀疏辨识（SSI）框架，将磁芯损耗建模转化为数据驱动的符号回归问题，从实测数据中直接恢复可解释的解析方程，在Fair-Rite 78/79/95上分别取得1.89%、1.01%、1.56%的测试MAPE，且仅含10、10、7个有效项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁芯损耗的紧凑核心损耗方程对磁性元件设计至关重要，但现有方法存在精度与可解释性的权衡：经验模型（如Steinmetz方程SE）表达式紧凑但精度有限，机器学习方法精度高却是黑箱预测器。

Method: 提出符号稀疏辨识（SSI）框架，不预设函数形式：首先构建远丰富于SE的符号候选项库；其次通过稀疏回归从库中分离出主导项；最后用AdamW优化器精修保留项的系数，以最大化精度并形成解析方程。

Result: 在Fair-Rite 78、79、95上的测试平均绝对百分比误差分别为1.89%、1.01%、1.56%，有效项数仅10、10、7；与经验、数值及符号模型的对比量化了其在精度与紧凑性之间的平衡。

Conclusion: SSI为高频磁特性表征与设计提供了一种显式且具物理引导的方法，兼顾精度与可解释性。
