Abstract: Compact core loss equations are essential tools in magnetic component design, but existing approaches trade accuracy against interpretability. Empirical models such as the Steinmetz Equation (SE) yield compact expressions of limited accuracy, while machine learning methods achieve high accuracy as black-box predictors. This paper bridges the gap with a Symbolic Sparse Identification (SSI) framework, which casts core loss modeling as a data-driven symbolic regression problem and recovers unknown equations directly from measured data, without presuming a functional form. First, a comprehensively expanded library of symbolic candidate terms far richer than the SE is constructed. Second, the governing terms are isolated from this library by sparse regression. Third, the coefficients of the retained terms are refined with an AdamW optimizer to maximize accuracy and form an analytical equation. Experiments on Fair-Rite 78, 79, and 95 yield test mean absolute percentage errors of 1.89%, 1.01%, and 1.56%, respectively, with only 10, 10, and 7 active terms. Comparisons with empirical, numerical, and symbolic models quantify the resulting balance of accuracy and compactness. SSI thus provides an explicit and physically guided approach for high-frequency magnetic characterization and design.

</details>


### [36] [Federated Learning with Uncertainty Quantification on Pseudo Label Exchange](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33395)
*Jingqiao Tang,Phuoc-Toan Huynh,Richard Archibald,Feng Bao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FedSON 两阶段联邦随机算子学习框架，解决 SPDE 联邦学习中客户端数据分布异构（支持集不相交）与不确定性量化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习可让客户端在不共享原始解数据的前提下协同训练，但传统联邦平均在客户端分布差异大、尤其输入函数支持集不相交时性能显著退化；同时需要具备不确定性量化能力。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段交换本地训练好的确定性神经算子及其输入函数，生成伪标签辅助数据，使联邦模型跨异构输入分布学习而无需共享原始解轨迹；第二阶段在冻结的确定性算子之上训练随机算子网络，刻画随机波动并量化预测解的不确定性。

Result: 在热方程、粘性 Burgers 方程和二维对流扩散方程上的数值实验表明，FedSON 在异构客户端分布下显著提升确定性预测精度，且不确定性估计与集中式训练相当。

Conclusion: FedSON 通过辅助数据构造与随机算子学习相结合，有效缓解分布异构带来的联邦平均退化，并在不共享原始数据的前提下实现可比的精度与不确定性量化。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate federated stochastic operator learning for stochastic partial differential equations (SPDEs), with an emphasis on heterogeneous client data distributions and uncertainty quantification. Federated learning enables clients to collaboratively train models without exchanging their original solution data, but conventional federated averaging can suffer significant performance degradation when client distributions differ, particularly when their input function supports are disjoint. To address these challenges, we propose the Federated Stochastic Operator Network (FedSON), a two-phase framework that combines auxiliary data construction with stochastic operator learning. In the first phase, locally trained deterministic neural operators and their corresponding input functions are exchanged to generate pseudolabeled auxiliary data, enabling the federated model to learn across heterogeneous input distributions without sharing original solution trajectories. In the second phase, a stochastic operator network is trained on top of the frozen deterministic operator to characterize stochastic fluctuations and quantify uncertainty in the predicted solutions. Numerical experiments involving the heat equation, viscous Burgers' equation, and two-dimensional advection-diffusion equation demonstrate the FedSON substantially improves deterministic prediction accuracy under heterogeneous client distributions while producing uncertainty estimates comparable to those obtained through centralized training.

</details>


### [37] [Sequentially Acquiring Concept Knowledge to Guide Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33399)
*Shivanand Kundargi,Kowshik Thopalli,Tejas Gokhale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SACK（Sequentially Acquiring Concept Knowledge），一种即插即用技术，通过将类别知识分解为细粒度概念并度量新样本与已学概念间的语义关系来对新样本排序与优先采样，从而在稳定性-可塑性权衡之外为持续学习提供增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习方法主要聚焦于平衡稳定性与可塑性、缓解灾难性遗忘，而新数据中样本的顺序与性质对训练的影响仍被忽视。作者认为CL算法应能依据样本与先验知识的关系及其对学习过程的作用对输入样本进行排序。

Method: SACK将模型的类别知识拆解为细粒度概念，在每个experience中计算已学概念与新概念之间的关系，并利用该关系知识对新样本进行优先级排序（scoring and prioritizing）。其设计为与现有CL方法解耦的插件式模块。

Result: 在正则化、回放、基于提示（prompt-based）等多类CL方法上，于class-incremental与task-incremental两种设置下验证：集成SACK后准确率提升、遗忘减少，并能处理长尾分布、聚焦语义可解释区域，同时获得更好的校准性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 样本排序与语义关系驱动的优先采样是持续学习中一个被低估但有效的维度；SACK作为即插即用组件可普遍增强现有CL方法的性能与模型质量。

Abstract: The goal of continual learning (CL) is to adapt to new data (plasticity) while retaining the knowledge acquired from old data (stability). Existing methods are explicitly designed to focus on balancing stability and plasticity to mitigate the challenge of catastrophic forgetting while promoting learning. However, the impact of order and nature of samples from the new data that the network is trained on remains an under explored factor. We argue that a CL algorithm should have the ability to rank incoming samples in terms of their relationship with prior data and their effect on the learning process. In this work, we investigate if scoring and prioritizing incoming data based on their semantic relationships with the model's current knowledge can benefit CL. We propose SACK, short for Sequentially Acquiring Concept Knowledge, a plug and play technique for continual learning methods. SACK dissects categorical knowledge of the model into fine-grained concepts, computes the relationships between previously learned concepts and new concepts in each experience, and uses this relationship knowledge for prioritizing new samples. Experiments across several types of CL methods (regularization, replay, and prompt based) in class incremental and task incremental settings demonstrate that integration of SACK not only improves accuracy and reduces forgetting but also handles long tail distribution, helps focus on semantically interpretable regions, and yields better calibrated and robust models compared to prior approaches.

</details>


### [38] [Temporal Selective Exploration for Reinforcement Learning-Guided Continuous-Discrete Flow Matching in 3D Molecular Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33417)
*Lianghong Chen,Yan Yi Li,Gen Zhou,Yuxi Long,Ganlin Feng,Mike Domaratzki,Pingzhao Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种RL引导的连续-离散流匹配框架，结合时间选择性探索与奖励优势校准，用于3D从头分子设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配生成能力强，但其基于ODE的确定性轨迹不利于RL优化；将整条轨迹转为SDE虽可引入随机性，却带来无效探索噪声；多目标优化中还存在目标支配、部分目标更新不足的问题。

Method: 联合优化原子坐标的连续流匹配与分子身份的离散流匹配；仅在主要贡献目标属性的时间步上进行探索（时间选择性探索）；引入基于奖励的优势校准机制缓解多目标支配。

Result: 在EGFR（研究充分）与Pin1（具挑战性）靶点上设计抑制剂，识别出若干新颖候选分子，并获得in silico验证支持。

Conclusion: 该框架在连续-离散混合设置下有效实现RL引导的流匹配生成，减少无效探索并改善多目标平衡，具备3D从头分子设计的实用潜力。

Abstract: Flow matching has shown strong potential in generative tasks, while its optimization via Reinforcement Learning (RL) remains underexplored, especially in continuous-discrete mixed settings. Moreover, flow matching is formulated as an Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE), whose deterministic trajectories do not naturally support RL optimization. Introducing stochasticity by converting the entire trajectory into a Stochastic Differential Equation (SDE) is a common practice. Nevertheless, not all timesteps contribute to the final outcomes, and redundant exploration introduces additional noise. Furthermore, in multi-objective settings, some objectives may dominate the optimization while others receive insufficient updates. In this study, we propose an RL-guided continuous-discrete flow matching framework with temporal selective exploration for 3D de novo molecular design. Specifically, our method jointly optimizes continuous and discrete flow matching for atomic coordinates and molecular identities, respectively, while restricting exploration to timesteps that primarily contribute to target properties, reducing ineffective exploration. We also introduce a reward-based advantage calibration mechanism to mitigate objective dominance in multi-objective optimization. The framework is applied to design inhibitors for both the well-studied target protein EGFR and the challenging Pin1 protein. We identify some novel candidate inhibitors, supported by in silico validation.

</details>


### [39] [Yet Another Concept Drift Benchmark? A Principled Framework for Evaluating Drift Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33424)
*Lorenzo Iovine,Giacomo Ziffer,Emanuele Della Valle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套有原则的概念漂移检测基准，基于显式的漂移可观测性与告警语义形式化，引入有限检测窗口与重复告警机制，分离检测准确性、时效性与稳定性，并定义归一化指标与综合评分，通过10个检测器、9种学习器配置、16个数据集的压力测试验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念漂移检测的实证评估依赖宽松定义的协议、含糊的告警语义和不一致的度量指标，导致结果难以比较与复现。

Method: 构建基于显式漂移可观测性与告警语义的基准框架：引入有限检测窗口和重复告警以区分检测准确性与时效性、稳定性；采用可复现的"观察者"提供受控且可解释的性能信号；在此基础上定义归一化评估指标与综合评分。

Result: 在16个数据集、9种学习器配置上对10个漂移检测器进行大规模压力测试，验证框架的有效性，并展示综合评分相对现有度量的改进。

Conclusion: 该工作为概念漂移检测提供了语义明确、指标归一且可复现的评估范式，有助于公平比较不同检测器并推动数据流学习评估的规范化。

Abstract: Concept drift detection is a fundamental component of data stream learning, yet its empirical evaluation remains based on loosely defined protocols, ambiguous alarm semantics, and inconsistent metrics. We propose a principled benchmark grounded in an explicit formulation of drift observability and alarm semantics. Our framework introduces finite detection windows and repeated alarms, enabling a clear separation between detection accuracy, timeliness, and stability, and employs a reproducible observer to provide a controlled and interpretable performance signal. Building on this formulation, we define normalized evaluation metrics and a composite score addressing limitations of existing measures. We validate the framework through a large-scale stress-testing evaluation of 10 detectors across 9 learner configurations and 16 datasets.

</details>


### [40] [Scalpel and Brush: Pruning and Targeted Weight Editing of a Small Language Model by a Single LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33426)
*Sergei Koltcov,Vera Ignatenko,Anton Surkov,Rabie Abdultawab Ramadan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文用统一的稀疏权重编辑框架（修复与剪枝）检验“LLM 代理编辑语言模型权重”的效果来源，发现在 ODE 生成与 GoEmotions 分类上代理编辑带来显著提升，但无 LLM 的标准流程在获得代理找到的位置与形式后能复现大部分性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作显示 LLM 可自行编辑语言模型权重，但尚不清楚该结果中哪部分归因于代理本身，哪部分可被同形状的随机编辑、提示中的指令或无 LLM 的标准流程复现，因此需要受控的对照分析。

Method: 将修复与剪枝统一为以指定指标（以 BLEU 为例）为目标的稀疏权重编辑；设定三项任务：定向权重修复（brush）、结构化 MLP 神经元剪枝（scalpel，剪除权重置零）、带修复的层移除。由 DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash 驱动的 DeepSeek Harness 代理执行，dev 评估上限 50 次。研究对象为 Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct，每任务三次独立运行，最终在两个隐藏划分上评测；并设置独立替换检验与无 LLM 标准流程（如等份额逐层剪枝）作为对照。

Result: ODE 任务中改动不超过 0.0084% 的权重使二阶方程测试 BLEU 提升 24–32%，多项式右端方程提升 1.8–2.5 倍；替换检验表明 BLEU 提升反映记号对齐而非正确性改善。剪除 12.5–15% MLP 神经元使模型缩小 9.4–11.2%，两个隐藏划分上平均 BLEU 损失不超过 0.01，而等份额逐层标准剪枝在所有测试比例下均未满足约束。GoEmotions 七分类中，编辑使 test macro-F1 由 0.100 升至 0.396–0.451，holdout 由 0.175 升至 0.455–0.476；将岭回归分类器写入代理选定的权重行得到 0.424 与 0.464。同等评估预算下剪枝搜索结果剪除 15.9% 神经元，代理运行中通过隐藏检查者为 14–21.4%。

Conclusion: 代理式权重编辑在修复、剪枝和层移除上均有效，但一旦给定代理找到的干预位置与形式，无 LLM 的标准流程即可复现大部分性能；代理式与非代理式搜索的系统比较仍是开放问题。

Abstract: It has recently been shown that an LLM can itself edit the weights of a language model, yet it remains unclear which part of such a result is due to the agent and which part is reproduced by a random edit of the same shape, by an instruction in the prompt, or by a standard procedure without an LLM. We unify repair and pruning as sparse weight edits to optimize a metric we specify, using BLEU as an example: three tasks -- targeted weight repair ("brush"), structured MLP neuron pruning ("scalpel", zeroing pruned weights), and layer removal with repair - are assigned to a DeepSeek Harness agent powered by DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash, with up to 50 dev evaluations. We study Qwen2.5-Math-1.5B-Instruct in three independent runs per task, with final evaluation on two hidden splits. On ordinary differential equations, changing at most 0.0084% of weights raised test BLEU by 24-32% on second-order equations and by a factor of 1.8-2.5 on equations with polynomial right-hand sides. An independent substitution check showed that higher BLEU reflects notation alignment rather than improved correctness. Masks that remove 12.5-15% of MLP neurons shrink the model by 9.4-11.2% with mean BLEU loss of at most 0.01 on both hidden splits, whereas standard pruning with equal shares per layer failed the constraint at all tested shares. On seven-class GoEmotions classification, edits raise macro-F1 from 0.100 to 0.396-0.451 on test and from 0.175 to 0.455-0.476 on holdout. A ridge-based classifier written into agent-selected weight rows yields macro-F1 of 0.424 and 0.464. Pruning search with the same evaluation limit removes 15.9% of neurons versus 14-21.4% for agent runs passing the hidden check. Standard procedures without an LLM reproduce much of the performance once given the location and form of intervention found by the agent; a comparison of agentic and non-agentic search remains open.

</details>


### [41] [Fast Muscle-Driven Whole-Body Motion Tracking with a Differentiable World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33460)
*Kaibo He,Maciej Bakowicz,Shihe Zhou,Yusen Feng,Yanan Sui,Dhanush Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将 SuperMimic 扩展至 700/354 肌肉的全身肌骨模型，通过针对肌肉系统重设超参与四项改动，将训练从数小时缩短至数分钟（37×/34× 加速），并验证了肌肉特有的调参规律与测试时动作精化的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身肌骨模型的运动捕捉跟踪需在接触下协调数百块肌肉，现有控制器训练需数小时至数天的 GPU 时间，调参代价极高，缺乏高效训练方案。

Method: 基于 SuperMimic（通过可学习世界模型 rollout 反向传播训练跟踪策略），将其扩展到 MS-Human-700 与 MyoFullBody，并把 MuJoCo 的激活动力学纳入世界模型。加速配方：沿用 Unitree G1 的加速设置，替换其中 3 项在肌肉上失效的配置，并新增四项改动——观测不含肌肉几何、数据采集与参数更新并发、Beta 探索噪声（保证每维激励在 [0,1]）、50 Hz 控制。另在测试时借助世界模型精化策略动作。通过消融分析肌肉特定行为。

Result: 单张 RTX 5090 上 MS-Human-700 用 6.3 分钟达到 SuperMimic 默认超参 3.9 小时的留出回报（37× 加速），20 分钟即低于其 10 小时的跟踪误差；MyoFullBody 无需重调参即加速 34×。消融显示：较高策略学习率使半数运行停滞；裁剪高斯噪声引入强直性共收缩，而 Beta 噪声可避免。测试时世界模型动作精化进一步降低位置误差 26%，无留出片段失败，且达到同等回报仅需 5.1 分钟。

Conclusion: 面向肌肉驱动的世界模型跟踪可通过针对性重设配置与并发数据采集实现数量级加速，且肌肉系统的探索噪声与学习率选择具有特殊性；测试时动作精化可进一步同时提升精度与样本效率。

Abstract: Tracking motion capture with a whole-body musculoskeletal model means coordinating hundreds of muscles through contact, and current controllers take hours to days of GPU time to train, so every tuning decision is expensive. SuperMimic trains a tracking policy by backpropagating through the rollouts of a learned world model. We extend it to MS-Human-700 (700 muscles) and MyoFullBody (354 muscles), with MuJoCo's activation dynamics inside the world model. To make its training fast, our recipe takes the speedups developed for the Unitree G1, replaces three of their settings that fail on muscles, and adds four changes of our own: observations without muscle geometry, data collection concurrent with the updates, Beta exploration noise that keeps every excitation in $[0,1]$, and 50 Hz control. On one RTX 5090 it trains MS-Human-700 in 6.3 minutes to the held-out return that SuperMimic's published hyperparameters reach in 3.9 hours, 37$\times$ sooner, and in 20 minutes to lower tracking errors than theirs after 10 hours. It trains MyoFullBody 34$\times$ sooner without retuning. Ablations show what is specific to muscles: a higher policy learning rate stalls half of the runs, and clipped Gaussian noise adds a tonic co-contraction that Beta noise avoids. Finally, refining the policy's actions through the world model at test time lowers the position error by a further 26% with no held-out clip failing, and reaches the same return after 5.1 instead of 6.3 minutes of training. Code, the training recipe and videos are on the project page https://muscle-supermimic.beanpow.top.

</details>


### [42] [Differentially Private Policy Optimization in Linear MDPs with Adversarial Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33469)
*Yi He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在未知转移、全信息反馈、自适应对抗线性奖励的回合制线性MDP中，提出结合批量PPO更新与乐观策略评估的联合差分隐私（JDP）策略优化算法，隐私仅带来K的低阶附加代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化服务中的强化学习依赖敏感交互数据，需要保护轨迹与奖励函数；同时该设定下轨迹被反复复用于策略评估、奖励可能依赖已发布的私有统计量，带来隐私保护与非隐私效用保持的双重挑战。

Method: 批量近端策略优化（PPO）更新 + 乐观策略评估；使用私有回归统计量与私有奖励估计，在联合差分隐私（JDP）框架下同时保护轨迹与奖励函数。

Result: 高概率遗憾界在其余参数固定时保持与非隐私批量乐观PPO相同的$\widetilde{O}(K^{3/4})$主项对K的依赖；附加隐私项分别为$\widetilde{O}(K^{5/8})$与$\widetilde{O}(K^{1/2})$，均为低于主阶的加性代价。

Conclusion: 即使在自适应对抗奖励下，差分隐私也只引入K的低阶加性遗憾代价，说明隐私保护在此类线性MDP设定中可与主阶最优批量乐观PPO遗憾基本兼容。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) in personalized services often relies on sensitive interaction data. Motivated by the need to protect these data, we study differentially private (DP) policy optimization in episodic linear Markov decision processes (MDPs) with unknown transitions and adaptive adversarial linear rewards under full-information feedback. This setting poses two key challenges: past trajectories are repeatedly reused for policy evaluation, and reward choices may depend on previously released private statistics. The proposed algorithm combines batched proximal policy optimization (PPO) updates with optimistic policy evaluation, using private regression statistics and reward estimates to protect both trajectories and reward functions under joint differential privacy (JDP). With all other parameters fixed, our high-probability regret bound retains the same leading $\widetilde{O}\left(K^{3/4}\right)$ dependence on the number of episodes $K$ as the corresponding non-private bound for batched optimistic PPO. The additional privacy terms scale as $\widetilde{O} \left(K^{5/8}\right)$ and $\widetilde{O} \left(K^{1/2}\right)$. Thus, privacy incurs only lower-order additive costs in $K$, even under adaptive adversarial rewards.

</details>


### [43] [The Geometry Is the Memory: Task-Density Routing for Continual LoRA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33487)
*Alireza Heidari,Thomas Veasey,Mariano Tepper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 CONTLORA：用紧凑类别统计量替代存储样本，实现 LoRA 适配器的选择与分类，在无图像/特征保留条件下达到接近存储特征基线（差距≤0.07pp）的持续学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结旧适配器并不能保证持续学习器在推理时正确选择并使用它们；多适配器并存时，路由选择以及判断哪个适配器的表示空间与查询答案匹配都很困难。现有示例回放方法需保留图像或特征向量，存在存储与隐私代价。

Method: 以高斯类别模型在冻结表示空间中选择 LoRA 适配器；通过累积特征矩精确重建类平衡岭分类器（固定训练特征下等价）；训练过程不保留图像或特征向量；并引入以竞争类为中心的似然分数用于新类检测。

Result: 20 任务 CIFAR-100 达 88.55%，CUB-200 达 86.99%；三项基准的匹配组件研究中，平均最终准确率与独立训练的存储特征对照最多相差 0.07 个百分点；消融显示任务覆盖提升不一定带来分类提升，适配器组合方式同样重要；在留出类上，竞争类中心似然评分提升新类 AUROC 且不改变路由排序。

Conclusion: 在所评估设定下，统计记忆可作为示例保留方法的有力替代；任务歧义（task ambiguity）仍是持续存在的局限。

Abstract: Freezing older adapters does not ensure that a continual learner will select and use them properly at inference time. When multiple adapters exist, routing among them and identifying the adapter whose representation space matches the query's answer can be difficult. We propose CONTLORA, which replaces stored examples with compact class statistics for adapter selection and classification. Gaussian class models in a frozen representation select LoRA adapters, while accumulated feature moments exactly recover a class-balanced ridge classifier for fixed training features. Trained without retaining images or feature vectors, CONTLORA reaches 88.55% accuracy on 20-task CIFAR-100 and 86.99% on CUB-200. Across three benchmarks in the matched component study, its mean final accuracy differs from an independently trained stored-feature counterpart by at most 0.07 percentage points. Ablations show that improved task coverage need not improve classification and adapter composition also matters. On held-out classes, competitor-centered likelihood scores improve novelty AUROC over raw likelihood without altering routing ranks. These results support statistical memory as an alternative to example-retention approaches in the evaluated settings, while identifying task ambiguity as an ongoing limitation.

</details>


### [44] [Evaluation and Restoration of Safety in Reasoning-Tuned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33515)
*Ehsan Latif,Roberto Dailey,Risto Miikkulainen,Elliot Meyerson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文指出针对推理优化的开源大模型（如VibeThinker）虽然提升了能力可达性，但缺乏监管与外部护栏；实验证明推理能力越强安全性反而越差，并提出用"进化策略+后训练护栏"（ESPG）的方法低成本恢复安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源推理优化LLM广泛可得却缺乏监管与外部护栏，安全整合进人类社会至关重要。主流直觉认为推理提升会带来安全提升，但近期证据显示可能相反，论文旨在刻画并缓解这一问题。

Method: 1) 构建一个用于评估推理优化LLM的度量指标；2) 提出ESPG方法，将进化策略（Evolution Strategies）与后训练护栏（post-training guardrail）结合，对模型进行安全后训练。

Result: 在多个模型家族上，推理能力增强均导致不安全率上升；相比GRPO等安全后训练方法，ESPG实现简单、除推理显存外无需额外GPU内存、仅需极少样本（约30条）即可显著提升模型安全性。

Conclusion: 推理能力与安全性之间存在负相关，需针对性干预；ESPG为开源AI模型的安全后训练提供了一条低成本、易落地的可行路径。

Abstract: Open-source LLMs that are optimized for reasoning, such as VibeThinker, aim to democratize AI by bringing high-capability models to users with limited resources. They are broadly available but lack regulation and external guardrails; therefore, in order to integrate them safely into human society, it is critical that they perform in a safe manner. There are popular and intuitive arguments for why improved reasoning should improve safety, but recent evidence suggests that, in fact, the opposite may be true. This paper aims to characterize and mitigate this issue. First, it develops a metric for evaluating LLMs optimized for reasoning, and shows that increasing reasoning across multiple model families increases the unsafe rate in all cases. Second, it proposes a method for restoring safety by combining evolution strategies with a post-training guardrail (ESPG). Compared to alternative methods for safety post-training such as GRPO, ESPG is simple to implement, requires no GPU memory beyond that of inference, and works with remarkably few training samples (e.g.\ 30), yet makes the model significantly safer. The study thus establishes a path towards safe post-training of open-source AI models.

</details>


### [45] [A Perturbation-Driven Framework for Sensitivity Analysis of Dimensionality Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33523)
*Md Afridi Hasan,Jaemin Jo,Takanori Fujiwara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种模型无关、以扰动为驱动的降维方法敏感性评估框架，通过多类扰动与多强度重复实验，量化结构保持的变化并可视化诊断不稳定结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实数据集是有限、含噪的底层分布样本，降维结果不应在数据微小变化下产生显著不同结构；而传统保真度指标（trustworthiness、continuity）只评估单次结果，无法反映方法对合理输入变化的敏感性。

Method: 构建与模型无关的扰动驱动敏感性评估框架：将扰动视为对合理数据变化的受控探针，在多种扰动族与强度水平下重复"扰动输入—执行降维—多指标评估"，为每个降维方法导出敏感性剖面，并用于方法比较、行为分析与鲁棒性改进案例研究。

Result: 在传统失真指标下表现良好的方法（如t-SNE）仍可能高度敏感，导致轨迹、簇等视觉显著结构不稳定；某些方法（如MDS）在特定扰动下结构保持反而改善。

Conclusion: 敏感性分析对降维方法比较、诊断扰动敏感视觉模式以及指导更鲁棒方法的设计具有重要价值，有助于可信的可视分析。

Abstract: Low-dimensional representations produced by dimensionality reduction (DR) are commonly evaluated using distortion measures such as trustworthiness and continuity. In this paper, we study the sensitivity of DR methods to plausible input variations by quantifying changes in structural preservation and visually diagnosing how these changes manifest in the resulting representations. This perspective reflects the fact that real-world datasets are finite, noisy samples of an underlying distribution, and DR should not produce substantially different structures under small data changes. We introduce a model-agnostic, perturbation-driven sensitivity evaluation framework that treats perturbations as controlled probes of plausible data variation. Across multiple perturbation families and strength levels, we repeatedly perturb the input, apply a DR method, and evaluate the resulting low-dimensional representations using a set of distortion measures to derive sensitivity profiles for each DR method. We demonstrate the utility of our framework through case studies on DR method comparison, behavior analysis, and robustness improvement. We find that methods performing well according to conventional distortion measures (e.g., t-SNE) can still exhibit substantial sensitivity, leading to instability in visually salient structures such as trajectories and clusters, while certain methods (e.g., multidimensional scaling) may exhibit improved structural preservation under specific perturbations. These results highlight the importance of sensitivity analysis for comparing DR methods, diagnosing perturbation-sensitive visual patterns, and guiding the development of more robust DR methods toward trustworthy visual analytics.

</details>


### [46] [Deletion You Can Check: Auditing LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33525)
*Mayank Gulati,Gerhard Wunder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑"成员推断分数接近随机（AUC≈0.5）即说明模型已遗忘被删记录"这一常见判据：该判据仅在比较总体正确代表"有效删除后的反事实行为"时成立。作者在2×2受控实验（ArxivMIA/BookMIA × Llama-3.2-1B/Qwen2.5-1.5B）中用精确重训练验证成员参考值，发现BookMIA上参考值偏离随机带（0.122），诊断出总体失配；在合格的ArxivMIA/Llama轴上，GA+GD、NPO-RT、SCRUB均无一配置通过联合判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似遗忘（unlearning）评估普遍把"成员推断AUC≈0.5"当作遗忘成功的证据，但这一解读隐含一个未经检验的前提：用于对比的参考总体，能够代表该记录在有效删除后应有的行为。若参考总体本身失配，同一指标可能误判真实的删除效果，因此需要对该前提做受控验证。

Method: 设计2×2受控研究：两个成员推断基准（ArxivMIA、BookMIA）配对两个语言模型（Llama-3.2-1B、Qwen2.5-1.5B）。用精确重训练（exact retraining）校验成员参考值是否落在预设随机带内；对BookMIA/Qwen单元做事后诊断，将基准中label-0记录替换为同池、从未微调的记录，观察参考值移动。对每个近似遗忘检查点从三方面联合评判：成员性移除、超出非删除波动的删除定向位移、以及效用保持。比较GA+GD、NPO-RT、SCRUB三类方法。

Result: 精确重训练的成员参考在ArxivMIA上落在预设随机带内，但在BookMIA上达到0.122，导致同一指标反而"拒绝"了经验性删除参考。事后BookMIA/Qwen诊断中，替换为同池未微调记录后参考值由0.257升至0.523，支持该单元存在总体失配。在合格（admitted）的ArxivMIA/Llama轴上，GA+GD、NPO-RT、SCRUB均无任何测试配置通过联合判据。

Conclusion: 分数接近随机并不构成遗忘的证据，除非先由参考总体确立"随机"在此语境下的含义。成员推断基准的参考总体需经精确重训练等方式验证，否则近似遗忘评估可能产生系统性误判。

Abstract: A common indication that a fine-tuned model has forgotten a deleted record is a membership-inference score near chance (ROC-AUC $\approx 0.5$). This reading is sound only if the comparison population represents how the record should behave after a valid deletion---a counterfactual that is rarely validated. We test this premise in a controlled $2\times2$ study pairing two membership-inference benchmarks, ArxivMIA and BookMIA, with two language models, Llama-3.2-1B and Qwen2.5-1.5B. Exact retraining validates the membership reference: it lies within the prespecified chance band on ArxivMIA but reaches $0.122$ on BookMIA, causing the same metric to reject an empirical deletion reference. A post-hoc BookMIA/Qwen diagnostic replaces benchmark label-0 records with same-pool, never-fine-tuned records and moves the reference from $0.257$ to $0.523$, supporting population mismatch in that cell. Each approximate-unlearning checkpoint is judged jointly on membership removal, deletion-targeted shift beyond non-deletion variation, and utility. On the admitted ArxivMIA/Llama axis, no tested configuration of gradient ascent with retain descent (GA+GD), negative-preference optimization with retain training (NPO-RT), or teacher--student unlearning (SCRUB) passes. A score near chance is not evidence of forgetting until a reference establishes what chance means.

</details>


### [47] [Casper: A Projection-Based Neurosymbolic Layer for Scalable & Guaranteed Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33527)
*Lohith Konathala,Luca Andolfi,Eleonora Giunchiglia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Casper：将约束满足转化为闭式欧氏投影的可微神经符号层，单次前向即可保证精确满足约束且可扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实问题常要求精确满足约束，但现有神经符号方法存在两难：保证满足的方法无法扩展，可扩展的方法只能近似；传统深度学习则完全无保证。目标是同时实现“保证满足、推理可扩展、全自动免工程、完全可微”。

Method: 把约束满足表述为向可行域的闭式欧氏投影，可在一次前向传播中计算；作为神经符号层与任意神经预测器在训练和推理阶段组合。

Result: 在 MNIST 算术（最多 1024 位）、数独求解、Warcraft 寻路任务上验证：30×30 Warcraft 网格中精确基线超时、近似方法有效率低于 7%，Casper 达 100%。

Conclusion: Casper 是唯一在大规模下仍输出可保证有效解的方法，兼具可微性与免问题特定工程，可作为通用神经符号约束层。

Abstract: Many real-world problems demand exact constraint satisfaction to be meaningfully solved, yet existing neurosymbolic methods face a stark trade-off: those that guarantee satisfaction fail to scale, while those that scale must resort to approximation. Meanwhile, traditional deep learning approaches offer no satisfaction guarantees at all. In this paper, we propose Casper, a neurosymbolic layer that simultaneously (i) guarantees constraint satisfaction, (ii) scales at inference time, (iii) is fully automated, requiring no problem-specific engineering, and (iv) is fully differentiable, making it composable with any neural predictor both at training and at inference time. Casper casts constraint satisfaction as a closed-form Euclidean projection onto the constraint-feasible region, computable in a single forward pass. Experiments across MNIST arithmetic (up to 1024 digits), Sudoku solving, and Warcraft pathfinding show that Casper is the only method that produces guaranteed-valid outputs at scale: on $30\times30$ Warcraft grids, exact baselines time out and approximate ones produce valid paths less than 7% of the time, while Casper produces them 100% of the time.

</details>


### [48] [DiffER$^2$: Diffusion Ensembles for Retrosynthesis Prediction with SMILES Adapted Particle Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33557)
*Sean Current,Daniel Adu-Ampratwum,Xia Ning,Srinivasan Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将逆合成预测视为SMILES序列生成任务，构造离散时间与连续时间扩散模型的集成，并引入粒子引导机制提升分子输出多样性与top-k准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的逆合成预测多以SMILES序列到序列生成处理，但现有方法在输出多样性（唯一分子数）上存在不足，需要提升扩散模型在化学逆合成中的效用。

Method: 构建离散时间与连续时间扩散模型的集成用于分子生成，并加入引导机制；提出将粒子引导（particle guidance）适配到SMILES序列生成中，用于扩散集成。

Result: 粒子引导适配显著提升了扩散集成生成的唯一分子数量，同时提高了top-k准确率。

Conclusion: 结果进一步拓展了离散扩散在SMILES生成中的有效性，实证分析为扩散模型在化学逆合成中的能力提供了新见解。

Abstract: Computational retrosynthesis prediction has the potential to reduce development time for newly discovered drugs by automatically generating potential reactions for a target product. Data-driven approaches commonly treat this as a sequence-to-sequence generation task on SMILES strings. In this work, we construct ensembles of discrete-time and continuous-time diffusion models for molecular generation and incorporate guidance mechanisms for improved output diversity. We propose an adaptation of particle guidance to SMILES sequence generation which significantly improves the number of unique molecules generated by diffusion ensembles while increasing top-k accuracy. These results further expand the efficacy of discrete diffusion for SMILES generation, and our empirical analyses offer new insights into the capabilities of diffusion models for chemical retrosynthesis.

</details>


### [49] [Trigger-Invariant Contrastive Unlearning for Backdoor Removal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33571)
*Santosh Chapagain,Shah Muhammad Hamdi,Soukaina Filali Boubrahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出触发不变对比遗忘框架TICU，通过对触发词插入保持预测与表示不变性来消除文本后门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度上升(GA)等遗忘方法通过最大化毒化样本损失来移除后门，但存在关键缺陷：触发词的影响可能被转移到其他类别而非真正消除。

Method: TICU构造干净样本与插入触发词的反事实样本对，微调模型使二者输出分布一致且隐层表示对齐；同时保留干净数据上的监督分类损失，并约束参数相对原模型的漂移。

Result: 在多数据集、多模型架构、多攻击设置下，相比现有基线，TICU提升了触发准确率、降低了触发引起的预测偏移，同时保持有竞争力的标签翻转性能并限制干净任务性能退化。

Conclusion: 以触发不变性（预测+表示层面）为核心的对比遗忘范式，比单纯最大化毒化损失更有效地消除文本后门，且能在后门移除与干净任务性能之间取得更好平衡。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a severe threat to modern NLP models by embedding hidden triggers that induce malicious behavior at inference time. Recent unlearning methods such as gradient ascent (GA) attempt to remove backdoors by maximizing the loss on poisoned samples; however, these methods can suffer from a critical limitation: they may shift the trigger's influence to other classes rather than eliminating it. In this work, we propose Trigger-Invariant Contrastive Unlearning (TICU), a framework for mitigating textual backdoor behavior by enforcing prediction and representation invariance to trigger insertion. TICU constructs clean and trigger-inserted counterfactual pairs, then fine-tunes the model so that both inputs produce consistent output distributions and aligned hidden representations. To preserve clean-task performance, TICU also retains the supervised classification loss on clean data and constrains parameter drift from the original model. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets, model architectures, and attack settings show that TICU improves triggered accuracy and reduces trigger-induced prediction shifts compared with existing baselines, while maintaining competitive label-flipping performance and limiting clean-task degradation.

</details>


### [50] [Amortizing Exact Orthogonality: Drift-Triggered Restoration for Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33589)
*Lanxin Zhao,Xiaoyu Li,Dai Shi,Junbin Gao,Andi Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 RTOFT：用一阶切向更新 W(I+S) 训练，仅在漂移 ‖S‖₂ 达到阈值 τ 时用极分解恢复严格正交性，从而在每个前向传播中把正交缺陷严格约束在 τ² 以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正交微调（OFT）需在每个优化步构造精确或近似的正交变换；对 OFTv2 的 Cayley-Neumann 截断分析表明截断阶数并不能决定正交缺陷，默认阶数下参考实现的块缺陷跨越五个数量级，缺乏可控且与训练过程无关的正交性保证。

Method: 以缺陷 ‖AᵀA−I‖₂ 度量非正交程度；采用偏对称 S 的一阶更新 W(I+S)，其缺陷恰为 ‖S‖₂²；每次更新后读取漂移，仅当 ‖S‖₂ 达到容差 τ 时通过极分解恢复精确正交，并在结束时做最终恢复。

Result: GLUE 上将块对角、子空间与 butterfly 参数化的训练时间从 LoRA 的 1.10–1.58× 降至 0.97–1.13×；块对角版本五种子六任务平均超出 LoRA 1.98±0.39 分。τ=0.10 时，DeBERTaV3、Qwen 与扩散模型中训练期最大漂移保持在 0.10–0.12，而每 epoch 恢复一次时为 0.17–1.65；七项消融中无恢复的切向更新均更差，逐步恢复相对按容差恢复无精度收益且每步多耗 4.5–27.5%。

Conclusion: RTOFT 将正交性从依赖截断阶数的经验性质转化为与损失、数据、模型、优化器无关的确定性上界（≤τ²），在效率与精度上均优于 LoRA 与现有 OFT 实现，按容差触发恢复是精度与开销的最优折中。

Abstract: Orthogonal finetuning (OFT) adapts a pretrained weight by an orthogonal transform and preserves its singular values and row angles, but existing methods construct an exact or approximate orthogonal transform at every optimization step. We measure how far a transform $A$ is from orthogonal by its orthogonality defect $\|A^\top A-I\|_2$. For OFTv2, which truncates a Cayley-Neumann series, we show that the truncation order does not determine the defect. At its default order, block defects in checkpoints from the reference implementation span five orders of magnitude. We propose Restarted Tangent Orthogonal Finetuning (RTOFT), which uses the first-order update $W(I+S)$ of a weight $W$ with a skew-symmetric $S$ and restores exact orthogonality by a polar decomposition only when the drift $\|S\|_2$ reaches a tolerance $τ$. The defect of $I+S$ is exactly $\|S\|_2^2$. Therefore, reading the drift after every update and restoring before the next forward pass guarantees a defect of at most $τ^2$ in every forward pass, independently of the loss, data, model, and optimizer. A final restoration makes the returned transform exactly orthogonal. On GLUE, RTOFT reduces the training time of block-diagonal, subspace, and butterfly parameterizations from $1.10$-$1.58\times$ to $0.97$-$1.13\times$ that of LoRA, and its block-diagonal version exceeds LoRA by $1.98\pm0.39$ points on the six-task average over five seeds. At $τ=0.10$, the largest drift read during training stays between $0.10$ and $0.12$ across DeBERTaV3, Qwen, and diffusion models, whereas it ranges from $0.17$ to $1.65$ when restoring once per epoch. In all seven settings of our ablation, the tangent update is worse without restoration than with it, and restoring after every step yields no measurable accuracy gain over restoring at the tolerance and costs $4.5$-$27.5\%$ more per step.

</details>


### [51] [State Space Attention: Learning Context-Dependent Memory Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33625)
*Ege Çakar,Cengiz Pehlevan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 State Space Attention (SSA)：用可学习打分器为长上下文保留有界记忆，并以标准注意力读取，实现恒定推理内存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力 KV cache 随上下文线性增长；若要有界化，必须回答"序列模型为长上下文推理应保留什么"。作者主张该选择应被学习，而非固定保留最近 token。

Method: SSA 维护有界数量的过去表示，由学习到的 scorer 针对新到来上下文及记忆中其余内容对每个 chunk 重新打分，再由标准注意力读取；固定上下文 Transformer 为"始终保留最近 token"的特例。scorer 与 reader 联合训练，采用从可微软加权逐步过渡到硬保留（或一开始即硬保留）两种方式；并提出 recovery 训练技巧，通过生成监督样本为 scorer 提供信号。

Result: 在检索、变量追踪、多跳推理与语言建模上与循环模型、有界记忆注意力方法及全注意力 Transformer 比较。检索与推理基准上多数任务持平或仅差几分；在每项长上下文检索任务上远超同等可读预算的 Transformer，并追平/超越约 4 倍预算者，推理内存恒定。小规模下与全注意力最大差距出现在 RULER。另预训练并后训练 400M 参数模型，测量训练与推理成本随上下文长度和模型规模的扩展。

Conclusion: SSA 使固定数量 token 在更长上下文中保持有用；因其以标准注意力读记忆，推测可与 KV cache 驱逐方法叠加。整体实验刻画了学习式记忆保留在长上下文序列建模中的能力与局限。

Abstract: Standard attention's key-value cache grows linearly with context length. Bounding it raises the question of what a sequence model should retain for long-context reasoning. We argue this choice should be learned, and introduce State Space Attention (SSA), which learns to keep a bounded set of past representations and reads them with standard attention. A learned scorer rescores every chunk against newly arriving context and the rest of memory. A fixed-context Transformer is then the special case that always keeps the most recent tokens. We train the scorer and reader jointly, either moving gradually from a differentiable soft weighting of memories to hard retention, or with hard retention from the start. To produce signal for the scorer, we generate supervised samples during training, a technique we call recovery. We compare SSA with recurrent models, bounded-memory attention methods, and full-attention Transformers across retrieval, variable tracking, multi-hop reasoning, and language modeling. On our retrieval and reasoning benchmarks, SSA matches or comes within a few points of full attention on most tasks, far outperforms a Transformer with the same readable budget on every long-context retrieval task we evaluate, and matches or outperforms one with about 4 times that budget, while using constant inference memory. The largest remaining gap to full attention at small language model scale is on RULER. SSA makes a fixed number of tokens useful over a longer context, and since it reads memory with standard attention, we propose that it can be stacked with KV cache eviction methods. We also pretrain and post-train 400M-parameter models and measure how SSA's training and inference costs scale with context length and model size. Together, these experiments examine the capabilities and limitations of learned memory retention for long-context sequence modeling.

</details>


### [52] [Pretraining for Forecasting in Relational Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33645)
*Ziyue Lin,Sebastian Regalado Lozano,Michael Guerzhoy,Ricardo Baptista,George Stein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Query-Conditioned Relational pretraining（QCRP），一种自监督框架，让标准RDL骨干网络通过结构化时序查询语法生成多样化预测目标，学习可复用的冻结表征，实现在关系数据库上跨任务、跨实体类型的通用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实预测任务日益依赖关系数据库中异步的事务日志，而非均匀采样的数组。现有RDL模型虽能利用复杂历史做预测，但通常针对每个特定预测目标和根实体类型分别训练，缺乏通用性与表征复用。

Method: QCRP利用结构化时序查询语法自动生成多样化的预测目标，并通过一个共享的条件预测器交错训练这些目标，将标准RDL骨干训练为通用预测器；训练后得到鲁棒的冻结编码器，可通过轻量预测器或零样本接口复用于下游任务。

Result: 在大型RelBench数据库上评估，QCRP学到的冻结表征在多任务、多实体类型上均支持有竞争力的预测性能；单一统一的QCRP检查点可与单独训练的、针对特定实体的专家模型相媲美。

Conclusion: 结果证明，将标准RDL骨干网络学习到的表征复用于关系数据库预测任务是可行且有前景的，QCRP为实现数据库级通用预测器提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Real-world forecasting increasingly relies on asynchronous transaction logs in relational databases rather than uniformly sampled arrays. While relational deep learning (RDL) models can forecast from these complex histories, they are typically trained separately for each specific target and root entity type. We introduce Query-Conditioned Relational pretraining (QCRP), a self-supervised framework that enables a standard RDL backbone to act as a universal forecaster across a database. QCRP generates diverse forecasting objectives using a structured temporal query grammar and interleaves them through a shared conditional predictor. This produces a robust, frozen encoder reusable for downstream tasks via lightweight predictors or a zero-shot interface. Evaluated on large-scale RelBench databases, QCRP learns frozen representations that support competitive forecasting across tasks and entity types. Furthermore, a single unified QCRP model checkpoint is competitive with isolated, entity-specific specialists. We demonstrate promising results for reusing the learned representations from standard RDL backbones for forecasting tasks in relational databases.

</details>


### [53] [The Danger of Double Precision: Working Precision as a Hidden Constraint in Gaussian Process Maximum Likelihood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33647)
*Thomas A. Scott*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 高斯过程超参数通常用双精度最大化边际似然拟合，但光滑核与无噪数据下协方差矩阵的条件数常超出双精度可解析范围。作者通过变精度重拟合表明双精度拟合是不同的模型，拟合需在有效位数超过协方差条件数的以2为底对数（约70比特）后才收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究双精度边际似然拟合GP超参数是否可靠：光滑核、无噪数据下协方差矩阵条件数往往超出double精度（约53比特）可分辨的范围，可能导致拟合到"错误的模型"并影响下游（后验方差估计、主动学习闭环）。

Method: 在含贝叶斯优化聚类的设计上对平方指数核进行跨有效位数宽度的重拟合，并以高精度参考为基准；分析Cholesky失败作为隐式条件约束（主要源于核矩阵元素的舍入），给出由工作精度预测的受约束解并与Powell拟合比较，对理想化问题给出局部展开证明；在43组核/设计/问题设置（含阴性对照）上检验所需位数与参考最优处条件数对数加容差偏移的关系；还将整个似然提升至80位有效数字与固定jitter方法进行对比。

Result: 双精度拟合与高精度参考对应不同模型；拟合在宽度超过log2(条件数)（约70比特）后才收敛。Cholesky失败实为隐式条件约束，主要由核元素舍入引起；仅由工作精度预测的受约束解在40–64比特六个精度中的五个上与最佳Powell拟合相差在20%以内。43组设置中所需位数≈参考最优处条件数对数+容差相关偏移；最宽松容差下双精度足以应对Matern 1.5核，但对聚类设计上的平方指数核不足。双精度超参数会在设计聚类附近高估后验标准差，并使似然趋向平坦极限的二次目标上的闭环主动学习停滞。

Conclusion: 将整个似然计算提升至80位有效数字可在所有测试的固定设计上恢复参考精度，而固定jitter只是以失败换后验偏差。因此对于光滑核与聚类设计，应使用扩展精度而非双精度或jitter来稳健拟合GP超参数。

Abstract: Gaussian process (GP) hyperparameters are routinely fitted by maximizing the marginal likelihood in IEEE double precision, although for smooth kernels and noiseless data the covariance matrix is often more ill conditioned than double precision can resolve. For a squared-exponential kernel on a design with a Bayesian-optimization cluster, refits over a range of significand widths, judged against a high-precision reference, show that the double-precision fit is a different model and that fits converge only once the width exceeds the base-2 logarithm of the covariance condition number, about 70 bits. Cholesky failure acts as an implicit conditioning constraint, caused mostly by rounding of the kernel entries; the constrained solution predicted from the working precision alone is within 20% of the best Powell fits at five of the six precisions from 40 to 64 bits, with a local expansion proved for the idealized problem. Across 43 kernels, designs and problem settings, including negative controls that double precision already resolves, the bits needed follow the log condition number at the reference optimum plus a tolerance-dependent offset, and at the loosest tolerance double precision suffices for the Matern 1.5 kernel but not for the squared-exponential kernel on clustered designs. Double-precision hyperparameters overstate the posterior standard deviation near the design cluster and, on a quadratic objective whose likelihood heads to the flat limit, stall closed-loop active learning. Promoting the whole likelihood to 80 significand bits recovers the reference accuracy on every fixed design tested, whereas a fixed jitter trades failures for a posterior bias.

</details>


### [54] [PRIOR: Pre-Call Reasoning in SFT and Its Influence on Tool-Use Retention under RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33648)
*Kaiwen Hu,Hyunin Lee,Joseph Suh,Serina Chang,Shuai Shao,Yong Zhang,Namyong Park,Xiaoqiang Luo,Luke Simon,Somayeh Sojoudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究SFT阶段学到的工具使用行为在后续RL中为何会退化，发现"工具调用前推理长度"是关键因素，并据此提出按平均调用前推理长度筛选SFT数据的策略，在不明显增加工具调用率的前提下提升RL后的准确率与含工具条件答题成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SFT阶段习得的工具使用能力在RL阶段并不总能保留，但导致这种保留差异的SFT数据因素尚不清楚；作者希望定位可解释、可操作的数据维度来解释并改善工具使用行为的RL保留。

Method: 构造Low/Medium/High三组token预算大致匹配、但工具调用深度不同的SFT数据集，在相同RL配方下比较；观测各数据集中每次工具调用前的推理量差异，提出"预调用推理影响工具使用校准与保留"的假设。随后建立理想化理论分析，给出选择性保留视角：在仅结果优化（outcome-only）下，整体与工具条件准确率优势可被保留，而工具发起仅在含工具轨迹优于无工具替代时才上升。基于该分析，按平均调用前推理长度对原High轨迹排序，在近似匹配累计SFT token曝光的前提下保留排名最高的样本。

Result: 三种初始化在RL训练准确率上相近，但工具使用动态显著不同：工具发起在Low上升、Medium下降、High坍缩，且High轨迹的调用前推理显著更少。按预调用推理长度筛选High数据后，在多个基准上提升了RL后准确率与工具条件最终答案成功率，且并未一致地提高工具调用发起率。

Conclusion: 调用前推理是SFT监督中一个有价值的维度，可用于理解并改善下游RL中的工具使用保留；简单的数据筛选即可带来性能收益，说明SFT数据结构而非仅数量决定了RL阶段行为保留。

Abstract: Tool use learned during supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is not always retained during reinforcement learning (RL). We study how properties of SFT data may affect this retention by constructing Low, Medium, and High SFT datasets with approximately matched token budgets but different tool-call depths. Under the same RL recipe, the three initializations reach similar RL training accuracy yet exhibit markedly different tool-use dynamics: tool initiation increases in Low, declines in Medium, and collapses in High. We observe that these datasets also differ substantially in the amount of reasoning preceding each tool call. In particular, High trajectories contain considerably less pre-call reasoning, motivating the hypothesis that pre-call reasoning may affect how well tool use is calibrated and, consequently, whether the corresponding behaviors are retained during RL. An idealized theoretical analysis provides a selective-retention view of this process: advantages in overall and tool-conditioned accuracy can be preserved under outcome-only optimization, while tool initiation increases only when tool-using trajectories are more successful than the tool-free alternative. Guided by this analysis, we rank the original High trajectories by average pre-call reasoning length and retain the highest-ranked examples while approximately matching cumulative SFT token exposure. This simple filtering strategy improves post-RL accuracy and tool-conditioned final-answer success without consistently increasing tool initiation across multiple benchmarks. These results suggest that pre-call reasoning is a useful dimension of SFT supervision for understanding and improving tool-use retention under downstream RL.

</details>


### [55] [Self-supervised Clipped-objective Reinforcement Leaning Framework for Risk-aware Portfolio Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33652)
*Zongshen Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自监督裁剪目标组合管理框架SCP，通过两阶段架构融合微观市场结构与宏观指标并稳定策略学习，在A股三大指数数据集上取得更优的风险-收益权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度强化学习的组合管理模型存在两点缺陷：一是忽略tick级日内市场动态（如订单流失衡、流动性波动）所反映的资金快速流动；二是强化学习固有的高方差导致学习过程不稳定，在非平稳金融市场中部署困难。

Method: SCP采用两阶段架构：市场特征整合阶段将微观市场结构模式与宏观金融指标相结合，提升对日内资产趋势动态的敏感性；策略优化阶段利用中间资产状态表示，通过自监督裁剪目标策略学习器导出最优组合配置，兼顾有效性与计算效率。

Result: 在中国A股市场三个主要指数数据集上的大量实验表明，SCP在风险-收益权衡方面优于当前最先进基线；探索性实验与消融研究进一步验证了关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 融合微观结构与宏观指标的多尺度特征并结合自监督裁剪目标优化，可有效缓解强化学习组合管理中的信息缺失与高方差问题，实现稳健且高效的组合配置。

Abstract: Portfolio management involves balancing diverse financial assets, a critical yet challenging task in quantitative trading. Although deep reinforcement learning has emerged as an active research domain for solving complex sequential decision-making problems over the past decade, deploying reinforcement learning frameworks in non-stationary financial markets raises formidable difficulties. Most existing reinforcement learning-based portfolio management models fail to account for tick-level intraday market dynamics (e.g., order flow imbalances and liquidity fluctuations), which reflect rapid capital movements. Simultaneously, these models suffer from unstable learning processes due to reinforcement learning's inherent high variance. This paper presents a Self-supervised Clipped-objective Portfolio management framework (SCP), which addresses these limitations through a two-phase architecture. Market feature integration phase (application contribution) integrates micro-level market microstructure patterns with macro-level financial indicators to enhance sensitivity to intraday asset trend dynamics. Policy optimization Phase (AI contribution) employs intermediate asset state representations to derive optimal portfolio allocations via a self-supervised clipped-objective policy-learner, ensuring both effectiveness and computational efficiency. Extensive evaluations on three major index datasets from China's A-share market demonstrate that SCP's superior performance in balancing risk-return trade-offs compared to state-of-the-art baselines. Exploratory and ablation studies further validate the contributions of SCP's key components.

</details>


### [56] [Flipping the Coins: Training Physical Simulators through Hard Switches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33716)
*Tianrun Gao,Ruiqi Feng,Haomin Wen,Tailin Wu,Liangqiong Qu,Xiaomeng Huang,Ruiqi Shu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EventFlip提出一种针对含硬切换（跳变）的混合仿真器的无偏梯度估计方法：通过翻转近阈值开关并回放求解器，用反事实轨迹的损失差构造跳变项，与连续路径梯度结合，在电网级联、爆轰波、皮层扩散性去极化三个任务上均取得最低误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合仿真器中嵌入神经网络的学得组件依赖参考轨迹训练，但许多科学求解器包含硬开关（如线路跳闸、网格点火、脑组织去极化），损失在开关翻转处存在离散跳变。自动微分只能捕捉光滑部分；现有范式陷入两难：梯度松弛会平滑开关并跨跳变引入偏差，无导数方法把求解器视为黑箱而丢弃连续动力学路径梯度。

Method: EventFlip从存储状态翻转一个近阈值开关，回放原求解器，测量两次运行的损失差，即跨开关的有限差分。对每个阈值施加小高斯噪声，将每个开关建模为随机二值决策，从而导出无偏梯度估计器（连续路径梯度 + 由反事实回放构造的跳变项）。EventFlip-Rand 优化噪声平均目标；EventFlip-Hard 直接作用于部署时的确定性硬开关仿真器，可证明针对完整的不连续损失。

Result: 在相同前向评估预算下，与十二个基线在电力网级联、爆轰波、皮层扩散性去极化三个任务上对比，EventFlip在每个任务上误差最低，分别将最强基线的误差降低1.5倍、1.4倍、1.4倍。

Conclusion: 反事实开关翻转+噪声平滑可同时保留连续路径梯度并正确计入离散跳变，为含硬切换的科学混合仿真器提供无偏、实用的训练信号，并兼容部署时的确定性硬开关模型。

Abstract: Hybrid simulators embed learnable components such as neural networks in physical solvers and train them on reference trajectories. Many scientific solvers feature hard switches, such as a tripping power line, an igniting grid cell, or depolarizing brain tissue, where the loss exhibits discrete jumps across switch flips, leaving standard automatic differentiation to capture only its smooth part. Existing training paradigms face a fundamentally challenging trade-off, as gradient-based relaxations smooth the switches and introduce bias across jumps, whereas derivative-free methods treat the solver as a black box and discard path gradients of the continuous dynamics. Here we introduce EventFlip, which flips a near-threshold switch from a stored state, replays the original solver, and measures the loss difference between the two runs, in effect a finite difference across the switch. By perturbing every threshold with small Gaussian noise, we cast each switch as a stochastic binary decision and derive an unbiased gradient estimator that combines the continuous path gradient with a jump term constructed from these counterfactual rollouts. EventFlip-Rand optimizes this noise-averaged objective, whereas EventFlip-Hard operates on the deterministic hard-switch simulator used at deployment and provably targets the complete discontinuous loss. Under an identical forward evaluation budget, we benchmark EventFlip against twelve baselines across a power-grid cascade, a detonation wave, and cortical spreading depolarization, achieving the lowest error on every task and reducing the strongest baseline's error by factors of 1.5, 1.4, and 1.4, respectively.

</details>


### [57] [Understanding Mode Collapse in Diffusion Models under Classifier-Free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33719)
*Daniel Pfrommer,Chenyang Yuan,Frank Permenter,Ali Jadbabaie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个可解析的高斯混合模型，用来刻画无分类器引导（CFG）的作用机制，并给出引导分布在任意噪声尺度与引导强度下的闭式解，揭示CFG导致的均值轴方向平移与向奇异点持续偏斜聚集两种效应，并在大规模预训练图像扩散模型上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFG在扩散与flow模型中广泛使用且对生成质量至关重要，但由于对任意normalizing flow难以求解相应输运方程，其经验与理论理解都很不足。作者希望给出一个可解析的框架来解释CFG的效应来源。

Method: 观察到CFG效应源于条件分布与无条件分布表示能力不匹配（条件分布更复杂、保真度更高）。据此构造解析模型：条件分布为均值落在椭球上的高斯混合，无条件分布为近似该混合的单个高斯。该混合产生的流在缩放变换下封闭，从而可推导引导分布在所有噪声尺度和引导强度下的解析闭式解。

Result: 解析解表明CFG产生两类效应：(a) 沿均值轴方向的平移；(b) 持续向条件样本（从零噪声出发）所对应的奇异点偏斜并聚集。作者在预训练大规模图像扩散模型上验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 该工作为CFG提供了可解析的理论刻画，将引导效应归因于条件/无条件分布表示能力差异，并给出与大规模图像扩散模型实验一致的预测，为理解与改进引导方法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The use of classifier-free guidance in diffusion and flow models has proven critical to their empirical success, as it allows for the controllable generation of lower-temperature and higher-fidelity samples. Despite its widespread use, classifier-free guidance remains both empirically and theoretically poorly understood, owing to the difficulty of solving the associated transport equations for arbitrary normalizing flows. In this paper, we observe that the effects of classifier-free guidance arise from a mismatch between the representation capabilities of the conditional and unconditional distributions, wherein the conditional distribution exhibits higher complexity and fidelity. We propose an analytical model to capture this observation: the conditional distribution is a Gaussian mixture whose means lie on an ellipsoid, and the unconditional distribution is a single Gaussian approximating the mixture. The flows arising from this mixture are closed under scaling, enabling us to derive analytic closed-form solutions for the guided distribution at all noise scales and guidance levels. Our analytic solution shows that classifier-free guidance induces (a) a shift along a mean-axis direction and (b) a persistent skew toward, and concentration around, a singular point corresponding to the conditional sample starting from zero noise. We validate our theoretical predictions on pretrained, large-scale image diffusion models.

</details>


### [58] [CovNovBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Time Series Foundation Models under Covariate Novelty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33732)
*Wenhao Pan,Ravi Kiran Selvam,Michael W. Mahoney,Mayank Jauhari,Mengfei Cao,Dmitry Efimov,Boris N. Oreshkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文定义了"协变量新颖性"（covariate novelty）问题，并构建CovNovBench基准（88,476条评估序列），系统评测6个协变量感知时序基础模型在干预性预测下面对超出历史范围的处理协变量时的零样本表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协变量感知时序基础模型（TSFMs）可利用未来视界的协变量进行干预性预测（如新定价策略、新治疗方案）。但现有模型仅基于观测模式训练，当未来处理协变量超出历史取值范围（数值型越界或类别型出现未见策略）时行为不明确，且此类"协变量新颖性"场景在现有基准中缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 给出协变量新颖性的操作性定义；基于四个数据集（含模拟器与真实世界数据）生成或筛选构建CovNovBench，共88,476条评估序列，覆盖类别型与数值型处理协变量、平稳与趋势目标；以零样本方式评测六个协变量感知TSFM，采用点预测与方向准确率两类指标。

Result: 无模型在本基准上持续可靠：协变量新颖性下点误差几乎总是上升，方向性表现变化非一致，表现最优的模型随数据集与指标而变。

Conclusion: CovNovBench为开发在协变量新颖性下更可靠预测的TSFM提供了系统化测试平台。

Abstract: Covariate-aware time-series foundation models (TSFMs) condition their forecasts on additional input variables, called covariates, that are supplied over the future horizon. This capability makes them appealing for interventional forecasting, in which a target is predicted under a planned action such as a new product pricing policy or a new patient treatment schedule. We refer to covariates that encode such actions as treatment covariates. Current covariate-aware TSFMs, however, are trained on observed patterns, and their behavior is unclear when future treatment covariates fall outside the range seen in the history. Examples include a numerical treatment that leaves its historical range and a categorical treatment that takes a previously unseen policy. We call this practically common setting covariate novelty, and note that it is not systematically represented in existing benchmarks. We introduce an operational definition of covariate novelty and use it to construct CovNovBench, a benchmark of 88,476 evaluation series generated or filtered from four simulator-based and real-world datasets. The benchmark spans categorical and numerical treatment covariates as well as stable and trending targets. We evaluate six covariate-aware TSFMs zero-shot using point and directional accuracy metrics. No model is consistently reliable in our benchmark: point error almost always increases under covariate novelty, directional performance changes non-uniformly, and the best observed model varies across datasets and metrics. CovNovBench provides a systematic testbed for developing TSFMs that forecast more reliably under covariate novelty.

</details>


### [59] [Don't Waste the Population: Post-Anneal Refinement for Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33766)
*Yuma Ichikawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出I-PRP：一种确定性、无需训练的迭代路径重连后处理方法，利用并行退火器产生解群体的多样性，在局部最优解之间沿代价引导路径探索并精炼中间状态，迭代吸收新解，从而在不改动退火过程的前提下获得更优解，并具理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并行退火器会产生大量多样化候选解，但传统后处理通常只精炼其中最优的一个，群体多样性的潜力被浪费。

Method: I-PRP 选择有前景的候选解，对局部优化后的解之间沿成本引导路径进行探索，并对路径上的中间状态做精炼以发现更优局部极小；新发现的改进解被迭代地加入种群以驱动进一步探索。方法确定性、免训练，且不修改底层退火过程。

Result: 理论上证明 I-PRP 相对同一群体上的传统单候选抛光不会降低解质量，并在有限次迭代内收敛；在 MaxCut、MaxClique、图着色等组合优化基准上取得显著提升，配对评估验证了非退化性，匹配运行时间对比显示利用既有群体多样性可优于增加退火计算量。

Conclusion: I-PRP 是一种有效且可靠的并行退火精炼策略，能充分挖掘群体多样性，为组合优化后处理提供有保证的改进路径。

Abstract: Parallel annealers generate diverse candidate solutions; yet, conventional post-processing typically refines only the best, leaving much of the population's potential untapped. We introduce Iterative Path-Relinking Polish (I-PRP), a deterministic, training-free method that exploits this diversity to discover better solutions without modifying the underlying annealing process. I-PRP selects promising candidates, explores cost-guided paths between locally optimized solutions, and refines intermediate states to uncover better local minima. Newly discovered improvements are iteratively incorporated into the population, enabling further exploration. We theoretically guarantee that I-PRP never degrades solution quality relative to conventional single-candidate polishing on the same population and converges in finitely many iterations. Experiments across challenging combinatorial optimization benchmarks, including MaxCut, MaxClique, and graph coloring, demonstrate substantial improvements in several instances. Paired evaluations confirm non-degradation, while matched-runtime comparisons show that exploiting existing population diversity can outperform additional annealing. These results establish I-PRP as an effective and reliable refinement strategy for parallel annealing.

</details>


### [60] [On Grokking and the Dynamics of Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33825)
*Malik Amir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将Litman-Guo的grokking信号通道理论拓展至总体风险层面，用训练耗散与评估迁移刻画任务可见性，并证明仅凭训练动力学一般无法推断泛化的发生与时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有信号通道理论对grokking的解释尚未与总体风险（population risk）建立定量联系，训练耗散与评估分布迁移如何共同决定"训练损失低而总体风险高"的延迟泛化，缺乏统一刻画。

Method: 针对带时变核的预条件梯度流，区分累积耗散不足与迁移微弱两类评估可见性受限来源；给出任务可见性的定量界及其在信号滤波与评估分布变化下的稳定性；通过谱激活或传输刻画任务方向进入有效信号通道；建立迁移信号降低平方总体风险的充要条件，并在间隔假设下导出分类误差界。

Result: 得到同一训练轨迹在一评估分布下泛化、在另一分布下失败的充分条件；证明谱学习率差异可造成任意长的低训练损失与低总体风险之间的延迟；构造一族梯度流使泛化延迟趋于无穷，而零到全精度过渡时长与总体风险变化量均趋于零，且训练中无特征演化；最后证明泛化发生与时机一般不可仅由训练动力学推断，而取决于评估迁移几何及其与目标的关系。

Conclusion: 泛化并非训练动力学的内禀属性，而是训练耗散、迁移几何与评估目标三者交互的结果；这为区分"记忆式低损失"与真实泛化提供了理论判据。

Abstract: We extend the signal-channel theory of grokking introduced by Litman and Guo by relating training dissipation and evaluation transfer to population risk. For preconditioned gradient flows with time-dependent kernels, we distinguish two sources of limited evaluation visibility, namely small cumulative dissipation and weak transfer. We obtain quantitative bounds for task visibility and establish their stability under signal filtering and changes of evaluation distribution. We describe how task directions enter the effective signal channel through spectral activation or transport. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for the transferred signal to decrease squared population risk and derive classification error bounds under margin assumptions. These results yield sufficient conditions for the same training trajectory to generalize under one evaluation distribution while failing under another. We show that differences in spectral learning rates can produce arbitrarily long delays between low training loss and low population risk. By relating classification accuracy to the distribution of margin crossing times, we construct a family of gradient flows in which the generalization delay tends to infinity, while both the duration of the transition from zero to full accuracy and the corresponding variation of population risk tend to zero. These phenomena occur without feature evolution during training. Finally, we prove that the occurrence and timing of generalization cannot generally be inferred from training dynamics alone, but depend on the geometry of evaluation transfer and its relation to the target.

</details>


### [61] [FEAST: Fair and Efficient Adaptive Sampling for Task-Free On-Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33827)
*Yongxi Zhu,Ziyi Wang,Yichen Wu,Ruite Lu,Liao Chang,Haipeng Li,Ying Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文形式化了自生成经验学习中分配历史影响学习效率的Quota-Efficiency Law (QEL)，并提出任务无关的自适应采样框架FEAST，将机会分配与算力分配解耦，在同等rollout预算下提升平均性能并保护弱能力的学习进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体的rollout预算有限且需在异构能力间分配。常见做法是优先投入当前学习信号强的能力，但在on-policy学习中学习信号是内生的：过去的分配会改变策略，进而改变未来可用于分配的证据，形成反馈回路，使训练不足的能力获得的机会越来越少，难以改进。

Method: 提出Quota-Efficiency Law (QEL)，形式化说明即便在能力特定接触量匹配的情况下，分配历史仍会改变学习效率。基于此提出FEAST：一个task-free的自适应采样框架，将机会分配与算力分配分离。它动态发现能力簇并保留其学习历史，维持各簇持续的采样机会，为每个被选中的簇预留rollout预算，并在簇内逐步将计算集中到奖励差异更具信息量的prompt上。

Result: 在GURU-18K和SMC上，FEAST在同等rollout预算下平均性能优于有竞争力的自适应采样基线，同时更好地保持较弱能力上的学习进展。消融实验支持跨簇机会保护与簇内选择性分配两者的互补作用。

Conclusion: 分配历史本身会影响学习效率，因此需要保护跨簇的持续接触机会；将机会分配与算力分配解耦的FEAST是有效方案。代码将在论文录用后发布。

Abstract: Language agents trained from self-generated experience must allocate a limited rollout budget across heterogeneous capabilities. A common strategy is to prioritize capabilities with strong current learning signals, but in on-policy learning these signals are endogenous: past allocation changes the policy and therefore the evidence available for future allocation. This can create a feedback loop in which under-trained capabilities receive increasingly fewer opportunities to improve. We formalize this effect through the Quota-Efficiency Law (QEL), which shows that allocation history can change learning efficiency even under matched capability-specific exposure. Motivated by this, we introduce FEAST, a task-free adaptive sampling framework that separates opportunity allocation from compute allocation. FEAST dynamically discovers capability clusters while preserving their learning histories, maintains continued sampling opportunities across clusters, and reserves a rollout budget for each selected cluster. Within each budget, it progressively concentrates computation on prompts with informative reward variation. Across GURU-18K and SMC, FEAST improves average performance over competitive adaptive sampling baselines under matched rollout budgets, while better preserving learning progress on weaker capabilities. Ablation studies further support the complementary roles of cross-cluster opportunity protection and within-cluster selective allocation. Code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [62] [Mitigating Overgeneralization in RND via Spectral Target Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33850)
*Minseok Jeong,Yechan Lee,Hyewon Choi,Jeongyong Yang,SooJean Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从核理论视角分析RND新颖性信号在分布外输入上崩溃的"过度泛化"问题，将其归因为"谱激励不足"，并据此提出谱目标设计原则，以带宽可控的RFF目标替代随机网络目标，在D4RL、Atari与Fetch任务上取得更优的新颖性区分度与RL性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RND是RL中可扩展的新颖性信号，但预测器会在数据流形之外外推随机目标，导致陌生输入的新颖性分数塌缩（过度泛化）。现有工作缺乏对其失效机理的刻画，作者希望给出理论解释并据此指导设计。

Method: 在GP目标模型与NTK区间预测器的假设下，将RND新颖性分数刻画为"交叉核残差方差"，说明它由目标协方差核与预测器NTK共同决定，而非任一单独决定；由此将过度泛化识别为谱激励不足。基于该观点提出"谱目标设计"原则，具体实例为用带宽可控的Random Fourier Feature（RFF）目标替换隐式随机网络目标，将残差信号重新分配到被预测器衰减较少的模态上。

Result: 在离线D4RL、在线Atari与Fetch机械臂操作基准上，改进的方法提升了新颖性区分度及下游RL性能；RL实现代码已开源。

Conclusion: RND的失效源于目标核与预测器NTK联合作用下的谱衰减，通过显式设计目标谱（如RFF带宽控制）可有效重塑新颖性几何，兼顾理论可解释性与实际收益。

Abstract: Random Network Distillation (RND) is a scalable novelty signal for RL based on a fixed random target network and a trained predictor. However, it can overgeneralize: the predictor extrapolates the random target off the data manifold, causing novelty scores to collapse on unfamiliar inputs. This paper analyzes this failure through a kernel-theoretic lens. Under a GP target model and an NTK-regime predictor, we show that the RND novelty score is a cross-kernel residual variance: it is governed jointly by the target covariance kernel and the predictor NTK, rather than by either one alone. This identifies overgeneralization as spectral under-excitation. Motivated by this view, we formulate spectral target design as a principle for shaping RND novelty geometry. As a concrete instance, we replace the implicit random-network target with a bandwidth-controlled Random Fourier Feature (RFF) target, redistributing residual signal toward modes less attenuated by the predictor. Experiments on offline D4RL, online Atari, and Fetch manipulation benchmarks show improved novelty discrimination and downstream RL performance. The code for the RL implementation is available at https://github.com/tuuktuc86/RFF-RND.

</details>


### [63] [Normalizing Flows are Capable World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33872)
*Zijia Li,Yong-Hyun Park,Shusheng Yang,Jiatao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Normalizing-Flow World Model (NFWM)，用可逆观测编码器配合动作条件概率预测器，以精确条件似然联合训练表征与动力学，并可直接在潜空间规划与解码可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型需让智能体预判动作后果并以想象未来辅助决策；核心挑战在于潜空间预测分布能被准确映射回观测空间，从而使表征学习与动力学学习耦合一致。

Method: NFWM 由可逆（normalizing flow）观测编码器与动作条件概率预测器组成。编码器可逆使潜状态分布经逆变换定义出未来观测分布，故可通过最大化观测的精确条件对数似然端到端联合训练编码器与动力学。智能体在潜空间直接规划，并解码采样轨迹以检视可能的未来；目标条件控制中引入辅助逆动力学目标，塑造用于比较预测状态与目标的潜坐标距离。

Result: Teleport 任务中采样能刻画同一动作的两种可能结果；Flappy Bird 仅用似然训练的世界模型支撑基于奖励的规划，十局均达 100 管道；LIBERO 上基于该距离选择成功动作序列的频率高于 LeWM 与 SMWM；Atari Boxing 中用想象轨迹训练可提升行为克隆初始化策略的性能。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式似然、可逆潜表征的 normalizing flow 是构建世界模型的有效范式，可同时支撑精确似然训练、潜空间规划、目标条件控制与想象式策略改进。

Abstract: A world model lets an agent anticipate the consequences of its actions and use imagined futures to make decisions. We introduce Normalizing-Flow World Model (NFWM), which pairs an invertible observation encoder with an action-conditioned probabilistic predictor. Because the encoder is invertible, the predicted distribution over latent states defines a distribution over future observations. Maximizing the exact conditional likelihood of those observations therefore trains the representation and dynamics jointly. An agent can plan directly in the learned latent space and decode sampled trajectories to inspect possible futures. On Teleport, these samples capture the two possible outcomes of the same action. In Flappy Bird, a world model trained by likelihood alone supports reward-based planning that reaches 100 pipes in all ten evaluated games. For goal-conditioned control, an auxiliary inverse-dynamics objective shapes the coordinates used to compare predicted states with a goal. On LIBERO, NFWM uses this distance to select successful action sequences more often than LeWM and SMWM. In Atari Boxing, training on imagined trajectories improves a policy beyond its behavior-cloned initialization. Together, these results establish normalizing flows as capable world models.

</details>


### [64] [Random Voronoi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33875)
*Ray Verma,Pau Mateo-Bernardó,Daniel Mas Montserrat,David Bonet,Marçal Comajoan Cara,Alexander G. Ioannidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Random Voronoi Networks（RVN），用采样的Voronoi成对比较将最近邻几何嵌入三层MLP，第一层计算Random Voronoi Features（RVF），第二层聚合为Voronoi单元或top-k邻域分数，读出层给出预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近邻方法局部、简单、可解释，但非参数形式导致内存占用大、推理开销高，且难以融入可微神经网络架构。作者希望在最近邻规则、随机特征与现代表格模型之间建立桥梁。

Method: 构造三层结构化MLP：第一层基于所选训练样本间的仿射成对分隔器生成RVF；第二层将比较聚合为Voronoi单元或top-k邻域分数；第三层读出分类/回归结果。理论上证明包含全部样本与成对比较时，阶跃RVN可精确还原欧氏k-NN，ReLU RVN给出1-NN的违反分数实现并松弛为可微软加权；子采样得到可控单元数与特征数的近似RVN。并研究多种无监督采样方法，在随机覆盖、方向多样性、坐标稀疏性与经验特征方差之间权衡。

Result: RVF是一种高效的数据编码策略，可为从线性模型到现代表格深度模型的下游方法带来性能提升；同时因其基于k-NN构造并与成对局部方向相关联，RVF提供可解释的特征映射。

Conclusion: 该Random Voronoi形式化在最近邻规则、随机特征与现代表格模型之间起到桥梁作用，兼顾可微性、可控复杂度与可解释性。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor methods are local, simple, and interpretable, but their non-parametric form makes them memory-intensive, expensive at inference, and difficult to integrate into differentiable neural architectures. We introduce Random Voronoi Networks (RVNs), a family of structured three-layer MLPs that express nearest-neighbor geometry through sampled Voronoi comparisons. The first layer computes Random Voronoi Features (RVF) from affine pairwise separators between selected training samples, the second layer aggregates these comparisons into Voronoi cells or top-k neighborhood scores, and the readout layer produces classification or regression predictions. We show that, when all samples and pairwise comparisons are included, step RVNs recover exact Euclidean k-NN, while ReLU RVNs provide a violation-score realization for 1-NN that can be relaxed into a differentiable soft weighting. Subsampling yields approximate RVNs with explicit control over the number of cells and features. We study several unsupervised sampling methods that trade off random coverage, directional diversity, coordinate sparsity, and empirical feature variance. Empirically, RVFs produce a highly effective data encoding strategy, providing performance boosts to downstream methods ranging from linear to modern tabular deep learning models. Finally, we showcase that RVF provides an interpretable feature map due to its k-NN based construction and ties to pairwise local directions. These results position our Random Voronoi formulation as a bridge between nearest-neighbor rules, random features, and modern tabular models.

</details>


### [65] [The Gradient Difference Induced by Detaching the Denominator in QK Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33887)
*Yuanqi Zhang,Haocheng Wang,Congping Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文分析QK归一化中"分离(detach)RMS分母"对梯度的影响：推导出梯度差异的闭式表达，并将其精确分解为径向、切向与上游三个分量，实验验证分离分母会抬升测试损失、增大W_Q平衡范数并减小角更新量，而引入可学习缩放后损失差距消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QK归一化在计算注意力分数前对每个query和key除以各自的RMS范数；实现中常在反向传播时将该分母视为常数（detach）。这一做法不改变前向，却改变参数梯度，但其具体影响缺乏理论刻画与实证检验，作者希望厘清该实现细节是否会实质影响训练与泛化。

Method: 推导detach规则与标准规则下梯度差的闭式解：单token情形下该差异与自身query平行，仅改变query范数，归一化后无一阶影响；由于query由跨token共享的投影矩阵W_Q产生，W_Q上的梯度差等于W_Q右乘输入的加权二阶矩，并精确分解为径向分量（仅改变W_Q范数）、切向分量（改变其他token的query方向）与上游分量（改变传给前层的梯度）。结合AdamW分析径向分量对平衡范数与每步角更新的作用，并用小型Transformer语言模型做配对实验（跨硬件、评测数据集与学习率）。

Result: 分离query分母持续抬升测试损失，且该差距在硬件、评测数据集、学习率变化下均稳健存在；三个分量在整个训练过程中均不可忽略，其中切向分量最大。范数增长与更小的角更新仅出现在被分离的投影矩阵上，另一投影矩阵基本不受影响。但在引入可学习缩放时，未观察到可分辨的损失差距。

Conclusion: detach QK归一化分母并非无害的实现细节，它通过W_Q的径向分量在AdamW下导致范数膨胀与角更新减小，并带来稳定的泛化损失；该效应可被可学习缩放消除，提示归一化分母的尺度建模是影响结果的关键。

Abstract: QK normalization divides each query and key by its own RMS norm before the attention scores are computed. Treating this denominator as a constant during backpropagation leaves the forward pass unchanged but alters the parameter gradients. We derive a closed form for the difference between the gradient under this detached rule and the gradient under the standard rule. For a single token, this difference is parallel to the token's own query; it changes only the query norm and has no first-order effect after normalization. The queries, however, are computed by a projection matrix $W_Q$ shared across all tokens. The gradient difference on $W_Q$ therefore equals $W_Q$ right-multiplied by a weighted second moment of the inputs, and it decomposes exactly into three components: a radial component that changes only the norm of $W_Q$, a tangential component that changes the query directions of other tokens, and an upstream component that changes the gradient passed to earlier layers. Under AdamW, the radial component drives $W_Q$ to a larger equilibrium norm and reduces its angular update per step. Paired experiments on a small Transformer language model are consistent with this analysis. Detaching the query denominator consistently raises the test loss, and the gap persists across hardware, evaluation datasets and learning rates. All three components remain non-negligible throughout training, with the tangential component the largest. Norm growth and the smaller angular update appear only on the detached projection matrix, while the other projection matrix is essentially unchanged. With a learnable scale, however, we observe no resolvable loss gap.

</details>


### [66] [On the Learnability of Model Reprogramming: A Reachability and Relabeling Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33889)
*Zesheneg Ye,Pin-Yu Chen,Feng Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文从wrapper结构出发，分析了模型重编程（model reprogramming）所诱导假设类 F_MR 的有限样本可学习性：给出估计误差的agnostic-PAC上界（样本复杂度在可达性与重标记两项上可加），并给出近似误差的精确分解与源条件上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型重编程经验上进展显著，但其wrapper假设类 F_MR 的有限样本可学习性（需要多少目标样本、与目标Bayes风险的差距）尚未被充分研究。

Method: 针对仅暴露硬源标签的源模型，利用wrapper结构（输入变换选择可达源标签、输出映射对其进行重标记）将逐样本复杂度分解为可达性项与条件重标记项；据此推导agnostic-PAC估计误差界，并在加性输入变换下得到闭式表达，用显式二分类构造的下界匹配；近似误差则精确分解为源标签信息损失与输出映射限制两部分，并推广到暴露logit的源模型。

Result: 估计误差界在两项上可加地依赖样本复杂度，加性变换下为闭式且由显式二分类下界匹配；近似误差被精确分解并给出源条件上界，logit情形下该上界可经验估计。

Conclusion: wrapper侧分析将估计与近似两类误差定位到具体组件，为重编程实践提供了结构性视角。

Abstract: Model reprogramming adapts a frozen source model to a target task by wrapping it in a learnable input transformation from target input to the source-model input space, and an output mapping from source-model outputs to target predictions. Despite extensive empirical progress, the finite-sample learnability of the induced wrapper hypothesis class $\mathcal{F}_{\rm MR}$ remains underexplored. We address this for source models that expose hard source labels and analyze it through the wrapper structure: on any target samples, the input transformation selects reachable source labels that the output mapping relabels. Through this lens, we answer two questions: (1) how many target samples are needed to approach the best predictor in $\mathcal{F}_{\rm MR}$, and (2) what gap remains between the optimum over $\mathcal{F}_{\rm MR}$ and the target Bayes risk. For (1), the wrapper structure factorizes the sample-wise complexity of $\mathcal{F}_{\rm MR}$ into a reachability term counting reachable source-label patterns, and a conditional relabeling term counting their relabelings. This yields an agnostic-PAC bound on the estimation error, with sample complexity scaling additively in the two terms; this bound becomes closed-form on additive input transformations and is matched by a lower bound on an explicit binary classification construction. For (2), the same structure decomposes the approximation error exactly into the source-label information loss and output-mapping restriction with a source-conditional upper bound on this gap. We extend the approximation analysis to logit-exposing source models, where the upper bound is empirically estimable. The wrapper-side analysis localizes both errors to specific components, giving a structural view of reprogramming practice.

</details>


### [67] [Looped Models are Fast Multi-Token Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33896)
*Ayhan Suleymanzade,Sean McLeish,Sehoon Kim,Jinwoo Kim,Antoine Bosselut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Floodgates，一种简单自投机解码方法：在输入后缀追加一块掩码 token，通过低秩适配并行草拟多个未来 token，从而大幅加速循环式语言模型推理，在大多数设置下为最快的无损解码方法，最高提速 7.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环式语言模型可通过调整循环层数来调节计算量而无需按比例扩大参数量，已有工作利用浅层循环作快速起草器、深层循环作验证器实现自投机解码。但现有方法中起草仍使用全部参数，且每次只前向草拟一个 token，加速潜力未被充分释放。

Method: 将一段掩码 token 块作为后缀追加到序列末端，利用低秩适配（LoRA）训练浅层循环的起草器并行预测该块内多个未来 token；随后由深层循环进行验证，形成自投机解码流程。分析表明浅层起草器可借助验证阶段计算的深层前缀缓存，间接利用全深度计算信息。

Result: 在七个模型、五个基准、多种批大小以及贪心与采样解码设置下，Floodgates 在多数情况下是最快的无损解码方法，最高实现 7.5 倍加速。

Conclusion: 循环式模型的全深度训练使浅层起草器具备复用深层前缀缓存的并行多 token 草拟能力，这是提速的关键来源；该方法为循环架构的高效推理提供了简单有效的无损加速范式。

Abstract: Looped language models can adapt computation for predicting a token without a proportional rescaling in unique model parameters by adjusting the number of loops. This property has been used for self-speculative decoding, taking shallow loops as fast drafters and deep loops as verifiers, which is effective since drafting still uses full parameters. In this paper, we show that shallow loops can be also predictive of several future tokens and leverage this to drastically accelerate the inference of looped language models. To that end, we present Floodgates, a simple self-speculative decoding method that appends a block of mask tokens as a suffix and learns via low-rank adaptation to draft these tokens in parallel. Across seven models, five benchmarks, a range of batch sizes, and both greedy and sampled decoding, Floodgates is the fastest lossless decoding method in most cases, attaining up to 7.5x acceleration. Through analysis, we show that this speedup is enabled by all-depth training of looped models, with the shallow drafter's ability to leverage full-depth computation by conditioning on deep prefix cache evaluated during verification.

</details>


### [68] [Correlation-Aware Evidential Fusion for Trusted Multi-View Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33900)
*Tianyang Wang,Mengchi Zuo,Cai Xu,Yilin Zhang,Ziyu Guan,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出相关性感知的证据融合框架CAFE，通过互补性信任折扣与相关性约束抑制相似证据的重复计数，提升多视图分类的准确性与不确定性校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可信多视图分类方法将各视图视为独立证据源并通过主观逻辑的聚合规则融合观点，忽视视图间相关性。高相关视图产生相似证据，被当作独立源后会重复计入一致信息、赋予其过高权重，同时削弱提供补充信息的互补证据，导致融合结果既可能限制分类性能，又会产生校准不良的不确定性估计。

Method: 提出CAFE（Correlation-Aware Fusion of Evidence）框架：1）对视图私有专家输出的证据施加基于互补性的信任折扣；2）引入相关性感知约束，降低相似证据的重复贡献；3）从全局多视图上下文学习样本特定权重，将经相关性调整的私有证据与跨视图协作证据自适应加权融合。

Result: 在六个真实世界数据集上的大量实验表明，CAFE在常规设置下持续提升分类与校准性能，且当视图间存在强相关时增益尤为显著。

Conclusion: 视图相关性是多视图证据融合中不可忽略的因素；显式建模互补性与相关性并进行样本自适应的协作融合，可在保持可信不确定性估计的同时有效提升分类表现。

Abstract: Trusted multi-view classification aims to make accurate and reliable predictions by integrating information from multiple views. Existing methods typically estimate class evidence and uncertainty for each view and combine the resulting opinions through aggregation rules. However, they generally treat different views as independent information sources, overlooking the correlations among them. Highly correlated views tend to produce similar evidence, and treating them as independent sources may repeatedly count consistent information and give it disproportionate influence, while weakening complementary evidence that provides additional information. Consequently, such correlation-unaware fusion may not only limit classification performance but also produce miscalibrated uncertainty estimates. To address this issue, we propose Correlation-Aware Fusion of Evidence (CAFE), a framework for trusted multi-view classification. CAFE applies complementarity-based trust discounting to the evidence produced by view-private experts and introduces a correlation-aware constraint to reduce the repeated contribution of similar evidence. It then learns sample-specific weights from the global multi-view context to adaptively combine the correlation-adjusted private evidence with cross-view collaborative evidence. Extensive experiments on six real-world datasets demonstrate that CAFE consistently improves classification and calibration performance in general settings, with particularly significant gains when strong correlations exist among views.

</details>


### [69] [InstructTTS-OPD: On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Objective Instruction-Following TTS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33937)
*Haitao Li,Tian Tan,Yuguang Yang,Guanrou Yang,Wenhao Guan,Chenglin Li,Shan Yang,Xie Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将 on-policy distillation (OPD) 引入 Instruct-TTS 后训练，用三个单目标 GRPO 教师的速度场融合替代单标量奖励的 GRPO，并提出冲突感知速度融合，缓解指令跟随与音质之间的跷跷板效应，使三项指标同时提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Instruct-TTS 需同时满足指令跟随、转写发音正确、听感自然三项要求。现有做法是用单一标量奖励（通常配合 GRPO）联合优化，但各目标相互竞争，共享更新会漂向最易提升的目标，导致训练不稳定并出现"跷跷板"式的此消彼长。

Method: 1）先为各维度构建可靠奖励：用蒸馏的 Qwen-Omni 代理替代昂贵的 Gemini 评判、用去方言数据计算 WER 奖励、以 DNSMOS 替代存在语言偏置的 UTMOS；2）用 GRPO 分别训练三个单目标教师（指令跟随、WER、MOS）；3）用 OPD 在学生自身 rollout 上匹配各教师的速度场进行融合，按数据域将每个 rollout 路由到指令跟随或 WER 教师，并让所有 rollout 额外跟随 MOS 教师；4）提出冲突感知速度融合：融合前将音质目标投影到与主目标相反方向的正交补上。

Result: 指令跟随与可懂度可同时提升，无此消彼长；但音质目标与指令跟随天然冲突，简单地"加法式"纳入音质目标会重新引入跷跷板。冲突感知速度融合显著降低了指令跟随/音质权衡；最终融合模型在三项指标上均优于标量奖励 GRPO。

Conclusion: 多目标 Instruct-TTS 后训练中，以多教师 on-policy 蒸馏的"速度场融合"替代单标量奖励 RL 是更稳定的范式；关键在于各维度奖励的可靠设计以及对目标间冲突的显式处理（梯度投影式冲突消解）。

Abstract: Instruction-following text-to-speech (Instruct-TTS) renders a target transcript in the manner described by a free-form natural-language instruction, and a good rendition must succeed on three fronts at once: following the instruction, pronouncing the transcript correctly, and sounding natural. Reinforcement learning has become the standard way to post-train such generative models toward human preferences, and TTS systems increasingly adopt it. When the three objectives are optimized together under a single scalar reward, typically with group relative policy optimization (GRPO), training tends to be unstable: the objectives compete, and the shared update drifts toward whichever one is easiest to improve, producing a seesaw among them. We instead adapt on-policy distillation (OPD), which fuses several single-objective teachers by matching their velocity fields on the student's own rollouts.
