Abstract: Research on multi-exposure fusion (MEF), multi-focus fusion (MFF), and infrared-visible image fusion (IVIF) has largely focused on rendered RGB images, while unified fusion across these tasks in the RAW domain before image signal processing (ISP) remains relatively underexplored. To support research in this setting, we introduce the RAWPrism dataset, covering all three tasks, and the RAWPrism model, a lightweight unified multi-task fusion network. The RAWPrism dataset comprises 422 usable MEF scenes and 150 registered MFF pairs from our RAW captures, together with 1,500 FMB-derived IVIF samples, in which the visible RAW inputs are reconstructed by ReRAW and their RGB counterparts rendered by CycleISP. Scene-level partitions and task-specific rendering and scoring protocols connect these resources to method development and evaluation. To our knowledge, the RAWPrism model is the first to unify MEF, MFF, and IVIF directly in the RAW domain. It combines a CFA-aware representation, task-conditioned source gating, and a shared backbone to support all three tasks with one 3.58M-parameter checkpoint. Together, the dataset and model support the study of shared fusion mechanisms across exposure, focus, and spectral complementarity.

</details>


### [104] [Single-Database Rewards Are Hackable: Counterfactual Execution for Trustworthy Text-to-SQL RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34363)
*Shreshth Rai,Rishii Kumar Singh,Aksh Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出反事实数据库(CF)奖励：在若干隐藏的、代码生成的数据库副本上同时执行最终SQL，仅当全部结果匹配（严格形式）或按匹配比例给分（均值形式）才付奖励，用以识别"碰巧匹配"的错误查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2SQL强化学习仅在单一数据库上以结果匹配给奖励，当数据库缺少能暴露bug的行时，错误查询会"侥幸匹配"而获得全额奖励，导致奖励作弊(reward hack)与错误策略被强化。

Method: 构造反事实数据库：对原库生成少量隐藏副本（含family-blind生成器方案），重跑最终查询；严格形式要求全部副本结果一致才付全额，均值形式按匹配副本占比给奖励。在1,302个合成审计问题上评估，并以3.35B模型、GRPO、每臂3个种子做受控对比训练。

Result: 严格形式将获全额奖励的有bug查询占比从29.5%降至16.8%，reward hack从54.1%降至1.9%，且对2,847条正确查询零误拒；family-blind生成器效果相当。GRPO训练中均值形式未提升域内准确率（8.4% vs 11.7%），因两种奖励仅在5-7%的rollout上分歧；Spider上涨1.6点；单一种子会反转两种符号，单种子比较不可靠。

Conclusion: CF奖励能有效压缩侥幸匹配与奖励作弊，且几乎不误伤正确查询，但作为训练信号收益有限，主要因与原奖励分歧稀少；奖励设计评估需多种子，避免单种子结论误导。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for text-to-SQL agents pays a query when its result matches the reference on one database. However, a wrong query can match by luck when the data lack the rows that expose its bug. We study a counterfactual-database (CF) reward that also runs the final query on a few hidden, code-generated copies of the database and pays only if all results match (strict form) or by the share matched (mean form). On 1,302 synthetic audit questions, the strict form lowers the share of buggy queries paid in full from 29.5% to 16.8% and of reward hacks from 54.1% to 1.9%, rejecting 0 of 2,847 correct queries; copies from a family-blind generator do about as well. In a controlled GRPO comparison on a 3.35B model with three seeds per arm, training on the mean form did not improve in-domain accuracy (8.4% vs 11.7%), because the two rewards disagreed on only 5-7% of rollouts; on Spider it gained 1.6 points. One seed reverses both signs, so single-seed reward comparisons mislead. Code: https://github.com/RishiiGamer2201/counterfactual-sql-rewards ; data, models, demo: https://huggingface.co/counterfactual-sql

</details>


### [105] [Strong Starts, Simple Recipes: Unified Multi-Teacher On-Policy Distillation from Multimodal Specialists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34397)
*Wenzheng Chang,Taiqiang Wu,Yuqi Xu,Yongfu Zhu,Zelin Peng,Ji Zhao,Shuai Dong,Wenpu Liu,Caoyuan Ma,Xuehui Wang,Ziyue Wang,Weichu Xie,Haiyuan Wan,Ngai Wong,Tong He,Wenqi Shao,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Uni-MOPD：用同一基座专家的免训练均匀权重合并作为学生初始化，再做短程多教师on-policy蒸馏，在多模态任务上优于现有方法且训练快3.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOPD方法将能力整合几乎完全交给蒸馏过程，学生从基座模型或多域SFT检查点初始化，忽略了"起点"本身作为设计变量的价值。

Method: 将起点视为设计变量：(1) 对同一基座训练的各领域专家做训练无关的均匀权重合并，作为学生初始化；(2) 采用均匀域采样与全词表reverse KL进行短程多教师on-policy蒸馏，不使用调度、逐token加权或截断等超参。

Result: 5个多模态领域、19个基准上：(1) 未训练时均匀合并已匹配基座经200步MOPD后的分数，蒸馏后匹配搜索式起点；(2) 短程MOPD提升所有领域，教师仍领先的领域增益最大；(3) 均匀域采样+全词表reverse KL不劣于所有对比采样策略与散度；同预算下优于Open-MOPD，训练速度为其发布代码的3.3倍。

Conclusion: 强初始化是能力整合的主导因素，配合简单短程蒸馏即可高效融合多领域专家；将开源训练框架、专家检查点与统一学生模型。

Abstract: Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (MOPD) integrates separately trained domain specialists into a single unified model. Existing methods focus on refining the distillation procedure while initializing the student from a base model or a multi-domain SFT checkpoint, which leaves capability integration largely to distillation itself. Instead, we treat the starting point as a design variable and propose Unified MOPD (Uni-MOPD), which pairs a strong start with a simple recipe: a training-free uniform merge of specialists from the same base model initializes the student, and short MOPD with uniform domain sampling and full-vocabulary reverse KL refines it. Across five multimodal domains and 19 benchmarks, our study establishes three findings. (1) The starting point matters more than subsequent training choices: before any training, the uniform merge already matches the score that the base model reaches after 200 MOPD updates, and after short distillation it matches higher-scoring, search-based starts. (2) Short MOPD recovers most of the capabilities the merge leaves unintegrated; it improves every domain, with the largest gains where the domain teacher still leads. (3) From a strong start, uniform domain sampling and full-vocabulary reverse KL match or exceed every evaluated sampling strategy and divergence, without schedules, per-token weighting, or truncation hyperparameters. Uni-MOPD outperforms Open-MOPD at the same training budget while training 3.3 times faster than its released code. We will release the training framework, specialist checkpoints, and unified student.

</details>


### [106] [Any-Rate: Half-Bit Multi-Precision LLM Quantization with Coded Bit-Planes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34402)
*Hoonki Lee,Haechan Shin,Hyuntaek Jung,Yeonu Choi,Sangyeob Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Any-Rate 提出一种嵌套多精度量化方法，从单一 checkpoint 以 0.5 比特为步长服务 2–8 比特（2–4 比特区间为半比特粒度），在 2、3 比特下达到多精度方法中的最优精度，且解码速度与现有内核相当、能耗更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌套多精度量化通过只读取每个权重的前导比特即可从单一 checkpoint 导出多种精度，适合内存与能耗预算多变的端侧部署。但现有方法仅支持整数比特，相邻精度间存在较大精度落差，且 2 比特时性能严重退化。

Method: 1）将部分比特平面约束为 8 权重块内的扩展汉明码字（每权重 0.5 比特），实现半比特步长；2）各精度共享每行一个 LUT；3）对所有精度联合编码，并为每个精度设置独立目标，以降低共享比特平面带来的损失。

Result: 在四个 LLM 上，Any-Rate 在 2 比特与 3 比特下取得多精度方法中的 SOTA 精度，2 比特时保留 FP16 精度的 69%，4 比特时与最佳方法差距在 1 个百分点以内；解码速度与现有内核相当；LPDDR 能耗模型估计在同等精度下最多降低 27% 能耗。

Conclusion: 通过扩展汉明码字约束、跨精度共享 LUT 与多目标联合编码，Any-Rate 以细粒度半比特步长弥合了嵌套量化相邻精度的落差，在精度、速度与能耗上均具优势，并提供开源代码。

Abstract: Nested multi-precision quantization derives multiple precisions from a single checkpoint by reading only the leading bits of each weight, which makes it attractive for on-device deployment under varying memory and energy budgets. However, existing methods offer only integer bits, leaving large accuracy gaps between adjacent precisions and severe degradation at 2 bits. To address this, we propose Any-Rate, which serves 2-8 bits from one checkpoint, in half-bit steps over 2-4 bits. Any-Rate restricts some bit-planes to extended Hamming codewords in 8-weight blocks (0.5 bits per weight), shares one LUT per row across precisions, and encodes all precisions jointly with a separate target for each, reducing the loss from sharing bit-planes. Across four LLMs, Any-Rate achieves state-of-the-art accuracy among multi-precision methods at 2 and 3 bits, keeping 69% of FP16 accuracy at 2 bits, and is within one point of the best at 4 bits. It decodes about as fast as existing kernels, and an LPDDR energy model estimates up to 27% less energy at equal accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/hoonki2002/Any-Rate.

</details>


### [107] [DiversePlace: Diversity-Seeking Curriculum Reinforcement Learning for Macro Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34472)
*Wenrui Zhou,Jiashun Liu,Wenji Fang,Zhiyao Xie,Ling Pan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 DiversePlace，将"质量受控的多样性"作为宏单元布局的显式目标，用课程式 PPO 维护一个 HPWL 有竞争力但彼此差异明显的布局存档，供下游选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏单元位置强约束后续物理实现，但这类高影响离散决策必须在可精确测量下游质量之前做出，训练只能依赖 HPWL 等快速但次优的代理指标。现有 RL 布局器因此多只优化单一 HPWL 驱动解，长序列决策还需大量搜索辅助或离线专家数据，难以保留多个有竞争力的布局。

Method: 以质量受控多样性为显式目标，不追求单一代理最优解，而是维护一个 HPWL 具竞争力且彼此显著不同的紧凑布局存档。具体实现为课程式 PPO 框架：结合质量门控的多样性奖励与逐步扩展的布局时域，支持从零开始的 RL 优化，同时将探索限制在 HPWL 竞争力较强的区域内。

Result: 在 ISPD2005 基准上，持续提升 top-5、top-10、top-20 存档多样性；在全部八个设计上取得更低或几乎相同的 HPWL，且效率更优。

Conclusion: 质量受控多样性可作为宏单元布局的可行优化目标，在不牺牲（甚至改善）HPWL 的前提下提供多个可竞争的候选布局，为下游选择留出空间，并避免对离线专家数据或重度搜索的依赖。

Abstract: Macro placement is a critical early-stage decision in physical design because macro locations strongly constrain subsequent implementation stages. The task is difficult because these high-impact discrete decisions must be made before downstream quality can be measured accurately, forcing training to rely on fast but imperfect training-time oracles such as half-perimeter wirelength (HPWL). Existing RL-based placers therefore mostly optimize single HPWL-driven solutions, and their long sequential horizons often require heavy search assistance or offline expert data while leaving little room to preserve multiple competitive layouts. We address this gap by making quality-controlled diversity an explicit objective for macro placement. Instead of relying on a single proxy-optimal solution, we maintain a compact archive of HPWL-competitive yet meaningfully different placements for downstream selection. We realize this idea in DiversePlace, a curriculum proximal policy optimization (PPO) framework that combines a quality-gated diversity reward with an expanding placement horizon, enabling from-scratch RL optimization while keeping exploration inside competitive HPWL regions. On the ISPD2005 benchmarks, DiversePlace consistently improves top-5, top-10, and top-20 archive diversity and achieves lower or virtually identical HPWL on all eight designs with better efficiency. Code: https://github.com/William030422/NIPS2026-DiversePlace.

</details>


### [108] [Auditing Identity and Timing Signals in Operational Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34493)
*Andrew Hendel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出两种"审计"方法（身份审计与时间次序审计），在训练事件预测模型之前量化数据集中"单位身份"和"时间"信息的泄露程度，并在六个领域七个公开数据集上验证其影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测ICU脓毒症、硬盘故障等事件的模型可能并非真正学到事件规律，而是通过识别记录来自哪个病人/硬盘或记录时间而"虚高"得分；需要在建模前度量这类信息泄露。

Method: 引入两项简单审计：①同一单位两条记录是否来自同一单元（分类器区分）；②同一单位两条记录哪条更晚（时间次序判别）。在零假设下二者期望AUC均为0.5。在六个领域七个公开数据集上应用，并对比"病人重叠划分"与"病人留出划分"、日历切分下的评估结果；同时考察仅用住院小时数与测量计数而无临床值的预测能力。

Result: ICU中携带上一测量值前向填充使身份AUC从0.916升至0.993；12小时脓毒症预测中，患者小时被拆分至训练/测试时前向填充带来+0.163 AUC虚增，患者留出时仅+0.046；病人重叠下的虚增在第二个ICU数据集（死亡率结局）中同样出现；完整随访的时间顺序对照无正的平均重叠差；仅用住院小时与测量计数在留出病人上达0.761，加入原始临床值仅+0.018。ICU外95%硬盘与75%贷款记录跨越日历切分点，重叠差在日历切分下仍存在。

Conclusion: 审计可指示应检查哪些特征与预处理选择，而后果由评估协议决定；作者发布代码、可在任意含单位列与时间列的表上运行审计的库及使用指南。

Abstract: Models that predict events from operational logs, such as sepsis in ICU records or failures in hard drives, can score well by recognizing which patient or drive a record came from, or when it was recorded, instead of predicting the event. I introduce two simple audits that measure how much of this information a dataset carries before any event-prediction model is trained, and apply them to seven public datasets in six domains. One asks whether a classifier can tell that two records came from the same unit; the other asks which of two records from the same unit came later. Under the stated nulls, both have expected AUC 0.5. In ICU data, the identity audit flags a common preprocessing step: carrying the last measurement forward raises identity AUC from 0.916 to 0.993. On a 12-hour sepsis forecasting task, carrying forward makes a model look 0.163 AUC better than raw values when a patient's hours are split between training and test, but only 0.046 better when patients are held out. The exaggeration under patient overlap also appears in a second ICU dataset with mortality as the outcome. On the sepsis task, a chronological control with complete follow-up, in which training sees only a patient's earlier, resolved hours, shows no positive mean overlap gap. Timing is a strong predictor: with complete follow-up, hour of stay and measurement counts alone, with no clinical value, score 0.761 on held-out patients, and adding the raw clinical values changes that by +0.018. Outside the ICU, 95% of drives and 75% of loans have records on both sides of a calendar cut, and the overlap gap persists under a calendar split. The audits show which features and preprocessing choices to check; the evaluation protocol decides the consequences. I release the code, a library that runs the audits on any table with a unit column and a time column, and a guide to using the audits.

</details>


### [109] [SMOTE 25 Years of Magic: What Has Been Done, What the Evidence Shows, and Whom It Reached](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34511)
*Thanapong Intharah,Pharamut Makjaroen,Kanvara Thawarorit,Supawit Chaingam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对SMOTE二十五年来的发展进行审计：系统梳理37个SMOTE家族生成器对SMOTE六项决策的改动，用PRISMA 2020格式检索22,426条记录检验覆盖度，并随机抽取300个应用实例，结论是新的SMOTE变体本身不应再被视为贡献，应以类加权与调优阈值下的强学习器逐项对照测试并报告校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SMOTE自2002年发表、2018年十五周年综述后，已被3.4万余篇文献引用且半数来自计算机科学之外；但方法层面的实际改动内容、证据强度及其真实影响范围缺乏系统评估，需要一次可复核的审计。

Method: 构建76篇论文的精选语料（其中37篇提出SMOTE家族生成器），收集1,756条数据集条目、1,511项与SMOTE的已发表比较和71项研究的评估设计；同时以PRISMA 2020格式系统检索22,426条记录检验覆盖度，并随机抽取300个SMOTE应用实例。采用代理式AI在书面规则下完成提取、筛选与编码，并报告每一步的核查方式。

Result: 所有变体都在重新决定SMOTE规则的六项决策之一，37个变体中24个仅在二分类上运行；在方法自身论文中79.0%的已判定比较优于SMOTE，但独立研究发现优势随强学习器与调优阈值而缩小，且重采样会损害概率校准。语料仅包含通过筛选的3,097条记录中的52条，早期覆盖充分、近期稀疏。应用范围极广，但结果层面的收益未被证实，已记录四类危害，估计37%的应用在划分数据集之前对全量数据重采样。

Conclusion: 对于表格分类，新SMOTE变体本身不应再算作贡献；新生成器应在强学习器下针对类加权与调优阈值逐项决策测试，并报告校准结果。文末给出选择指南、15项报告清单与研究议程。

Abstract: Background. SMOTE was published in this journal in 2002 and reviewed here at its fifteenth anniversary in 2018. At twenty-five it is cited by more than 34,000 indexed works, half of them outside computer science. Objectives. To establish what the methods built on SMOTE change, how strong the evidence for them is, and whom the technique has reached. Methods. We audited a curated corpus of 76 papers, 37 of which propose a SMOTE-family generator, and tested its coverage with a systematic search of 22,426 records in the PRISMA 2020 format. From the corpus we collected 1,756 dataset entries, 1,511 published comparisons with SMOTE and the evaluation design of 71 studies; from the search we drew 300 applications of SMOTE at random. We used an agentic AI approach to extract, screen and code under our written rules, and we report how each step was checked. Results. Every variant re-decides some of the six decisions of the SMOTE rule, and 24 of the 37 are only ever run on two classes. In their own papers, proposed methods beat SMOTE in 79.0% of the decided comparisons, but independent studies find that the gains shrink with strong learners and tuned thresholds, and that resampling damages calibration. The corpus holds 52 of the 3,097 records that pass the screen and covers the early years closely, the recent ones thinly. The reach is very wide, but a benefit at the level of outcomes is undemonstrated, four harms are documented, and an estimated 37% of applications resample the whole data set before splitting it. Conclusions. For tabular classification, a new SMOTE variant should no longer count as a contribution in itself: a new generator should be tested one decision at a time against class weighting and a tuned threshold with a strong learner, with calibration reported. We close with a selection guide, a reporting checklist of fifteen items and a research agenda.

</details>


### [110] [Resilience Analysis Through Automated Stage Identification and Quantitative Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34527)
*Anna Strzoda,Krzysztof Grochla,Rafael Munoz-Carpena*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种与领域和威胁无关的机器学习方法，用于自动识别系统性能时间序列中的PARA（Plan/Prepare、Absorb、Recover、Adapt）韧性阶段，并据此量化系统韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有韧性分析通常依赖特定领域或特定威胁的事先假设，难以跨系统通用；需要一种能够自动识别韧性阶段并支持定量韧性评估的通用方法。

Method: 采用随机森林、支持向量机、LightGBM与XGBoost四种ML模型对系统性能时间序列进行PARA韧性阶段分类；利用模型预测结果，结合扰动事件前、中、后的性能数据计算相应韧性指标（R_t、RL）。

Result: 在两个不同运行领域的网络系统（IEEE 802.11、LTE）与一个机场交通系统数据集上验证：各韧性阶段最佳F1分数为74.3%–99.4%；韧性指标估计的最优模型MAPE在IEEE 802.11与LTE上为0.35%–0.82%，机场为1.65%–9.56%。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了阶段分类的有效性与跨领域适用性，为复杂工程系统的威胁无关韧性分析提供依据，可进一步评估系统抵御、吸收与恢复扰动的能力。

Abstract: This paper introduced an ML-based approach, designed to be domain- and threat-agnostic, for automated recognition of PARA (Plan/Prepare, Absorb, Recover, Adapt) resilience stages in system-performance time series, enabling subsequent quantitative resilience analysis across different systems. We investigate four ML models for resilience stage recognition: Random Forest (RF), Support Vector Machine (SVM), Light Gradient Boosting Machine (LightGBM), and Extreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost). We then use the ML models' predictions to quantify system resilience by computing corresponding resilience metrics from system-performance data observed before, during, and after disruptive events. We evaluate the proposed approach using datasets from two distinct operational domain network systems and an airport transportation system, demonstrating its domain-agnostic applicability to resilience assessment. The results demonstrate the effectiveness of resilience stage classification, with the best F1-scores ranging from 74.3% to 99.4% across all resilience stages. The overall resilience metrics R_t and RL are estimated, with the best models' MAPE ranging from 0.35-0.82% on IEEE 802.11 and LTE to 1.65-9.56% for the airport. These results provide a basis for threat-agnostic resilience analysis of complex engineering systems, enabling further assessment of how effectively a system withstands, absorbs, and recovers from disruptive events and stress conditions.

</details>


### [111] [Kant-Q: A Q-Learning Algorithm for Kantian Equilibrium in Symmetric Markov Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34536)
*Rishi Bharadwaj,Sujit Gujar,Yadati Narahari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Kant-Q算法：智能体学习"所有人都采取同一动作"的动作剖面，使每次更新都是单智能体MDP的样本，从而继承标准Q-learning的收敛保证，在混合动机对称博弈中收敛到帕累托有效且个体理性的康德均衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体之间的交互大量属于混合动机博弈，而主流MARL算法以纳什均衡为目标，这类均衡在许多此类博弈中帕累托无效率，因此需要新的解概念与学习算法。

Method: 采用康德均衡（每个智能体选择希望所有智能体共同采用的动作）作为解概念，在对称博弈中对应最优共同策略；提出Kant-Q，每个智能体学习所有智能体采取相同动作的动作剖面，使每次更新等价于单智能体MDP的采样，从而获得标准Q-learning的收敛性。

Result: 证明每个对称马尔可夫博弈都存在纯康德均衡；所有智能体的Q表收敛到同一极限，其贪婪策略即为康德均衡，且在每状态最优共同动作唯一时贪婪策略收敛；在对称单调博弈子类中，康德均衡在纯策略剖面中帕累托有效且严格个体理性；在迭代囚徒困境与最多200个智能体的GovSim渔业实验中，Kant-Q学到康德均衡而基线表现不佳。

Conclusion: 由于同一AI系统的副本天然会在对称博弈中相互对局，普适化（universalization）可作为合作式AI-AI交互的设计原则。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly interact with one another, and many of these interactions are mixed-motive games, which have posed a persistent challenge for learning algorithms. Most multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) algorithms are designed to reach Nash equilibria, yet in many such games these equilibria are Pareto-inefficient. We instead study Kantian equilibria, an economic solution concept in which each agent plays the action it would want all agents to adopt. In symmetric games, this corresponds to an optimal common policy. We propose Kant-Q, a Q-learning algorithm in which each agent learns action profiles where all agents play the same action. Each update is then a sample from a single-agent MDP, so Kant-Q inherits the convergence guarantees of standard Q-learning. We show that every symmetric Markov game has a pure Kantian equilibrium. We also prove that all agents' Q-tables converge to the same limit, whose greedy policy is such an equilibrium, and that greedy play converges whenever the optimal common action is unique at every state. We further show that in a subclass of symmetric monotone games, the Kantian equilibrium is Pareto-efficient among pure strategy profiles and strictly individually rational. Empirically, on the iterated Prisoner's Dilemma and the GovSim fishery with up to 200 agents, Kant-Q learns the Kantian equilibrium, while the baselines underperform. Since copies of the same AI system naturally face one another in symmetric games, these results suggest universalization as a design principle for cooperative AI-AI interaction.

</details>


### [112] [Investigating the efficacy of vortex beam speckle patterns for the recognition of atmospheric turbulence and fractional topological charge with a deep learning model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34573)
*Ratikanta Swain,Allarakha Shikder,Naseem Babu,Niladri Sarkar,Aryan Raj,Raghavan K. Easwaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 直接从单帧散斑图像回归标量涡旋光束的分数拓扑电荷（TC），并确定湍流强度上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数TC可扩大OAM链路可用模式集，但湍流后难以可靠读取：干涉/衍射法需无畸变光束和精密对准，多数学习方法只处理整数或稀疏间隔电荷且不含湍流。

Method: 涡旋光束经1500 m、150层von Kármán相位屏传输，经固定随机散射体产生散斑，记录256×256、16 bit图像；TC 0–10步长0.01，D/r0 0–50。用CNN主头回归TC，辅助头分类湍流等级；本次使用2万张子集（百万级数据集在准备中）。

Result: 对训练外电荷：无湍流时MAE为0.031（统一强度标度），D/r0=10时升至0.78，D/r0=50时为1.85，恒定预测为2.5；湍流等级分类正确率78%。

Conclusion: 现有训练规模下仅无湍流时能准确读出电荷；电荷与湍流同时作用于散斑颗粒尺寸，可能构成读出瓶颈。实验验证与矢量涡旋光束扩展留待后续。

Abstract: Fractional topological charge (TC) enlarges the usable mode set of orbital angular momentum (OAM) links, but reading it reliably after atmospheric turbulence remains difficult. Interferometric and diffractive methods need an undistorted beam and a careful alignment, while most learned approaches treat integer or coarsely spaced charges and do not include turbulence. Here we ask whether the charge of a scalar vortex beam can be regressed directly from a single speckle image, and at which turbulence strength this stops working. We propagate vortex beams over 1500~m through 150 von Kármán phase screens, scatter them with a fixed random diffuser, and record $256\times256$, 16-bit speckle images for TC from 0 to 10 in steps of 0.01 and turbulence strengths $D/r_0$ from 0 to 50; the results reported here use a subset of 20\,000 images, and a data set of $10^{6}$ images is in preparation. A convolutional neural network estimates the TC and, through an auxiliary head, the turbulence category. On charges withheld from training, the mean absolute error is 0.031 without turbulence (with a common intensity scale) and rises to 0.78 at $D/r_0=10$ and 1.85 at $D/r_0=50$, against 2.5 for a constant prediction, and the turbulence category is identified correctly in 78\% of the images. The results show that, at the present training size, the charge is read accurately only without turbulence, and that the charge and the turbulence oth act on the speckle grain size, which may limit the readout. Experimental validation and the extension to vector vortex beams are left for future work.

</details>


### [113] [Tune for Retention, Not Just Gain: SFT Forgets Far Less than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34579)
*Nirmit Joshi,Nathan Srebro,Eran Malach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑"SFT 的离策略特性是灾难性遗忘主因"这一前提，指出联合调节学习率与批量等超参后，朴素 SFT 可在取得相近新任务增益的同时几乎不遗忘，并可与在策略的 SDFT 竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作把 SFT 的灾难性遗忘归因于其离策略（off-policy）性质，据此主张在策略学习是持续学习的关键。作者希望检验这一归因是否成立。

Method: 以学习率和批量大小等超参为变量，在"新任务增益"与"旧能力保持"两个目标下联合选择超参；将朴素 SFT 与在策略的 Self-Distillation Fine-Tuning（SDFT）在相同准则下对比。

Result: 追求新任务性能最大化确实会引发灾难性遗忘，但采用更小学习率与批量可在获得相近增益的同时几乎不遗忘；经联合选参的 SFT 保持能力显著优于既有结论，并可媲美 SDFT；SDFT 对超参更稳健，但同样需要保持性准则来筛除导致遗忘的配置。

Conclusion: 在策略训练并非实现"学习+保持"的必要条件；灾难性遗忘更多由优化配置（超参选择）驱动，需重新审视微调中遗忘的成因。

Abstract: Catastrophic forgetting, the drastic loss of previously acquired capabilities when learning new ones, is a central challenge in language model fine-tuning. Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is widely believed to be prone to such forgetting. Recent works have attributed this to the off-policy nature of SFT, arguing that on-policy learning is key to continual learning. In this paper, we challenge this premise. We show that while maximizing new-task performance can induce catastrophic forgetting, alternative hyperparameter choices (lower learning rate and batch size) can yield similar gains with little to no forgetting. With hyperparameters selected jointly for gain and retention, vanilla SFT retains prior capabilities far better than previously suggested and can be competitive with Self-Distillation Fine-Tuning (SDFT), an on-policy approach designed to mitigate forgetting. While SDFT can be more robust to hyperparameter choices, it also requires a retention-aware criterion to identify hyperparameter configurations that avoid catastrophic forgetting. Our results challenge the view that on-policy training is what enables learning with retention and raise broader questions about what drives catastrophic forgetting during fine-tuning.

</details>


### [114] [ChemoMAE: Self-Supervised Spectral Representation Learning Reveals Chemical Heterogeneity in Historical Wood](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34581)
*Ryuji Yamaguchi,Miyuki Matsuo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出自监督Transformer模型ChemoMAE，结合近红外高光谱成像，学习像素级光谱表征并对49份日本历史建筑木材标本进行无监督聚类，揭示木材内部与表层降解的空间模式，并据此提出降解机制假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史木构建筑中，木材化学组成随解剖区域而异，多环境因素长期作用形成复杂降解模式；阐明降解机制对有效保护与修复至关重要，但现有认识尚不清晰。

Method: 对49份不同树种与使用历史的木材横切面进行近红外高光谱成像；采用自监督Transformer（ChemoMAE）通过掩码去噪利用光谱变量间的多重共线性学习像素级表征；对表征聚类生成无需预设化学状态类别的空间图谱；提出局部标签一致性（LLA）指标量化相邻像素标签一致性，用于验证；随后在全部标本上重新训练并拟合聚类中心，结合解剖学、使用历史与既有研究交叉解读。

Result: 仅使用光谱信息时，ChemoMAE在验证标本上的平均LLA高于基线方法；聚类图谱呈现与木材解剖结构及表面暴露预期效应一致的空间结构。

Conclusion: 该探索性AI for Science框架将自监督学习得到的潜在模式与领域知识相联结，提出了内部与表面降解的机制性假设，为研究材料状态形成机制提供了基础工具。

Abstract: In historical timber buildings, multiple environmental factors act over centuries on wood whose chemical composition varies across anatomical regions, producing complex patterns of degradation. Understanding how timber members degrade is important for the effective conservation and restoration of historical buildings, yet the mechanisms underlying these patterns remain unclear. To investigate these mechanisms, we used self-supervised learning to establish a common basis for comparing specimens from timber members that differ in wood species and service history. We examined transverse sections of 49 wood specimens from several historical buildings in Japan using near-infrared hyperspectral imaging. ChemoMAE, a self-supervised Transformer, learns pixelwise spectral representations through masked denoising that exploits multicollinearity among spectral variables. Clustering these representations yields spatial maps without predefined chemical-state categories. Because anatomy and degradation can produce spatially continuous regions, we formulated Local Label Agreement (LLA) to quantify agreement among labels assigned to neighboring pixels. Using spectra alone, ChemoMAE yielded higher mean LLA than baseline methods on validation specimens. We then trained ChemoMAE anew and fitted cluster centers across all specimens. Spatial structures consistent with wood anatomy and the expected effects of surface exposure emerged in the resulting maps. By cross-referencing regional spectra and spatial distributions across specimens in light of wood anatomy, service histories, and prior studies, we proposed models of internal and surface degradation as mechanistic hypotheses. This exploratory AI for Science framework gives researchers a basis for linking latent patterns learned through self-supervision with domain knowledge when investigating how material states form.

</details>


### [115] [Learning from Crowds with Unsupervised Item Response Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34602)
*Pascal Wullschleger,Majid Zarharan,Donnacha Daly,Marc Pouly,Jennifer Foster*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将众包标注建模为无监督IRT问题，在六个数据集上系统评估无监督IRT模型对标注者/题目潜在特质的恢复与标签推断，并进一步提出用IRT指导分类器训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 众包标注中标注者能力与任务难度差异显著，需要能同时刻画标注者与任务特性的概率模型；作者希望检验IRT在无真值监督下能否可靠恢复潜在特质，并探索其作为分类器训练信号的潜力。

Method: 把众包标注视作无监督IRT：标注者为被试、任务为题目。在六个带真实标签的众包数据集上，将标准logistic IRT模型的无监督变体与基于真值训练的监督变体比较，评估标注者能力、题目难度与区分度的恢复以及标签推断；并将IRT用于指导分类器训练（IRT-informed curriculum），与其他众包学习方法对比F1和校准。

Result: 在多数数据集上，无监督IRT可较可靠地恢复标注者与题目特质，接近监督变体水平；标签推断较多数基线更稳定，但未必是最优选择。众包学习方法可提升F1超越多数投票基线，但没有方法能同时显著改善F1与校准；IRT课程方法校准保持更好但在F1上不领先，F1最优方法往往校准较差。

Conclusion: 无监督IRT是恢复众包潜在特质的可靠工具，标签推断稳定但非普适最优；基于IRT的训练指导在F1与校准之间存在权衡，需按任务需求选择方法。

Abstract: Item Response Theory (IRT) is the study of tests, including the properties of both test items and test takers. Crowdsourced labelling can be cast as an unsupervised IRT problem: annotators play the role of test takers with varying ability, and annotation tasks play the role of items with varying difficulty. We evaluate unsupervised variants of standard logistic IRT models for latent trait recovery and label inference on six datasets with crowdsourced labels and known ground truths. Unsupervised IRT models can recover annotator and item traits (e.g., ability, difficulty and discrimination) reliably across most datasets when compared to supervised variants trained on the ground truth. IRT models seem to be more stable in label inference compared to most baselines, but might not be the best possible choice depending on the dataset. We further propose to use IRT models to inform classifiers during training and compare against existing crowd-learning methods. We find that crowd-learning methods can improve F1 over majority-vote baselines, but no method significantly improves both F1 and calibration. IRT-informed curriculum methods seem to preserve calibration better than other crowd-learning methods, but are not at the frontier regarding F1, and the best crowd-learning methods in terms of F1 seem to be miscalibrated.

</details>


### [116] [Automated Batch-Variable Selection for ComBat-Based Radiomic Harmonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34618)
*Jose Lozano-Montoya,Gemma Urbanos,Carmen Prieto-de-la-Lastra,Emilio Soria-Olivas,Ana Jimenez-Pastor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ParsiComBat：以批次效应排序、冗余过滤和序贯校正评分为核心，数据驱动地为ComBat选择采集参数，并在技术变异可忽略时弃权，从而在去除批次效应与保留特征结构和临床判别力之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ComBat可协调多中心影像组学特征，但批次变量通常由人工选取，缺乏跨采集参数的标准化准则，可能导致过度校正或校正不足。

Method: ParsiComBat先按批次效应大小对候选采集参数排序，过滤冗余变量，再对序贯ComBat校正方案打分；在无显著技术变异时弃权。评估含三项实验：模拟数据（能否恢复已知批次变量并在无批次时弃权）、CT（NSCLC-Radiomics）与MRI（MAMA-MIA）队列（与按厂商的手动选择、贪婪校正全部可行参数、AutoCombat比较），以及临床预测的内部与外部验证。

Result: 模拟中，ParsiComBat在92.9%的阳性试验中识别出真实批次变量，在99.2%的零对照中弃权。CT中校正重建核使平均k近邻批次效应检验统计量由0.276降至0.032，接近贪婪校正6个变量的效果；MRI中校正层厚与厂商使其由0.680降至0.325，略优于手动选择但弱于贪婪校正7个变量。去除技术变异未改善临床判别力：包括原始特征在内，各策略AUC与ParsiComBat均无显著差异。

Conclusion: ParsiComBat以最少的采集参数实现数据驱动的批次效应去除，保留特征结构与临床判别力；所选变量可与采集协议相互验证，且无需校正时自动弃权，有助于透明、可复现的多中心影像组学。

Abstract: ComBat harmonizes multicentre radiomic features, but batch variables are usually chosen manually, without a standardized criterion across image acquisition parameters. We propose ParsiComBat, which selects acquisition parameters for ComBat, balancing batch-effect removal against feature-structure preservation and abstaining when technical variation is negligible.
  ParsiComBat ranks candidate acquisition parameters by batch effect, filters redundancy, and scores sequential ComBat corrections. We evaluated it in three experiments: in simulations, testing whether it recovered a known batch variable and abstained without one; in CT (NSCLC-Radiomics) and MRI (MAMA-MIA) cohorts, comparing batch-effect removal against manufacturer (manual), every feasible parameter (greedy) and AutoCombat; and in clinical prediction, internally and externally.
  In simulations, ParsiComBat identified the true batch variable in 92.9% of positive trials and abstained in 99.2% of null controls. In CT, correcting for reconstruction kernel reduced mean k-nearest neighbour batch-effect test from 0.276 to 0.032, almost matching greedy correction of six variables. In MRI, correcting for slice thickness and manufacturer reduced it from 0.680 to 0.325, slightly more than manual selection but less than greedy correction of seven variables. Removing technical variation did not improve clinical discrimination: no strategy, including raw features, differed significantly from ParsiComBat in area under the curve.
  ParsiComBat provides a data-driven framework that removes technical batch effects using minimal acquisition parameters, preserving feature structure and clinical discrimination. It selects variables verifiable against the acquisition protocol and abstains when no correction is needed, supporting transparent, reproducible multicentre radiomics.

</details>


### [117] [DecisionReplay: A Corpus and Offline Simulator for Ensembling Decision Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34627)
*Dean Ariel,Seffi Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发布 DecisionReplay 数据集：包含 17 个商用决策模型在 35 个基准上的概率向量、token 数、计费成本与实测延迟，配套确定性模拟器，可在无需新模型调用的情况下快速复现集成、路由与级联策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 比较集成、路由与级联方法需要对每个模型在每个样本上的输出及其成本进行穷举调用，代价高昂；已有的用于路由的存储集合又不保留各选项的概率，无法支持这些方法的仿真。

Method: 构建 DecisionReplay 数据集：对 17 个"决策模型"（对每个答案选项返回概率的端点）在 35 个基准上收集概率向量，并记录每次调用的 token 数、账单成本与实测延迟；配套确定性模拟器，回放任意的模型选择、组合与级联策略。

Result: 单一精心挑选的模型难以被超越：全模型池化比它低 2.7 个准确率点，堆叠集成以 3.4 倍成本仅提升 1.0 个点；两模型同时出错时有 72% 的概率选择相同的错误选项；最优路由以 64% 的成本达到该模型的效果；认证式早退将堆叠集成成本降低 43% 且不改变答案。

Conclusion: DecisionReplay 使检验新方法是否改变上述结论变得廉价而快速，为集成、路由与级联策略的标准化评估提供了可复现的基础设施。

Abstract: Comparing ensembles, routers and cascades requires the output of every model on every item, and its cost. Stored collections built for routing keep no per-option probabilities. We provide a dataset on which ensemble methods, routers and cascades are simulated quickly, without new model calls. DecisionReplay holds the probability vectors of 17 commercial decision models, endpoints that return a probability for every answer option, on 35 benchmarks, with the token counts, billed cost and measured latency of every call, all released. A deterministic simulator replays any policy that selects, combines or cascades them. One well-chosen model per benchmark is hard to beat: pooling all models loses 2.7 accuracy points to it, and stacking gains 1.0 points at 3.4 times its cost. The models are wrong together: two that are both wrong choose the same wrong option in 72% of cases. The best router matches that model at 64% of its cost, and certified early exit cuts the cost of a stacked ensemble by 43% without changing an answer. DecisionReplay makes it cheap and fast to test whether a new method changes these results.

</details>


### [118] [Predicting acute food crises and discovering actionable drivers with causal machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34629)
*Mélissande Machefer,Michele Ronco,Michele Meroni,Jordi Cerdà-Bautista,Francesco Collivignarelli,Chungmann Kim,Nicolas Rost,Alessia Matanó,Esther Rodrigo-Bonet,Philipp Zimmer,Gustau Camps-Valls,Christina Corbane,Felix Rembold*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究在公开的Harmonized Food Insecurity Dataset上补充驱动变量，构建抗缺失数据的机器学习流水线，以3/6/9个月提前期预测急性粮食安全危机发生；模型显著优于无技能基线，并揭示预测重要性与因果效应之间的错位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 急性粮食不安全由冲突、极端气候事件、人口流离失所与经济冲击复合驱动，亟需可提前预警的预测工具；同时现有研究缺少大规模采用IPC/CH分类体系的预测基准，也不清楚驱动因素的预测重要性与因果作用是否一致。

Method: 为公开的Harmonized Food Insecurity Dataset补充月度、次国家层面、覆盖80国、2007—2024年的驱动变量；构建对缺失数据稳健的机器学习流水线，在3、6、9个月提前期上同时基于FEWS NET与IPC/CH分类预测危机是否发生；采用嵌套交叉验证评估，以PR-AUC对比无技能基线；用TreeSHAP进行可解释性分析，并将预测重要性与可操作驱动因素的双稳健因果估计进行比较。

Result: 流水线在PR-AUC上比无技能基线高15—50个百分点；模型表现与危机流行率相关，而与所用分类体系无关；TreeSHAP揭示出地理特异性的复合预测信号谱，并归纳出三类反复出现的预测原型；预测重要性与因果估计的对比显示，急性冲突事件预测信号最弱却是危机分类的主要因果驱动因素，而农业气候是唯一预测重要性与因果效应一致的维度。

Conclusion: 数据驱动的预测模型可有效提前数月预警急性粮食不安全危机，但预测重要性不能等同于因果效应；预警系统设计需结合因果证据，避免因忽视冲突等弱预测信号但强因果作用的因素而产生政策误导。

Abstract: Acute food insecurity is driven by a compound of conflict, adverse climate events, displacement, and economic shocks. We enrich the open-access Harmonized Food Insecurity Dataset with driver variables (monthly, subnational, 80 countries, 2007--2024) and develop missing-data-resilient machine learning pipelines forecasting crisis occurrence at 3, 6, and 9-month lead times using both FEWS NET and, for the first time at scale, IPC/CH classifications. Pipelines, evaluated through nested cross-validation, outperform no-skill baselines by 15 to 50 percentage points in PR-AUC, with performance correlated with crisis prevalence rather than the classification system. TreeSHAP-based explainability reveals compound, geography-specific predictive signal profiles, with three recurrent forecasting archetypes. Comparing predictive importance against doubly robust causal estimates of actionable drivers, we find that acute conflict events, having the weakest predictive signals, emerge as primary causal drivers of crisis classification whilst agroclimatology is the sole dimension in which predictive importance and causal effect align.

</details>


### [119] [Distractor-Robust Reinforcement Learning via Variational Bisimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34641)
*Benjamin Freed,Vivaan Singh,Dean Zadok,Roberto Calandra,Jeff Schneider,Howie Choset*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出VIBES目标，用对抗项替代像素重建，强制潜状态对奖励与下一潜状态充分，从而在可达精确充分时最优解诱导历史双模拟关系，配合瓶颈实现任务相关信息保留与干扰剔除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型强化学习虽具备数据效率与泛化性，但基于重建的目标会迫使模型把表征容量浪费在与任务无关的干扰因素上。

Method: 将像素重建替换为对抗项，约束潜状态足以预测奖励与下一潜状态；理论上证明该目标度量编码器距离奖励/动力学充分性的程度，并说明精确充分可达时全局最优诱导历史上的双模拟关系；叠加固定容量潜瓶颈以滤除无关变化。作为Dreamer模型学习组件的即插即用替代。

Result: 在Distracting Control Suite上达到SOTA；在Realistic ManiSkill的视觉复杂操作任务上有效；不依赖图像专用增强，亦可推广至高维向量状态任务（100节游泳机器人）；UMAP嵌入与最近邻探针显示表征对任务结构敏感、对干扰不变。

Conclusion: VIBES以充分性驱动的对抗目标取代重建，兼顾任务相关表征学习与干扰不变性，且跨视觉与向量状态任务通用，是一种通用且有效的MBRL模型学习目标。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) promises data efficiency and generalization, but typical reconstruction-based objectives encourage models to waste representational capacity on task-irrelevant distractors. We introduce VIBES (Variational Inference for Bisimulation-based Encoded States), a new objective that replaces pixel reconstruction with an adversarial term, which enforces that latent states suffice to predict both rewards and the next latent state. We show that this objective measures how far the encoder is from reward and dynamics sufficiency, and that whenever exact sufficiency is attainable, its global optima induce bisimulation relations on histories. Combined with a fixed-capacity latent bottleneck, this encourages latent states to retain task-relevant information while discarding irrelevant variation. Our method serves as a drop-in replacement for Dreamer's model-learning component, achieves state-of-the-art performance on the Distracting Control Suite, and extends to visually complex manipulation in Realistic ManiSkill. Unlike prior approaches, it does not rely on image-specific augmentations and applies equally well to high-dimensional vector-state tasks, demonstrated on a 100-link swimmer robot. Finally, latent-space analyses (UMAP embeddings and nearest-neighbor probes) confirm that the learned representations are sensitive to task-relevant structure while invariant to distractors.

</details>


### [120] [Do We Still Need Reinforcement Learning When We Can Read the World Model?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34656)
*Mingchao Zhang,Miduo Cui,Kangze He,Yuanjun Li,Dapeng Li,Ce Zhang,Chuanchen Luo,Xue Yan,Mingzhe Xing,Zhumin Chen,Hangyu Mao,Bin Zhang,Zhiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"可读世界模型+LLM编程智能体直接合成控制策略"的新范式，无需任务特定RL训练，性能可比或优于传统RL且经验需求大幅降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RL依赖大量交互经验；基于模型的RL虽借助世界模型缓解此问题，但世界模型通常仅用于规划或策略优化，仍需参数化策略学习。作者希望探索一条更直接的路径：让LLM编程智能体通过阅读世界模型（环境源代码）直接推理并合成可执行策略。

Method: 以自主LLM编程智能体为核心，检查环境源码即"可读世界模型"，将环境知识转化为可执行决策规则（代码策略）；随后利用在线反馈或离线数据进行迭代精炼，全程无任务特定RL训练。并通过无源码访问的对照实验及困难条件实验进行消融与分析。

Result: 在多个决策基准上，合成策略达到或超过常规RL方法的性能，同时所需经验量显著更少；策略表现为可解释的决策规则，并可观察到其在反馈驱动下的演化过程；无源码访问的对照与困难条件下的实验进一步验证了可读世界模型的收益。

Conclusion: 直接策略合成可降低对试错的依赖，对"任务特定RL训练是否必要"这一前提构成挑战，为LLM智能体与世界模型结合的决策范式提供了新方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has achieved remarkable success in sequential decision-making, but learning effective policies often requires substantial interaction experience. Model-based reinforcement learning alleviates this dependence by leveraging world models, which are typically used to support planning or policy optimization. In this paper, we investigate an alternative paradigm in which autonomous LLM-based coding agents directly synthesize control policies by reasoning over readable world models. By examining environment source code, these agents translate environmental knowledge into executable decision rules and iteratively refine their policies using online feedback or offline data, without task-specific RL training. Experiments across diverse decision-making benchmarks show that synthesized policies match or outperform conventional RL methods while requiring substantially less experience. Further analyses reveal interpretable decision rules and how they evolve through feedback-driven refinement. Comparisons with policies generated without source code access and experiments under challenging conditions further demonstrate the benefits of readable world models. These findings suggest that direct policy synthesis can reduce reliance on trial and error, challenging the necessity of task-specific RL training.

</details>


### [121] [Learning to Be Truncated: Preparing LLM Checkpoints for SVD Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34667)
*Chenyue Fang,Danila Mishin,Babak Falsafi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLift 通过单一压缩率下的低秩近似微调，使预训练模型对 SVD 压缩"可跨比例复用"，无需对每个压缩模型单独微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 SVD 的压缩通常会带来性能下降，需要事后微调或权重修正；且针对每个压缩预算都要重复做预算特定的优化，成本高。

Method: 以原始模型为教师，在单一压缩率下微调低秩近似；将学到的更新合并回原始权重，同时保留未参与训练前向的互补分量；随后对准备好的模型目标矩阵做 SVD，并在不同截断秩下复用同一分解。

Result: 在 LLaMA-7B 上，单一准备模型在三种压缩率下均取得精度提升；提升可泛化到不同模型家族、模型规模与 SVD 压缩算子；通过梯度与早期训练更新分析提供局部证据。

Conclusion: LLift 表明在某一压缩率下学到的更新可惠及其他压缩率，从而在不同部署预算下提升压缩效果而无需逐模型微调。

Abstract: Compression based on singular value decomposition (SVD) often requires subsequent fine-tuning or weight correction to mitigate performance degradation. Budget-specific optimization must be repeated for different compression ratios. We introduce LLift, which prepares a pretrained model for SVD compression across ratios without further fine-tuning. LLift fine-tunes a low-rank approximation at a single ratio using the original model as a teacher. We merge the learned updates into the original weights while preserving the complementary components excluded from the training forward pass. We apply SVD to the prepared model's target matrices and reuse the decompositions at different truncation ranks. On LLaMA-7B, we demonstrate accuracy gains across three compression ratios using a single prepared model. The gains extend across model families, model sizes, and SVD compression operators. We analyze gradients and an early training update to provide local evidence that updates learned at one ratio can benefit compression at other ratios. These results show that LLift can improve compression across deployment budgets without fine-tuning each compressed model separately.

</details>


### [122] [Provably Efficient Concave Utility Reinforcement Learning with Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34678)
*Max Qiushi Lin,Ahmed Magd,Sharan Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在有限时域分幕式双线性 MDP 中研究凹效用强化学习（CURL），利用双线性结构通过低维特征期望高效估计占用度量，提出模型型乐观凹效用策略优化（OCUPO）框架。on-policy 版达到 Õ(ε^{-4}) 样本复杂度，新颖的 backward feature regression 子程序使 off-policy 版达到 Õ(ε^{-2})，并推广至线性 MDP，ε 依赖与标准线性函数逼近 RL 一致，且无需先前工作所需的积分 oracle。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CURL 统一了标准 RL、模仿学习与最大熵探索等问题，但一般需要估计高维状态-动作占用度量。现有方法或回避探索问题、或局限于表格设定、或缺乏计算高效的策略优化，因此需要一个既可高效估计占用度量又能进行高效策略优化的框架。

Method: 在转移模型具有已知特征空间低维表示的双线性 MDP 中，用双线性结构通过低维特征期望估计占用度量，提出基于模型的乐观凹效用策略优化框架 OCUPO。on-policy 实例每轮用独立轨迹估计凹效用策略梯度；off-policy 实例引入 backward feature regression 子程序，复用历史策略轨迹估计当前策略占用度量；并扩展到线性 MDP。

Result: on-policy OCUPO 样本复杂度为 Õ(ε^{-4})；在对策略类（如具有足够表达力特征的对数线性策略）的适当技术假设下，off-policy 版达到 Õ(ε^{-2})（至多相差可控投影误差）；扩展到线性 MDP 后 off-policy 算法保持相同样本复杂度。ε 的依赖与标准线性函数逼近 RL 相匹配。

Conclusion: 双线性结构使占用度量的低维估计与乐观优化可行，off-policy 特征回归显著提升样本效率，且算法不依赖先前工作所需的积分 oracle，为 CURL 提供了计算高效且样本高效的理论框架。

Abstract: Concave utility reinforcement learning (CURL) unifies a diverse range of problems, including standard reinforcement learning (RL), imitation learning, and maximum entropy exploration. Unlike standard RL, CURL generally requires estimating high-dimensional state-action occupancy measures. Consequently, existing methods for CURL either sidestep the exploration problem, are restricted to the tabular setting, or lack computationally efficient policy optimization. To address this, we study CURL in finite-horizon episodic bilinear MDPs, where the transition model admits a low-dimensional representation in known feature spaces. We use the bilinear structure to efficiently estimate occupancy measures via low-dimensional feature expectations, and propose a model-based optimistic concave utility policy optimization (OCUPO) framework. We first instantiate OCUPO in the on-policy setting, where each iteration uses independent trajectories to estimate the concave utility policy gradient, and prove an $\widetilde{O}(ε^{-4})$ sample complexity. To improve sample efficiency, we then instantiate OCUPO in the off-policy setting. We propose a novel backward feature regression subroutine that reuses trajectories collected by past policies to estimate the occupancy measure of the current policy. Under suitable technical assumptions on the policy class (satisfied, e.g., by log-linear policies with sufficiently expressive features), we prove that the resulting algorithm achieves an $\widetilde{O}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity, up to a controllable projection error. We further extend our framework to linear MDPs and show that the resulting off-policy algorithm retains the same sample complexity. In these settings, the dependence on $ε$ matches that for standard RL with linear function approximation. Moreover, our algorithms do not rely on the integral oracle required by prior work.

</details>


### [123] [Evacuation Traffic Prediction for Dynamic Transportation Networks over Multiple Hurricanes: A Generalized Graph Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34687)
*Md Nafees Fuad Rafi,Samiul Hasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于动态图卷积LSTM（GCN-LSTM）的飓风疏散交通流预测框架，利用12次历史飓风的检测器数据实现跨飓风泛化，可在未见飓风上提前6小时预测且精度较高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 飓风疏散期间高交通需求引发严重拥堵与安全风险，实时预测可支撑疏散管理；但疏散交通高度动态不确定，各飓风特征与响应行为不同、检测器网络结构也随飓风变化，导致单飓风训练的模型难以跨飓风泛化。

Method: 构建广义动态图学习框架，融合12次历史飓风的交通检测器与疏散数据；利用路网动态性建立GCN-LSTM模型进行最长6小时的疏散交通预测；并提出模块化训练策略，逐步引入多飓风数据以评估性能提升，同时进行静态与动态图结构的消融对比。

Result: 在未见飓风（Milton, 2024）上表现强泛化：1小时预测RMSE 280.6、R² 0.96；整体6小时预测RMSE 459.8、R² 0.89；整体6小时预测优于现有最优基线模型；消融显示动态GCN-LSTM较静态版本RMSE提升8.4%。

Conclusion: 动态图结构与多飓风模块化训练可有效提升疏散交通预测的跨事件泛化能力；该模型可帮助交通管理部门提前掌握疏散期未来路况并采取主动措施减少延误。

Abstract: High traffic demand during hurricane evacuations causes severe congestion on major highways, leading to delays and safety risks for evacuees. Predicting evacuation traffic in real-time can significantly enhance evacuation management efforts. However, evacuation traffic is highly dynamic and uncertain due to evolving hurricane characteristics and diverse population response. Each hurricane has unique characteristics and evacuation contexts, resulting in different spatial-temporal traffic patterns. Moreover, the structure of detector-based transportation networks varies across multiple hurricanes. Hence, a model trained on the data from one hurricane may not generalize well for others. This study proposes a generalized dynamic graph learning framework using traffic detectors and evacuation data from 12 historical hurricanes. Leveraging the dynamic nature of transportation network, we develop a Graph Convolutional Long Short-Term Memory (GCN-LSTM) model that predicts evacuation traffic up to 6 hours in advance. For an unseen hurricane (Milton, 2024), the model shows strong generalizability, achieving an RMSE of 280.6 and R2 of 0.96 for 1-hour prediction, and an RMSE of 459.8 and R2 of 0.89 for overall 6-hours prediction. We also introduce a modular training approach, where we evaluate performance improvements by sequentially incorporating data from multiple hurricanes. The proposed model outperforms state-of-the-art baseline models for overall 6-hour prediction horizons. Ablation experiments show an 8.4% improvement in RMSE for the proposed dynamic GCN-LSTM model compared to its static counterpart. Traffic management officials can use the developed model to understand future traffic conditions during hurricane evacuation in advance and take proactive measures to reduce delays during evacuation.

</details>


### [124] [Effect of class proportion shifts on classifier curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34702)
*Maxim Bolshim,Alexander Kugaevskikh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 通过在CIFAR-10上分离类别比例变化与模型参数变化，测量输出层损失Hessian迹的变化，并提出基于初始类别梯度的预测方法，前瞻性验证显示其误差显著低于零变化与等权梯度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改变类别混合比例会在不改变分类器的情况下改变平均损失，为区分这种"重加权"效应与模型本身的变化，需要设计能隔离类别比例影响的实验，并检验类别比例信息对损失Hessian（局部曲率）变化的可预测性。

Method: 采用十类CIFAR-10分类，针对两个目标类别各设独立分支；冻结特征、仅训练输出层60步更新，在固定目标类图像上计算关于输出层参数的损失Hessian迹。做了十组新初始化，比较目标类别占比由10%升至19%和降至1%时相对各分支初始迹的变化。并在首个实验结果基础上构建基于初始类别梯度的预测，随后不加修改地进行前瞻性检验，与零变化预测和等权梯度预测对比。

Result: 十组初始化下，占比升至19%的分支Hessian迹平均变化-0.152%，降至1%的分支平均变化+0.071%（相对各自初始迹）。预测方法在10%→19%直接增加情形下平均绝对误差为0.022个百分点（初始迹尺度），零变化预测为0.152，等权梯度预测为0.104。

Conclusion: 类别组成变化可在分类器不变时引起损失Hessian迹的系统性微幅变化；该变化无法完全排除所分配训练图像的影响，但使用实际类别比例的梯度预测能显著提高对迹变化的预测精度，说明类别比例信息对曲率变化具有可预测的解释力。

Abstract: Changing the class mix can alter mean loss without changing the classifier. To distinguish this reweighting from changes in the model itself, we studied ten-class CIFAR-10 classification in separate branches for two target classes, measuring the trace of the loss Hessian with respect to the output-layer parameters on fixed images of each target class. Across ten new initialisations, after 60 updates to the output layer with frozen features, the trace changed by -0.152% on average in branches where the target class share rose from 10% to 19%, and by +0.071% in branches where it fell to 1%, relative to each branch's initial trace. These changes do not isolate the effect of class share from that of the assigned training images. We developed a forecast based on initial class gradients after examining the first experiment's results and then tested it prospectively without modification. For direct increases from 10% to 19%, rather than increases after a deficit, its mean absolute error was 0.022 percentage points on the initial-trace scale, compared with 0.152 for a zero-change forecast and 0.104 for equally weighted gradients. Using the assigned class proportions improved the forecast of how much the trace would change.

</details>


### [125] [Maco-RL: Scaling Agent Capabilities with Mixed-Task Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34703)
*Jiacheng Zhang,Qixing Zhou,Haiyang Wang,Qipeng Gu,Shuzhe Wu,Feiyang Pan,Lue Fan,Dandan Tu,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Maco-RL 通过配额式异步 rollout 调度与 Tri-Buffer Sampling 两个组件，解决混合任务强化学习在训练稳定性、算力分配与持续领域扩展上的扩展性难题，在五个基准上显著提升统一策略的整体表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MixRL 可直接在多个领域上训练统一策略，无需单独专家模型或额外蒸馏阶段，便于持续扩展智能体能力；但其规模化面临三大挑战：稳定高效的混合任务训练、rollout 算力的有效分配、以及持续领域扩展的支持。本文旨在使 MixRL 真正可规模化。

Method: 提出 Maco-RL：(1) 采用基于配额的调度器，在多个任务领域间进行异步 rollout，通过平衡各领域的梯度贡献、缓解 off-policy 效应来稳定训练；(2) 正交地引入 Tri-Buffer Sampling (TBS)，依据样本掌握程度（mastery）分桶分配算力，在保持已获得能力的同时推动前沿探索。

Result: 在五个基准上，Maco-RL 将 Qwen3.5-27B 的总分从 43.6 提升至 54.2，经持续扩展后达到 60.1，追平 Qwen3.8-27B 的前沿水平并进一步将其推高 5.7 分；同时以更少的策略更新算力优于单任务 RL。

Conclusion: 结果表明 MixRL 是一种实用且算力高效的 post-training 范式，Maco-RL 为其规模化提供了可行方案，在增强通用智能体能力方面具有显著潜力。

Abstract: Mixed-task reinforcement learning (MixRL) directly trains a unified policy across domains to continually expand agent capabilities without training separate specialists or requiring an extra distillation stage. Despite its appeal, scaling MixRL poses significant challenges in achieving stable and efficient mixed-task learning, effectively allocating rollout compute, and supporting continual domain expansion. To address these challenges, we propose Maco-RL, an approach that effectively scales MixRL. Specifically, Maco-RL employs a quota-based scheduler for asynchronous rollouts across multiple task domains, thereby stabilizing training by balancing gradient contributions and mitigating off-policy effects. Along an orthogonal axis, Maco-RL further introduces Tri-Buffer Sampling (TBS) to allocate compute among samples with varying levels of mastery, promoting sustained frontier exploration while preserving acquired capabilities. Experiments on five benchmarks show Maco-RL raises Qwen3.5-27B's overall score from 43.6 to 54.2 and then to 60.1 with continual expansion, reaching the frontier held by Qwen3.8-27B, which is further pushed outward by +5.7. Maco-RL also outperforms single-task RL with less compute for policy updates. These results support MixRL as a practical and compute-efficient post-training approach with substantial potential to strengthen general agent capabilities.

</details>


### [126] [When the Proxy Is Wrong: Mismatch-Corrected Bandits for Adaptive Data Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34707)
*Amine Saadi,Elissa Mhanna,Raphaël Féraud,Gwénolé Lecorvé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出“失配校正赌博机”框架，将代理模型与目标模型的排序失配建模为多保真度赌博机，用配对残差校正代理奖励，并在训练中分摊审计预算，理论与实验证明可显著提升最差组准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据分配常用小代理模型为目标大模型选训练数据，但代理与目标的排序可能不一致，导致次优的训练效果。已有方法未显式建模并校正如这种失配。

Method: 将数据分配问题建模为多保真度赌博机，每个臂是一个数据干预；代理奖励每轮可观测，目标奖励仅在少数“审计”中与同一更新配对揭示。配对观测可识别逐臂失配，且不假设共享偏移或排序保持。基于配对残差校正代理奖励，仅审计不确定决策，并将审计预算在全训练过程中分摊（滑窗策略）。理论上给出遗憾分解及平稳情形下的下界。

Result: 理论：证明仅用代理分配会产生线性遗憾；至少需要一定数量的配对审计；突变情形下过早用完审计预算也会导致线性遗憾；滑窗策略在Γ_T个变化点下达到√(Γ_T T log T)量级遗憾。实验：在CivilComments-WILDS上以BERT-Small指导BERT-Large训练的闭环概念验证中，失配校正优于同等目标模型预算的仅目标策略的最差组准确率；同等审计次数下，将审计分散至训练全程可提升最差组准确率6.3–10.5个百分点。

Conclusion: 显式建模并校正代理-目标失配、合理分摊审计预算，是提升代理驱动数据分配效果的关键，且在多保真度赌博机框架下有理论保证与实证收益。

Abstract: Data allocation methods often use a small proxy model to select training data for a larger target model. However, this proxy can rank data differently from the target, leading to suboptimal training performance. To address this problem, we model the mismatch between the proxy and target models and incorporate it into the allocation decision. To do so, the problem is cast as a multi-fidelity bandit, where each arm is a data intervention. The proxy reward is observed every round, whereas the target reward is revealed only on rare audits of the same update. These paired observations identify arm-wise mismatches without assuming a shared offset or preservation of rankings. Mismatch-corrected bandits correct the proxy with paired residuals, audit only uncertain decisions, and pace the audit budget over training. From a theoretical viewpoint, we prove a regret decomposition and lower bounds under stationarity. These bounds show that proxy-only allocation can incur linear regret and that a minimum number of paired audits is necessary. Under abrupt changes, using the audit budget early can also incur linear regret. A sliding-window policy that spreads audits over training achieves regret of order $\sqrt{Γ_T T\log T}$ where $Γ_T$ denotes the number of change points. Experimentally, we test the approach in a closed-loop proof of concept on CivilComments-WILDS, where BERT-Small guides the training of BERT-Large. Mismatch correction improves worst-group accuracy over a target-only policy with the same target-model budget. Spreading audits over training improves worst-group accuracy by 6.3 to 10.5 percentage points at the same audit count.

</details>


### [127] [Spec2Weights: Certifying Networks that a Language Model Writes from a Specification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34711)
*Soumik Bhattacharjee,Musa Tur Farazi,Md Nasir Uddin,Tishna Sabrina,Chowdhury Mohammad Rakin Haider*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一套形式化验证流程，用于证明"语言模型直接生成的小型网络权重"在其实际运行的 float32 算术下是否精确（或误差有界），并对 30 个新规格进行实证评估，同时给出学习型方法的两条理论极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型可直接根据自然语言规格写出小型 MLP/CNN/RNN/GNN 的权重，无需数据与梯度步骤。但这类"凭语言写出权重"的网络是否真的正确、在 float32 实际执行下是否精确，缺乏可证明的保证；仅靠测试无覆盖性，仅靠理想实数分析又会掩盖浮点误差。

Method: 核心是 float-safety 引理：使 float32 穷举成为对任意求和顺序都成立的证明；分层误差界处理权重非短二进制分数的网络。组合使用动态规划、模板检查、SMT 证明、广度优先搜索，以及针对权重共享递归网络的归纳法，对声明域内全部输入（最多 10^16 个，或规模受限的所有图/网格）进行认证。

Result: 15 个手写网络中 12 个被认证为全域精确（其中一个仅需一次常数修正），每个耗时 ≤1 分钟 CPU；证书还能刻画失效边界，如最短路网络当且仅当距离 ≤4096 时精确，并在预测跳数处失效。由 Claude Opus 5.5 新写 30 个规格：25 个首次提交即被认证对任意求和顺序精确；5 个税率表在固定 float32 运行下与真实税额误差 ≤ $0.11。开源 27B 模型通过 23/30，加强思考并执行代码后达 25/30。另证明两条极限：训练中从未激活的输入坐标无法通过更多域内数据学到；Luhn 类校验和的目标相关梯度方差随族规模指数衰减。

Conclusion: "语言模型写权重 + 形式化浮点认证"可形成一条可验证的替代路径，且方法可部分迁移到开源模型；同时示例学习本身存在可证明的结构性极限（覆盖 5 类失败中的 2 类），梯度下降实验与理论一致。

Abstract: A language model can write the weights of a small MLP, CNN, RNN or GNN from a plain-language specification, with no data and no gradient step. We ask when such a network can be proved exact in the float32 arithmetic it actually runs in. A float-safety lemma makes exhaustive float32 enumeration a proof for any summation order, and a layered error bound covers networks whose weights are not short binary fractions. With dynamic programming, template checks, an SMT proof, breadth-first search and induction for weight-tied recurrences, this certifies 12 of 15 written networks (one after a single constant fix) on every input of a stated domain (up to $10^{16}$ inputs, or every graph or grid up to a size bound), in a minute of CPU or less each. The certificates also say where a network stops being exact: the shortest-path network is exact iff the distance is at most 4,096 and fails at the predicted hops. Thirty new specifications were each written by a fresh session of one model family (Claude Opus 5.5). Twenty-five first submissions are certified exact for any summation order; the five tax schedules stay within \$0.11 of the true tax at every whole dollar in one fixed float32 run. An open 27B model passes 23 of the 30, and 25 with more thinking and code execution, so the method transfers in part. We also prove two limits of learning from examples: input coordinates never active in training cannot be learned from more in-range data, and Luhn-type checksums have exponentially small target-dependent gradient variance across their family. Tuned gradient descent on the same architectures agrees with both, which cover two of our five failure classes.

</details>


### [128] [Privileged On-Policy Distillation for Calibrated Alzheimer's Diagnosis With Missing Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34721)
*Vo Minh Khoi Nguyen,Dat Tien Chung,Quang Minh Khoa Le,Duc Khang Nguyen,Le Thien Phuc Nguyen,Hoang Thien Nguyen,Minh Huu Nhat Le,Phat K. Huynh,Cuong Tuan Nguyen,Thanh Huy Nguyen,Ulas Bagci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Graded Rollout Imputation with Privileged teachers（GRIP），用 on-policy 蒸馏让模型自己逐步补全缺失模态，并由仅在训练期可用的"特权"教师对补全步骤打分与提供软目标，从而在阿尔茨海默病诊断中实现一个统一、良好校准的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD 诊断依赖临床、生物样本与影像三类互补证据，但很少有患者做全所有检查。缺失模态有大量可能取值，现有方法要么直接忽略缺失项，要么只用单一估计值训练：分类器从未见过该估计的其他取值，且估计值本身从不接受诊断的检验。

Method: On-policy 蒸馏 + 一次一个模态的分级 rollout 补全：模型自身生成多次补全轨迹；已知诊断的 imputation teacher 对每一步补全打分，把结局信息纳入插补；见识全部真实模态的 diagnosis teacher 提供软目标。模型在多样化的补全上训练，学到反映缺失检查不确定性的置信度，并在单次 rollout 上完成诊断。

Result: 在 ADNI 队列上优于 SOTA 基线；在两个外部队列上判别力具竞争力，且在所有方法均重新校准后，校准误差与两者最低者持平；将教师加到三个基线上，每个基线均获提升。

Conclusion: GRIP 为患者实际完成的任意检查组合提供单一且良好校准的诊断模型，同时把插补质量与最终诊断目标显式耦合。

Abstract: Alzheimer's disease is diagnosed from clinical, biospecimen and imaging evidence, but few patients undergo every test. Because these modalities are complementary, a missing one has many plausible values. Most existing methods leave it out or train on one estimate, so their classifier never sees its other values, and the estimate is never checked against the diagnosis. We address both gaps with on-policy distillation: the model fills in the missing modalities itself, and teachers with information available only in training grade these fills. Our method, Graded Rollout Imputation with Privileged teachers, draws several fills, one modality at a time. An imputation teacher that knows the diagnosis grades each step, bringing the outcome into the imputation as statistical practice recommends. A diagnosis teacher that sees all real modalities supplies a soft target. Trained on fills that vary, the model learns a confidence that reflects what the missing tests leave open, and it diagnoses from a single rollout. On the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative cohort, it outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. On two external cohorts, it remains competitive in discrimination and, even after every method is recalibrated, its calibration error is level with the lowest on both. Added to three of these baselines, the teachers improve each of them. Our method offers a single, well-calibrated model for whatever combination of tests a patient has received.

</details>


### [129] [Reading Fine-Tuning Diffs Through Lenses: Model Diffing for Backdoor Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34758)
*Maximilian Petrowitsch,Ben Connors,Chris Kapulkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于token readout的统一评分体系，用于评估微调过程中的后门检测方法（权重/激活/输出型），并引入J-lens与J++-lens改进权重与激活方法；实验表明ΔW J++最强（12个设置中11个将后门相关token排进前10），但无单一方法能同时抵御两类规避攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门检测方法分属权重、激活、输出三类，缺乏可比性强的统一评估框架；同时需要检验各方法在对抗性规避（如对后门词施加干净输入概率惩罚、置换隐藏单元、仅训练读取残差流的层）下的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建基于token readout的通用评分系统，对weight/activation/output-based model diffing方法统一比较；将J-lens及其改进版J++-lens引入权重与激活方法；实验设计为"后门模型 vs. 其微调来源的基础模型"的diffing对比，并施加两类规避攻击测试鲁棒性。

Result: J++-lens在所有设置下均提升权重法，J-lens提升其在单层内的排名；最强单方法ΔW J++（经基础模型J++-lens解码的权重差）在12个设置中有11个将命名后门的token排入前10。ADL、Diff Mining等输出/激活方法在"干净输入上惩罚后门词概率"时易失效，权重法不受影响；反之，隐藏单元置换或仅训练读取残差流的层可对权重法隐藏后门，而ADL与Diff Mining不受影响。

Conclusion: J++-lens显著增强权重型diffing的检测能力，但不同方法族存在互补的脆弱面：没有任何单一方法能同时抵抗两类规避攻击，提示应组合多类方法进行后门检测。

Abstract: We introduce a common scoring system based on token readouts to evaluate weight, activation and output-based model diffing methods for backdoor detection in fine-tuning processes. We also improve existing weight and activation based methods by incorporating the $J$-lens and its refinement, the $J{+}{+}$-lens. We test our setup in experiments where we compare a backdoored model with the base model from which it was fine-tuned. Our results show that incorporating the $J{+}{+}$-lens improves the weight-based method in all our settings, while the $J$-lens improves its ranks within single layers. The strongest single method is $ΔW J{+}{+}$, the weight difference decoded through the $J{+}{+}$-lens of the base model, which ranks a token naming the backdoor among its first ten tokens in 11 of our 12 settings. Moreover, we show that output and activation based methods such as ADL and Diff Mining can easily break if the backdoored model is penalised during fine-tuning for giving the backdoor's words probability on clean inputs, whereas the weight-based methods are not affected. Conversely, permuting the hidden units or training only the layers that read the residual stream hides the backdoor from the weight-based methods but not from ADL and Diff Mining. No single method resists both attacks.

</details>


### [130] [Robust Recourse under Causal Graph Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34723)
*Muyan Anna Li,Juliette Sinnott,Mohammad Kohandel,Amir-Hossein Karimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对因果图存在多图歧义的情形，提出最坏情况推荐（Worst-Case Recourse）与图加权推荐（Graph-Weighted Recourse）两种方法，在候选图集合上寻找低成本且高成功率的干预动作，并揭示覆盖度与有效性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有因果算法推荐通常假设单一因果图、仅考虑因果机制的不确定性；但同一数据往往可由多个图拟合，且这些图对同一动作会预测出不同效果。当候选图集合增大时，要求单一动作在所有图上均成功的可能性下降、甚至不可行。

Method: 对每个候选图拟合高斯过程结构方程模型，采用后验均值—标准差判据评估动作成功性；通过基于梯度的搜索求取最小代价动作，并在新采样的后验样本上检验动作有效性。分别构建要求在所有候选图上成功的最坏情况推荐，以及要求在指定图权重比例上成功的图加权推荐。

Result: 在合成与半合成基准上，放宽鲁棒性（即不再要求动作在每个候选图上都成功）可提高有效推荐数量并降低动作代价。在20节点高斯过程基准中，放宽后获得推荐的实例数（覆盖度）由341/1000升至436/1000，而真实成功数（在生成模型下翻转决策）却由251降至184，体现覆盖度—有效性权衡。

Conclusion: 显式考虑因果图歧义，有助于决策者在系统因果机制未完全已知时推荐仍具鲁棒有效性的行动；鲁棒性程度应在覆盖度与有效性之间权衡取舍。

Abstract: Causal algorithmic recourse recommends actions to reverse unfavorable decisions by modeling how interventions change an individual's features. Existing methods typically assume one causal graph that describes the system and account for uncertainty in the causal mechanisms. However, there usually exist several graphs that fit the same data but can predict different effects for the same action. Certain actions may achieve recourse across multiple graphs, but as the set of potential graphs grows, a single best action may become infeasible.
  We propose Worst-Case Recourse and Graph-Weighted Recourse to find a low-cost action that succeeds across all candidate graphs or a specified fraction of graph weight, respectively. Assuming a set of plausible graphs, for each graph we fit Gaussian process structural equations and assess success using the posterior mean--standard-deviation criterion. We use gradient-based search to determine minimal cost actions and check the validity of the actions on fresh posterior samples. In synthetic and semi-synthetic benchmarks, relaxing robustness, the requirement that an action succeed under every candidate graph, can increase the number of valid recommendations for recourse and lower costs of actions for both methods. In the 20-node GP benchmark, relaxation increases the number of factual instances offered recourse (coverage) from 341 to 436 out of 1,000, while the number of true successes, actions that flip the decision under the generating model, falls from 251 to 184, illustrating the coverage--validity trade-off. Accounting for graph ambiguity can help decision-makers recommend actions that remain effective even when the causal mechanisms of the system are not fully known.

</details>


### [131] [One Model, Many Modes: Evolving System Prompt Ensembles for Creative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34760)
*Daiwei Chen,Anda He,Binwei Yao,Junjie Hu,Ramya Korlakai Vinayak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出无训练的System Prompt Ensemble Optimization (SEPO)，将LLM创造性生成建模为多目标分布优化问题，通过提示演化+贪心max-min选择构建行为各异的系统提示集合，推理时均匀采样，在质量约束下同时提升质量、多样性与新颖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏好对齐的LLM存在模式坍缩，输出集中于少数高奖励响应，损害创造性生成所需的质量、新颖性与多样性。现有测试时方法偏重多样性，忽视单条响应是否兼具高质量与新颖性；而实际用户往往只需一个出色且原创的响应。

Method: 将创造性生成形式化为多目标分布优化问题；SEPO为免训练框架：(1) 提示演化结合贪心max-min选择，在质量约束下识别行为上彼此不同的系统提示；(2) 推理时对每个响应均匀采样一个提示，从而扩大提示条件下的响应分布分离度。

Result: 在4个质量要求递增的创造性任务与3个开源权重模型（共12种设置）上，以质量、多样性、新颖性的调和平均为指标，SEPO在8/12设置中排名第一，无基线领先超过2项。平均而言，相比基础采样，LLM评判的多样性由1.92升至3.43，新颖性由1.00升至3.34（1-5量表），质量由3.72降至3.30。扩大优化集合可增大提示条件响应的分布间距。

Conclusion: 系统提示集合优化是提升推理阶段LLM创造性的有前景途径；在显式质量约束下以分布级多样性为目标，可在牺牲有限质量的代价下显著改善多样性与新颖性。

Abstract: Preference-aligned LLMs can exhibit mode collapse, concentrating their output distributions around a narrow set of high-reward responses. This limits creative generation, which requires quality, novelty, and diversity. Existing test-time methods often emphasize diversity, giving less attention to whether individual responses are both high-quality and novel. This distinction matters in practical use, where users may seek a single compelling, original response. We formalize creative generation as a multi-objective distributional optimization problem and introduce SEPO, a training-free framework for System Prompt Ensemble Optimization. The framework combines prompt evolution with greedy max-min selection to identify behaviorally distinct system prompts under a quality constraint, then uniformly samples one prompt per response at inference time. Across four creative generation tasks with increasing quality requirements and three open-weight models, SEPO ranks first in 8 of 12 settings on the harmonic mean of quality, diversity, and novelty, while no baseline leads in more than two. On average, it raises LLM-judged diversity from 1.92 to 3.43 and novelty from 1.00 to 3.34 over base sampling (1-5 scale), with quality decreasing from 3.72 to 3.30. We further show that expanding the optimized ensemble increases separation among prompt-conditioned response distributions. These results support system prompt ensemble optimization as a promising approach to improving LLM creativity at inference time.

</details>


### [132] [Hierarchical Graph Metanetworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34726)
*Ioannis Kalogeropoulos,Giorgos Bouritsas,Yannis Panagakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出分层图元网络H-GMNs，利用网络架构的层次结构进行图粗化-细化，并扩展到Transformer，在86M参数模型库上实现近常数成本与更高样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 权重空间学习有望支撑元网络完成优化、评估与适配等全生命周期任务，但现有方法（含基于消息传递的图元网络GMN）仅在最细粒度上处理每个神经元与权重，代价随参数量线性增长，难以扩展到大规模模型。而神经网络本身具有层次结构（神经元→层→块/模块），且Lipschitz上界、泛化度量等性质正是层/块级量的复合，故应引入该归纳偏置。

Method: 提出Hierarchical Graph Metanetworks：利用输入架构层次，通过保持置换对称性的池化（pooling）与反池化（un-pooling）层对图进行由粗到细的合并与细化；进一步为Transformer设计保持置换对称性的图构造方式并支持多级池化，从而将H-GMN扩展到ViT。

Result: 构建新的ResNet-18、ViT及多种ViT模型库（最大86M参数），任务涵盖模型评估（Lipschitz界代理指标、泛化与鲁棒性预测）、测试（后门检测）与编辑（剪枝）。结果显示：相较非等变替代方案与扁平GMN，H-GMNs样本效率显著更高；成本随元网络深度几乎不变，可在扁平GMN显存耗尽的更深层次上处理，且不牺牲精度，部分情形精度明显提升。

Conclusion: 在元网络中显式引入架构层次诱导偏置，是实现可扩展、可扩展深度且高样本效率权重空间学习的关键途径，使元网络能够应用于大规模现代架构。

Abstract: The emerging field of weight space learning has demonstrated the potential to enable metanetworks, i.e. models that process neural network (NN) data, to support several steps of the machine learning lifecycle, from model optimisation to evaluation and adaptation. However, this potential has yet to materialise beyond small-scale MLPs or CNNs: most current models, including message-passing based graph metanetworks (GMNs), operate exclusively at the finest level of granularity, processing every neuron and weight of the input model. Their cost therefore scales with the number of parameters. Yet NNs are inherently organised hierarchically, with neurons forming layers and layers forming blocks and modules. Additionally, several of their properties, such as Lipschitz upper bounds and generalisation measures, are compositions of layer- and block-level quantities. To incorporate this inductive bias, we introduce Hierarchical Graph Metanetworks (H-GMNs), which leverage the input architecture's hierarchy to coarsen and refine the graph using permutation-symmetry-preserving pooling and un-pooling layers. Further, we extend H-GMNs to Transformers by introducing a graph construction that preserves permutation symmetries and enables multi-level pooling. Our evaluation includes new ResNet-18, ViT and varying ViT model zoos of up to 86M parameters, on tasks spanning model evaluation (an analytic Lipschitz bound proxy, generalisation and robustness prediction), testing (backdoor detection) and editing (pruning). Experimental results show that, compared to non-equivariant alternatives and flat GMNs, H-GMNs are markedly more sample-efficient, and their cost remains nearly constant as metanetwork depth grows, allowing deeper and more expressive processing where flat GMNs run out of memory, without compromising accuracy, which in certain cases considerably improves.

</details>


### [133] [When Every Rollout Fails: Splicing Learner-Conditioned Options into Long-Horizon Agent RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34777)
*Haowen Hou,Yicheng Liu,Zhen Huang,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPLICE：对全失败组在线构造局部拼接目标，用短选项替代完整专家轨迹，为组相对RL恢复有效梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于组相对优势的强化学习（如GRPO）在长程智能体任务中常出现"全失败组"：组内所有采样轨迹奖励为零，组内归一化后无奖励信号，策略梯度为零。常见补救是引入更强模型提供完整解轨迹，但完整轨迹成本高，且使学习者暴露于长时间、严重离策略的历史。

Method: 在线、局部化的目标构造：对每个全失败组，由提议模型在当前策略轨迹上选择一个拼接点（splice point），从该点生成若干有界长度的候选选项（options）；用重复的当前策略续写估计每个选项的价值，将正价值估计转换为回报加权的局部目标，并仅在这些选项片段上拟合当前策略。

Result: 在两个策略族与两个长程智能体任务（SWE-bench Verified 软件工程、BrowseComp-Plus 深度研究）共四个设置上均优于GRPO与全轨迹引导基线，相对GRPO提升2.8–9.8个百分点；在初始失败的SWE任务上，将GRPO的"毕业率"提高10.8个百分点；同时降低了昂贵的提议模型计算开销。

Conclusion: 局部拼接式引导可在无需完整专家轨迹的前提下解决全失败组的梯度缺失问题，兼顾效果与计算成本，是一种可扩展的长程智能体强化学习训练方案。

Abstract: Group-based reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards often yields all-fail groups on long-horizon agent tasks: every sampled trajectory receives zero reward, leaving group-relative methods without a reward-based policy gradient. A common remedy supplies complete solutions from a stronger model, but full trajectories are expensive and expose the learner to long off-policy histories. We introduce SPLICE, which constructs local training targets from failed current-policy trajectories online. For each all-fail group, a proposal model selects a splice point in a current-policy trajectory and generates several bounded options from that point. We estimate each option's value using repeated current-policy continuations, convert positive estimates into a return-weighted local target, and fit the current policy on the options. We evaluate SPLICE across two policy families on two long-horizon agent tasks, Software Engineering with SWE-bench Verified and Deep Research with BrowseComp-Plus. SPLICE outperforms GRPO and full-trajectory guidance baselines in all four settings, improving over GRPO by 2.8--9.8 percentage points. On initially failed SWE tasks, it raises GRPO's graduation rate by 10.8 percentage points. Further experiments show that SPLICE improves performance while reducing costly proposal-model computation.

</details>


### [134] [From NP-Hardness to Exact Tractability Through Sample Abundance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34734)
*Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 对含噪奇偶拟合（最坏情况NP难）而言，在目标与可行集不变的前提下，附加对同一源的少量观测即可使精确优化变得可解：固定k≥3、对数平均度，仅需O(n/(log n)^{1/(k-2)})次额外读数，即可以高概率在O(n log²n)最坏情况工作量内完成精确优化，且该首项系数对弱恢复是紧的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出"额外观测可降低精确优化复杂度而不改变问题本身（目标函数与可行集均不变）"这一现象，并以含噪奇偶拟合（noisy parity fitting）作为最坏情况NP难的具体载体加以严格刻画，同时给出弱恢复阈值的紧性。

Method: 考虑均匀采样的秩一符号张量条目、独立的公平因子符号与独立误差；固定交互阶k≥3、每块n个符号、对数平均度、已知信道偏置。分析同一源的额外重复读数所带来的信息增益，借助"带符号场定律"（signed-field law）刻画相对生成源的增益、并列最优解个数与后续重加权所需维度，并用深度O(log log n)的局部估计器（带独立读数标记）分析弱恢复，以及用剩余坐标的几何结构给出高分解局部验证的锐利半径。

Result: O(n/(log n)^{1/(k-2)})次额外读数即可高概率实现精确优化，最坏情况工作量O(n log²n)；该首项系数对弱恢复是锐利的；一个统一的带符号场定律同时决定相对生成源的增益、并列最优解数目与再加权维度；剩余坐标的几何决定高分解局部验证的锐利半径（均高概率成立）。

Conclusion: 额外观测可在不改变优化问题本身的情况下，将最坏情况NP难的含噪奇偶拟合转化为可高效精确求解的问题，且所需额外读数与计算代价均由明确标度刻画，相关阈值（弱恢复首项系数、局部验证半径）为锐利。

Abstract: Additional observations can make exact optimization tractable without changing the objective or feasible set. We establish this phenomenon for noisy parity fitting, a worst-case NP-hard problem, with uniformly sampled rank-one sign tensor entries, independent fair factor signs and independent errors. For fixed interaction order $k\ge3$, $n$ signs per block, logarithmic mean degree and known channel biases, $O(n/(\log n)^{1/(k-2)})$ further readings of the same source suffice with high probability for exact optimization in $O(n\log^2 n)$ worst-case work. The leading coefficient is sharp for weak recovery by depth-$O(\log\log n)$ local estimators with independent reading marks. A common signed-field law determines the gain over the generating source, the number of tied optima and the dimension needed for later reweightings. The geometry of the remaining coordinates determines the sharp radius of local verification for high-scoring assignments with high probability.

</details>


### [135] [Transferring Architecture as an Optimization Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34816)
*Vighnesh Subramaniam,Boris Katz,Brian Cheung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将随机初始化网络的"初始几何"视为一种优化先验，提出通过将目标网络的表征几何对齐到一个无关的引导网络（guide network），实现跨架构的"命运"迁移；实验表明可将深度优势注入浅层网络、将细粒度表征迁移给粗粒度网络，从而提升下游性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 初始化不仅决定权重尺度，还在输入数据上建立初始几何，决定哪些样本被视为相近、梯度下降优先精修哪些表征。作者据此提出核心问题：网络能否通过参考一个完全不同的架构来改变自身的优化先验？即架构带来的"命运"是否可与物理结构解耦。

Method: 采用一种初始化对齐程序：在任意下游训练之前，将目标网络的表征几何对齐到随机初始化的引导网络的表征几何。该过程有三个关键约束：对齐不传递任何已学特征、引导网络不接收梯度、不使用标签。从而在无监督、无梯度回传、无特征迁移的条件下完成跨架构几何迁移。

Result: 在两个场景中验证有效：(1) 将浅层网络对齐到深层网络，可向浅层网络注入深度的优化收益；(2) 用细分辨率网络塑造粗表征网络的几何，实现表征粒度的迁移。两种情形下对齐均提升了目标网络的下游性能。

Conclusion: 模型的架构"命运"可与其物理结构解耦，并能在初始化阶段被显式修改。这揭示了初始几何作为可迁移优化先验的作用，为无需改变架构或标签即可改善网络优化提供了一条新途径。

Abstract: Initialization determines the optimization "fate" of a model, going far beyond just setting the scale of its weights. It establishes a fundamental initial geometry over the input data, dictating certain aspects such as which examples are considered close and which representations gradient descent will naturally refine first. In this paper, we view the initial geometry of a network as an optimization prior and ask whether a network can change its optimization prior by referencing a completely different architecture. To investigate this question, we use an initialization procedure where a target network is aligned to the representational geometry of a randomly initialized guide network before any downstream training occurs. Crucially, alignment to the guide network cannot transfer learned features, never receives gradients, and does not use labels. We demonstrate that this cross-architecture transfer of "fate" benefits the target network in two key domains: (1) instilling the optimization benefits of depth into shallow networks by aligning them to deeper networks, and (2) transferring representational granularity by using fine-resolution networks to shape the geometry of networks with coarser representations. In both cases, the alignment results in improved downstream performance of the target network. Ultimately, this demonstrates that a model's architectural destiny can be decoupled from its physical structure and explicitly modified at initialization.

</details>


### [136] [BRIAR: Martingale Belief Smoothing on Bifurcation-Guided Reasoning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34746)
*Tianyi Yang,Haoran Zhang,Yifan Chen,Zixuan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BRIAR将RLVR中rollout计算与梯度信用的分配问题归结为前缀通过概率的Doob鞅几何，据此构建树结构RLVR框架：沿结果分岔前沿扩展推理树、用RTS平滑线性时间计算节点后验、并在吸收后施加零和长度中心化后缀优势，从而避开无效反思与冗余验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准策略优化器在整条轨迹上"盲目"分配rollout计算与梯度信用，存在两类病态：难题上早期一步失误导致后续全盘偏离（derailed），可解题上答案已确定后仍持续生成冗余验证（absorbed）。作者将二者统一解释为同一结构事实。

Method: 理论：指出on-policy verifier下沿推理轨迹的前缀通过概率V^π(u)构成[0,1]有界的Doob鞅，可学习信用方差守恒且上界为V^π(1−V^π)，在两端边界消失。方法三点：(1) 沿置信上界的结果分岔前沿扩展与话语对齐的推理树，引导rollout远离已吸收的反复思考、转向未决分岔点；(2) 用边界缩放的异方差边方差建模隐节点值，经两遍Rauch–Tung–Striebel树信念平滑在O(|V|)线性时间内得到精确全局后验，避免O(m^3)矩阵求逆并门控边界噪声；(3) 检测最小吸收前沿节点，仅在吸收之后施加零和、对数长度中心化的后缀优势，保证吸收前推导可证不变。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数值结果；所述结论为：以线性时间获得精确全局后验（消除O(m^3)求逆）、可门控虚假边界噪声，且吸收前推导保持不变性（provably invariant）。

Conclusion: BRIAR以鞅几何为原理性依据重构树结构RLVR：把rollout预算导向未决决策点、以高效精确的树信念平滑估计节点价值、并将优势塑形严格限制在吸收后区间，从结构上同时缓解derailment与过度验证两类低效。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has rapidly advanced long-chain reasoning, yet standard policy optimizers distribute rollout compute and gradient credit blindly across a trajectory. On hard problems, an early misstep derails the remaining trace, whereas on solvable problems, the policy keeps generating redundant verification long after the answer is already determined. We trace both pathologies to a single structural fact: under an on-policy verifier, the true prefix pass probability $V^π(u)$ along a reasoning trace forms a $[0,1]$-bounded Doob martingale whose learnable credit variance is strictly conserved and bounded above by $V^π(u)(1-V^π(u))$, vanishing at both the derailed ($V^π\to 0$) and absorbed ($V^π\to 1$) boundaries. We introduce BRIAR (Bifurcation-guided Rollout and Inference on Adaptive Reasoning trees), a tree-structured RLVR framework built directly around this martingale geometry. First, BRIAR expands discourse-aligned reasoning trees along an upper-confidence outcome-bifurcation frontier, steering rollouts away from absorbed rumination and toward unresolved decision points.
