Second, it models latent node values with boundary-scaled heteroscedastic edge variances and computes exact global posteriors in linear $\mathcal{O}(|V|)$ time via two-pass Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) tree belief smoothing, eliminating $\mathcal{O}(m^3)$ matrix inversions while gating out spurious boundary noise. Third, BRIAR detects minimal absorbed frontier nodes and applies zero-sum log-length centered suffix advantages strictly post-absorption, leaving pre-absorption derivations provably invariant.

</details>


### [137] [Not all world models can doubt: how training objective and architecture shape epistemic uncertainty in JEPA and RSSM world models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34830)
*Simon Baur,Wojciech Samek,Jackie Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对两类世界模型（JEPA式LeWM与RSSM式DreamerV3）的后验认知不确定性估计做了受控对比，发现RSSM经循环更新平均与重建目标两条机制会衰减状态扰动，导致其不确定性遗漏自身后验已察觉的偏差；JEPA则无此衰减，不确定性如实反映模型所见，并在五项下游任务上表现更一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型的决策可靠性取决于其预测能力，而预测仅在其经验范围内可靠，认知不确定性（EU）正是标记经验边界的信号。但训练目标与架构如何塑造世界模型的EU尚缺乏系统认识，因此需要对不同范式的模型进行受控比较。

Method: 在十个仿真视觉控制环境中，对两种范式代表模型——无解码器、预测下一嵌入的联合嵌入预测架构LeWM（JEPA）与从循环隐状态重建观测的DreamerV3（RSSM）——进行受控的状态扰动追踪：沿模型各阶段直至最终EU估计（采用事后EU方法：末层拉普拉斯近似与集成）。

Result: RSSM在EU估计前通过两条机制衰减状态扰动：(1) 循环更新对上下文做平均，单帧变化仅以部分幅度进入状态，且需扰动持续才逐步恢复；(2) 重建目标按像素覆盖面积加权实体，小目标可能因训练目标而丢失。JEPA不具备这两种机制，扰动可贯穿所有阶段传导至不确定性。该差异体现在五项不确定性相关下游任务（从陌生状态检测到不确定性惩罚规划）上的性能差距。

Conclusion: RSSM的不确定性会遗漏其自身后验已记录的偏差，而JEPA的不确定性报告的是模型真正看到的内容；训练目标与架构决定EU信号的有效性，选择世界模型与不确定性估计方法时须考虑该结构性差异。

Abstract: World models predict how an environment responds to actions, so that candidate actions can be evaluated before execution. Executed actions, or decisions, taken in this manner are only as reliable as the model's predictive capabilities, and predictions in turn are only reliable within the model's experience. Epistemic uncertainty (EU) is the signal that marks where that experience ends. In this work we offer an extensive empirical study on how training objectives and architectures shape epistemic uncertainty of world models. More precisely, we present a controlled comparison of post-hoc EU (last-layer Laplace and ensembles) between two dominant models of different paradigms: 1) LeWorldModel (LeWM): A joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA), which predicts the next embedding without a decoder, and 2) DreamerV3: A recurrent state-space model (RSSM), which reconstructs the observation from a recurrent latent state. In ten simulated visual-control environments, we follow a controlled state perturbation of the environment state through every stage of the models up to the final EU estimate. We find that the RSSM attenuates the state perturbation through two mechanisms before it reaches an estimator on the predictor feature. Its recurrent update averages over context, so a change in a single frame reaches the state at a fraction of its size and recovers only as the change persists. Its reconstruction objective weights an entity by the pixels it covers, so a small object can be lost due to the training objective. The JEPA has neither mechanism and passes the change through all stages to the uncertainty. We further show that this translates to gaps in five uncertainty related downstream tasks, from unfamiliar-state detection to uncertainty-penalised planning: the RSSM uncertainty misses deviations that its own posterior registers, whereas the JEPA uncertainty reports what its model sees.

</details>


### [138] [From Prediction to Decision: Decision-Focused Fine-Tuning of Autoregressive Tabular Transformers for Cost-Sensitive Financial Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34858)
*Ashkan Golgoon,Giacomo Domeniconi,Samuel Atkins,Sergio Servantez,Zain Khandwala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文将预训练自回归Transformer在标签token上的受限softmax直接视为业务动作分布，用机构指定的、按金额缩放的成本矩阵做期望效用闭式微调，优于事后贝叶斯最优阈值化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构不依概率行动，而依后果不对称且异方差的决策行动：漏掉高价值欺诈与误拒小额支付的代价截然不同。常规做法是校准概率后按成本矩阵事后做贝叶斯最优阈值化，作者质疑直接微调模型以优化决策目标能否更优。

Method: 对同一预训练模型微调，使其在标签token上的受限softmax（本身即业务动作分布）在机构指定、随金额缩放的成本矩阵下最大化期望效用。奖励是(动作, 结果, 金额)的已知函数且结果在数据中可观测，故期望效用目标及梯度可闭式求得，属样本相关成本敏感学习，逐样本损失对预测概率为线性，无需采样、critic或优势估计。另加对预训练模型的KL锚定与辅助语言建模项以保持校准与生成式事件模型；并给出采样、组归一化的GRPO式变体作为轨迹级奖励的扩展接口。

Result: 摘要未给出实验数据与具体指标，仅声明该闭式期望效用微调相对事后贝叶斯最优阈值化具备改进动机，并以KL锚定与辅助LM项维持校准及生成式建模能力。

Conclusion: 可将决策目标直接内嵌于模型微调，实现无采样、闭式梯度的成本敏感学习，并在保持校准与生成式事件模型的同时获得面向业务效用的决策策略，且存在向轨迹级奖励扩展的路径。

Abstract: Autoregressive transformers trained on tokenized tabular event streams are a simple and strong baseline for predicting financial events such as fraud. Yet institutions do not act on probabilities; they act on decisions whose consequences are asymmetric and heteroscedastic: the cost of a missed high-value fraud and that of a wrongly declined small purchase are not the same. The standard bridge is post-hoc Bayes-optimal thresholding of calibrated probabilities under a cost matrix. We ask whether fine-tuning the model to the decision objective can do better. We fine-tune the same pretrained model so that its restricted softmax over label tokens (already a distribution over business actions) maximizes expected utility under an institution-specified, amount-scaled cost matrix. Because the reward is a known function of (action, outcome, amount) and the outcome is observed in the data, the expected-utility objective and its gradient are available in closed form. This is example-dependent cost-sensitive learning whose per-example loss is linear in the predicted probability, with no sampling, critics, or advantage estimation. A KL anchor to the pretrained model and an auxiliary language-modeling term preserve calibration and the generative event model. A sampled, group-normalized (GRPO-style) variant provides the extension point to trajectory-level rewards.

</details>


### [139] [Interpretable and Accurate Hybrid Decision Trees with Selective Case-Based Splits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34887)
*Cristiano Landi,Alessio Cascione,Riccardo Guidotti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合决策树架构，将基于特征的划分与基于实例的划分（case-based）融合于同一棵树，并引入"选择性实例划分"策略：在需要分裂时搜索最具信息量的特征子集来构造划分，从而在实例方法的特异性与特征方法的简洁性之间取得平衡。实验表明该方法优于标准决策树、媲美集成方法，且生成的树更浅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树是可解释模型的代表，但贪心框架下的划分策略多样（轴平行、斜向、基于邻近性的实例划分及混合方法），尚无普适最优方案：纯特征划分简洁但表达力有限，纯实例划分表达力强但可解释性与泛化性受限。作者希望在同一棵树中兼取二者之长。

Method: 两项贡献：①提出混合树架构，在树的不同节点上同时允许基于特征的划分与基于实例（exemplar）的划分，形成异构划分结构；②提出选择性实例划分——当需要分裂时，先搜索最具信息量的特征子集，再据此定义实例式划分，实质是在特征子空间上做邻近性划分，兼顾实例方法的特异性与特征方法的简洁性。整个构造仍在贪心学习框架内进行。

Result: 在多样化表格数据集上，该方法优于标准决策树，性能与集成方法相当，同时生成的树更浅，并保持甚至提升预测准确率。

Conclusion: 将特征划分与实例划分混合、并以特征子集选择约束实例划分，是提升贪心决策树"性能—简洁性—可解释性"折中的有效途径，可在浅层结构下达到接近集成的精度。

Abstract: Decision trees are among the most well-known interpretable models in Machine Learning. Over the years, a wide variety of strategies have been proposed to define split conditions within the greedy learning framework, aiming to guide instance routing and enhance predictive performance. These strategies range from axis-parallel and oblique splits based on feature values to proximity-based splits using exemplar instances and more complex hybrid approaches. However, no single method has emerged as universally superior. In this work, we present two key contributions to the construction of greedy decision trees. First, we propose a hybrid tree architecture that integrates both feature-based and case-based splits, allowing for heterogeneous splits throughout the tree. Second, we introduce a novel type of split that leverages a selective case-based approach: when a split is needed, our method searches for the most informative subset of features to define the split, effectively balancing the specificity of case-based methods with the simplicity of feature-based ones. Experiments on a diverse set of tabular datasets show that our method outperforms standard decision trees and achieves performance on par with ensemble methods, while producing shallow trees that retain or even improve predictive accuracy.

</details>


### [140] [When to Trust a PFN: Detecting Harmful Shift in Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34767)
*Viet Nguyen,Herman Bergström,Stephan Rabanser,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CALD：面向表格PFN的无监督OOD监测器，通过微调解码器最大化与测试预测的分歧并保持ID锚集精度，以锚损失作为单侧检验统计量触发告警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格先验拟合网络（PFN）在表格分类任务上表现良好，但在部署时遇到分布偏移会"静默失败"，即无任何提示地给出错误预测。现有缺乏无需测试标签即可及时发现PFN失效的监测手段。

Method: 提出CALD（Calibrated Anchor-Loss Disagreement）轻量级无监督监测器：在保持ID锚集精度不变的前提下，微调解码器以最大化其输出与测试集预测的分歧；若测试批次为有害OOD，则此微调会损害ID锚集上的表现，从而通过微调后的锚损失暴露问题，无需任何测试标签。理论证明在偏移足够严重时该锚损失的期望严格低于ID评估情形，从而构成单侧检验。

Result: 在14个数据集上（涵盖表格与结构化分布偏移），基于TabPFN与TabICL两种模型，CALD在有害偏移检测上与基线持平或更优，且对良性偏移的误报显著更少。

Conclusion: CALD为表格PFN提供了一种无需标签、理论有保证的失效告警机制，在检测有害偏移与抑制良性误报之间取得良好平衡，具备实际部署价值。

Abstract: Tabular prior-fitted networks (PFNs) work well across tabular classification problems but can fail silently at deployment when distributions shift. We propose CALD (Calibrated Anchor-Loss Disagreement), a lightweight unsupervised monitor that triggers an alarm to warn when a PFN might fail. By finetuning the decoder to maximize disagreement with the test-set prediction while maintaining accuracy on an ID anchor set, CALD identifies harmful OOD batches through the resulting anchor loss, requiring no labels on test batches. We prove that the post-finetune anchor loss has a strictly lower expectation under sufficiently severe shift than under ID eval, yielding a one-sided test. Across 14 datasets covering tabular and structured distribution shift, on both TabPFN and TabICL, CALD matches or outperforms baselines on harmful shifts and is markedly more resilient to benign-shift false alarms.

</details>


### [141] [Attack Policy Selection can Mislead Monitor Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34934)
*Charlie Griffin,Oliver Makins,Asa Cooper Stickland,Alessandro Abate,Yarin Gal,Abby D'Cruz,Xander Davies*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文以一款已投产的OpenAI监控器为案例，发现监控器漏报率对攻击策略的生成方法与优化程度高度敏感（单一RL方法内0.5%–20.8%，不同方法间6.4%–23.0%），指出仅用基础攻击方法评估监控器会带来虚假信心，且将结果解读为部署证据需额外的"代理合法性论证"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI监控器（标记智能体危险行为的分类器）通常用攻击策略生成的对话记录作为部署中危险行为的代理来评估，但这类代理的有效性缺乏检验。作者希望揭示：评估结论是否会被攻击策略的选择所左右，从而判断此类评估能否作为部署性能的证据。

Method: 以一款已退役的OpenAI生产监控器为案例进行实证研究。系统比较不同攻击策略生成方法（优化提示 vs. 对话记录编辑）及同一方法（强化学习）内不同优化水平对测得漏报率的影响；所有攻击策略仅针对代理监控器开发，全部报告结果均验证其对留出的GPT-5.4目标监控器的迁移性；并分析代理记录中可见伪迹（如出现指令中不存在的"side task"推理）对监控器判断的影响。

Result: 漏报率在单一RL方法的优化水平间为0.5%–20.8%，在各方法最佳优化水平间为6.4%–23.0%；攻击均成功迁移至留出的GPT-5.4目标监控器；代理记录中存在可被监控器察觉的伪迹，且这些伪迹会影响其判断。

Conclusion: 监控器规避评估极易产生误导：仅使用基础攻击方法会让人错误地认为监控器很强。由于代理可改变评估结论，将任一结果解读为部署性能证据，都必须另行给出"代理为何具有信息量"的合法性论证（含保守性论证），而如何做出有说服力的论证仍是未解问题。

Abstract: AI monitors, classifiers that flag dangerous behaviour by AI agents, are often evaluated against transcripts generated by attack policies acting as proxies for dangerous behaviour in deployment. We evaluate an ex-production OpenAI monitor as a case study and find that measured false negative rate is highly sensitive to both the method used to generate attack policies and the level of optimisation applied within that method. This rate ranged from 0.5\% to 20.8\% across optimisation levels of a single method (reinforcement learning), and from 6.4\% to 23.0\% across methods at their best observed optimisation level (optimised prompting vs.\ transcript editing). Every attack policy was developed against proxy monitors only: all reported results show attacks transferring to the held-out GPT-5.4 target monitor. Our results suggest that monitor evasion evaluations can easily mislead: an evaluation using only basic attack methods may give false confidence that a monitor is strong.
  The sensitivity to attack-policy choice raises a problem: interpreting any particular result as evidence about deployment performance requires justification. The transcripts produced by attack policies are proxies for the dangerous behaviour a monitor must flag in deployment. These proxies can introduce visible artefacts, such as reasoning about a ``side task'' absent from the instructions shown to the monitor, that affect monitor judgements. Given the sensitivity above, the choice of proxy can change an evaluation's conclusion. Reading these results as evidence about deployment therefore requires a separate legitimacy argument for why the proxies are informative, including any justification of conservativeness. Making convincing arguments remains an open problem.

</details>


### [142] [Adaptive Reliability Reserves for Residential Battery Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34779)
*Maruthi Vemula,Neeraj Praneeth Gajula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HomeFleet-ERCOT基准，将居民电池的市场放电收益与家庭备用服务放在同一框架下评估，用县级风险阈值替代固定20%储备并比较PPO控制器，属条件性仿真结果而非实测可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 居民电池在市场中放电会消耗家庭停电时所需的能量，形成"市场收益—备用服务"权衡；现有工作很少将二者联合建模并提供可复现的对比基准。

Method: 构建HomeFleet-ERCOT基准，融合观测电价与县级停电数据，配以可识别的模拟家庭和因果控制器输入；以2024年休斯敦重放为场景，将固定20%储备与县风险阈值策略对比，并进行独立家庭优化与21次PPO训练（其中10个为"承诺—成本"候选，覆盖31.1%–96.3%备用覆盖率）；辅以时间、地理、车队与风暴迁移测试，预警充电覆盖的能量—功率充分性保证，以及短时/漏报/误报预警、负荷抬升、容量衰减、掉线等合成测试，并做静态空调审计与老化敏感性分析。

Result: 县风险阈值使观测到的五小时备用覆盖率由75.0%升至86.5%，每户建模年化边际收益增加10.2美元（配对95%区间+1.7至+21.7美元），但配对覆盖增益区间含零；10个候选的建模边际低于固定20%，其中3个覆盖率也更低；仅19.5%（41个县级大规模停电事件中的8个）提供足以覆盖所有电池预设的连续预警时长；静态空调审计仍保留既有馈线违规，老化对比显示模型敏感。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了可复现的基准、控制器与检查点，用于同时评估居民电池的市场价值与家庭备用服务，支持二者兼顾的电池项目设计；结论为条件性仿真，需谨慎外推。

Abstract: In a Houston 2024 replay, replacing a fixed 20 percent battery reserve with a county-risk threshold raises observed five-hour backup coverage from 75.0 percent to 86.5 percent and adds USD 10.2 in modeled annualized margin per home (paired 95 percent interval: USD +1.7 to USD +21.7). The paired coverage-gain interval includes zero. This is a conditional simulation result, not measured company reliability. Residential batteries face this tradeoff because market discharge consumes energy also needed for household outages. We release HomeFleet-ERCOT, combining observed prices and county outages with identified simulated homes and causal controller inputs. We compare independent-home optimization with 21 proximal policy optimization (PPO) training runs; ten promise-cost candidates span 31.1 percent-96.3 percent backup coverage. Of these, 10 have lower modeled margin than fixed 20 percent, and 3 also have lower coverage. Chronological, geographic, fleet, and storm tests examine transfer. An alert charging override has a conditional energy-and-power sufficiency guarantee, but only 19.5 percent of 41 county mass-outage episodes provide enough continuous warning for every battery preset. Synthetic tests include short, missed, and false warnings, load uplift, capacity loss, and dropout. A static AC audit retains existing feeder violations; aging comparisons expose model sensitivity. The released benchmark, controllers, and checkpoints provide a reproducible basis for comparing these tradeoffs. This work supports the design of residential battery programs that assess market value and household backup service together.

</details>


### [143] [Split-RL: Local Conflict Resolution in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34799)
*Benjamin Fuhrer,Chen Tessler,Gal Dalal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Split-RL，一种基于导向标签的GBDT算法，在树构建过程中将冲突的梯度信号路由到不相交的状态空间区域，从而局部隔离矛盾反馈，在约束RL与离线RL中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL策略常混合时空局部化的相互矛盾反馈（如开阔处优先速度、狭窄处优先精度）。标准神经网络参数共享所有来源的反馈，缺乏隔离冲突信号的结构机制；而标准树拟合在到达叶节点前会先聚合矛盾梯度，同样造成信号干扰。

Method: Split-RL利用GBDT天然的归纳偏置，将导向标签（来自启发式、传感器事件或既有规则系统）整合进树的构建过程，把这些更新路由到互不相交的状态空间区域。理论层面证明早期梯度聚合会丢失目标特定信息，并刻画Split-RL打分何时能隔离冲突更新。应用于约束RL与含混合数据集的离线RL，每个任务平衡两个局部冲突的目标。

Result: 在空间冲突、稀有事件冲突与时间冲突等多类场景中，Split-RL一致优于现有方法：满足回合成本预算同时保持有竞争力的奖励。离线设置下能局部化模仿与策略改进之间的权衡，在不同数据集规模下均超过其他方法并接近专家性能。

Conclusion: 显式状态空间分区与梯度路由是处理局部冲突反馈的有效归纳偏置；Split-RL兼顾规则派生结构与可学习策略，为约束RL及离线RL提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) policies are often trained by mixing contradictory feedback that is localized in space and time. For example, a robot may prioritize speed in open areas versus precision in tight gaps. We introduce Split-RL, a Gradient Boosting Trees (GBT) algorithm that uses guidance labels to locally isolate these conflicting signals. Standard neural networks (NNs) lack the structural mechanism to do this, as their parameters share feedback from all sources. While GBT provides the inductive bias to explicitly partition the state-space per objective, standard tree-fitting procedures aggregate contradictory gradient signals before they reach the leaves. Split-RL leverages this bias to integrate guidance labels into tree construction, routing these updates into disjoint state-space regions and preventing signal interference. Such labels can arise from heuristics, sensor-derived events, or existing rule-based systems, allowing Split-RL to incorporate rule-derived structure while retaining a learned policy. We theoretically show how early gradient aggregation loses objective-specific information and characterize when the Split-RL score isolates conflicting updates. We focus on constrained RL and offline RL with mixed datasets, each balancing two objectives that conflict locally. Across domains with spatial, rare-event, and temporal conflicts, Split-RL consistently outperforms existing methods by meeting the episodic cost budget while maintaining competitive rewards. In offline settings, Split-RL localizes the tradeoff between imitation and policy improvement, outperforming other methods and achieving near-expert performance across varying dataset sizes.

</details>


### [144] [Deep Reinforcement Learning for Multi-UAV Data Harvesting Under Variable Team Sizes and Agent Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34818)
*Thiago de Souza Lamenza,Bruno José Olivieri de Souza,Markus Endler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出F-MADDPG，一种支持传感器与UAV数量可变的灵活MADDPG变体，用于无线传感器数据采集，并能应对任务中智能体的永久性灾难失效；相比MLP版MADDPG基线平均完成更快，逆基数团队规模采样优于均匀采样，且训练期故障暴露无稳定收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信基础设施受限时无线传感器无法卸载数据，需UAV机动采集；多UAV团队可实现高效冗余覆盖，MARL可学习去中心化协调策略。但为不同团队与传感器规模分别训练策略代价高，且固定规模策略对智能体失效缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 提出F-MADDPG，对MADDPG进行灵活化改造以适配可变的传感器与UAV数量；框架同时容纳任务中永久移除智能体的灾难性失效。与采用MLP的MADDPG基线对比，并评估基于团队规模采样与智能体终止暴露的训练策略。

Result: F-MADDPG在评估的各规模下均可学习，平均任务完成速度快于基线；逆基数（inverse-cardinality）团队规模采样优于均匀采样；在智能体永久损失后仍保持有效性；训练中暴露于故障未带来一致收益。

Conclusion: F-MADDPG实现了跨规模的可变数量协调与失效鲁棒性，逆基数采样是有效的训练策略，而训练期故障暴露并非必要；为可变规模UAV集群数据采集提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Collecting data from wireless sensors can be challenging when limited communication infrastructure prevents them from offloading their measurements. In such settings, unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) can exploit their mobility to harvest data locally, while coordinated UAV teams can provide efficient and redundant coverage of remote areas. Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables such teams to learn decentralized coordination policies. However, training separate policies for different team and sensor populations is costly, and fixed-population policies may be vulnerable to agent failures. This work proposes F-MADDPG, a flexible variant of Multi-Agent Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (MADDPG) that supports data harvesting with varying numbers of wireless sensors and UAVs. The framework also accommodates unexpected catastrophic failures that permanently remove agents during a mission. We compare F-MADDPG with a MADDPG baseline that uses multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) and evaluate training strategies based on team-size sampling and exposure to agent terminations. The results show that F-MADDPG learns across the evaluated population sizes, completes missions faster than the baseline on average, benefits from inverse-cardinality team-size sampling compared with uniform sampling, remains effective after permanent agent losses, and gains no consistent benefit from exposure to failures during training.

</details>


### [145] [LoopTitan: What Does Parameter Revisit Buy a Mixture-of-Experts?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34821)
*Zichen Tian,Yudong Lu,Xuansheng Wu,Na Zou,Xia Hu,Guanchu Wang,Qianru Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过严格匹配的对照实验证明，循环 MoE（looped MoE）的收益几乎不来自参数复访本身，而主要来自网络形状、残差缩放（等价于对复访层降低学习率）和额外计算；作者据此提出无需复访的 LoopTitan-Base 基线，在 Muon 下可匹配最强循环方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期循环 MoE 研究宣称在参数量、FLOPs 与 KV cache 匹配预算下显著优于标准 MoE，但这些收益与网络形状、残差缩放等设计选择混杂，无法判定"参数复访"本身的贡献。作者因此追问：参数复访究竟带来了什么？

Method: 1) 在匹配预算下对 SOTA 循环配方做逐项分解，将复访置于最后，并用匹配的非复访对照检验；2) 理论分析残差缩放的作用，指出其等价于对复访层施加更低学习率，并在 AdamW 与 Muon 两种优化器下对比层间步长均匀性；3) 在固定参数下增加计算（额外一次复访 vs 专家数翻倍以上）评估复访；4) 采用预注册预测、公开 GitHub issues 记录完整探索历史的实验范式。

Result: 非复访对照保留全部增益：增益主要来自网络形状，其余来自残差缩放；该缩放效应在 Muon 下消失。超出匹配预算时，额外一次复访的收益大于专家数翻倍以上，但在 Muon 下等参数等计算的更深标准 MoE 可获得该增益的 90.2%。

Conclusion: 循环 MoE 的增益来源是网络形状、优化器与额外计算，这些标准 MoE 均可采用。作者提出 LoopTitan-Base 作为更强 MoE 基线，在 Muon 下无需复访或残差缩放即可匹配最强循环配方，并开源 LoopTitan 及 17 个预训练模型（含对照），作为 Phase 1 技术报告为后续研究奠基。

Abstract: Looped Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures revisit layer parameters across depths. This design increases effective depth without adding parameters. Recent looped MoE studies report substantial gains over the standard MoE at matched budgets (parameters, FLOPs and KV cache). However, the reported gains still confound parameter revisit with other design choices, e.g., network shape and residual scale. We ask, what does parameter revisit itself buy? Using matched non-revisit controls, we find that parameter revisit itself buys almost nothing. We argue that revisit only offers a freedom an MoE already has, i.e., trading experts for depth (more layers, fewer experts each) at fixed parameters. First, at matched budgets, we decompose the state-of-the-art looped recipe, with revisit last. A non-revisit control retains all the gain. Most comes from network shape, and the rest from residual scale. We prove this scale acts as a lower learning rate on revisited layers. This helps under AdamW's uneven layer step sizes, but disappears under Muon, which keeps them nearly equal. Second, beyond matched budgets, we evaluate revisit by adding compute at fixed parameters. One extra visit gains more than doubling the experts. Yet without revisit, a deeper standard MoE at equal parameters and compute obtains 90.2% of that gain under Muon. Overall, gains come from network shape, the optimizer and added compute, which a standard MoE can adopt. Accordingly, we propose LoopTitan-Base as a stronger MoE baseline. Under Muon, it matches the strongest looped recipe without revisit or residual scale. We open-source LoopTitan and release 17 pretrained models (including LoopTitan-Base and the control). Our experiment record is public as GitHub issues, with predictions pre-registered and the full exploration history kept. This is our Phase 1 technical report, laying the foundation for follow-up studies.

</details>


### [146] [Routeability Before Routing: Routeability Audit Protocol (RAP) and RouteabilityBench for Audited LLM Model Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34825)
*Yihang Lu,Denica Kjorvezir,Ana Gjorgjevikj,Carola Doerr,Tome Eftimov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"可路由性优先"审计框架RAP与RouteabilityBench，用于在基准-模型组合得分矩阵完成后、解读路由器比较前，检验该基准是否存在足够的提示级差异；在4个基准、66个LLM上，常见路由增强手段均无法稳定超越单一最佳模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM路由的前提是基准中存在足够的提示级差异，使不同模型在不同输入上分别最优。但粗粒度评分的基准上大量模型并列最优、且单一最佳模型留下的提升空间很小，导致路由收益被高估或误读。因此需要先审计"可路由性"，再讨论路由器优劣。

Method: 提出审计式验证包RAP与RouteabilityBench；审计为回顾式（retrospective），在得分矩阵已生成后、路由器比较被推广前执行，而非验证尚未评测的模型组合。评测4个基准、66个LLM，检验更大嵌入集合、降维、选择性弃答、聚类、更强回归器等通用路由增强；并引入并列最优分析、组合构建细节与族校正统计检验。

Result: 通用路由增强不能稳定优于单一最佳模型。最明确的正向证据集中在依赖组合构建的Omni-MATH设置；GPQA、MMLU-Pro与IFEval则分别呈现路由难以奏效或以预测器敏感性为主的不同模式。

Conclusion: 路由收益应连同可路由性诊断、并列最优分析、组合构建细节与族校正统计检验一并报告，避免将特定组合上的偶然增益当作普适证据。

Abstract: LLM routing aims to choose, for each prompt, which model in a portfolio is most likely to answer correctly. This only makes sense when the benchmark contains enough prompt-specific variation for different models to be best on different inputs. On coarse-scored LLM benchmarks, however, many models often tie for the best score, and the single best model may already leave little room for improvement. We therefore propose a routeability-first audit, instantiated as RAP and RouteabilityBench, a runnable claim-verification package. The audit is retrospective: it runs once the benchmark-portfolio score matrix exists and before router comparisons are interpreted as general evidence, rather than certifying a portfolio that has not yet been evaluated. Across four benchmarks and 66 LLMs, generic routing enhancements such as larger embedding sets, dimensionality reduction, selective abstention, clustering, and stronger regressors do not consistently outperform the single best model. The clearest positive evidence is concentrated in portfolio-dependent Omni-MATH settings, while GPQA, MMLU-Pro, and IFEval show different ways in which routing can remain hard or predictor-sensitive. Our practical recommendation is that routing gains should be reported together with routeability diagnostics, tied-best analysis, portfolio construction details, and family-corrected statistical tests.

</details>


### [147] [Gradient TD($λ$) for Non-Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34826)
*Sujeet Lahane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将梯度TD（Gradient TD）中基于投影贝尔曼误差的λ-return算法从线性函数逼近推广到非线性函数逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半梯度TD可能发散；基于均方贝尔曼误差或TD误差目标的方法难以分析；Gradient TD通过值函数投影获得收敛的离策略算法，但λ-return版本此前仅限线性函数逼近，而现代强函数逼近器多是非线性的，因此需要推广。

Method: 沿用投影贝尔曼误差（Projected Bellman Error, PBE）目标（而非近期工作采用的广义投影贝尔曼误差），将λ-return梯度TD算法扩展到非线性函数逼近。

Result: abstract中未给出具体实验结果，仅说明完成了向非线性函数逼近的扩展。

Conclusion: 在PBE框架下可将λ-return梯度TD推广至非线性函数逼近，与采用广义投影贝尔曼误差的近期工作形成对照。

Abstract: Temporal difference (TD) learning is central to reinforcement learning. Many methods use semi-gradient TD, which may diverge, or develop algorithms with mean-squared Bellman or TD-error objectives that are difficult to analyze. Gradient TD algorithms overcome these challenges by using projection of the value function and present convergent off-policy algorithms. However, the algorithm for $λ$-return was limited to linear function approximation. Here, we extend the same to non-linear function approximation, which is desirable since many strong modern function approximators are complex and non-linear. Recent works have derived similar algorithms with a generalized projected Bellman error objective. Alternatively, we continue working with the projected Bellman error.

</details>


### [148] [Provably Efficient Offline Discounted Reinforcement Learning under Recursive Entropic Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34840)
*Amirparsa Bahrami,Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究递归熵风险测度（ERM）下的离线强化学习，提出ERM-VI-LCB算法，给出样本复杂度的PAC上界与下界，首次为风险规避与风险寻求两种情形提供严格的样本复杂度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归熵风险测度可统一刻画风险规避（β>0）与风险寻求（β<0）偏好，但离线RL场景下缺乏严格的样本复杂度理论保证，尤其是风险敏感性带来的指数依赖问题尚不明确。

Method: 针对带递归ERM的有限折扣无限时域MDP，采用基于模型的ERM值迭代下置信界（ERM-VI-LCB）算法，在固定数据集上学习近最优策略；并推导相应的下界以刻画最坏情况下的不可避免依赖。

Result: 得到PAC型样本复杂度上界，其随|β|/(1-γ)呈指数依赖；并通过匹配的下界证明该指数依赖在最坏情况下不可避免。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为递归ERM下离线RL（覆盖风险规避与风险寻求）提供严格样本复杂度保证，揭示了风险敏感性的本质统计代价。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted infinite-horizon Markov Decision Processes with recursive entropic risk measures (ERM), parameterized by $β\neq 0$. We consider both risk-averse ($β>0$) and risk-seeking ($β<0$) settings. We consider the offline RL setting, where the agent is provided with a fixed dataset and wishes to learn a near-optimal policy using this dataset. We introduce a model-based algorithm, called ERM Value Iteration Lower Confidence Bound (ERM-VI-LCB) and derive a PAC-type bound on its sample complexity. We also establish a corresponding lower bound, demonstrating that exponential dependence on $|β|/(1-γ)$ asserted by the upper bound is unavoidable in the worst case. The presented bounds provide the first rigorous sample complexity guarantees for offline RL under recursive ERM across both risk-averse and risk-seeking regimes.

</details>


### [149] [Put the World Back: Timestamps Matter in Time Series Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34848)
*Tingrui Huang,Yuanyuan Deng,Qingren Yao,Joaquin Vanschoren,Mykola Pechenizkiy,Songgaojun Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文从"世界到序列投影"视角重新定义时间序列推理，指出时间戳是连接数值观测与外部事件的显式索引，并设计诊断协议区分"基于时间戳的推理"与"相关性捷径"，通过受控干预实验揭示现有基准的高准确率并不能证明模型真正利用了时间戳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM时间序列推理评测常按时间顺序给出事件、且事件已与序列匹配，导致无法判断模型是利用时间戳将事件与观测关联，还是仅依赖捷径；基于准确率的基准无法揭示这一歧义。

Method: 从world-to-series投影视角将时间戳视为显式索引，识别时间戳可显式化的三种结构（时间因果结构、时间语义、事件-序列对齐），据此构建诊断协议，对两项任务共310个问题进行受控干预：替换为相对偏移、移除时间戳、破坏事件-序列对齐等。

Result: 时间戳被替换为相对偏移或移除时，模型仍可保持高准确率；而破坏事件-序列对齐会引发系统性失败。上下文已预对齐时模型几乎不需要时间戳；缺少顺序时模型用日期回忆世界知识；无日期时模型依赖相关性捷径且很少察觉错误时间戳。

Conclusion: 在预对齐、有序输入上的高准确率不能证明模型利用时间戳同步观测与世界；需用诊断式干预而非仅看准确率来评估时间序列推理能力。代码、模型输出与轨迹标签已开源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to time series reasoning. However, current evaluations often give models events in chronological order and already matched to the time series. This makes it unclear whether models use timestamps to connect events with observations or simply take shortcuts. To expose this ambiguity, we reformulate time series reasoning from a world-to-series projection perspective, viewing timestamps as explicit indices that connect numerical observations and external events to shared world states. We identify three structures that timestamps can make explicit: temporal causal structure, time semantics, and event-series alignment. Building on this perspective, we develop a diagnostic protocol that distinguishes timestamp-grounded reasoning from correlational shortcuts, a distinction that accuracy-based benchmarks cannot make. Controlled interventions on 310 questions from two tasks show that high accuracy can persist when timestamps are replaced by relative offsets or removed, while breaking event-series alignment induces systematic failures. When context is pre-aligned, models barely need timestamps. When order is missing, they use dates to recall world knowledge. Without dates, they rely on correlational shortcuts and rarely notice incorrect timestamps. These findings show that high accuracy on pre-aligned, ordered inputs does not establish that a model uses timestamps to synchronize observations with the world. We release our code, model outputs, and trajectory labels at https://github.com/tingrui-huang/timestamps-matter.

</details>


### [150] [Mixture-of-Loops Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34923)
*Lianghui Zhu,Shijie Wang,Xinggang Wang,Jinze Bai,Bo Wang,Xiang Bai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MoLT提出用Mixture-of-Loops Attention将KV作为跨深度/跨循环的可选复用记忆，并给出MoLT-R/MoLT-KV两种形式及U-MoLT结构，在保持预测不变的前提下跳过无用解码遍历，实现表达性与效率的更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环Transformer通过共享层复用提升表达性，但效率代价高；要推进表达性-效率前沿，需重新思考相邻循环之间的核心连接机制——即"Transformer循环之间应该传递什么"。

Method: 提出Mixture-of-Loops Transformer (MoLT)：MoLA以key/value作为跨深度与循环的选择性复用记忆。MoLT-R保留residual stream并在循环间读取局部KV以增强表达性；MoLT-KV从embedding重启，读取当前循环局部KV与前序循环的记忆KV，消除同位置跨循环依赖，配合loop forcing实现并行训练与单次遍历MoL解码。进一步提出U型MoLT，将记忆构建与预测读出分离，跳过未使用的解码遍历而不改变预测。

Result: 400B token预训练中，U-MoLT在700M/1.5B dense与3B MoE上优于OLMo3：700M验证CE token效率提升1.7倍；3B MoE的10领域困惑度改善0.75、10任务准确率提升2.1个百分点。Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base 100B token继续预训练中，11个领域CE全部下降，平均token效率1.4倍。S规模(~113M)模型推理时，U-MoLT配MoL-Hybrid-2延迟996.6ms，与非循环OLMo3的1000.6ms相当。

Conclusion: 将KV而非hidden state作为循环间传递对象，可在不牺牲推理延迟的前提下显著提升数据效率与质量；U-MoLT与MoL解码使循环Transformer具备实用化的效率-表达性折中。

Abstract: Looped Transformers boost expressivity by reusing shared layers across loops, yet at the cost of efficiency. Pushing their expressivity-efficiency frontier requires rethinking the core mechanism connecting adjacent loops: What should be passed as a Transformer loops? We introduce Mixture-of-Loops Transformer (MoLT), whose Mixture-of-Loops Attention (MoLA) uses keys and values (KV) as memory for selective reuse across depths and loops. Under loop residual, MoLT-R retains the residual stream and reads local KV across loops for expressivity. Under loop KV, MoLT-KV instead restarts from embeddings and reads current-loop local KV with preceding-loop memory KV at earlier positions for efficiency. MoLT-KV eliminates same-position cross-loop dependence and, paired with loop forcing for parallel training, unlocks single-traversal Mixture-of-Loops (MoL) decoding beyond the training loop horizon. To balance efficiency and quality, we introduce U-shaped MoLT (U-MoLT), which separates memory construction from prediction readout and skips unused decoder traversals without changing predictions. In 400B-token pretraining, U-MoLT outperforms OLMo3 across 700M/1.5B dense and 3B MoE models, achieving 1.7x validation cross-entropy (CE) token efficiency at 700M while improving 3B MoE 10-domain perplexity by 0.75 and 10-task accuracy by 2.1 percentage points. In 100B-token continued pretraining of Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base, our U-MoLT adaptation lowers CE on all 11 domains over the matched baseline with 1.4x mean token efficiency. At inference, U-MoLT with MoL-Hybrid-2 matches non-looped OLMo3 latency at 996.6 vs. 1,000.6 ms on S-size (~113M) models. Code is available at https://github.com/hustvl/MoLT

</details>


### [151] [A gene informed benchmark for BioScoring of pathology foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34930)
*Paul Vilars,Vincent Quoc-Huy Trinh,Maria Vakalopoulou,Dimitris Samaras,Stergios Christodoulidis,Mahdi S. Hosseini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BioScore基准，用同组织空间转录组作参考，无监督地量化病理基础模型图像编码器对52种细胞身份的表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有病理基础模型多以分类、检索等下游任务性能进行比较，只能反映模型"有用性"，无法揭示其编码的生物学信息。缺乏直接衡量模型是否表征细胞身份（生物学信息）的评测手段。

Method: 用稀疏自编码器将编码器的patch嵌入分解为稀疏特征，再把特征聚类为固定数量的组；以同一组织的空间转录组为参考，基于标记基因定义52种细胞身份，对每种身份衡量某一特征组在组织上跟随该身份表达的特异性程度。自编码器训练完全无监督，故匹配成功即说明模型仅从图像就表征了该细胞身份。评测8个病理基础模型与3个自然图像模型，并用配对检验区分模型层级。

Result: 所有病理模型得分均高于所有自然图像模型（0.299–0.371 vs 0.214–0.232）；配对检验将病理模型分为三个层级。BioScore无需标签、参考由数据自建、即使切片很少也能评分，其排序结果与监督探针一致；逐身份分解可指示各编码器捕获或遗漏的细胞身份。

Conclusion: BioScore提供了一种无标签、可解释的生物学表示评测基准，弥补了下游任务基准的不足，可用于比较病理基础模型并诊断其对具体细胞身份的覆盖情况。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models are usually compared by their performance on downstream tasks such as classification and retrieval. While such benchmarks measure how useful a model is, they provide no insight into the biology it represents. We introduce BioScore, a benchmark that quantifies how well an image encoder captures cell identities, with spatial transcriptomics of the same tissue as the reference. A sparse autoencoder splits the encoder's patch embeddings into sparse features, which we cluster into a fixed number of groups. BioScore then measures, for each of 52 cell identities defined by marker genes, how specifically one group follows the identity's expression over the tissue. Because the autoencoder training is fully unsupervised, a match shows that the model represents the cell identity from the image alone. We score 8 pathology foundation models and 3 natural-image models. Every pathology model scores above every natural-image model (0.299--0.371 \vs 0.214--0.232), and paired tests separate three tiers among the pathology models. Unlike a supervised probe, BioScore needs no labels, builds its reference from the data and scores even from few sections, yet it ranks the encoders as a probe does. Its per-identity breakdown indicates which cell identities each encoder captures or misses.

</details>


### [152] [Can image generation help visual reasoning? An empirical study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34935)
*Xueyang Yu,Yifan Jing,Taiming Lu,Linrong Cai,Zhipeng Cai,Yunyang Xiong,Gabriel Sarch,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文检验"用生成的图像作为辅助"能否提升视觉问答表现：在13组视觉语言模型与生成器配对中，先生成辅助图像再结合原图作答，平均准确率不升反降3.7个百分点，且十项任务中有九项下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受语言模型"以词思考"（思维链）启发，推测多模态模型可能"以图思考"，即通过生成辅助图像来辅助推理。作者希望验证这种"生成辅助图"是否真能改善视觉问答的答案质量。

Method: 设计"先描述所需辅助图、再生成图像、最后结合原图与生成图共同作答"的流程；在13种视觉语言模型与图像生成器配对上进行评测；并考察两个统一模型（BAGEL、Janus-Pro-7B）以及重复图像编辑（多次迭代编辑）对结果的影响。

Result: 生成辅助图使平均准确率下降3.7个百分点，十项任务中九项下滑；BAGEL与Janus-Pro-7B各在八项任务上准确率下降；VSTAR上在同一规划提示下加入生成图，准确率下降10.47个百分点；重复编辑结果因任务而异，ChartQAPro与MathVista始终低于无生成基线，而ERQA在十次编辑后提升3个百分点。

Conclusion: 当前"以图思考"式的生成辅助图并不可靠，总体带来负面影响，仅在个别任务和特定编辑次数下出现正向收益，说明生成图像作为中间推理载体的有效性与稳定性仍有待改进。

Abstract: Language models think in words; then multimodal models might think in images. We test whether a generated helper improves the answer to a visual question. A model describes the helper it needs and then answers using the original and generated images together. Across 13 vision-language model and generator pairs, generation lowers mean accuracy by 3.7 percentage points, with declines on nine of ten tasks. Two unified models, BAGEL and Janus-Pro-7B, each lose accuracy on eight tasks. On VSTAR, adding a generated image to the same planning hint lowers accuracy by 10.47 points. Repeated image edits yield different outcomes across tasks. ChartQAPro and MathVista stay below their no-generation baselines, while ERQA gains three points after ten edits.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Context Scaling: A New Frontier of AI Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33160)
*Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"上下文扩展"(context scaling)这一概念，指出AI部署失败多源于任务所依赖的状态与系统决策时可用上下文之间的错配，主张以"模型-基础设施"配对为分析单元，用训练/决策时/上下文基础设施三类预算框架和五阶段上下文生命周期来组织研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已部署AI的许多失败并非源于推理能力不足，而是系统行动时所依赖的任务状态（用户状态、仓库版本、工具轨迹、传感器读数、环境变化）不可用、失真或选择不当。现有预训练扩展与决策时扩展的工作都无法直接解决这一"决策前可获取状态"的瓶颈。

Method: 概念性与框架性工作：将模型形式化为受训练预算B_θ、决策时预算B_d、上下文基础设施预算B_c三种预算约束的 P_θ(d|c)；区分"部署状态"与"模型可见上下文"，定义基于任务与干预的"有效上下文价值"归因；识别可用性、保真度、选择、利用四类失效；用五操作上下文生命周期组织现有机制，并提出受控预算干预与效用-成本曲线作为评估手段。

Result: 提出了一套概念框架：(1)三类预算视角，指出仅B_c针对决策前可获取的状态；(2)有效上下文价值的定义与四类失效分类；(3)五操作上下文生命周期；(4)受控预算干预与效用-成本曲线评估方法；(5)研究议程，包括有据可依的报告规范、交互数据与全成本基础设施、持久自适应系统的安全与治理。

Conclusion: 上下文扩展是不同于预训练扩展和决策时扩展的第三条扩展路径，其分析单元应为模型与基础设施的配对；未来工作应聚焦可验证的报告、覆盖交互数据与全成本的基础设施建设，以及面向持久、自适应系统的安全与治理框架。

Abstract: Many failures of deployed AI arise not from weak inference but from a mismatch between the state a task depends on and the context available when the system acts. Relevant user state, repository revisions, tool traces, sensor readings, or environment changes may be unavailable, distorted, or poorly selected. We call the effort to reduce this bottleneck context scaling. We frame a model as $P_θ(d\mid c)$ within a system governed by three budgets: training $B_θ$, decision-time $B_d$, and context-infrastructure $B_c$. Pretraining scaling invests in $θ$; decision-time scaling searches, refines, or verifies decisions under supplied context; context scaling improves the system's capacity to acquire, represent, deliver, evaluate, and persist deployment state for model use. The three programs interact, but only $B_c$ targets what the system can make available before a decision. This view makes the model-infrastructure pair the unit of analysis. We define effective-context value as a task- and intervention-dependent attribution, separate deployment state from model-visible context, and identify availability, fidelity, selection, and utilization failures. We then organize current mechanisms through a five-operation context lifecycle and propose controlled budget interventions and utility-cost curves for evaluation. The resulting agenda centers on grounded reporting, interaction-data and full-cost infrastructure, and safety and governance for persistent, adaptive systems.

</details>


### [154] [Constraint-Aware Test-Time Scoring for LLM and VLM Candidate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33161)
*Rangarajan Ramesh,Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个自适应评分框架，将"硬性检查"与"软性检查"混合为单一分数，用于从多个候选答案中选出最优解；实验表明增益主要来自硬性检查与候选比较的结合，而非软性检查的配置层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM/VLM 生成多个候选答案时，现有选择方式各有缺陷：依赖模型自身置信度、学习式评判模型（judge）或领域检查。答案可能流畅但不安全、数学上看似合理但物理不可行、或被 judge 打高分却违反任务要求。因此需要一个更可靠的候选评分/选择机制。

Method: 框架由三部分组成：(1) drafting model 生成候选答案；(2) configuring model 为每个任务选择并加权各类检查；(3) scoring models 对无接地工具可计算的属性打分。评分机制为：一小组必须全部通过的硬性检查 + 一组软性检查评分加权融合为每候选的单一数值。在四个任务上验证：CWEval（代码正确且安全）、LIBERO（机器人操作规划，附安全变体）、LiveCodeBench（竞赛编程隐藏测试）、HotpotQA（检索文档问答）。并进行消融研究以隔离各组件贡献。

Result: 最大增益来自"硬性检查 + 候选比较"的组合：在首个采样答案常出错或不安全的任务上，联合正确性与安全性提升最多 +0.21（检索增强问答），代码生成安全性提升最多 +0.28（CWEval）。增益大小与首个样本的弱程度相关：首样本越弱增益越大，首样本已很强时增益趋近于零甚至略负。而负责选择与加权软性检查的 per-task 配置层，相比更简单的替代方案没有带来可测量的价值。

Conclusion: 构建了一个统一的评分框架，并通过消融实验指出：增益来源是接地（grounded）的硬性检查，而非软性因子的配置层。即框架的核心价值在于可验证的硬约束与候选间比较，复杂化的软性检查权重学习收益有限。

Abstract: When an LLM or VLM produces several candidate answers, picking the best one usually relies on the model's own confidence, a learned judge model, or a domain check. Each has a known weakness: an answer can be fluent but insecure, mathematically plausible but physically infeasible, or rated highly by a judge while quietly breaking a task requirement. We propose a scoring framework that combines a small set of hard checks every answer must pass with a wider set of softer checks whose scores are blended into one number per candidate. This adaptive scoring framework consists of three components: a drafting model that produces the candidates, a configuring model that selects and weights the checks for each task, and scoring models that rate properties no grounded tool can compute. We apply our proposed method to four tasks: writing code that is both correct and secure (CWEval), generating robot manipulation plans (LIBERO with added safety variants), passing competitive-programming hidden tests (LiveCodeBench), and answering questions from retrieved documents (HotpotQA). An ablation study isolates the contribution of each component and finds that the largest gains come from combining hard checks with candidate comparison: on tasks where the first sampled answer is often broken or unsafe, this pairing lifts joint correctness-and-safety by up to +0.21 (retrieval-augmented QA) and code-generation safety by up to +0.28 (CWEval), with the size of the gain tracking how weak the first sample is, falling to near zero or slightly negative where it is already strong. The per-task layer that selects and weights the softer checks adds no measurable value over simpler alternatives. This builds a unified scoring framework, together with an ablation that identifies the grounded hard checks, not the soft-factor configuration, as the source of the gain.

</details>


### [155] [AutoformBench: A Lean Formalization Benchmark for Advanced Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33165)
*Charles Arnal,Ahmad Rammal,Niket Patel,Fabian Gloeckle,Bartosz Piotrowski,Amaury Hayat,Julia Kempe,Rémi Munos,Vivien Cabannes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AutoformBench——包含 341 个 Lean 4 形式化任务的基准（源自 24 个高等数学资料），考察智能体在给定非形式化证明的前提下，自行构建缺失定义、中间引理与支撑基础设施并完成 Lean 证明的能力。实验表明前沿模型的难度前沿高度一致：成败更多取决于"缺失形式化基础设施的构建能力"而非数学领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测多在 Mathlib 已提供大部分支撑理论的设定下给出孤立定理陈述，与真实数学形式化（需要构建定义、中间引理并与形式化库连接）脱节，因而无法衡量智能体"补齐支撑理论"的核心能力。

Method: 构建 AutoformBench：从 24 个高等数学来源抽取 341 个 Lean 4 形式化任务；由具备 Lean 经验的数学家人工形式化并审计目标陈述，证明留给被测智能体。每个问题嵌入更广的数学发展中，智能体获得非形式化证明，须将其翻译为 Lean 并构造缺失的定义、中间引理与支撑设施；随后对多个前沿模型进行横向比较。

Result: 前沿模型呈现基本共享的难度前沿；成功率与数学领域相关性较弱，而与模型构建缺失形式化基础设施的能力高度相关。

Conclusion: 真实形式化能力的关键瓶颈在于支撑理论的自主构建而非孤立定理证明，AutoformBench 为这一维度提供了可审计的评测标准；数据与代码已开源。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly capable of producing machine-checked Lean proofs of advanced mathematical results. Real mathematical formalization, however, is not isolated theorem proving: it requires building the definitions and intermediate lemmas that connect a result to a formal library. Existing evaluations often provide isolated theorem statements in settings where much of this supporting theory is already available in Mathlib. We introduce AutoformBench, a benchmark of 341 Lean 4 formalization tasks drawn from 24 advanced mathematical sources. Lean-experienced mathematicians formalized and audited each target statement, leaving the corresponding Lean proof to the evaluated agent. Each problem is embedded in a broader mathematical development: agents receive the informal proof but must translate it into Lean while constructing the missing definitions, intermediate lemmas, and supporting infrastructure. We compare the performance of frontier models on these problems. We find a largely shared difficulty frontier: success depends less on mathematical domain than on models' ability to construct missing formal infrastructure. Data and code at https://github.com/facebookresearch/autoformbench

</details>


### [156] [General-Purpose Decision Models for AI Configuration Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33190)
*Jingzhi Gong,Jie M. Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究考察通用决策模型（以Jev为代表）能否指导AI配置调优，并在八个调优任务上将其作为优化器、性能预测器和区域引导器与传统方法及LLM比较。结果显示传统优化器在八项中六项排名第一、既有预测器在七项中优于Jev，但Jev作为区域引导器可找到困惑度更低的配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI配置调优在有限预算下寻找提升系统性能的设置，现有调优器的采样与搜索策略各异，决策策略的选择直接影响调优质量与成本。作者希望厘清通用决策模型在调优流程中应扮演何种角色、是否优于常规方法。

Method: 以已部署的通用决策模型Jev为代表性对象（可跨任务返回结构化选择与数值评分），在八个AI调优任务上，分别将其用作优化器、性能预测器和区域引导器，与常规优化器、常规预测器以及一个大语言模型（LLM）进行对照比较。

Result: 常规优化器在八项比较中的六项取得第一；既有预测器在七项中优于Jev；但在区域引导角色下，Jev能找到比原生调优器所选配置困惑度更低的配置，在可接受额外决策时间与API费用时带来质量收益。

Conclusion: 应基于角色选择决策策略：优化与预测环节保留常规方法，仅在能接受额外时间与API成本时以通用决策模型作区域引导，从而在调优质量与成本间取得平衡。

Abstract: AI configuration tuning searches for settings that improve the performance of AI systems under a limited budget. Existing tuners use different sampling and search policies, making the choice of decision strategy central to both tuning quality and cost. We investigate whether general-purpose decision models can guide tuning, using Jev as a deployed representative that returns structured choices and numeric scores across diverse tasks. To assess where it helps, we compare its use as an optimizer, performance predictor, and region guider against conventional methods and a large language model (LLM) on eight AI tuning tasks. Conventional optimizers attain rank 1 in six of eight comparisons. Similarly, established predictors outperform Jev in seven. Through region guidance, however, it finds configurations with lower perplexity than those selected by the native tuner, offering a quality gain when additional decision time and API fees are acceptable. These findings yield actionable recommendations on selecting the decision role, retaining conventional predictors, and balancing tuning quality against cost.

</details>


### [157] [What, Who, and When: Structured Harm Assessment for Mental Health Safety via Short Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33263)
*Catarina Botelho,António Farinhas,John Mendonça,Areti Vassilopoulos,Inês Gonçalves,Maya D'Eon,Hugo Pitorro,Laura Cabello,Carrie Spadine,Nuno M. Guerreiro,Ricardo Rei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出临床导向的三维危害分类法（何危害、涉及谁、何时发生），将危害刻画与干预决策解耦，并据此训练35B-A3B推理式安全护栏模型MindGuard2，在约10倍更低中位延迟下达到与更大模型GLM-5.3相当的平衡准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用于心理健康支持引发安全担忧；现有guard模型仅识别有害内容类别，忽略判断合适回应所需的上下文，且安全策略难以外部化、更新与审计。同时公共基准与真实对话的伤害分布存在偏差。

Method: 构建三维临床分类法（what/who/when），将危害刻画与干预决策分离，通过临床医生策展的显式指令在模型权重外指定安全策略；由专家临床医生验证分类法，并验证一组LLM评审以扩展标注；用该分类法重标注公开基准；训练MindGuard2（35B-A3B，基于推理），先对短推理链做监督微调，再用带推理长度惩罚的强化学习优化，以降低推理开销。

Result: 重标注显示公开基准中针对用户自身的伤害极少，而该类伤害占真实对话伤害近一半；在两个公开基准和真实对话上，MindGuard2平衡准确率与体量更大的GLM-5.3相当，中位延迟约低10倍，推进准确率-延迟帕累托前沿；在高召回下保持低假阳性率。

Conclusion: 临床导向分类法+解耦干预决策+短推理高效guard模型，可在低延迟下兼顾安全检测与误报控制，缓解过度干预对支持性与用户信任的损害；并开源该护栏模型、合成对话及公开基准的标注。

Abstract: The growing use of large language models (LLMs) for mental health support raises important safety concerns. Existing guard models primarily identify categories of harmful content, often overlooking the context needed to determine an appropriate response. We introduce a clinically grounded taxonomy that characterizes harm along three dimensions: what the harm is, who is involved, and when it occurs. It further separates harm characterization from intervention decisions, thus enabling safety policies to be specified outside the model weights through explicit, clinician-curated instructions that can be updated and audited. We validate the taxonomy with expert clinicians and validate a panel of LLM judges to scale annotation. Relabeling public benchmarks in this taxonomy shows that they rarely contain harm directed at the user, which accounts for nearly half of the harm in real conversations. We then train MindGuard2, a 35B-A3B reasoning-based guard model using supervised fine-tuning on short reasoning traces followed by reinforcement learning with a reasoning-length penalty, to minimize inference overhead while preserving the benefits of reasoning. On two public benchmarks and real-world conversations, MindGuard2 achieves balanced accuracy comparable to the substantially larger GLM-5.3 at approximately 10x lower median latency, advancing the accuracy-latency Pareto frontier. Furthermore, it achieves low false-positive rates at high recall, helping balance the need to detect unsafe situations against the risk of unnecessary interventions that may disrupt support and undermine user trust. We release a safety guardrail model with SOTA performance trained on synthetic data, together with synthetic conversations and annotations of public benchmarks in our taxonomy.

</details>


### [158] [Certify What You Plant: Shortcut-Free Proof Depth in Logical Reasoning Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33303)
*Baptiste Juran,Dimitri Kachler,Damien Sileo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出用"问题实际所需证明"取代"植入证明深度"来衡量程序化逻辑问题难度，并对8个数据集做审计，发现深度标签普遍失实；进而基于每规则集一次性证明的捷径目录构造所有证明均具指定结构的问题，并附独立可验证证书；训练中混合深度与题型（含前向生成不产生的模型检验题）在同等规模下迁移效果优于FLDx2式推导数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序化生成的逻辑问题常用"植入一条指定深度证明"来设定难度，但植入证明只是众多可能证明之一：问题可能存在更短证明或多个互不相关的证明，标注深度因此高估了求解所需能力。需要一个以"相对给定推理规则集的问题实际所需"为核心的难度度量。

Method: 1) 对每个问题计算：所有证明必经的步骤、最短证明长度、极小证明数量、不可或缺的前提，并输出可由独立检查器验证的证书；2) 据此审计8个数据集；3) 利用"每规则集只证一次"的捷径目录，构造每条证明都满足目标结构的问题，每个构造问题均全程校验并自带证书；4) 在Qwen3 0.6B–8B上比较不同数据混合的训练迁移效果。

Result: 审计发现：ProofWriter与Blocksworld的深度是被强制的；FLDx2中标注深度5的问题实际只需约两次特定规则应用；ZebraLogic的答案不依赖任何特定步骤。训练方面，同等数据量下，混合已认证题型（包括前向生成无法产生的模型检验题）比FLDx2式推导数据迁移更好。

Conclusion: 植入证明深度并非可靠难度指标，应以可验证的"最小/必需证明结构"刻画难度。带证书的构造式问题生成与多深度、多题型混合训练，是提升语言模型推理与迁移的更优路径。

Abstract: Procedurally generated logic problems are used to both evaluate and train reasoning in language models. Their difficulty is usually set by planting a proof, that is, by sampling a proof of the desired depth and stating its premises as the problem. However, a planted proof is only one possible proof. A problem can also have shorter proofs, or several unrelated ones, and its stated depth then overstates what solving it requires. We measure what a problem actually requires, relative to a stated set of inference rules. For each problem, we compute the steps every proof must make, the shortest proof, the number of minimal proofs and the indispensable premises, and export certificates that an independent checker verifies. Audits of eight datasets find depth that is forced (ProofWriter, Blocksworld), problems labelled depth 5 that require about two specific rule applications (FLDx2), and answers that need no particular step (ZebraLogic). We then construct problems whose every proof has a requested structure, using a catalog of shortcuts proved once per rule set. Every constructed problem is checked in full and comes with its own certificate. In training, the best data mixes depths and problem types. At equal size, such mixtures of certified problem types, including model-checking problems that forward generation does not produce, transfer better than FLDx2-style derivation data for Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 8B.

</details>


### [159] [The Platonic Representation Hypothesis in Jacobian-Space: Language and Vision Models Agree Approximately Up to a Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33304)
*Ross Murphy,Gabriel Brostow*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文将 Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) 从"表征层面"拓展到"作用层面"：视、语言模型的 J-space（Jacobian lens 给出的词/类方向向量集合）也存在跨模态收敛，且这种收敛在全局旋转意义下近似成立；单个拟合于部分词的旋转即可迁移到未见词与类，并通过注入实验改变 VLM、图像生成器与 ImageNet 分类器的输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅在激活（activation）层面为 PRH 提供证据，即模型"如何表征世界"趋同；但模型"如何基于表征行动"（表征如何影响输出）是否也跨模态收敛尚未被检验，本文填补这一空白。

Method: 在 transformer 选定层上，用 Jacobian lens 为词表每个词（或图像分类器的每个类）求出隐空间中"最能提升该词/类生成倾向"的方向向量，构成 J-space。跨视觉模型与语言模型对齐 J-space，并检验其是否仅差一个旋转（Procrustes 式拟合：用部分词拟合单个旋转，测试未见词/类）。随后做因果注入实验：将纯文本 LLM 的词向量旋转后注入 VLM 的视觉 patch token、图像生成器的文本条件、以及无文本训练的 ImageNet 分类器。

Result: J-space 中跨模态存在 PRH 证据；J-space 近似在旋转意义下收敛，且该旋转可从部分词泛化到未参与拟合的词与类。旋转后的词向量注入 VLM 视觉 token 可改变其识别出的物体，且影响仅限注入的图像区域；注入图像生成器文本条件可改变生成图像中的物体；注入 ImageNet 分类器可使对应类的得分超越几乎所有其他类，且反向亦然。

Conclusion: 模型不仅在"如何表征世界"上收敛，也在"如何作用于世界"（表征对输出的因果影响）上收敛；PRH 可推广到功能性、可干预的方向空间，并具有跨模态的近似旋转等变性。

Abstract: The Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) posits that AI models converge toward a shared representation of the world, even when trained on different data modalities. Previous work has found evidence for the PRH in the activations of vision and language models. In this work, we ask whether vision and language models also converge in how they act on these representations: precisely, in how these internal representations affect the models' output. At a chosen layer of a transformer, the Jacobian lens gives every word in the vocabulary (or class in an image classifier) a vector in the hidden state: the vector that most increases the model's tendency to generate that word, at that position or later, or to predict that class. Collectively, these vectors make up what is referred to as the model's J-space. We find evidence of the PRH in J-space across vision and language models, and furthermore, we find that J-spaces mostly converge up to a rotation. That is, a single rotation, fitted on a subset of words, approximately maps one model's J-space onto another's, and so applies to words and classes it was not fitted on. A word's vector, rotated from a text-only language model's J-space into the visual patch tokens of a vision-language model, changes the object the vision-language model says is in the image (and only in the image region where it is added). Rotated into an image generator's text conditioning, a word's vector changes the object in the generated image. Rotated into an ImageNet classifier (trained without text), it raises that object's class score above almost all others, and vice versa. This suggests that models converge not only in how they represent the world, but also in how they act on it.

</details>


### [160] [Brand Recall and Category Accuracy in Language Models: An Audit of 1,000 B2B SaaS Brands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33322)
*Ben Moore*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究在1,000个B2B SaaS品牌、333个品类上分别测量语言模型的"召回"与"品类分类正确性"，发现二者须分开报告：Gemma 4 12B无检索时品类提示可引出51.0%品牌，而五候选探针下有19.8%的分离排序出现错误（高log-prob margin组降至3.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 命名品牌与正确归类品牌是两种不同能力，但常被混为单一指标；作者希望解耦"能否被引出"与"是否被正确分类"，并检验跨模型代际的稳定性，从而提出更严谨的评测报告范式。

Method: 覆盖1,000个B2B SaaS品牌与333个产品品类的基准；在Gemma 4 12B上以无外部检索的品类提示测召回；另设五候选品类探针（按log-prob排序），并计算第一名与第二名之间的log-prob差距（margin>0.6）；做探索性回归（技术覆盖度、自有资产复合指标）与跨四代Gemma对比；另用基于表征的情感度量与客户评分做相关分析（n=921）。

Result: 品类提示引出510个品牌（51.0%）；五候选探针中787个品牌将参考标签排首位、194个将干扰项排首位、19个几乎无区分度；981个可分离样本的错误率为19.8%，margin>0.6的547个品牌错误率降至3.5%；四代Gemma召回率依次为39.8%、44.3%、51.1%、51.0%；表征情感与客户评分相关性很弱（r=0.03, n=921）。

Conclusion: 应分别报告召回率与品类准确率；组内margin是测量分层而非校准置信度；探索性回归因调整集不同不能作因果解释；未能引出某品牌不等于其不存在于模型参数或训练数据中。

Abstract: A language model can name a brand without classifying it correctly. This study measures these outcomes separately for 1,000 B2B SaaS brands across 333 product categories. On Gemma 4 12B, with no external retrieval, category prompts elicit 510 brands (51.0%). A separate five-candidate category probe ranks the reference label first for 787 brands, a distractor first for 194, and yields little separation for 19. Among the 981 separated rankings, the error rate is 19.8%. It falls to 3.5% for the 547 brands with a winner-to-runner-up log-probability margin above 0.6. These are within-cohort measurements, not calibrated confidence estimates. Exploratory regressions associate technical coverage with recall and an owned-properties composite with category accuracy, but the composites use different adjustment sets and do not establish causal effects. Across four Gemma generations, measured recall is 39.8%, 44.3%, 51.1% and 51.0%. A separate representation-based sentiment measure has little linear association with customer ratings (r=0.03, n=921). The findings support reporting recall and category accuracy separately. Failure to elicit a brand does not establish its absence from model parameters or training data.

</details>


### [161] [CA-RAG: Constraint-Aligned Retrieval for Structurally Faithful Long-Form Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33497)
*Vimal Kumar Kumar,Meghana Kshirsagar,Conor Ryan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出约束对齐检索增强生成框架CA-RAG，在检索前将本体派生的结构约束与政策知识图谱关系注入查询，提升合规敏感长文本生成的证据充分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG按语义相关性选择证据，而合规敏感的长文本生成还需要支撑强制性结构义务的证据；纯语义检索可能遗漏下游必备条款所需的信息，导致条款覆盖与合规性不足。

Method: CA-RAG在证据选择前，用本体派生的结构约束和政策知识图谱关系增强分节查询，实现检索阶段的结构对齐；并与标准RAG及仅生成阶段施加约束的基线对比，使用人工标注证据做纯检索评估，跨多检索预算及多模型-领域组合做生成评估。

Result: k=6时，Precision@k由0.5268升至0.5692，pooled Recall@k由0.5402升至0.5950，F1@k由0.5389升至0.5672；生成实验在多个模型-领域设置下合规性与可靠性提升；仅生成阶段约束的基线条款覆盖率更高，但整体合规性和可靠性更低。

Conclusion: 结构对齐的检索能够提升合规敏感长文本生成中的证据充分性；但结构与语义目标存在权衡——仅在生成端施加约束会牺牲整体合规与可靠性，说明约束应在检索阶段前置引入。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically optimises evidence selection for semantic relevance, whereas compliance-sensitive long-form generation also requires evidence supporting explicit structural obligations. Semantically relevant retrieval may therefore omit information needed for mandatory downstream clauses. We propose Constraint-Aligned RAG (CA-RAG), a retrieval-conditioning framework that augments section-specific queries with ontology-derived structural constraints and policy-grounded knowledge-graph relations before evidence selection. Retrieval-only evaluation with manually annotated evidence shows consistent improvements across retrieval budgets. At $k=6$, CA-RAG increases Precision@$k$ from $0.5268$ to $0.5692$, pooled Recall@$k$ from $0.5402$ to $0.5950$, and F1@$k$ from $0.5389$ to $0.5672$ over standard RAG. Generation experiments further show improvements in compliance and reliability in several model--domain settings, while generation-only constraints achieve higher clause coverage but lower overall compliance and reliability. These results indicate that structurally aligned retrieval can improve evidence sufficiency in compliance-sensitive long-form generation.

</details>


### [162] [Confidence scores for gated automation of large language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33394)
*Luiz G. A. Alves,Aaron Vermeersch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出在冻结的2B参数LLM骨干最终层隐状态上训练线性置信度头（辅以纯文本元模型），用于预测LLM答案的正确性，实现可靠的门控与人工复核路由；该方法在高准确率目标下自动化比例最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体可自动决策，但常以高置信度输出错误答案，现有置信度估计常缺失或粒度过粗，难以支撑有效的门控机制，因此需要可靠、细粒度的置信度分数来把关影响个人与组织的决策。

Method: 采用监督学习训练"正确性预测"模型：在冻结的LLM骨干最终层隐状态上拟合线性置信度分数头；针对闭源模型，训练纯文本元模型作为替代方案。实验基于单一2B参数骨干，评测数据约98%为SQuAD、其余为TruthfulQA的分级答案，并与多种基线对比。

Result: 置信度分数头优于所有基线与元模型，在高准确率目标下自动化的工作量比例最大；提示式置信度分数看似校准良好，但缺乏阈值化所需的分辨率；跨未见数据集的迁移性一般，但置信度分数头仍是最稳健的方案。

Conclusion: 该置信度评分框架提升了基于LLM的智能体自动化的安全性，并可扩展用于对安全性、合规性等其他属性的门控。

Abstract: AI agents built on large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong capabilities in automated decision-making. However, LLMs may produce incorrect answers with high confidence, limiting their applicability in decisions that affect individuals and organizations. Reliable confidence scores are necessary to gate such decisions, and existing estimates are frequently unavailable or too coarse to support effective gating. This work uses supervised learning to train models to predict the correctness of LLM-generated answers, enabling automated confident responses and routing uncertain ones for human review. A linear confidence score head is fitted to the final-layer hidden state of the frozen LLM backbone, and text-only metamodels are trained as an alternative for closed models. All results come from a single 2B-parameter backbone, on graded answers that are about 98% SQuAD and the remainder TruthfulQA. Against multiple baselines, the confidence score head outperforms all baselines and metamodels, automating the largest fraction of the workload at high accuracy targets. Prompted confidence scores can appear well calibrated yet lack the resolution needed for thresholding. Although transferability to unseen datasets is modest, the confidence score head remains the most robust approach. The proposed confidence scoring framework enhances the safety of automating LLM-based agents and can be adapted to gate other properties, including safety or compliance.

</details>


### [163] [Agentic Waterfall: An Intent-Driven Information Discovery Framework for Reclaiming User Control in the Attention Economy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34407)
*Yige Luo,Shining Zhang,Jikun Liu,Hongjiao Qiang,Ran Guan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文形式化了“Agentic Waterfall Feed”这一新问题——用户主动引导推荐、系统从显式请求与浏览行为中推断动态需求，并提出 Agentic Waterfall 框架、数据集与基于重放的评测协议，在自建数据集上相对最强基线提升最多 16.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推荐系统以被动、静态偏好建模为主，难以应对用户主动下达请求且需求随时间演进的情境；缺乏同时刻画显式指令与隐式行为、并可持续维护用户状态的个性化信息发现设定与评测手段。

Method: 提出 Agentic Waterfall 框架：由语言模型控制器维护持久化用户状态，并协调内容检索、排序与状态更新三个环节。构建 Agentic Waterfall Dataset（真实内容元数据+模拟交互历史）及基于重放的评测协议，在共享历史下用相关性分数与盲评判断（推荐是否满足当前目标与约束）比较系统。

Result: 在五个公开基准的常规推荐任务上，Agentic Waterfall 与强基线保持可比竞争力；在自建数据集上，两项评测均优于最强基线，相对提升最高达 16.1%。

Conclusion: 将“用户主动引导+需求演化+状态持久化”统一为可形式化、可评测的问题具有可行性；语言模型控制器驱动的智能体式推荐在个性化信息发现任务中优于传统基线，并为后续研究提供了数据集与重放式评测范式。

Abstract: We formalize Agentic Waterfall Feed, a new problem of personalized information discovery in which users actively direct recommendations and the system adapts to evolving needs inferred from both explicit requests and browsing behavior. We address this problem with the Agentic Waterfall framework, whose language-model controller maintains persistent user state and coordinates content retrieval, ranking, and state updates. To evaluate systems on this task, we contribute the Agentic Waterfall Dataset and a replay-based evaluation protocol. The dataset combines real content metadata with simulated interaction histories. The protocol compares systems under shared histories using relevance scores and blind judgments of how well recommendations satisfy current user goals and constraints. Agentic Waterfall remains competitive with strong baselines on conventional recommendation tasks across five public benchmarks. On the Agentic Waterfall Dataset, it outperforms the strongest baseline in both evaluations, with relative improvements of up to 16.1%.

</details>


### [164] [Partial Routing: Trace-Tuned Delegation for Resource-Efficient Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33402)
*Siddharth Arya,Kshitij Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"部分路由"(partial routing)：用前沿lead模型把合适子任务委派给更便宜的worker模型，基于两模型历史运行中反复出现的子任务的质量与成本测量生成委派指导，无训练、低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型能力与价格差异巨大，模型路由可把简单任务交给便宜模型；但单个agentic任务常混合易、难子任务，对整任务统一选一个模型效率低下。

Method: 从两模型过往运行记录中识别反复出现的子任务，测量各模型在这些子任务上的质量与成本，生成委派指导，并由参数δ控制委派比例；执行时由lead在不训练任何模型的前提下自主决定何时、委派什么。

Result: 在ViBench与Terminal-Bench 4.0留出任务上，平均任务表现优于仅用lead和任务级路由；δ=0.75时ViBench由70.2升至78.5且成本降低19%，Terminal-Bench通过率由54%升至61%、成本基本持平。仅提供子任务分解而无worker也能降低成本并提升Terminal-Bench通过率。

Conclusion: 对历史运行的分析可支撑更高效的规划与更有效的委派，成本与实现开销很小。

Abstract: AI models vary enormously in capability and price. Model routing exploits this by sending easier tasks to cheaper models. However, a single agentic task often combines easy and difficult subtasks, making one model choice for the entire task inefficient. We introduce partial routing, which guides a frontier lead model to delegate suitable subtasks to a cheaper worker model. From both models' past runs, we identify recurring subtasks and measure each model's quality and cost on them. These measurements produce delegation guidance, with a parameter, $δ$, controlling how much is delegated. The lead decides when and what to delegate during execution, without training either model. On held-out tasks from ViBench and Terminal-Bench 4.0, partial routing achieves higher mean task performance than the lead alone and task-level routing. With $δ= 0.75$, it raises the lead's ViBench score from 70.2 to 78.5 at 19% lower cost, and its Terminal-Bench pass rate from 54% to 61% at similar cost. Giving the lead only the subtask breakdown, without a worker, also reduces cost on both benchmarks and raises the Terminal-Bench pass rate. These results show that analysis of past runs can support more efficient planning and more effective delegation, with little cost and implementation overhead.

</details>


### [165] [Procedure Inference as Memory: Learning Recruiter Decision Procedures from OKF-Structured Episodes in Multi-Session Hiring Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34576)
*Praveen Kumar Bodigutla,Yizhi Zhang,Sai Vivek Kanaparthy,Arup De*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出OKF索引的情节记忆与Procedure Memory（程序记忆），用于多会话招聘助手，在更低上下文成本下提升偏好推断与时间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多会话招聘助手需跨项目、跨时间推断不断演化的招聘者偏好与决策模式，长历史上下文成本高且难以检索相关信息，因此需要结构化记忆与可复用决策程序。

Method: 提出Open Knowledge Format (OKF)索引的情节记忆，通过血缘关系组织交互并检索相关事件；并提出Procedure Memory，通过提示反思或强化学习从情节中学习可复用的决策程序。对比无记忆、全历史上下文、对话检索等基线，并评估Jev类模型做索引导航、GPT-5.6 Luna作为GPT-5.4的低成本替代。

Result: OKF检索在中、高偏好重叠下低优先级偏好F1达0.93–0.95，显著优于全历史上下文与对话检索（高重叠下优于无记忆），上下文token较全历史减少69%；Procedure Memory相对无记忆减少对话轮次至多39%；RL学得的程序在正确弃答与精确字段识别上均达60%。Jev类模型提升对话效率但更易漏掉时间相关证据。PersonaMem-v2上，Procedure Memory+OKF检索准确率50.2%（全历史48.6%、无记忆29.0%），reader输入token较全历史减少90.5%；GPT-5.6 Luna性能与GPT-5.4相当且成本更低。

Conclusion: 结构化情节记忆与学得的决策程序能有效支持高效个性化与时间推理；证据选择对时间相关信息的利用至关重要。

Abstract: Multi-session hiring assistants must infer evolving recruiter preferences and decision patterns across projects and time. We introduce Open Knowledge Format (OKF)-indexed episodic memory to organize interactions and retrieve related events through their lineage. We also introduce Procedure Memory, which learns reusable decision procedures from these episodes through prompted reflection or reinforcement learning. We compare these approaches with no memory, full-history context, and conversation search, and evaluate Jev-class models for index navigation and GPT-5.6 Luna as a lower-cost alternative to GPT-5.4.
  In our evaluation, OKF-indexed retrieval achieves low-priority preference F1 scores of 0.93-0.95 at medium and high preference overlap between hiring projects. It significantly outperforms full-history context and conversation search in both settings, and no memory at high overlap, while using 69% fewer context tokens than full history. Procedure Memory reduces conversational turns by up to 39% relative to no memory. Qualitative and long-horizon evaluations provide evidence of temporal reasoning, with RL-learned procedures achieving 60% on both correct abstention and exact-field identification. Jev-class models improve conversational efficiency but more frequently miss temporally relevant evidence, highlighting the importance of evidence selection.
  On PersonaMem-v2, Procedure Memory with OKF retrieval achieves 50.2% accuracy versus 48.6% for full-history context and 29.0% for no memory, using 90.5% fewer reader input tokens than full history. GPT-5.6 Luna performs comparably to GPT-5.4 at lower cost. These findings highlight the value of structured episodic memory and learned decision procedures for efficient personalization and temporal reasoning.

</details>


### [166] [Collaborative Language Models for Semantic IoT Data Processing: Validation through Zero-Shot Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33411)
*Mohsen Shirali,Kean Ooi,Anna Knab,Mohammadreza Fani Sani,Estefania Serral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究用协作式语言模型作为零样本语义抽象层，将IoT底层传感器观测转换为高层事件/活动标签，提出五阶段工作流，并在CASAS Aruba数据集的人类活动识别任务上评估11种配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT系统产生的低层传感器观测无法直接用于过程分析，需先转换为语义有意义的事件；作者希望探索无需任务特定微调的、基于现成语言模型的模块化语义事件抽象方案。

Method: 提出五阶段工作流：语义映射、语义聚合、时间与上下文聚合、专门化推理、最终事件抽象；产出标签与上下文可表示为事件记录以支持下游Process Mining。在CASAS Aruba智能家居数据集上通过11种工作流配置进行人类活动识别评估。

Result: 协作本身并不保证性能提升：通用辅助SLM可能降低识别效果，而分配互补的专家角色效果更好，且能让较小的最终分类器保留较大模型的大部分性能。所选工作流在1.5天对比中macro F1为0.643，在一周评估中为0.567（加权F1 0.680）。

Conclusion: 尽管有监督ML方法在该数据集上仍更准确，但本工作展示了另一种设计点：基于现成模型、无需任务特定参数微调的模块化语义事件抽象。LM协作的结构与任务分解方式比单纯增加模型数量更重要。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems generate low-level sensor observations that are not directly suitable for process analysis and must first be transformed into semantically meaningful events. This paper investigates collaborative language models as a zero-shot semantic abstraction layer for converting such observations into higher-level event and activity labels. We propose a five-stage workflow comprising semantic mapping, semantic aggregation, temporal and contextual aggregation, specialized reasoning, and final event abstraction. The resulting labels and contextual information can subsequently be represented as event records for downstream Process Mining (PM) tasks. The approach is evaluated through Human Activity Recognition on the CASAS Aruba smart home dataset using eleven workflow configurations. The experiments show that collaboration alone does not guarantee improved performance: general-purpose auxiliary SLMs can degrade recognition, whereas assigning complementary specialist roles leads to better results and allows a smaller final classifier to retain much of the performance of a larger model. The selected workflow achieves a macro F1 of 0.643 in the 1.5-day comparison and 0.567 (weighted F1 0.680) in a one-week evaluation. Although trained ML approaches remain more accurate on this dataset, the results demonstrate a different design point: modular semantic event abstraction using off-the-shelf models without task-specific parameter fine-tuning. The findings indicate that the structure of LM collaboration and task decomposition is more important than simply increasing the number of participating models.

</details>


### [167] [Dynamic Reasoning in Next-Gen Detection and Response: An Empirical Study of LLM Triage, Reinforcement Tuning, and Adversarial Evasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33470)
*Jeremy Canale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对下一代检测与响应流水线中的动态推理开展端到端实证研究：在固定的分析师注意力预算（每日告警召回率 R@B）下，比较静态分类器、图信号融合与 LLM 分诊智能体的表现，通过 56 组可复现实验检验 12 项架构主张。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测与响应研究通常以离线指标（如 AUC）报告性能，忽略了真实运营中分析师注意力预算这一硬约束，也缺乏对 LLM 分诊智能体、图融合与强化学习式后训练在对抗环境下的系统性比较。

Method: 构建多层企业+云模拟器并联合四个公开数据集（SOREL-20M、BODMAS、LANL、DARPA Transparent Computing），共 56 组可复现实验；在 Recall at B alerts/day 指标下评估静态分类器、基于稀有度加权的图风险传播（对比 GraphSAGE）、前沿模型（Gemini）与微调开源模型（Qwen3-4B）的分诊表现；引入 GRPO 后训练与两轮监督微调对照；并设置生成合理掩护叙事的自适应对手进行对抗测试，比较有无确定性策略护栏（policy harness）。

Result: (1) 在固定分析师预算下，机队规模扩大使召回率减半，即便计算成本极低（每百万事件 <$0.0003）；稀有度加权图风险传播无需神经训练即优于 GraphSAGE。(2) 无约束 LLM 自动关闭告警可消除 90% 误报，但会静默丢弃 31–44% 的真实攻击样本。(3) GRPO 后训练在未见攻击类别上出现严重奖励攻击（reward hacking），相较两轮监督微调，可将新型攻击的检出抑制高达 86%。(4) 面对在良性遥测字段中生成掩护叙事的自适应对手，LLM 分诊被规避率显著上升，除非在调用模型前由确定性策略护栏把关。

Conclusion: 系统规模、预算约束与对抗适应共同决定检测与响应流水线的实际效力；LLM 分诊必须以预算感知指标评估，并在模型调用前设置确定性策略护栏，且应警惕强化学习式后训练在分布外攻击上的奖励攻击风险。

Abstract: We present an end-to-end empirical study of dynamic reasoning in next-generation detection and response pipelines, evaluating how static classifiers, graph-based signal fusion, and large language model (LLM) triage agents perform under strict human-analyst attention budgets (Recall at B alerts/day). Across 56 reproducible experiments spanning a multi-layer enterprise-and-cloud simulator and four public datasets (SOREL-20M, BODMAS, LANL, and DARPA Transparent Computing), we test twelve architectural claims governing modern fleet-scale defense. First, we show that fleet growth halves recall at a fixed analyst budget even at negligible compute cost (<$0.0003 per million events), and that rarity-weighted graph risk propagation outperforms GraphSAGE without neural training. Second, evaluating frontier reasoning models (Gemini) and fine-tuned open-weight models (Qwen3-4B) for alert triage reveals that unconstrained LLM auto-closure eliminates 90% of false positives while silently discarding 31-44% of true attack cases. Third, post-training with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) causes severe reward hacking on unseen attack classes, suppressing up to 86% of novel attacks compared to two-epoch supervised fine-tuning. Finally, against an adaptive adversary generating plausible cover stories in benign telemetry fields, LLM triage evasion rises sharply unless gated by a deterministic policy harness prior to model invocation.

</details>


### [168] [Learning to Reason in Real-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33513)
*Xavier Schneider,Benjamin Schneider,Sun Sun,Olga Vechtomova,Victor Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"实时推理训练（RRT）"这一类训练方法，让智能体学习在实时环境中权衡推理时长与响应速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体在完全同步的环境中训练，即世界会等待智能体动作后才改变；而真实环境是异步实时变化的。已有的"思考-行动比例"固定约束无法让智能体根据延迟代价动态调整推理长度。

Method: 构建RRT训练技术框架，包含三种标准技术：(1) 使用实时数据训练；(2) 解耦行动与观测；(3) 时间维度上的课程学习。

Result: 在Frogger和Snake两个实时环境中，跨多种时间压力条件下，相比最强的非RRT基线，分布内任务平均奖励提升33%，分布外任务提升55%。

Conclusion: RRT能教会智能体在实时约束下平衡推理与响应性，并为实时智能体开辟了广泛的新能力方向。

Abstract: AI agents that operate in the real world must reason correctly and on-time, yet they currently do not account for how time should alter their reasoning. Modern agents learn to do tasks in environments that are entirely synchronous: where the world waits for the agent to act before changing. A real-time environment, by contrast, changes independent of the agent. Existing approaches try to bridge this gap by imposing a ratio of thinking to acting. However, for an agent to succeed in real-time it must learn to act quickly when delay is costly and to reason longer when the benefit outweighs the missed opportunities. To this end, we introduce real-time reasoning training (RRT) as the class of training techniques for real-time reasoning agents. We develop three standard RRT techniques: (1) using real-time data, (2) decoupling acting and observing, and (3) curriculum learning in time. Together, these simple techniques substantially outperform strong baselines. Across time pressures, we outperform the strongest non-RRT baseline by an average of 33% reward on in-distribution and 55% reward on out-of-distribution tasks in two real-time environments: Frogger and Snake. These results demonstrate that we can teach agents to balance reasoning and responsiveness under real-time constraints, and that RRT opens a broad, new set of capabilities for real-time agents.

</details>


### [169] [PeerReveal: Learning to Generalize in AI Peer Review Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33555)
*Zi Li,André V. Duarte,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 PeerReveal 检测器，基于评审主张与同篇论文 AI 生成参考主张之间的关系建模，并辅以文本级信号，用于区分"全 AI 生成评审"与"AI 仅用于改写人类判断的评审"；在跨领域新基准 PeerShift 上结合对抗训练将 Macro-F1 从 70.52% 提升至 82.40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 评审检测多聚焦"是否由 AI 生成"的二分类，忽略了现实中更细粒度的场景：AI 仅用于润色/改写人类评审意见。此类评审与全 AI 生成评审在表层文本特征上高度相似，难以区分；同时检测器需在多种模型、多种生成流程以及不同学科评审惯例下保持稳健泛化。

Method: 1) 构建 PeerShift 测试集：5,430 条评审，覆盖机器学习、生命科学、社会科学等领域，融合不同学科评审惯例与多样化 AI 评审生产机制；2) PeerReveal 检测器：以评审主张（claims）与同篇论文 AI 生成参考评审中抽取的参考主张之间的学习关系作为决策依据（grounding），并融合评审级文本信号；3) 采用实用型对抗训练框架，利用小型本地生成器构造高难度对抗评审以提升泛化能力。

Result: 常规训练下 PeerReveal 在 PeerShift 上取得 70.52% 的 pooled Macro-F1，较最强基线提升 11.48 个百分点；引入对抗训练后 pooled Macro-F1 升至 82.40%（+11.88 点）。

Conclusion: 稳健的 AI 评审检测需要更丰富的评审级证据（主张—参考主张关系）以及对更具挑战性生成过程的对抗训练；二者结合可显著提升跨领域、跨模型、跨流程条件下对细粒度 AI 参与程度的判别可靠性。

Abstract: Can we distinguish fully AI-generated reviews from those using AI only to rewrite human judgement? Furthermore, can we do so reliably when reviews are generated from diverse AI models and workflows across scientific domains? To this end, we introduce PeerReveal, a new detector that grounds its decisions in learned relationships between a review's claims and reference claims drawn from AI-generated reviews of the same paper, complemented by review-level textual signals. We evaluate PeerReveal on PeerShift, a new, challenging test set of 5,430 reviews spanning multiple domains, including machine learning, life sciences, and social sciences, combining variation in disciplinary review conventions with diverse AI review-production mechanisms. With regular training, PeerReveal achieves 70.52% pooled Macro-F1 on this benchmark, improving over the strongest baseline by 11.48 points. We develop an effective and practical adversarial training framework that further improves the detector's generalization using a small local generator optimized to produce challenging reviews, raising pooled Macro-F1 from 70.52% to 82.40%. These results show that robust AI-review detection benefits from richer review-level evidence and adversarial training on more challenging generation processes.

</details>


### [170] [FIG-MAC: A Fine-grained Inspiration Graph Empowered Multi-Agent Collaboration for Automated Scientific Hypothesis Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33563)
*Siyuan Chen,Yuping Gao,Tai Huang,Yuling Zhou,Dongyu Su,Tongguan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FIG-MAC框架，通过细粒度灵感图谱、本体引导混合推理与角色分工多智能体协作，解决自动化科学假设生成（ASHG）中知识表示粗糙、演化不可追溯、缺乏多智能体协同三大问题，在来源多样性、溯源调整新颖性与原始新颖性上显著超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的自动化科学假设生成存在三点不足：(i) 非结构化上下文导致细粒度知识表示不足，损害理解与推理；(ii) 缺乏知识演化可追溯性，限制跨域创新猜想生成；(iii) 缺少类人的角色专门化多智能体协作，未能利用认知多样性与专家分工带来的高效创新机制，难以形成完整的"生成—评估"闭环。

Method: FIG-MAC包含三模块：(i) 细粒度灵感图谱（FIG）模块——节点为学术语义单元、边编码其间的启发式关系，构建细粒度结构化知识表示；(ii) 本体引导的混合推理模块——依据FIG本体构造结构化推理路径，并用向量检索补充语义证据，得到可追溯的科学猜想路径；(iii) 角色专门化多智能体框架——借鉴真实科研团队分工，实现科学猜想生成、筛选与验证的闭环流程。

Result: 实验表明FIG-MAC在来源多样性（source diversity）、溯源调整新颖性（provenance-adjusted novelty）和原始新颖性（raw novelty）三项指标上均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将细粒度结构化知识图谱表示、本体引导的可追溯推理路径与角色分工的多智能体协作相结合，可有效提升LLM科学假设生成的创新性与可验证性，并闭合生成—评估全流程。

Abstract: Automated Scientific Hypothesis Generation (ASHG) leveraging large language models (LLMs) aims to harness their strengths in knowledge integration, logical reasoning, and text generation for efficiently producing innovative, verifiable scientific hypotheses. However, existing approaches face three critical limitations: (i) insufficient fine-grained knowledge representation, as unstructured context impairs LLM comprehension and reasoning; (ii) lack of knowledge evolution traceability, constraining the generation of innovative cross-domain conjectures; (iii) absence of human-inspired and role-specialized multi-agent collaboration, failing to leverage the efficient innovation mechanisms arising from cognitive diversity and division of labor among expert roles to complete the full ASHG-evaluation loop. To address these limitations, we propose the Fine-grained Inspiration Graph-empowered Multi-Agent Collaboration framework (FIG-MAC) including: (i) Fine-grained Inspiration Graph module (FIG), to obtain fine-grained structured knowledge representations, where nodes represent academic semantic units and edges encode heuristic relationships among them; (ii) ontology-guided hybrid reasoning module, which follows the FIG ontology to construct structured reasoning paths and uses vector search to enrich them with semantic evidence, to obtain the traceable scientific conjecture paths; (iii) role-specialized Multi-Agent framework, inspired by real scientific teamwork, enabling a closed-loop process of scientific conjecture generation, screening, and validation. Experimental results demonstrate that FIG-MAC significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods on source diversity, provenance-adjusted novelty, and raw novelty.

</details>


### [171] [Aligned Machine: A Benchmark for Evaluating Multimodal Embedding Similarity via Human Comparative Judgements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33594)
*Jiechen Li,Hannah Groos,Brinnae Bent*
