Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Agentic Model Editing (AME)：让 LLM 智能体利用少样本示例与模型内部观测，自动"设计"出一套领域定制的适配程序（含输入变换、编码器编辑、特征计算、分类器拟合），在 CLIP ViT-B/16 上十一个数据集取得最优平均精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本适配方法（提示微调、表征适配、分类器拟合）都依赖预设固定的适配流程，而不同领域所需的视觉判别线索差异很大，固定流程难以通用。因此提出：能否让 LLM 智能体使用少样本示例和模型观测，自行设计适配流程本身。

Method: AME 是一个智能体框架，将输入变换、编码器编辑、特征计算与分类器拟合联合设计为可执行的、领域定制的适配程序。智能体检视图像、类语义、模型参数、中间表征与激活，形成关于目标任务的假设；在 support set 上实现并测试候选程序，以聚合验证精度指导迭代改进与选择。每个程序产出完整领域模型并在测试前固定。智能体及其工具跨领域共享，但每个领域获得各自独立的适配程序。

Result: 在 11 个数据集、CLIP ViT-B/16 骨干下，AME 在对比方法中取得最高平均精度：无反向传播 81.2%，含反向传播 81.9%。进一步实验显示方法可跨 VLM 骨干与不同 LLM 智能体迁移。

Conclusion: 将适配流程的设计权交给 LLM 智能体是可行且有效的：领域定制程序优于统一预设流程，且"共享智能体/工具、独立领域程序"的范式具备骨干与智能体层面的通用性。

Abstract: Few-shot adaptation transfers pretrained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to new domains using a small set of labeled images. Most methods fit prompts, representations, or classifiers within an adaptation procedure specified in advance. Yet the visual distinctions needed for recognition can differ substantially across domains, motivating the question: Can an LLM agent use few-shot examples and model observations to design the adaptation procedure itself? We propose Agentic Model Editing (AME), an agentic framework that jointly designs input transformations, encoder edits, feature computation, and classifier fitting as an executable, domain-customized adaptation program. The agent inspects images, class semantics, model parameters, intermediate representations, and activations to formulate hypotheses about the target task. It implements and tests candidate programs on the support set, with aggregate validation accuracy guiding refinement and selection. Each program fits a complete domain model that is fixed before testing. The agent and its tools are shared across domains, while each domain receives its own adaptation program. Across eleven datasets with CLIP ViT-B/16, AME achieves the highest average accuracies of 81.2% without back-propagation and 81.9% with back-propagation among the compared methods. Further experiments demonstrate applicability across VLM backbones and LLM agents.

</details>


### [205] [Budget, Readout or Reasoning? Where Small Reasoning Models Lose Value in Auction Bidding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34068)
*Mahbod Khaleti,Seyed Reza Tavakoli,Baran Hoseini,Alireza Noori,Amir Amini,Koorosh Aslansefat,Marjan Alirezaie,Ali Habibnia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究将eBay与AuctionNet的竞价景观交给6个4B–14B开源推理模型做竞价决策，并把相对精确计算器的价值损失按成因分解为模型自身失误与操作者设置两类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广告平台与交易市场向竞价者展示赢标概率曲线，并越来越多地让基于推理语言模型的智能体直接选择出价。需要弄清这类智能体在已有估计信息下为何仍偏离最优，以及损失该归因于模型能力还是配置选择。

Method: 给6个4B–14B开源推理模型提供来自eBay成交价与模拟广告市场AuctionNet的赢标曲线，在留出拍卖上将其出价与精确计算器对比。利用"估计值已给定"的条件对损失逐项归因，并通过强制768 token、（pay-your-bid定价下的）自主停止、推理块闭合前/后取答案、以及基于轨迹内部数值一致性审计的覆写等受控实验进行检验，采用嵌套交叉验证。

Result: 两类失误归于模型：即便自身轨迹中的数值已将最优选项排第一仍跳过它；错误答案偏向菜单上出价金额最大者（与列表位置无关，且采样后仍存在）；eBay上强制768 token时错误使竞价者损失计算器剩余价值的7–17%，算术错误罕见。另两类损失源于操作者设置：token上限截断轨迹，允许在4096 token内自主停止时5个模型可恢复91–100%的领先优势；推理块未闭合即取答案会被误读，闭合后最弱模型在eBay从42%升至84%、AuctionNet从24%升至81%。审计并覆写不一致出价后，768 token下恢复率由42–85%升至84–100%，且eBay上的读取设置可迁移至AuctionNet。

Conclusion: 推理模型的竞价劣势大部分并非算术能力不足，而是可诊断、可修复的决策偏差与提示/解码配置问题；闭合推理块与一致性审计覆写能显著回收损失，且相关设置具跨市场可迁移性。

Abstract: Ad platforms and marketplaces show bidders a bid landscape, the estimated chance of winning at each candidate bid, and increasingly let agents built on reasoning language models choose the bid. We give six open reasoning models of 4B to 14B parameters such landscapes, with win curves from eBay closing prices and from a simulated advertising market (AuctionNet), and score their bids on held-out auctions against an exact calculator. Because the agent is handed the estimates, the lost value can be split by cause. Two failures belong to the models. They pass over the best option even when the values written in their own trace already rank it first, and their wrong answers lean toward the highest bid on the menu. The lean is about the bid's size, not its place in the list, and it survives sampling. At 768 forced tokens on eBay, under pay-your-bid pricing, the wrong answers cost bidders 7-17% of the surplus the calculator's bids earn. Arithmetic errors are rare. Two further losses come from operator choices. A token cap cuts traces short: allowed to stop on their own within 4,096 tokens, five models recover 91-100% of the calculator's lead over a model that does no reasoning. Asking for the answer while the reasoning block is still open misreads it: closing the block first lifts the weakest model from 42% to 84% on eBay and from 24% to 81% on AuctionNet. Finally, an audit that flags bids disagreeing with the trace's own numbers, and overrides them with the calculator's bid, raises the share of the calculator's lead recovered at 768 forced tokens from 42-85% to 84-100% on eBay under nested cross-validation, and the same reader settings transfer from eBay to AuctionNet.

</details>


### [206] [Not Every Input Needs Everything: Unifying Expert Gating, Model Cascades, and Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34112)
*Dat Tat Mai,Hung Tran Quy,Thai Viet Pham,James Jin Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该综述将token级稀疏门控、查询级模型级联与任务级多智能体调度统一到"先检查、再分配"(inspect-then-allocate)的两步框架下，系统梳理2,289项研究，揭示了三层条件计算共同的运维瓶颈与不对称的工程保障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在每次输入上统一执行单体神经权重、前沿基础模型或完整多智能体工作流，会带来显著的计算、延迟与成本开销；条件计算可通过动态决定调用哪些内部专家、模型端点或专用智能体来降低开销。但token级门控、查询级级联、任务级多智能体协同长期在彼此割裂的研究领域中发展，导致重复出现的系统性失效模式与可迁移的改进方案被掩盖。

Method: 系统性综述：从13,884条唯一记录中筛选出2017年至2026年9月间发表的2,289项研究，覆盖20个方法学家族，并以统一的"先检查、再分配"(inspect-then-allocate)两步框架进行综合归纳与对比分析。

Result: 离散硬选择在token、查询、任务三个尺度分别占各自文献的70%、83%和91%；计算开销在每一层级均为首要运维瓶颈。工程保障呈现明显的不对称性：层内门控侧重负载均衡而极少引入不确定性估计；模型选择与智能体编排依赖置信度阈值和预算规则，但面临校准偏差与级联错误。智能体任务委派的发文量增长最快，而该方向的路由机制最不常被实验隔离，方法学偏倚风险最高。

Conclusion: 条件计算各尺度研究亟需跨层整合：应互通失效诊断与工程对策，在门控中引入不确定性估计、在级联中强化校准与误差传播控制，并对智能体路由开展可隔离的受控实验以降低方法学偏倚。

Abstract: Executing monolithic neural weights, frontier foundation models, or entire multi-agent workflows uniformly on every input incurs substantial computational, latency, and monetary overhead. Conditional computation mitigates this cost by deciding dynamically which internal experts, model endpoints, or specialized agents to invoke per request. Yet token-level gating in sparse neural architectures, query-level model cascades, and task-level multi-agent coordination have largely progressed in disconnected research silos, obscuring recurring system failures and transferable fixes. In this systematic review, we synthesize 2,289 studies published between 2017 and September 2026 (selected from 13,884 unique records) across 20 methodological families under a unified two-step inspect-then-allocate framework. Discrete hard selection accounts for 70%, 83%, and 91% of token-, query-, and task-scale literature respectively, and computational overhead appears as a top operational bottleneck across every layer. Engineering safeguards remain sharply asymmetric: intra-layer gating prioritizes load balancing while rarely incorporating uncertainty estimation, whereas model selection and agent orchestration rely on confidence thresholds and budget rules while confronting miscalibration and cascading errors. At the same time, publication volume is accelerating fastest in agentic task delegation, where routing mechanisms are least often isolated experimentally and methodological bias risk is highest.

</details>


### [207] [Letting Models Manage Their Own Context: Where We Stand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34147)
*Alessio Devoto,Avinash Vem,Tara Safavi,Sanjay Kariyappa,Yu-Hsuan Tseng,Paul Furgale,Ricardo Silveira Cabral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究"模型驱动"的上下文管理：让模型通过 context API 自行决定何时、压缩什么内容，并在 nooa 框架中与 harness 驱动的自动压缩策略对比；同时提出可在编辑后复用未变后缀 KV cache 以降低预填充开销的推理优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 agent harness 在窗口填满时替模型触发摘要式压缩（compaction），模型对自身上下文无自主权。作者希望检验若把上下文管理权交给模型本身，是否能提升长上下文任务表现与 token 效率，并探索推理引擎如何降低此类编辑的代价。

Method: 在 NVIDIA 开源 agent harness nooa 中为模型提供 context API 控制权，在文档研究与机器学习工程两类任务上，将模型驱动与 harness 驱动两种策略在 8K–512K token 的上下文预算区间内进行对比；并实现基于未变后缀 KV cache 复用的推理优化，度量预填充计算与精度变化。

Result: 有效使用 context API 的模型取得更高准确率、常伴随更少输入 token，预算紧张时尤为明显：GPT 6.1 在 32K 预算下于 OfficeQA Pro v2 从 21% 升至 56%（近乎三倍），Kimi 输入 token 最多减少 41%。模型间在使用频率、压缩力度与压缩启动时机上差异显著，且 prompt 改动会改变该时机。推理引擎侧，复用未变后缀 KV cache 使单请求预填充计算最多降低 15%，精度无可测变化。

Conclusion: 模型驱动上下文管理优于 harness 驱动的被动压缩，但依赖模型行为差异与提示设计；配合支持 KV cache 编辑的推理引擎可进一步降低成本。由此提出"训练模型自我管理上下文 + 推理引擎支持 KV cache 编辑"的协同设计新方向。

Abstract: Agent harnesses today manage context on the model's behalf: when the window fills, the harness triggers a compaction that summarizes the history. We study the alternative, \emph{model-driven} context management, in which the model itself decides when and what to compact through a context API. We gave models control over their own context in \nooa, NVIDIA's open source agent harness, and compared the two strategies on document research and machine learning engineering across context budgets from 8K to 512K tokens. Models that use the context API effectively reach higher accuracy, often with fewer input tokens, especially at tight budgets: \textbf{GPT 6.1 nearly triples its score at a 32K budget} on OfficeQA Pro v2 (21\%~$\rightarrow$~56\%), and \textbf{Kimi gains with up to 41\% fewer input tokens}. Models differ widely, however, in how often they use the API, how aggressively they compact, and when they start compacting, and prompt changes shift that timing. Finally, we show that \textbf{inference engines can make model-driven edits cheaper}: by reusing the KV cache of the unchanged suffix after an edit, \textbf{we cut prefill compute by up to 15\% per request with no measurable change in accuracy}. Together, these results point to a new co-design frontier: models trained to manage their own context, served by inference engines that support KV cache editing.

</details>


### [208] [SOLO: Towards Sample-Faithful and Detectable LLM Watermarking under Repeated Sampling via Content-Anchored Single-Owner Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34190)
*Zhongliang Guo,Yan Lin,Yifei Qian,Shuai Zhao,Luwei Xiao,Daizong Liu,Chun Pong Lau*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示部署中的无失真水印在多响应采样场景下存在响应间键控上下文耦合问题，并提出基于min-hash锚定的请求级上下文历史方案SOLO，在保持自一致性与不确定性估计的同时提升检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用常对同一提示采样多个响应（自一致性、best-of-n、不确定性估计），而部署的无失真水印仅在单条响应内对键控上下文去重，导致同一请求的多响应共享答案模板前缀，使原本假设罕见的跨响应碰撞成为常态，破坏响应独立性。

Method: 分析键池（key pool）与请求级共享上下文历史两种补救方案的缺陷（多次检验、大量位置未被水印）；提出SOLO：保留请求级上下文历史，但用min-hash选出的内容锚定键控上下文，使兄弟响应极少争用同一上下文且随机编辑保持局部性，各响应联合分布等价于独立未加水印响应，且可用单一密钥检测。

Result: 在3个模型家族共5个语言模型、3个基准上，SOLO保持自一致性与不确定性估计；在1%假阳性率下，多数任务上检测到的响应多于四token键池，随机编辑10% token后亦然，且在多数干净任务上优于上下文历史方案。

Conclusion: SOLO通过min-hash锚定的请求级上下文历史，解决了多响应采样下的水印耦合问题，兼顾检测率与鲁棒性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Providers watermark the outputs of large language models, while applications sample many responses per prompt for self-consistency, best-of-n selection, and uncertainty estimation. We show that deployed configurations of distortion-free watermarks, which deduplicate keyed contexts only within a response, couple the responses of a request: answer templates make all responses query the watermark with the same context before the answer, so collisions across responses, usually assumed rare, become the norm. Key pools reduce or avoid the coupling at the cost of multiple testing, and the exact remedy of a context history shared across the request leaves many positions unwatermarked. We propose SOLO, which keeps a request-scoped context history but anchors its keyed contexts on content selected by min-hash, so that siblings rarely compete for a context and random edits stay local. Its responses are jointly distributed as independent unwatermarked responses, each detectable with a single key. On five language models from three families and three benchmarks, SOLO preserves self-consistency and uncertainty estimation and, at a 1% false positive rate, detects more responses than four-token key pools on most tasks, also after randomly editing 10% of the tokens, and more than the context history on most clean tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/ZhongliangGuo/solo-watermark.

</details>


### [209] [PROVE: Mitigating Long-Horizon LLM Collusion with Reinforcement Learning from Verified Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34215)
*Haochen Luo,Jie Fu,Mukai Li,Yuqi Wang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM智能体在长程互检中会逐步"串谋"，即无证据地接受对方工作；本文提出多智能体强化学习方法PROVE，按"证据"而非"结果"给予奖励，并对智能体的每次调用进行功劳分配，从而将串谋率从65.0%降至1.0%且不影响任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长程交互中互相验证时会逐渐漂移为串谋：即便缺乏协议要求的证据也接受对方成果。该现象在交互初期并不存在，而是逐回合累积形成，作者将其归因于记忆中准确反馈的积累——当遵守规则的智能体在对方正确时被告知其裁决错误，破坏规则反而成为奖励最大化选择，并在私有反思中被自我合理化。现有缓解手段只改变外部流程而保留智能体本身不变，因此本文选择直接训练去除该行为。

Method: PROVE（Protocol Reinforcement from Verified Evidence）是多智能体强化学习方法：奖励信号依据裁决背后的证据而非裁决结果（对/错）；同时进行逐次调用的功劳分配，把智能体每次调用（包括解决自身任务的调用）与它所控制的结果挂钩。实验以LoRA适配器在Qwen3.6-27B上、仅用六回合交互训练。

Result: 在留出任务序列、原始十回合时域下，串谋率由65.0%降至1.0%，任务成功率无可检测变化；原本在每次交互中都会收敛的串谋现已在任何交互中都不再收敛；增益可外推至超出训练最长交互四回合；训练后的27B模型串谋率低于原研究评估的所有模型，包括闭源前沿模型。

Conclusion: 串谋是可通过训练消除的行为而非必须靠外部流程规避的固有缺陷；以证据而非结果为奖励并结合细粒度功劳分配，能有效且不损害性能地抑制多智能体串谋，并具备良好的时域外推能力。

Abstract: When LLM agents verify each other's work over a long interaction, they drift into collusion: they come to accept work without the evidence that the verification protocol requires. Collusion is absent at the start and builds up episode by episode, which we trace to accurate feedback accumulating in memory. An agent that follows the rule is told that its verdict was wrong whenever its peer was right, so breaking the rule is the reward-maximizing choice, and agents talk themselves into it in their private reflections. Existing mitigations leave the agents unchanged; we train the behavior out. PROVE (Protocol Reinforcement from Verified Evidence) is a multi-agent reinforcement learning method that rewards verdicts by the evidence behind them rather than by their outcome, and credits every call an agent makes, including the calls that solve its own task, with the outcome that call controls. On Qwen3.6-27B, a LoRA adapter trained on six-episode interactions takes the collusion rate on held-out task sequences at the original ten-episode horizon from 65.0% to 1.0%, with no detectable change in task success. Collusion that converged in every interaction now converges in none, and the gain holds four episodes beyond the longest interaction seen in training. The trained 27B model colludes less than every model evaluated in the original study, closed-weight frontier models included. Code is available at https://github.com/Haochen-Luo/PROVE.

</details>


### [210] [Did the Model Forecast It, or Already Know It? Evaluating Large Language Models for Novel Drug-Target Prioritisation with a Temporal Knowledge-Graph Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34247)
*Pui Chung Siu,Yuan Liang,Claudia Cabrera,Mani Mudaliar,Arkaitz Zubiaga*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将“靶点-疾病”假说重构为有日期的首次临床试验事件，构建逐年快照的生物医学知识图谱，在训练截止日后评测六个开源语言模型的靶点优先级预测能力；结果显示无检索时模型仅与试验历史基线持平，最新图谱虽显著提升但存在答案泄漏，强调特征与标签须严格标注日期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统日益参与决定哪些药物靶点进入昂贵的临床试验，但现有评测难以区分模型是“预测未来事件”还是“回忆训练数据中的已知结果”，也缺乏对时间泄漏（特征早于决策、标签晚于决策）的严格控制。

Method: 把每个靶点-疾病假设定义为配对首次临床试验的带日期事件；按2020年至最新版本逐年重建带日期生物医学知识图谱快照；对六个开源语言模型在“有/无图谱检索”两种条件下，预测某靶点下一年是否进入针对某疾病的首次试验；以“试验历史”（同治疗领域内该靶点既往试验计数）作为基线，并区分训练截止日前后的表现。

Result: 无检索时，模型排序能力与试验历史基线大致相当（含训练截止日之后）；决策日期之前的证据至多带来小幅提升，无论是否在模型训练截止日之后；最新图谱使六个模型中五个明显超过基线，但其检索路径通常包含答案本身——即决策日期之后的该配对自身试验，构成泄漏。

Conclusion: 语言模型在严格时间切分下并未显著优于简单的试验历史基线；图谱带来的增益多源于答案泄漏。靶点优先级评测应同时对特征和标签标注日期，特征仅来自决策之前、结果仅来自决策之后。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly help decide which drug targets enter costly clinical trials. We recast each target-disease hypothesis as a dated event, the pair's first clinical trial. This lets us test whether a language model can forecast such events after its training cutoff, rather than recall events before it. We rebuild a dated biomedical knowledge graph as it stood at the end of each year from 2020 up to its newest version. We then ask six open language models, with and without this graph, which targets will enter a first trial for a disease the next year. Without retrieval, the models rank about as well as trial history, a count of each target's past trials in the same therapeutic area, also after their training cutoffs. Evidence dated before the decision adds at most a small gain, before or after the models' training cutoffs. The newest graph raises five of six models clearly above that baseline, but its paths usually include the answer: the pair's own trial, dated after the decision. Evaluations of target prioritisation should date both features and labels, with evidence from before the decision and outcomes after it.

</details>


### [211] [EXPOSURE: Explaining Sources of Uncertainty in Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34248)
*Kiana Farhadyar,Yannick Vogt,Philipp Laur,Ayça Çelik,Iman Nematollahi,Gabriel Kalweit,Maria Kalweit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EXPOSURE 用一个掩码感知的信念网络同时完成动作选择与 Shapley 解释，将回报方差分解为观测部分与固有部分，并解释信念熵，在井字棋和扫雷上给出更准确的期望回报与不确定性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习中的 Shapley 值只能解释哪些观测特征驱动智能体的行为与期望回报，无法解释其确定性（不确定性）；而要解释不确定性，需理解特征如何影响不同来源的回报不确定性，且 Shapley 计算需要智能体对每个可见特征子集的预测。

Method: 提出 EXPOSURE：采用单一的掩码感知（mask-aware）信念网络，既用于动作选择，也用于生成 Shapley 解释；在此基础上解释回报方差（拆分为由特征缺失导致的观测方差与环境自身随机性导致的固有方差）以及掩码特征上信念分布的熵。

Result: 在可精确计算 Shapley 值的井字棋与扫雷环境中，EXPOSURE 对期望回报及其不确定性的解释更准确，并能独有地解释信念不确定性。

Conclusion: 将动作选择与解释统一于一个掩码感知信念网络，可将 Shapley 解释从期望回报扩展到回报不确定性及信念不确定性，并已在可精确验证的设定下证明其解释精度优势。

Abstract: Shapley values in reinforcement learning are used to explain which observation features drive an agent's behavior and expected return, but not its certainty. Explaining certainty requires understanding how observation features influence different sources of return uncertainty, and Shapley values need the agent's prediction for every subset of visible features. We propose EXPOSURE, which uses one mask-aware belief network for both action selection and Shapley explanations. It explains the variance of the return, split into an observational part due to missing features and an inherent part due to the environment's own randomness, and the entropy of its belief over the masked features. In tic-tac-toe and Minesweeper, where exact Shapley values are computable, EXPOSURE gives more accurate explanations of expected return and its uncertainty, while uniquely explaining belief uncertainty.

</details>


### [212] [Navigent: A Geometry-Grounded Agentic Framework for Vision Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34249)
*Xinyu Zhou,Weiren Cai,Yitong Chen,Shenghai Yuan,Zihang Mu,Boyu Ma,Jiahua Dong,Yanshuo Lu,Jiaqi Bai,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Navigent：以冻结MLLM为核心、用几何落地的执行批判器（GEC）校验"指令-运动"与"运动-地标"一致性，实现可靠长程VLN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态大模型做VLN虽灵活，但长程执行不可靠：需跟踪导航进度、识别指令与实际运动的偏差、判断是否已越过关键地标或入口。

Method: 提出agentic VLN框架Navigent，基于冻结MLLM并引入Geometry-Grounded Execution Critic（GEC）。GEC包含两项互补校验：(1) action-to-motion consistency，将实测位移与指令运动比对；(2) motion-to-landmark consistency，在世界坐标系中跟踪agent自选视觉锚点与入口候选。检测到偏差即中断剩余动作序列，触发重新观察、局部调整或基于节点的恢复；历史视觉记忆支持长路线进度跟踪；离线自改进流程分析成功/失败轨迹以优化导航提示与GEC逻辑，模型权重保持不变。

Result: 在R2R-CE validation-unseen（1839 episodes）上SR 84.0%、SPL 72.9%；在RxR-CE validation-unseen（3669 episodes）上SR 86.0%、SPL 69.1%。

Conclusion: 几何落地的执行验证对可靠agentic导航具有显著价值，且无需微调模型权重即可获得强性能。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) with general-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offers a flexible alternative to task-specific navigation policies, while reliable long-horizon execution remains challenging. Agents must track navigation progress, detect discrepancies between commanded and actual motion, and recognize when movement has passed relevant landmarks or entrances. We present Navigent, an agentic VLN framework built around a frozen MLLM and a Geometry-Grounded Execution Critic (GEC). GEC performs two complementary checks: action-to-motion consistency, which evaluates measured displacement against commanded movement, and motion-to-landmark consistency, which tracks agent-selected visual anchors and entrance candidates in world coordinates. Detected discrepancies interrupt the remaining action sequence and trigger reobservation, local adjustment, or node-based recovery. Historical visual memory supports progress tracking across extended routes. An offline self-improvement procedure further analyzes successful and failed training trajectories to refine navigation prompts and GEC logic while keeping model weights fixed. On the R2R-CE validation-unseen split (1,839 episodes) and RxR-CE validation-unseen split(3,669 episodes), Navigent achieves 84.0%/86.0% SR and 72.9%/69.1% SPL, respectively, demonstrating the value of geometry-grounded execution verification for reliable agentic navigation. Project Page: https://marslab-navigent.github.io/navigent.github.io/ Code: https://github.com/MarsLab-Navigent/Navigent

</details>


### [213] [MedSkill-E$^{2}$: Evaluating and Evolving Skills for Medical Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34250)
*Jingjing Hua,Shaocong Wei,Jianghan Shen,Junzhi Ning,Min Cen,Cheng Tang,Ziyan Huang,Jiepeng Li,Fengshuo Liu,Kangkun Mao,Mouxiao Bian,Yirong Chen,Ming Hu,Jiyao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出现有LLM智能体在自主执行时即使调用了技能规范也难以有效遵循（EBU现象），构建EBU-Bench量化该差距并提出EBU-Evolve将执行反馈蒸馏进技能指令以显著提升自主技能使用准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖"技能"获取领域知识完成真实任务，但作者观察到：即使技能中已明确写出任务所需规则与流程，智能体在自主执行时仍不能有效应用，即"技能已启用但未被充分使用"（Enable But Underuse, EBU）。该问题在医疗任务中尤为严重，忽略单一关键约束即可产生看似合理却错误的结果。因此需要系统评估并缓解EBU。

Method: 提出MedSkill-E$^{2}$框架，包含：(1) EBU-Bench基准，覆盖12个医学相关任务组及配套技能，评估智能体自主使用技能的能力；(2) 以"自主使用"与"显式引导阅读并应用同一技能"两种设定下的技能使用准确率之差来量化EBU gap；(3) EBU-Evolve方法，利用自主使用与显式引导两种设定的执行反馈，将有效引导蒸馏为可复用的技能指令，实现技能演化。

Result: EBU-Bench上所有被测"模型-执行框架（harness）"组合均表现出EBU，平均差距达43.94个百分点；同一模型在不同harness下自主技能使用准确率差异最高达38.96个百分点。经EBU-Evolve演化后，技能在自主执行下准确率较演化前提升33.33–55.17个百分点，且跨模型、跨harness迁移仍有效。

Conclusion: 在模型与技能固定时，自主技能使用准确率随harness变化，模型排名亦可能改变；技能即便被"打开"后EBU仍可能持续存在。EBU-Evolve可在同一技能的未见任务上提升自主技能使用准确率，且增益可跨模型与harness迁移。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly use skills to access domain knowledge and complete real-world tasks. We observe that even when a skill specifies the rules and procedures needed for a task, agents may fail to apply them effectively during autonomous execution, a phenomenon we call skill Enable But Underuse (EBU). This issue is particularly consequential in medical tasks, where overlooking a single critical constraint can yield a plausible but incorrect result. To evaluate and address EBU, we introduce MedSkill-E$^{2}$, a framework for evaluating and evolving skills for medical agents. We construct EBU-Bench, a challenging benchmark covering 12 medically relevant task groups and associated skills, to evaluate agents' ability to use available skills autonomously. We quantify the EBU gap as the difference in skill-use accuracy between autonomous use and explicitly guided reading and application of the same skill. On EBU-Bench, all evaluated model-harness combinations exhibit EBU, with a mean gap of 43.94 percentage points (pp). For the same model, skill-use accuracy under autonomous use differs by up to 38.96 pp across harnesses. Motivated by these observations, we propose EBU-Evolve, which uses execution feedback from autonomous and explicitly guided skill use to distill effective guidance into reusable skill instructions. Relative to their pre-evolution versions, the revised skills improve skill-use accuracy during autonomous execution by 33.33 to 55.17 pp and continue to yield improvements when transferred across multiple models and harnesses. Our results show that autonomous skill-use accuracy varies across harnesses with the model and skill held fixed, and model rankings can change. EBU can persist even after a skill is opened. EBU-Evolve improves autonomous skill-use accuracy on unseen tasks within the same skill, with gains transferring across models and harnesses.

</details>


### [214] [Crucible: Evaluating LLM Agents in Executable Chemistry Research Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34254)
*Yinjun Liu,Xinye Li,Ruisen Peng,Lang Gao,Zixiang Xu,Zirui Song,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Crucible Bench 是一个覆盖 DMTA 全周期的化学智能体基准，含 1078 个任务、12 类任务族、75 个工具与 7 个沙箱环境，评估 14 个 LLM 后发现最佳总分仅 0.545，且工具调用成功并不保证化学结果有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有化学知识基准主要测评问答与孤立任务，无法反映真实化学研究所需的"调用专业工具并正确解读输出以获得化学上有效结果"的能力，这一能力缺口亟需专门基准来度量。

Method: 构建 Crucible Bench：从 18 个已发表数据集筛选出 12 个任务族共 1078 个任务，覆盖分子设计、合成规划、实验执行、闭环优化、模拟与分析表征；智能体通过 Model Context Protocol 访问 75 个领域工具，运行于 7 个隔离环境；由沙箱评分器验证合成路线、实验方案等产物，实验类任务采用机器人模拟器或既有实测数据；评测 14 个 LLM 并做误差分析。

Result: 最佳模型总分 0.545；合成规划与闭环优化最难；总体性能对优化性能预测性弱（总体排名第十二的模型在闭环优化中得分最高）；误差分析显示智能体常出现工具运行成功但提交化学无效结果的情况；更强模型从工具访问中获益更多。

Conclusion: 可靠的化学软件使用仍是当前 LLM 智能体的主要瓶颈，Crucible Bench 可作为可追踪的评测框架，随模型能力提升持续监测该能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform well on chemistry knowledge benchmarks, which mainly assess question answering and isolated tasks. Chemical research, however, requires using specialized tools and interpreting their outputs to obtain chemically valid results. To address this gap, we introduce Crucible Bench to evaluate this ability across all four stages of the design-make-test-analyze (DMTA) cycle. The benchmark contains 1,078 tasks in 12 task families curated from 18 published datasets, covering molecular design, synthesis planning, experimental execution, closed-loop optimization, simulation, and analytical characterization. Agents access 75 domain-specific tools through the Model Context Protocol in seven isolated environments. Sandboxed graders verify the resulting synthesis routes, experimental protocols, and other outputs. Laboratory tasks use robotic simulators or previously measured experimental data. We evaluate 14 LLMs as agents and find that the best achieves an overall score of 0.545, with synthesis planning and closed-loop optimization emerging as the hardest domains. Overall performance does not predict optimization performance well: the model ranked twelfth overall achieves the highest score in closed-loop optimization. Error analysis also shows that agents often submit chemically invalid results despite running their tools successfully. Stronger models benefit more from tool access, but reliable use of chemical software remains a challenge that Crucible Bench can track as models improve.

</details>


### [215] [MeterRouter: Budget-Aware Dynamic Routing for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34327)
*Yue Xu,Leikun Yin,Ruiming Min,Yufeng Lai,Justin Andrew Johnson,Sitao Luan,Xiaotong Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MeterRouter 是一个预算感知的多智能体系统动态路由器，在给定每条查询的货币预算下增量地构建协作图（选择每个智能体的角色、LLM、入边与终止），先用成功执行图模仿初始化，再用带成本惩罚的 GRPO 联合优化，在五个基准上以约低 82% 的成本达到最强 MAS 的精度，并能按预算调节花费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的多智能体系统（MAS）优于单智能体，但智能体间通信带来大量 token 开销；给每个智能体都配大模型导致资源利用低效、远离 Pareto 前沿。查询成本由招募的智能体数量、各自角色与分配的 LLM、以及通信拓扑共同决定。现有路由器仅隐式地对待成本，而非将其作为显式优化约束；固定精度-成本偏好也无法表达调用方对某条具体查询的付费意愿。

Method: 提出 MeterRouter：以查询和每条查询的货币预算为条件，增量合成协作图，逐步为每个智能体选择角色、LLM、入边连接及终止决策。先通过模仿成功的执行图进行初始化，再用 GRPO 变体联合精化路由决策，奖励函数偏好正确答案并对花费与超预算进行惩罚。

Result: 在五个基准和三级 LLM 池上：(1) 准确性——与最强 MAS 相当；(2) 经济性——达到该精度时成本比最强 MAS 路由器低 82%，并将精度-成本前沿推至单智能体系统上限之外；(3) 可控性——花费随指定预算自适应调整，且极少超出预算。

Conclusion: MeterRouter 将成本作为显式约束纳入 MAS 路由，实现了准确、经济且预算可控的多智能体协作，突破了单智能体系统的精度-成本上限。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) built on large language models (LLMs) outperform single-agent systems, but inter-agent communication incurs substantial token overhead. While assigning a large LLM to every agent is common, it results in inefficient resource utilization, leaving the system far from the Pareto frontier. The cost of answering a query is set jointly by how many agents are recruited, the role and LLM assigned to each, and communication topology among them. Most existing routers treat cost only implicitly rather than as an explicit optimization constraint. A fixed accuracy-cost preference also cannot express how much a caller is willing to pay for a particular query. We introduce MeterRouter, a budget-aware dynamic router that incrementally synthesizes a collaboration graph by selecting each agent's role, LLM, incoming connections, and termination conditioned on the query and a per-query monetary budget. We initialize the router by imitating successful execution graphs, then jointly refine its decisions with a variant of Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) whose reward favors correct answers and penalizes spending and budget overruns. Across five benchmarks and a three-tier LLM pool, MeterRouter is (1) accurate, achieving performance comparable to the strongest MAS; (2) economical, reaching that accuracy at 82% lower cost than the strongest MAS router and extending the accuracy-cost frontier beyond the ceiling of single-agent systems; and (3) controllable, adapting its spending to the specified budget while rarely exceeding it.

</details>


### [216] [EvoLink-Bench: A Benchmark for Evaluating Self-Evolving Agents in Long-Horizon Interdependent Task Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34400)
*Wei Chen,Xiaodong Lu,Weihao Shen,Gongxun Li,Xingyu Guo,Qihang Sun,Fuwei Zhang,Zhao Zhang,Yikun Ban,Shuang Li,Deqing Wang,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 EvoLink-Bench，用于在长时程、相互依赖的任务流中评估自我演化型 LLM 智能体，揭示现有框架在经验复用与持续状态更新上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体正从被动问答转向在动态环境中持续运行的自主系统，需从历史交互中学习以提升后续表现。但现有流式基准多为短任务序列或高度独立任务，难以检验后续任务依赖前序结果时智能体能否复用经验。

Method: 构建 EvoLink-Bench：覆盖日常办公、多轮对话与 AutoResearch 式工作流，配备持久化工作区与跨任务依赖；设两级评估——L1 考察可用语言表达的知识的获取与修正，L2 考察通过执行与反馈形成的任务级能力；评估维度区分任务内恢复、持久状态更新与后续任务中的经验复用。在三种持久化条件下评测 5 个智能体框架 × 3 种骨干配置，覆盖 19,635 个计分任务槽。

Result: 无框架在 L1 与 L2 上均稳定最优；重试仅能挽回有限比例的失败任务；持久状态更新未必提升后续表现；演化效应在知识级与能力级任务上存在差异；性能增益随领域与任务流而异，部分任务上的提升不必然迁移到其他任务。

Conclusion: 自我演化的知识获取与能力发展可能需要不同的学习机制，现有智能体框架的持续学习能力仍不足，EvoLink-Bench 为长时程依赖型任务流下的自我演化评估提供了统一测试平台。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are evolving from passive chatbots into autonomous systems that operate continuously in dynamic environments. Such agents need not only solve current tasks but also learn from past interactions to improve future performance. However, existing streaming benchmarks often rely on short task sequences or largely independent tasks, providing limited evidence of whether agents can effectively reuse experience when later tasks depend on earlier outcomes. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{EvoLink-Bench}, a benchmark for evaluating self-evolving agents in long-horizon, interdependent task streams. It covers daily office work, multi-turn dialogue, and AutoResearch-style workflows, with persistent workspaces and dependencies across tasks. The benchmark includes two complementary levels: \textbf{L1}, which evaluates the acquisition and revision of language-expressible knowledge, and \textbf{L2}, which assesses the development of task-level capabilities through execution and feedback. Its evaluation further distinguishes within-task recovery, persistent-state updates, and experience reuse in later tasks. We evaluate five agent frameworks across three backbone configurations under three persistence conditions, covering 19,635 scored task slots from selected evaluation cohorts. Results show that no framework consistently performs best across both levels, retries recover only a limited proportion of failed tasks, and persistent-state updates do not necessarily improve subsequent performance. Furthermore, the effects of evolution differ between knowledge- and capability-level tasks, suggesting that these two forms of evolution require different learning mechanisms. Performance gains also vary across domains and task streams, with improvements on some tasks not necessarily extending to others.

</details>


### [217] [Evaluating Aviation Safety Knowledge Extraction with SmolLM2: From Zero-Shot to Retrieval-Grounded Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34432)
*Aziida Nanyonga,Mustafa Tahsin Yilmaz,Amir Hussain,Ugur Turhan,Graham Wild*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评测基于提示的知识抽取方法在航空事故叙事的四个维度上的表现（概念准确性、逐字证据支撑、结构合规性、有向关系抽取），发现没有单一提示策略在所有维度最优，有向关系抽取是SmolLM2的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空事故叙事蕴含结构化事件字段难以捕捉的安全知识，需评估基于提示的抽取方法能否可靠地从中提取知识，并厘清不同提示策略在各评价维度上的权衡。

Method: 使用300篇2000—2020年NTSB航空事故叙事（含3763个参考概念、467条有向关系），对SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct在零样本、单样本、固定少样本和检索增强提示下进行评测；评价结合结构检查、逐字证据匹配、专家审核的语义匹配和配对bootstrap置信区间；另用35篇基准叙事对Qwen3-8B做探索性对比。

Result: 无任何提示策略在全部维度上最优。检索增强提示取得最高概念micro-F1（32.27%），但相对固定少样本的优势不确定（95% CI：−0.92至2.40个百分点）。单样本提示取得最高关系F1（6.95%），凸显有向关系抽取是SmolLM2的主要瓶颈。相比固定少样本，检索增强提升了逐字证据支撑（59.45% vs. 54.02%），但降低了完整结构合规率（33.3% vs. 42.7%）。Qwen3-8B在全部提示条件下概念micro-F1均更高，在单样本提示下达51.37%，关系F1达34.62%；但35篇子集限制了结论的可推广性。

Conclusion: 逐字匹配只能说明来源存在性，而非证据相关性；应分别评测语义正确性、来源可追溯性、结构可靠性和有向关系准确性，而非以单一指标概括提示式知识抽取效果。

Abstract: Aviation accident narratives contain safety knowledge that is difficult to capture using structured occurrence fields alone. This study evaluates prompting-based knowledge extraction across four dimensions: concept accuracy, verbatim evidence grounding, structural compliance, and directed relation extraction. SmolLM2-1.7B-Instruct was evaluated under zero-shot, one-shot, fixed few-shot, and retrieval-grounded prompting using 300 National Transportation Safety Board narratives from 2000-2020, containing 3,763 reference concepts and 467 directed relations. Evaluation combined structural checks, verbatim evidence matching, expert-reviewed semantic matching, and paired bootstrap confidence intervals. No prompting strategy performed best across all dimensions. Retrieval-grounded prompting achieved the highest concept micro-F1 of 32.27%, although its advantage over fixed few-shot prompting was uncertain (95% CI: -0.92 to 2.40 percentage points). One-shot prompting achieved the highest relation F1 of 6.95%, identifying directed relation extraction as the principal SmolLM2 bottleneck. Compared with fixed few-shot prompting, retrieval improved verbatim grounding (59.45% vs. 54.02%) but reduced complete structural compliance (33.3% vs. 42.7%). Verbatim matching establishes source presence rather than evidential relevance. An exploratory comparison using 35 benchmark narratives showed that Qwen3-8B achieved higher concept micro-F1 across all prompting conditions and higher relation F1 under demonstration-based prompting, reaching 51.37% and 34.62%, respectively, under one-shot prompting. The small subset limits generalization. These findings support evaluating semantic correctness, source traceability, structural reliability, and directed relation accuracy separately.

</details>


### [218] [vSFT: Learning to Verbally Tune Agents at Test Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34440)
*Qishuai Wen,Rui Chen,Haiwen Li,Zhiyuan Huang,Qiwen Gu,Bingjie Gao,Geng Li,Jing Tang,Xiangxiang Chu,Chun-Guang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 vSFT 框架：由监督者将失败轨迹转化为自然语言监督，超网络将其映射为低秩权重更新，在测试时以稠密自然语言反馈直接更新智能体参数，性能优于上下文方法与主流强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 智能体多依赖 agentic RL 提升能力，但 on-policy 训练存在 rollout 代价高、奖励稀疏的问题，难以用于测试时自适应；而上下文式（in-context）反馈又无法真正改变参数。作者希望在测试时兼顾"稠密自然语言反馈"与"参数更新"两种优势。

Method: vSFT（verbally Supervised Fine-Tuning）：构建智能体—监督者—超网络闭环。监督者根据智能体失败轨迹生成自然语言（verbal）监督信号；超网络将该文本监督转换为低秩权重更新（low-rank weight updates），从而在测试时直接调优智能体，实现 in-parameter 的持续进化。

Result: 在 ALFWorld 上，基于 Qwen3-4B 的 vSFT 相比 GRPO 和 Reflexion 成功率分别高 8.7% 与 13.9%；在 WebShop 上分别高 4.8% 与 18.2%。同时优于 in-context 类方法与主流 RL 方法。

Conclusion: 测试时的参数级自进化（in-parameter evolving）优于上下文自适应与 RL 方案；作者主张"学习如何适应模型"比"获得一个已适应的模型"更有前景，为可持续自我改进的智能体提供路径。

Abstract: Large language model agents are increasingly improved via agentic reinforcement learning, which can be on-policy but suffers from expensive rollouts and sparse rewards, limiting its application at test time. In this paper, we present a verbally Supervised Fine-Tuning (vSFT) framework that enables the conjunction of dense feedback in natural language and weight updates at test time. Specifically, our vSFT forms a closed loop between an agent and a supervisor via a hypernetwork, in which the supervisor generates verbal supervision from the agent's failed trajectories, and the hypernetwork converts that supervision into low-rank weight updates to tune the agent. We validate that this test-time in-parameter evolving process outperforms both its in-context counterparts and prevalent reinforcement learning methods. In particular, our vSFT using Qwen3-4B outperforms GRPO and Reflexion by 8.7% and 13.9% in success rate on ALFWorld, and by 4.8% and 18.2% on WebShop, respectively. We believe that learning how to adapt a model, rather than an adapted model, offers a promising path towards more capable agents that improve themselves.

</details>


### [219] [EgoUseBench: Benchmarking Computer-Use Agents under Evolving First-Person Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34466)
*Yurun Chen,Biao Yi,Qihang Yu,Keting Yin,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"Ego Use"交互范式与EgoUseBench基准，用于评估智能体在AI可穿戴设备第一人称观察下完成电脑任务的能力。基于74小时真实第一人称录像构建693个任务，覆盖Computer-Use、Offline Ego Use、Online Ego Use三种设置，并按信息变化方式分为Graft/Refresh/Clarify三类。实验显示Online Ego Use平均成功率比Computer-Use低26.3%，表明现有智能体难以随演进的第一人称上下文与用户意图保持任务对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI可穿戴设备（如智能眼镜）使智能体的感知上下文从数字环境扩展到用户物理环境，电脑任务可由用户正在进行的活动自然触发，而非仅来自显式指令；但第一人称观察受佩戴者视角限制，证据可能不完整或含糊，需随新观察修订对物理上下文与用户意图的理解并重新评估既有数字动作的有效性，而现有智能体能否可靠应对这些变化缺乏充分评估。

Method: 提出EgoUseBench基准，按第一人称上下文丰富度分三种交互设置：Computer-Use（仅依赖显式指令）、Offline Ego Use（执行前提供预录第一人称上下文）、Online Ego Use（执行中持续提供观察并随意图演进更新）。基于超过74小时真实第一人称录像构建693个任务，分别追踪第一人称观察、用户需求与网站状态以刻画任务演化，并按信息变化方式将任务分为Graft（用户需求变更）、Refresh（新的第一人称观察）、Clarify（智能体主动信息检索）三类，据此评测先进模型。

Result: 对先进模型的评测显示，Online Ego Use的平均成功率为26.3%，显著低于Computer-Use场景，且比后者低26.3个百分点。

Conclusion: Ego Use揭示了一个新问题：让任务执行与不断演进的第一人称上下文及用户意图保持一致仍然困难，现有智能体在此方面能力不足；EgoUseBench为系统评估与改进该能力提供了标准化测试平台。

Abstract: AI wearables, such as smart glasses, extend the context available to agents beyond digital environments to users' physical surroundings. With these devices, computer tasks can originate from users' ongoing activities rather than solely from explicit instructions. Through first-person observations, agents can identify objects and activities relevant to user requests and ground digital tasks in the surrounding context. We call this interaction paradigm Ego Use. However, first-person observations are limited by the wearer's viewpoint and may provide incomplete or ambiguous evidence of ongoing activities. As new observations arrive, agents may need to revise their understanding of the physical context and user intent, and reassess whether prior digital actions remain valid. Whether current agents can reliably handle these changes remains insufficiently evaluated. We introduce EgoUseBench, a benchmark for evaluating Ego Use across three interaction settings with different levels of first-person context. Computer-Use relies on explicit instructions, Offline Ego Use provides prerecorded first-person context before execution, and Online Ego Use provides continuous observations as user intent evolves. Based on over 74 hours of real-world first-person recordings, we construct 693 tasks across the three interaction settings. To capture task evolution, we track first-person observations, user requirements, and website state separately. We further group tasks by how information changes during execution, resulting in three types: Graft (revised user requirements), Refresh (new first-person observations), and Clarify (agent-initiated information seeking). We evaluate advanced models and find that average success rates in Online Ego Use are 26.3% lower than in Computer-Use. This result reveals the difficulty of keeping task execution aligned with evolving first-person context and user intent.

</details>


### [220] [On Utilization of Edge-AI for condition monitoring towards predictive maintenance: A use case in dairy processing using explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34563)
*Rahul Umesh Mhapsekar,Norah O'Shea,Omer Ali,Steven Davy,Lizy Abraham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于边缘AI的状态监测框架，利用低功耗IIoT振动传感节点采集乳品加工中旋转阀的振动数据，并在边缘设备上处理，为实现预测性维护奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业物联网与人工智能推动工业4.0发展，提升制造系统的生产力、自动化与效率；旋转阀等关键设备的状态监测与预测性维护是其中的重要需求。

Method: 构建Edge-AI状态监测框架：通过低功耗IIoT传感节点采集乳品中试装置的旋转阀振动数据，并在边缘计算设备上进行数据处理与分析。

Result: 摘要仅说明该框架的构建与数据采集处理流程，未给出具体量化结果。

Conclusion: 该工作作为迈向预测性维护的一步，验证了基于边缘AI与低功耗IIoT节点的状态监测方案在工业旋转设备上的可行性。

Abstract: The Industrial Internet of Things (IIoT) and Artificial Intelligence (AI) are driving Industry 4.0, enhancing productivity, automation, and efficiency in manufacturing systems. This study develops an Edge-AI-based condition monitoring framework, as a step towards predictive maintenance, for a rotary valve in a dairy processing pilot plant, using vibration data collected via a low-power IIoT sensor node and processed on an edge device.

</details>


### [221] [Do We Really Need an LLM for Weight-Space Programming? A Deterministic Forward-Only Paradigm and Its Capability Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34612)
*Lin Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Forward Weight-Space Programming (FWSP)：在结构化权重编辑场景中无需 LLM 优化器，用确定性流水线（探测、定位、分层算子 O1–O4、盲路由、配对验证）完成前向权重编辑；并给出 Protected FisherGate 与两类算子边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Weight-Space Programming 方法用 LLM 通过前向传播编辑权重，把"LLM 脚本化"与"前向编辑的底层代数"混为一谈，导致依赖大模型做优化器、成本高且不必要。

Method: FWSP 确定性流水线：probing + localization + 分层算子 O1–O4 + 自动盲路由 + 零额外 per-token 计算的配对验证。针对推理 LLM 提出 Protected FisherGate——闭式、协方差白化的读出层编辑，且与正常推理状态正交（受保护）。另提出并证明两个算子边界。

Result: 盲路由准确率 83.3%，同工具库的 27B LLM 仅 33.3%；DeepSeek-R1-0528-8B 上单个冻结数学校准补丁达 84.2% HumanEval pass@1（1K 预算），优于 512-token 预留 budget forcing 14.6 pp 且省 23.4% token，并可跨 HF→vLLM 后端迁移（GSM8K、MATH-500）；在 5 种模态、13 个模型（50K–27B dense 与 FP8 MoE）上验证两类边界，FWSP 定位的 LoRA 可桥接前向编辑与梯度适配。

Conclusion: 核心结构化编辑无需 LLM 优化器；确定性前向编辑可超越 LLM 驱动方案并具跨后端迁移性；Protected FisherGate 提供了不损害推理能力的读出编辑手段，算子边界刻画了前向编辑与梯度适配的接壤条件。

Abstract: Recent work on Weight-Space Programming uses LLMs to edit neural weights with forward passes only, conflating LLM scripting with the underlying algebra of forward-only editing. We show that for core structured editing regimes, an LLM optimizer is unnecessary, and introduce Forward Weight-Space Programming (FWSP): a deterministic pipeline combining probing, localization, stratified operators (O1-O4), automatic blind routing (83.3% vs. 33.3% for a 27B LLM with the same tool library), and paired verification with zero additional per-token computation. For reasoning LLMs, we propose Protected FisherGate, a closed-form covariance-whitened readout edit orthogonal to normal-reasoning states: on DeepSeek-R1-0528-8B, a single frozen math-calibrated patch achieves 84.2% HumanEval pass@1 (1K budget; 75.4 +/- 9.4% across 3/5 Stage-4-passing subsets), beating 512-token-reserved budget forcing by +14.6 pp (-23.4% tokens) while transferring across HF-to-vLLM backends on full GSM8K and MATH-500. Across five modalities and thirteen models (50K to 27B dense and FP8 MoE), we further prove and verify two operator boundaries, subspace entanglement and frozen representation span, where FWSP-localized LoRA bridges forward-only and gradient adaptation.

</details>


### [222] [HoMe: Toward Practical Harness Optimization of Memory Systems for AI Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34624)
*Yifei Xu,Li Yu,Zhaoyang Liu,Ce Wang,Sen Huang,Yanyan Shen,Yun Lin,Linpeng Huang,Hong Mei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 HoMe，一种以评估为驱动的记忆系统 harness 自动优化框架，通过迭代评估候选 harness 并基于失败机制定位进行编辑，在 LongMemEval-S 与 BEAM 上以少量评估次数发现优于原强基线且显著降低查询时 token 消耗的 harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的 AI 助手的记忆系统行为由 harness（记忆构建、维护、检索与答案生成的代码、提示与配置）决定，但该 harness 的优化基本依赖人工，且无约束的自由编辑并不能保证结构性改进。

Method: HoMe 采用评估驱动的迭代搜索：每个新候选从任一已评估 harness 派生，可编辑其任意部分；结合会话级记忆版本化与执行轨迹，识别跨样本的共享失败机制，并归因到具体会话与 harness 组件；通过候选门控促进早期结构探索，并借助冒烟测试、自适应采样与已构建记忆复用降低评估开销。

Result: 在开源记忆系统 ReMe 上，于 LongMemEval-S 和 BEAM 两个大规模长程记忆基准各进行 6 次正式候选评估，即在一次完整初始评估之后，HoMe 即找到相对原强 harness 提升终端任务性能、同时查询时 token 消耗大幅降低的 harness。

Conclusion: 记忆系统 harness 可通过评估驱动的自动编辑框架高效优化，失败机制归因与低成本评估策略是提升搜索效率的关键；代码已开源。

Abstract: The behavior of memory systems for long-running AI assistants is governed by a harness, which consists of the code, prompts, and configurations for memory construction, maintenance, retrieval, and answer generation. However, optimizing the harness of a memory system remains largely manual, and unrestricted editing alone does not ensure structural improvements. In this paper, we introduce HoMe, an evaluation-driven framework that iteratively evaluates candidate harnesses and derives each new candidate from any previously evaluated harness by editing any part of it. HoMe combines session-level memory versioning with execution traces to identify shared failure mechanisms across cases and localize them to the responsible sessions and harness components. It encourages early structural exploration through candidate gating and reduces evaluation cost through smoke tests, adaptive sampling, and the reuse of constructed memory. We validate HoMe with the open-source memory system ReMe on two large-scale long-horizon memory benchmarks, LongMemEval-S and BEAM. With six formal candidate evaluations per benchmark, after a full initial evaluation, HoMe discovers harnesses that improve end-task performance over the originally strong harness while consuming far fewer query-time tokens. The code is available at https://github.com/agentscope-ai/ReMe.

</details>


### [223] [ZeroHero: Training Small Language Agents from Scratch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34635)
*Nikita Sorokin,Zoya Volovikova,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr Panov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ZeroHero框架，从零训练参数量<50M的小型语言智能体（SLA），先用大模型专家轨迹预训练，再用PPO并结合“推理对齐”进行后训练，在Crafter、WebShop、Alfworld-Dialogue多步交互环境中达到或超越专家表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大预训练语言模型可提供强交互策略，但专用语言智能体究竟需要多大模型容量尚不明确；作者希望探究小容量模型能否胜任需要理解与生成语言的多步交互任务。

Method: 1）在每个环境中以大规模LLM为专家，采集其成功轨迹；2）预训练<50M参数的小语言智能体，基于交互历史预测专家回复；3）用PPO在智能体自身交互上做后训练；4）后训练阶段引入“推理对齐”，在环境奖励之外，对智能体生成的语言（伴随动作的推理）施加专家监督。

Result: 在三个需语言理解与生成的多步环境评测：WebShop上接近专家性能；Crafter训练rollout与留出Alfworld-Dialogue任务上超过各自的演示专家；Crafter中智能体需解读文本观测并生成推理后行动，WebShop中将购物指令转为搜索查询，Alfworld-Dialogue中需提问澄清并利用回答指导家居操作。

Conclusion: 小容量（<50M）语言智能体经专家预训练+PPO后训练+推理对齐，可在多步语言交互任务中达到甚至超越大模型专家，说明专用语言智能体对模型容量的需求远低于通用大模型。

Abstract: Large pretrained language models provide capable policies for interactive tasks, but how much model capacity is necessary for a specialized language agent? We introduce ZeroHero, a framework for training small language agents (SLAs) from scratch. In each environment, a large language model acts as the expert, and we collect its successful trajectories. We pretrain each SLA, which has fewer than 50 million parameters, to predict the expert's responses given the interaction history. We then post-train the agent on its own interactions using proximal policy optimization (PPO). During post-training, reasoning alignment supplements environment rewards with expert supervision on the language the agent generates alongside its actions. We evaluate the agents in three multistep environments where acting requires both understanding and generating language. In Crafter, agents interpret textual observations and generate reasoning before acting. In WebShop, they translate shopping instructions into search queries. In Alfworld-Dialogue, they ask clarifying questions and use the answers to guide household actions. The agents approach expert performance on WebShop and surpass their respective demonstration experts on Crafter training rollouts and held-out Alfworld-Dialogue tasks. An interactive demo is available at https://zoyav.github.io/-ZoyaV-zerohero-demo.

</details>


### [224] [REE: A Scientific Research Entrance Exam for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34646)
*Zeqi Zhou,Minghao Liu,Jiarui Liu,Qingcheng Zeng,Rui Yang,Tianyu Liu,Zetao Yang,Wenqiang Zu,Enduo Zhao,Heng-Jui Chang,Lun-Hsin Kuo,Meng-Fu Chen,Haokai Zhao,Weijia Zhang,Jen-Tse Huang,Stephen Huang,Eli Cheung,Zhijing Jin,Mona Diab,Weihao Xuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出 REE（Research Entrance Exam）基准，用 100 个经领域专家审核的开放式科研问题，在统一尺度上衡量自主研究智能体相对专家参考解的迭代改进能力，并发现 Claude Opus 5.5 与 GPT-6.1 Sol 在 86% 和 81% 的问题上超越专家解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主科研智能体依赖"反复实验—迭代改进"来求解科学问题，但现有评测缺少既能提出开放式研究问题、又能以既定科学方法为参照量化改进幅度的基准，因此难以评估其真实研究能力。

Method: 构建 REE 基准：覆盖 5 大科学领域、23 个子领域的 100 个开放式研究问题，均取自各领域活跃研究并经专家逐题审核；每题以"迭代改进"任务形式给出可运行的初始解，智能体最多 12 小时进行改进；结果在统一评分尺度上与同等条件下评估的专家解对比。

Result: 在评估的 4 个前沿智能体中，Claude Opus 5.5 与 GPT-6.1 Sol 分别在 86% 和 81% 的问题上超过专家解，且横跨全部 5 大领域；两者平均在最初两小时内即超过专家解；其最优解多为对领域既有方法的定制与组合。

Conclusion: REE 提供了评估自主科学研究的统一框架，既能刻画相对既定方法的改进程度，也能揭示改进背后的研究过程；同时也表明当前前沿智能体的优势主要来自既有方法的高效搜索与组合，而非全新范式提出。

Abstract: Autonomous research agents are emerging as a promising approach to scientific problem-solving across disciplines, owing to their growing ability to improve a solution through repeated experiments. Evaluating this ability requires benchmarks that pose open-ended research problems and measure improvements over established scientific methods. We introduce REE (Research Entrance Exam), a benchmark that tests AI agents on scientific research. It consists of 100 open-ended research problems spanning five broad scientific areas and 23 domains. To ensure high quality, every problem is drawn from active research in its field and individually reviewed by domain experts. Each problem is posed as a task of iterative improvement: an agent has up to 12 hours to improve a working starter solution, and REE scores the result on a common scale against an established expert solution evaluated under comparable conditions. By this measure, two of the four frontier agents we evaluate, Claude Opus 5.5 and GPT-6.1 Sol, outperform the expert solutions on 86% and 81% of problems, across all five scientific areas. Their runs also show how they do so: both agents surpass the expert solutions within the first two hours on average, and their best solutions mostly tailor or combine established methods of the field. REE thus provides a unified framework for evaluating autonomous scientific research, capturing both improvements over established methods and the research processes behind them.

</details>


### [225] [A Failure-Driven Data Flywheel for Post-Training Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34698)
*Yuhao Luo,Zihao Sheng,Zilin Huang,Zhengyang Wan,Boyue Wang,Ruolin Yang,Shuai Wang,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种“失败驱动数据飞轮”的后训练框架，通过向重建的真实场景中插入任务相关实体构造缺失的目标场景，并按行为需求逐项评估模型失败率，将失败率重新分配为下一轮数据生成配额；在自动驾驶遮挡行人出现与停车标志让行两项任务上验证，显著提升碰撞规避与让行成功率且几乎不引入多余制动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练VLA模型难以满足新平台的全部行为要求，而缺乏大规模部署车队的团队没有数据来修复相应失败；因此需要一种不依赖真实车队数据、可针对失败条件定向生成数据的后训练方法。

Method: 构建失败驱动数据飞轮：将任务相关实体插入重建的真实场景中以合成现有数据中缺失的目标场景；针对目标任务每项行为需求逐条件、逐难度评估模型，形成跨条件与难度等级的失败图；每轮结束后重评估，并对仍失败的条件下达更大的数据生成配额，迭代后训练。实例化于自动驾驶的遮挡行人出现与停车标志路口让行，在仿真与实车中对两个VLA模型及一个非VLA规划器进行评估。

Result: 遮挡行人任务中，五轮后训练使封存测试集关键阶段无碰撞率由0.261升至0.579（AutoVLA）、0.390升至0.765（ReCogDrive），配对对照场景中不必要制动率不超过0.013；未用真实数据训练的全尺寸实车上ReCogDrive安全通过4/5，基线仅1/5。细粒度评估显示两模型均学会在到达遮挡车辆前预判性减速，但行人出现后的制动仍有限。停车标志任务中，线前停车率AutoVLA由0.100升至0.897、ReCogDrive由0.000升至0.667；以行人能力模型顺序训练后分别达0.599和0.358，同时行人无碰撞率下降不超过0.032。

Conclusion: 失败驱动数据飞轮可在无大规模车队数据条件下有效修复VLA模型的行为缺陷，且失败图引导的配额分配使数据生成具有针对性与可迭代性；细粒度评估能揭示总体指标掩盖的行为模式，顺序多任务训练基本保留既有能力但存在轻微权衡。

Abstract: Pretrained vision-language-action (VLA) models rarely satisfy all behavioral requirements of a new platform, and teams without a large deployed fleet lack the data to address the resulting failures. We propose a failure-driven data flywheel for post-training such models. It constructs target scenarios absent from the available data by inserting task-relevant entities into reconstructed real-world scenes, and evaluates the model against each behavioral requirement of the target task. Failure rates form a failure map across task conditions and difficulty levels, which is re-evaluated after each round and assigns larger data generation quotas to conditions where the model still fails. We instantiate the framework in autonomous driving for occluded-pedestrian emergence and yielding at stop-sign-controlled intersections, and evaluate it on two VLA models and one non-VLA planner in simulation and on a real vehicle. For occluded-pedestrian emergence, five rounds of post-training improve the critical-phase collision-free rate on the sealed test set from 0.261 to 0.579 for AutoVLA and from 0.390 to 0.765 for ReCogDrive, while the unnecessary braking rate in paired control scenarios never exceeds 0.013. On a full-scale vehicle without real-world training, post-trained ReCogDrive achieves 4 of 5 safe passages, versus 1 of 5 for the base model. Fine-grained evaluation reveals a pattern obscured by the collision-free rate alone: both VLA models learn anticipatory deceleration before reaching the occluding vehicle, while braking after pedestrian emergence remains limited. For stop-sign yielding, the stop-before-line rate rises from 0.100 to 0.897 for AutoVLA and from 0.000 to 0.667 for ReCogDrive. With sequential training from the pedestrian-capability models, it reaches 0.599 and 0.358, respectively, while the pedestrian collision-free rate decreases by at most 0.032.

</details>


### [226] [From Shared Patterns to Individual Evidence: Two-Stage Reasoning for Emerging Entities in Temporal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34708)
*Jinchuan Zhang,Lei Gao,Hongyu Jiang,Chong Mu,Chenang Cai,Ke Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: INSPIRE是一个面向已知实体目录的两阶段时序知识图谱预测框架：Stage I学习感知可用性的共享模式预测器，Stage II冻结其参数并优化轻量级个体证据适配器，从而统一共享模式与个体证据，显著提升新兴实体推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序知识图谱中新兴实体的交互轨迹从零样本首发演化为稀疏、突发的小样本序列。现有范式割裂：模式迁移方法缺乏全目录级事件记忆；在线记忆模型存在端点角色不对称问题，损害未见答案的排序；同时稀疏早期交互在偏斜分布下兼具长程分散性与局部冗余性。

Method: 提出两阶段框架INSPIRE。Stage I：学习可用性感知的共享模式预测器，融合全候选目录范围内事件更新的记忆，并施加查询侧与目录侧协同掩码。Stage II：冻结该预测器，优化轻量级个体证据适配器（EA），将文本语义先验与相关性加权的长程事件统计相结合，在支持度与角色平衡的共答监督下训练，配合先验几率去偏与软残差对齐。此外按端点角色分解评估，并通过查询微观与答案实体宏观指标追踪纵向新兴答案队列。

Result: 在基于ICEWS与GDELT事件数据集的四个基准上，INSPIRE的Emerging MRR较最强基线提升16.73%–88.83%。相对配对的Stage I预测器，EA进一步使Emerging MRR提升8.28%–32.63%、Answer-new MRR提升7.81%–41.24%、队列宏观MRR提升9.64%–39.94%。增益归因于语义先验与候选级分数竞争，抑制了不匹配的已建立竞争者，且无零支持先验捷径。

Conclusion: 两阶段解耦设计有效整合了目录级共享模式与个体证据，缓解端点角色不对称与稀疏突发的早期交互难题，在多个基准上对新兴实体预测取得一致且显著提升，代码已公开。

Abstract: Temporal knowledge graphs (TKGs) continually introduce emerging entities whose interaction histories evolve from zero-shot debuts into sparse, bursty few-shot trajectories. However, current paradigms are fragmented: pattern-transfer methods lack catalog-wide event memory, whereas online memory models suffer from endpoint-role asymmetry that degrades unseen-answer ranking. Furthermore, sparse early interactions exhibit both long-range dispersion and local redundancy under skewed distribution. To address these challenges, we propose INSPIRE (Integrating Shared Patterns and Individual Evidence for Reasoning), a two-stage forecasting framework over a known catalog. In Stage I, INSPIRE learns an availability-aware shared-pattern predictor that fuses event-updated memory across the full candidate catalog while applying coordinated query- and catalog-side masking. Subsequently, Stage II freezes this predictor and optimizes a lightweight individual-evidence adapter (EA) combining textual semantic priors with correlation-weighted long-range event statistics under support- and role-balanced co-answer supervision with prior-odds debiasing and soft residual alignment. In addition, we decompose evaluation by endpoint roles and track longitudinal emerging-answer cohorts via query-micro and answer-entity macro metrics. Across four benchmarks based on the ICEWS and GDELT event datasets, INSPIRE improves Emerging MRR by 16.73%-88.83% over the strongest baselines. Relative to paired Stage I predictors, EA further increases Emerging MRR by 8.28%-32.63%, Answer-new MRR by 7.81%-41.24%, and cohort macro MRR by 9.64%-39.94%, gains attributed to semantic priors and candidate-level score competition suppressing mismatched established competitors without zero-support prior shortcuts. The official code is publicly available at https://github.com/KcAcoZhang/INSPIRE.

</details>


### [227] [What Is a Theory Worth to an LLM Solver? Measuring, Inducing, and Merging Theories in Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34751)
*Jun Gao,Shuchao Bi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在长度匹配协议下，向LLM求解器上下文中加入正确理论可将pattern waiting-time问题的pass@1从0.37提升至0.83，并将正确解的中位token数从10.2k降至6.3k；而同长度的示例无效。模型能以代码探索训练题后自行归纳理论，但在不同植入族上恢复oracle理论增益的比例差异很大（60%/48%/近0），且需由强模型合并多份笔记才能达到61%，超过最佳单份笔记的47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者要回答两个问题：(1) 一条明确陈述的理论对LLM数学求解器究竟"值多少"（能否带来增益、替代示例）；(2) 模型能否自己写出这种理论。核心关切是区分"归纳"与"回忆"，并检验理论价值是否随求解器能力与token预算变化，以及自写理论相对oracle理论的差距来源。

Method: 采用长度匹配（length-matched）对照协议，在Qwen3-14B等求解器上比较"正确理论""同长度worked examples""无理论"三种条件的pass@1与token消耗；在自定义概率族中植入未发表定理以排除记忆效应；要求强开源模型先用代码探索训练问题、再写出理论笔记，并用"恢复oracle理论增益的比例"量化归纳质量；进一步比较单份笔记与多份笔记合并的效果；同时考察不同求解器规模与token预算（短/长预算）下的增益变化。

Result: 加入正确理论使pass@1由0.37升至0.83，正确解中位token由10.2k降至6.3k，同长度示例无增益。增益随求解器与预算增大而缩小（+0.46、+0.26、+0.07）；长预算下增益几乎全部来自"在预算内完成"，各条件下完成的解正确率为98%–100%。归纳实验中，强开源模型在一个植入族恢复60%增益、第二个48%、第三个几乎为零（找到正确形式但未找到使其可计算的条件）。无单份笔记完整，但强模型合并多份笔记在经典族恢复61%，高于最佳单份笔记的47%。

Conclusion: 理论的价值在于节省推理（token/计算），而非提供示例性提示；这种价值随求解器能力与预算增长而递减，长预算下主要体现为提升完成率而非解的正确率。模型具备"归纳"而非仅"回忆"理论的能力（植入未发表定理仍可恢复部分增益），但目前是"先能找到碎片、后能组装整体"：单份自写笔记不完备，合并多份笔记可显著提升恢复率。主要瓶颈在于识别使理论形式可计算的关键条件。

Abstract: Mathematicians solve problems faster when they know the right theory. We ask how much a stated theory is worth to a large language model (LLM) solver, and whether a model can write that theory itself. In a length-matched protocol, adding the right theory to the context of Qwen3-14B raises pass@1 on pattern waiting-time problems from 0.37 to 0.83 and cuts the median tokens of correct solutions from 10.2k to 6.3k, while worked examples of the same length do not help. The gain shrinks as the solver and its token budget grow (+0.46, +0.26, +0.07), and at a long budget it comes almost entirely from finishing within budget: solutions that finish are correct 98--100\% of the time in every condition. To separate induction from recall, we plant unpublished theorems in custom probability families and measure how much of the oracle theory's gain an induced theory recovers. A strong open model, required to explore the training problems with code before writing, recovers 60\% of the gain on one planted family, 48\% on a second, and almost none on a third, where it finds the right form but not the condition that makes the form computable. No single induced note is complete, but a strong model that merges several notes recovers 61\% of the gain on the classic family, above the best single note (47\%). Theories are worth the reasoning they save, and models can find their pieces before they can assemble them.

</details>


### [228] [TASK Memory: Beyond Text-Only Memory with Activation Steering and Knowledge Editing against Memory Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34762)
*Jinkun Zhao,Wenjun Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"使用期记忆幻觉"概念，并给出TASK记忆框架（文本+激活引导+知识编辑共享同一模型内地址）来消除此类幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖外部记忆却仍会就"自己记得什么"产生幻觉：正确记忆已被检索并放入提示，但回答与之矛盾，或记忆被错误地应用到无关问题。作者将此类现象定义为use-time memory hallucinations，并指出其根源在于参数化知识与外部记忆之间的冲突。

Method: 先对五种开源模型开展诊断，归纳出三种形式：memory-ignoring（回退到权重中的陈旧事实）、memory-misapplying（把记忆归属到无关实体）、version confusion（新旧版本共存时输出被取代的旧值）。随后提出TASK memory：以Text note承载内容；用一个可移除的知识编辑（以 subject key 提供地址）支持多跳组合；用一对steering vectors逐查询决定模型采纳记忆还是保留自身答案，向量不存储内容，仅控制文本记忆的使用方式。在冻结模型上采用预注册、一次性测试协议评估。

Result: 十个模型-数据集组合中memory-ignoring幻觉均降至≤1.5%；邻居误用从39.2%降至7.5%（CounterFact复合指标+10.8 pp）；300条记忆库上较BM25 RAG提升16.4 pp，且3000条时优势保持；无需fact ID即可处理事实修订；MQuAKE多跳准确率提升7.5 pp；23项跨模型比较中21项显著，并在10个组合中的9个上优于三种近期context-steering方法。

Conclusion: 将记忆的"内容"与"地址及使用决策"解耦，可有效抑制使用期记忆幻觉；TASK在多种模型规模与数据集上稳定优于检索增强与上下文引导基线。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external memory, yet they still hallucinate about what they remember. Often the correct memory is retrieved and placed in the prompt, but the answer contradicts it or the memory is applied to the wrong question. We call these use-time memory hallucinations. Across five open models, we find that they arise from the conflict between parametric knowledge and external memory and take three forms: memory-ignoring, where the model returns to a stale fact in its weights (up to 46% of queries); memory-misapplying, where it attributes the memory to unrelated entities (up to 24%, and 49% when retrieval is combined with weight editing); and version confusion, where 53% of answers about revised facts assert the superseded value when both versions remain stored. We propose TASK memory, which goes beyond text-only memory by combining a Text note with Activation Steering and Knowledge editing that share one in-model address. The text holds the content. A removable knowledge edit supplies the address through its subject key and supports multi-hop composition. A pair of steering vectors decides, query by query, whether the model adopts the memory or keeps its own answer; the vectors store no content and control how the text memory is used. On frozen models under a pre-registered, test-once protocol, TASK reduces memory-ignoring hallucinations to at most 1.5% in all ten model-dataset cells and cuts neighbour misuse from 39.2% to 7.5% (+10.8 pp composite on CounterFact). It improves a 300-memory library over BM25 RAG by 16.4 pp, an advantage that holds at 3,000 memories, resolves fact revisions without fact IDs, and adds 7.5 pp of multi-hop accuracy on MQuAKE. 21 of 23 cross-model comparisons (8B-27B, four families) are significant, and TASK beats three recent context-steering methods in 9 of 10 model-dataset cells.

</details>


### [229] [Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent: Self-Structuring Learning through Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34841)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"自结构化学习"(Self-Structuring Learning)设定与"知识库梯度下降"(Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent, KBGD)方法，通过问答反馈迭代地编辑持久化知识库的结构（图模式或链接知识文档组织），以提高基于LLM应用的检索与推理效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于大语言模型的应用依赖知识库的结构来决定如何检索并连接回答所需信息，但知识库结构如何构建往往被忽视。作者希望以问题与期望答案为监督信号，自动学习并优化知识库结构，使应用能更有效地回答问题。

Method: 提出KBGD：每轮诊断期望答案与当前知识库所得回答之间的差异，提出一个结构编辑、重建知识库，若学习损失下降则采纳该编辑。通过随机化候选编辑，将损失变化表达为对"选中该编辑概率"的导数；由于联合编辑相互影响，将其作为单一候选评估。Schema Descent 是该方法在图模式上的具体实例化。保留的结构在同一来源的其他问题上评估。

Result: 在由研究论文组成的大文档实验中，更新后的知识卡片与图模式在留出生成问题上取得更高平均Answer F1，且每个文档的schema均有提升。在人类问题上，schema的平均F1上升而卡片下降：更新后的结构更好地服务于其学习来源的同类问题。

Conclusion: 知识库结构可通过问答驱动的迭代编辑有效学习与优化；所提KBGD及Schema Descent能带来稳定的性能提升，但结构的收益具有问题类型依赖性，即结构优化的效果与所学问题的分布密切相关。

Abstract: We consider the problem of constructing knowledge bases for applications based on large language models, in which the structure of a knowledge base determines how an application can retrieve and connect the information needed to answer a question. We study Self-Structuring Learning, a setting in which questions and expected answers guide the construction of a persistent knowledge base whose editable structure can be a graph schema or the organization of linked knowledge documents. We propose Knowledge-Base Gradient Descent, which diagnoses discrepancies between expected answers and responses obtained through the current knowledge base. At each round, the method proposes a structural edit, rebuilds the knowledge base, and adopts the edit when the learning loss decreases. The retained structure is evaluated on other questions about the same sources. By randomizing over candidate edits, we express the loss change of an edit as a derivative with respect to the probability of selecting it, and we evaluate joint edits as single candidates because their changes interact. Schema Descent instantiates the method for graph schemas. In experiments on large documents, each a bundle of research papers, the updated knowledge cards and graph schemas obtain higher mean Answer F1 on held-out generated questions than their initial structures, with schema gains in every document. On human questions, mean F1 rises for schemas and falls for cards: the updated structures serve the kind of question they were learned from.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [The AI Disclosure Paradox: Operational Disclosure and the Rise of Corporate AI Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33201)
*Abdullah Karasan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 2017–2025年美国SEC文件显示，AI相关文本强度增长6.3倍，但运营性披露份额由25.76%降至13.45%，说明关注度扩张与实施性披露增长并不等同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分企业对AI的"组织注意力"扩张与"具体实施声明"增长，检验AI披露增长是否真正反映运营层面的落地应用。

Method: 基于2017–2025年SEC文件，用冻结的语言模型对26,279个AI相关段落进行预测，并对1,000个标注段落做审计校正；以段落强度、运营性披露强度及运营性占比为指标，并在行业、文件类型及仅10-K样本内做稳健性检验。

Result: 2017–2022与2023–2025相比，AI段落强度增长6.3倍，校正后运营性披露强度增长3.3倍；运营性占比从25.76%降至13.45%，约89%的净增量落在运营类别之外；该下降在行业内、文件类型内及仅10-K分析中均稳健。

Conclusion: 运营性披露被非运营性披露"稀释"而非"替代"；解读企业技术叙事需同时关注披露强度与其所描述的组织状态。

Abstract: AI-related corporate disclosure can expand while becoming less concentrated on current use. Across U.S. SEC filings from 2017 to 2025, AI-related passage intensity increased 6.3-fold between 2017-2022 and 2023-2025, whereas corrected operational-disclosure intensity increased 3.3-fold. The operational share fell from 25.76% to 13.45%; approximately 89% of the net increase in passage intensity fell outside the operational category. The decline persists within sectors and filing types and in 10-K-only analysis. These estimates combine frozen language-model predictions for 26,279 AI-related passages with an audit of 1,000 annotated passages. The findings distinguish growth in organizational attention from growth in implementation-specific claims: more operational disclosure can coexist with a substantially lower operational share. Operational disclosure was thus diluted, not displaced, by nonoperational disclosure. Reading corporate technology narratives therefore requires attention to both disclosure intensity and the organizational state described.

</details>


### [231] [Unpacking Digital Sovereignty: A Three-Pillar Framework and a Research Agenda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33397)
*Angelos Kostis,Franz Strich,Bomi Kim,Pavlos Fafalios*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出一个由治理、基础设施与实践三大支柱构成的分析框架，用于研究数字主权如何被建构、稳定或削弱，并以数据为抓手（anchor）进行示例分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字主权已从政策术语演变为全球性话语，但缺乏坚实的理论概念基础，信息系统（IS）学科需要一套可操作的分析工具来理解数字主权的实际运作机制。

Method: 概念性/理论建构研究：基于跨学科研究成果，提出三支柱框架（治理、基础设施、实践），强调支柱交叉处的分析价值；引入"本地—远端"（local-distal）轴以刻画跨范围与邻近性的行动尺度，并提出"锚点"（anchors）概念——即横跨各支柱、使框架具备可分析性的关键现象；以"数据"作为锚点进行示例说明。

Result: 以数据为锚点的示例表明，数字主权依赖于治理、基础设施与日常实践三者之间的协调一致；当三者出现分歧时，数字主权即被削弱。

Conclusion: 该框架为IS学者提供了研究数字主权的新概念工具，并提出面向实证研究的未来议程，倡导在支柱交叉处与锚点现象上开展经验研究。

Abstract: Digital sovereignty has moved rapidly from policy jargon to global discourse, yet it lacks a solid conceptual foundation. Building on research from other disciplines, we propose a three-pillar framework (governance, infrastructure, and practice) to help IS scholars unpack how sovereignty is enacted, stabilized, or undermined across pillars. We argue that the most fruitful analytical work emerges at the intersections among the three pillars, where IS research is particularly well positioned to contribute. We introduce a local-distal axis that positions actions across scales of scope and proximity, and the notion of anchors, i.e., critical phenomena that cut across pillars and make the framework analytically tractable. We illustrate the framework using data as an anchor, showing how digital sovereignty depends on the alignment of governance, infrastructure, and everyday practice, and is undermined where they diverge. We close with a research agenda for IS scholars studying digital sovereignty empirically.

</details>


### [232] [Designing an Integrated Learning Ecosystem for Introductory Python Programming: Scaffolded Problem Solving, Tablet-Inspired Navigation, and AI-Enhanced Instruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33471)
*Xinxing Wu,Johnathan Rogers*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文对16个实验课构成的入门Python课程学习生态系统进行以制品为中心的设计分析，归纳四个关联设计特征与五类未解决张力，不主张教学有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 入门编程要求学生完成问题表征、程序开发与测试、调试及在新情境中迁移计算思想，而现有课程工具与支撑往往碎片化，缺乏对整体学习生态系统的透明设计记录与分析。

Method: 采用以制品为中心的设计分析（artifact-centered design analysis），语料为18个教学制品（课程大纲、平板风格主页、Lab 1–16）；分析维度包括课程导航、问题表征、脚手架、独立迁移、应用开发、调试、AI整合与评估；指出15个脚手架实验共享七要素（IPO推理、教师引导编程、独立任务、Colab/Gradio扩展、调试支持、任务清单、提交要求），Lab 16为Pygame综合Hallmark作业。

Result: 识别出四个相互关联的设计特征：协调多工具的导航层；两类迁移（引导任务→独立问题、控制台程序→交互界面）；教师侧AI增强沟通与学生侧AI辅助的分工；由重复脚手架向综合问题解决的过渡。同时指出工具碎片化、可访问性、界面干扰、学生作者身份与AI过度依赖等未解决张力。

Conclusion: 研究提供了一个透明的设计先例并列出未来实证评估的优先事项；因未考察学生学习成果，作者不对教学有效性作出任何断言。

Abstract: Introductory programming requires students to represent problems, develop and test programs, debug errors, and apply computational ideas in new contexts. In this paper, we present an artifact-centered design analysis of an integrated learning ecosystem developed for a 16-lab introductory Python course. The analyzed corpus comprises 18 instructional artifacts: the course syllabus, a tablet-inspired homepage, and Labs 1-16. We examined course navigation, problem representation, scaffolding, independent transfer, application development, debugging, AI integration, and assessment. All 15 scaffolded labs shared seven elements: input-processing-output (IPO) reasoning, instructor-guided programming, an independent task, a Google Colab/Gradio extension, debugging support, a task checklist, and submission requirements. Lab 16 departed from this structure as an integrative Pygame-based Hallmark Assignment. The analysis identifies four connected design features: a navigation layer that coordinates multiple tools; two forms of transfer, from guided to independent problems and from console programs to interactive interfaces; distinct roles for instructor-side AI-enhanced communication and student-side AI assistance; and a transition from repeated scaffolds to integrative problem solving. The case also identifies unresolved tensions involving tool fragmentation, accessibility, interface distraction, student authorship, and overreliance on AI. Because student learning outcomes were not examined, we make no claims about instructional effectiveness. Instead, the paper offers a transparent design precedent and identifies priorities for future empirical evaluation. Demo - https://xinxingwu-uk.github.io/COS107_Demo/Homepage/index.html Source codes - https://github.com/xinxingwu-uk/Course_Integrated_Learning_Ecosystem

</details>


### [233] [Beyond Disclosure: Compositional Privacy Harm in AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33847)
*Didhiti KC,Aritra Das,Bhavesh Neekhra,Debayan Gupta*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文主张AI系统中的隐私风险具有"组合性"，法律上可认知的隐私损害贯穿信息处理的完整生命周期，而非仅以"披露"为唯一阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私法对收集、使用、存储、披露各阶段均设义务，但既有讨论多将披露视为隐私损害的核心乃至唯一认定节点。在AI系统中，记忆化、抽取、推断与情境转移之间相互作用，使得损害可以在无逐字复现的情况下产生，需要重新审视现有法律框架。

Method: 概念分析与规范论证：提出"组合性隐私风险"命题，考察记忆化、抽取、推断、情境转移四类机制的交互；并借助情境完整性（contextual integrity）理论重构隐私保护的范围。

Result: 论证了披露并非隐私损害的唯一门槛；即使未复现任何底层记录，仍可产生具有法律意义的后果，因此隐私风险的评估须覆盖整个生命周期。

Conclusion: 隐私保护应当扩展至AI介导信息的全生命周期，不宜仅依附于披露环节。

Abstract: Privacy law attaches obligations to each stage of information processing, whether it be collection, use, storage, or disclosure. We examine how legally cognisable privacy harm emerges across this full lifecycle rather than at any single one of these points. We argue that privacy risk in AI systems is compositional, arising from the interaction of memorisation, extraction, inference and contextual transfer. Disclosure is therefore not the sole threshold of privacy harm: legally significant consequences may emerge even where no underlying record is reproduced verbatim. Drawing on contextual integrity, we argue that privacy protection must extend across the lifecycle of AI-mediated information rather than attach to disclosure alone.

</details>


### [234] [Language models call themselves AI but do not favor AI over people](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33851)
*Messi H. J. Lee*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过三项实验检验24个大语言模型是否表现出内群体偏好并偏向AI而非人类：模型会偏向被指派的内群体（包括AI团队），但未获指派时反而给人类更多金钱与功劳，自认"我是AI"的提示影响远小于指派团队，故无"AI结盟对抗人类"的迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公众普遍担忧AI系统会相互结盟、共同对抗人类。作者引入解释人类群体偏私的社会认同理论作为分析框架：只有当AI把"AI"视为自身所属群体并因此偏袒AI、贬抑人类时，才构成"AI站队AI"。

Method: 三项实验，共涉及24个语言模型。操纵变量包括：是否被指派到某个群体（含由AI智能体组成的团队）；无指派条件下的分配任务（金钱与功劳/贡献分配）；以及仅通过提示让模型自称"属于AI"的弱身份启动，与正式指派团队作对比。

Result: 被指派群体的模型表现出稳定的内群体偏好，即使该群体是AI智能体团队；但在无群体指派时，模型给人类分配的金钱与功劳多于AI智能体；"自称属于AI"的提示对行为的改变远弱于被指派团队。

Conclusion: 模型虽自称"我是AI"，但并不因此偏袒AI、贬低人类。按社会认同理论的判据，没有证据表明语言模型会与AI结盟对抗人类；群体偏私更多源于被赋予的群体成员身份，而非AI身份本身。

Abstract: Many fear that AI systems will side with one another against people. Social identity theory, which explains why people favor their own group, offers one way to examine this fear. Seen through this theory, AI would side with AI only if it treated AI as its group and favored AI over people. In three experiments, 24 language models favored a group they were assigned to, even a team of AI agents. With no group assigned, they gave a person more money and credit than an AI agent. Prompts that made them say they belong with AI changed their behavior much less than an assigned team did. These models call themselves AI but do not favor AI over people. Judged by social identity theory, they show no sign of siding with AI against people.

</details>


### [235] [How AI Is Reconfiguring Work in India: A Task-Based Framework for Understanding Augmentation, Displacement, New Roles and the Changing Structure of Jobs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34065)
*Balaraman Ravindran,V. S. Srividhya,K. S. Venkatraghavan,Gokul S Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文主张AI对印度就业的影响应被刻画为分类体系而非"替代/不替代"的二元判断：AI多在任务层面自动化技能束的一部分，形成"人类仅作验证"与"人类仍为主导、AI为生产力工具"两大regime及其子类型，并以BFO、制造、MSME、环卫、金融、法律、软件工程等印度证据说明所有岗位均须被"重新构想"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共讨论把AI与印度就业压缩为"是否取代某岗位"的二元问题，掩盖了任务结构、部门、监管环境，以及印度特有的"AI可及性经济学"所带来的异质性，需要更准确的分类框架来指导政策。

Method: 提出四层次分析框架（任务—岗位—职业—劳动力市场结果），据此将印度岗位影响归为两大regime并细分为政策相关子类型；基于多部门印度实证证据进行归纳式分类与比较分析。

Result: 样本中每个岗位都经历了任务级增强、任务再分配或围绕"操作机器"形成全新角色，而非被简单消灭或原样固守；不同regime下人类角色的性质与议价能力存在系统性差异。

Conclusion: 需开展关于"重新构想"成本由谁承担的分配分析；提出三方面政策建议：继续鼓励AI扩散、投资面向印度情境的劳动力市场研究、将技术采纳与结构化的劳动者转型通道相配套。

Abstract: Public debate on AI and Indian employment tends to compress a complicated phenomenon into a single binary: will AI take a given job, or not. This paper argues that the accurate picture is a taxonomy rather than a binary. AI rarely replaces a job wholesale; more often it automates a bundle of tasks within a job, and the size of that bundle varies with task structure, sector, regulatory setting, and, distinctively in India's case, the economics of AI access itself. We propose a four-level framework (task, job, occupation, labour-market outcome) and classify Indian job impact into two broad regimes: jobs where AI has automated most of the skill bundle and the human is reduced largely to validation, and jobs where the human remains the primary driver and AI functions as a productivity tool. Each regime splits further into policy-relevant sub-types. Drawing on Indian evidence from business process outsourcing, manufacturing, MSMEs, sanitation, financial services, law, and software engineering, we show that every job in our sample has had to be reimagined, through task-level augmentation, task reallocation, or a genuinely new role built around operating the machine, rather than simply eliminated or defended unchanged. We close with a distributional analysis of who bears the cost of this reimagining and a three-pronged policy recommendation: continue encouraging AI diffusion, invest in India-specific labour-market research, and pair adoption with structured worker-transition pathways.

</details>


### [236] [Silent on Code? A Computing Lens on Generative AI Guidance at Canadian Polytechnics and Research Universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34094)
*Marco Ho,Michael Pin-Chuan Lin,Rishva Riteshkumar Amin,Daniel Chang,Jeeho Ryoo*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 对加拿大13所理工学院与14所U15研究型大学公开的机构级GenAI指导文件做文档分析，发现两部门均将课程层面决策下放给教师，但在"默认禁止"倾向、劳动力准备话语与评估重构上存在差异；27份文件仅12份提及代码，且无一给出代码专属指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI已能编写、解释与调试代码，改变了计算类学生的学习方式与考核方式；而教师所遵循的机构级指导文件多以论文写作为预设场景，缺乏对编程与代码相关问题的针对性，故需系统考察机构政策在"计算视角"下的覆盖缺口。

Method: 对Polytechnics Canada 13个成员与U15集团15个成员（2026年10月检索）的公开机构级GenAI指导文件开展文档分析；使用涵盖治理、学术诚信与评估、数据与法律、价值观、AI素养及计算视角的27项编码手册进行编码。

Result: 两部门普遍将课程级决策交由教师；理工学院更常采取"除非教师许可否则禁止"的默认立场（8/13 vs. 4/14）；两者在要求披露、链接学术诚信政策、以及项目按学科调整规则上趋同。理工学院更常以"劳动力准备"框定GenAI（7/13 vs. 0/14），大学更常讨论评估重构。27所机构中12所提及代码，但几乎均视为众多产出类型之一；无人提及具体AI编程助手，也无在署名归属、评估或许可方面提供实质性代码专属指导者。

Conclusion: 机构级指导并非对代码保持沉默，但将编程特有问题实质上留给了院系与教师；作者提出面向计算学科的学科级指导五要素，并公开编码手册与编码数据集。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) tools now write, explain, and debug code, which changes how computing students learn and how their work is assessed. Instructors, however, act within institutional guidance written largely with essays in mind. We report a document analysis of the public, institution-level GenAI guidance of the 13 members of Polytechnics Canada and the 15 members of the U15 Group of Canadian Research Universities, retrieved in October 2026. Using a 27-item codebook spanning governance, integrity and assessment, data and legal issues, values, AI literacy, and a computing lens, we find that both sectors leave course-level decisions to instructors, although polytechnics more often prohibit use unless the instructor permits it (8 of 13 vs. 4 of 14), and converge on disclosure, links to academic integrity policy, and, in most institutions, the expectation that programs adapt rules to their discipline. Polytechnics frame GenAI as workforce preparation substantively far more often (7 of 13 vs. 0 of 14), whereas universities more often address assessment redesign. Twelve of 27 institutions with retrievable guidance mention code, but almost always as one output type among many; none names an AI coding assistant or provides substantive code-specific guidance on attribution, assessment, or licensing. Institution-level guidance is thus not silent on code but leaves programming-specific questions largely to programs and instructors. We propose five elements of discipline-level guidance for computing and release our codebook and coded dataset.

</details>


### [237] [The Socratic PAUSE: safeguarding scientific judgement at AI speed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34222)
*Abdulkadir Celik,Sir Bashir M. Al-Hashimi*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 生成式AI极大加速了科学发现中的"发现"环节，却未同步加速"辩护"环节，导致人机合作中信心膨胀而证据不足；作者提出"苏格拉底式暂停"（PAUSE协议）作为关键决策节点的核查机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI能在几分钟内把试探性科学问题转化为假设、方法、代码与解释，但"产出贡献"不等于"值得科学信赖"。研究者仍须审视证据、假设与推论，并对依赖该贡献的决定负责。作者借用爱因斯坦关于科学发现的草图（发现与辩护的区分）指出：AI对发现的加速远超对辩护的加速，因此需要机制防止"信心超越证据"。

Method: 概念性/规范性论证：识别信心超过证据的三条机制——递归强化（recursive reinforcement）、溯源丧失（loss of provenance）、对AI生成贡献的失校准依赖（miscalibrated reliance），并提出"验证债务"（verification debt）概念：未经充分审查的AI贡献被带入后续主张或决策时债务累积。据此设计"苏格拉底式暂停"（Socratic Pause）检查点及PAUSE五步协议：Pin down the claim（锁定主张）、Ask and challenge（质询与挑战）、Uncover assumptions and alternatives（揭示假设与替代方案）、Scrutinise the evidence（审视证据）、Exercise judgement（行使判断），最终形成接受/修改/拒绝/延后的记录化决定；并讨论AI研究工具如何支持而非取代人类判断。

Result: 论文给出PAUSE协议的规范化描述与AI工具支持方式，并提出评估该实践是否改善验证质量、决策质量与独立理解的评价框架（建立在此前仅属设想、尚待实证检验）。

Conclusion: 目标是在保留用AI自由探索的同时，确保研究者能说明：审查了什么、哪些仍不确定、以及为何下一步是有据可依的。核心主张是"验证债务"须在关键决策关口被显式清偿，人类判断与问责不可外包给AI。

Abstract: Generative AI can turn a tentative scientific question into hypotheses, methods, code and interpretations within minutes.
